// === src/capabilities/scenario.ts ===
/**
 * Scenario Capability — generate client-facing quote presentation and manage
 * backup activation logic.
 *
 * Per spec: capabilities/scenario.md
 *
 * Responsibilities:
 * 1. Build a QuotePresentation object from DealContext (route, aircraft, price,
 *    safety verification, validity window)
 * 2. Pre-rank backup from sourcing.backupOperator — never present unless needed
 * 3. Substitution logic when primary falls through:
 *    - auto_within_10pct: auto-present backup if price within 10%
 *    - always_approve: pause for client approval
 *    - cancel_if_unavailable: close deal as failed
 * 4. Timer alert thresholds at 70% and 90% of validity window
 *
 * The orchestrator writes sentAt/timerStarted/clientDecision into
 * DealContext.presentation. This capability returns that shape.
 */

import type { DealContext, SubstitutionPref } from "../schemas/deal-context.js";
import type { ScenarioResult } from "../orchestrator/index.js";
import { QUOTE_VALIDITY } from "../config.js";
import { ScenarioError } from "../utils/errors.js";

// ── Quote Presentation (client-facing content) ───────────────────────────

export interface QuotePresentation {
  /** Route summary. */
  route: {
    departure: string;    // ICAO
    arrival: string;      // ICAO
    date: string;         // ISO date
    isRoundTrip: boolean;
  };
  /** Aircraft details. */
  aircraft: {
    type: string;
    tail: string | null;
    year: number | null;
    operatorName: string;
  } | null;
  /** All-in price — no hidden fees. */
  pricing: {
    totalAllIn: number;           // OSA price (includes margin)
    includes: string[];            // what's covered
    validUntil: string;            // ISO datetime
    validityMinutes: number;
  };
  /** Safety verification from Guardian. */
  safety: {
    operatorName: string;
    guardianVerified: boolean;
    guardianStatus: string;        // "clear" | "warning"
  };
  /** Passenger info. */
  passengers: number;
  /** Special requirements confirmed. */
  specialRequirements: string[];
}

/**
 * Build the client-facing presentation from deal context.
 * Returns null if required data is missing (operator, quote, trip).
 */
export function buildPresentation(ctx: DealContext): QuotePresentation | null {
  const { trip, quote, sourcing, guardian } = ctx;

  // Reject if critical data is missing
  if (!sourcing.selectedOperator) return null;
  if (!quote.osaPrice) return null;
  if (!quote.validUntil) return null;
  if (!trip.departure || !trip.arrival) return null;

  const validUntilMs = new Date(quote.validUntil).getTime();
  const nowMs = Date.now();
  const validityMinutes = Math.max(0, Math.round((validUntilMs - nowMs) / 60_000));

  return {
    route: {
      departure: trip.departure,
      arrival: trip.arrival,
      date: trip.date,
      isRoundTrip: trip.isRoundTrip,
    },
    aircraft: quote.aircraft
      ? {
          type: quote.aircraft.type,
          tail: quote.aircraft.tail,
          year: quote.aircraft.year,
          operatorName: sourcing.selectedOperator.operatorName,
        }
      : {
          type: sourcing.selectedOperator.aircraftType,
          tail: sourcing.selectedOperator.aircraftTail,
          year: null,
          operatorName: sourcing.selectedOperator.operatorName,
        },
    pricing: {
      totalAllIn: quote.osaPrice,
      includes: ["crew", "fuel", "landing fees", "FBO handling", "taxes"],
      validUntil: quote.validUntil,
      validityMinutes,
    },
    safety: {
      operatorName: sourcing.selectedOperator.operatorName,
      guardianVerified: guardian.currentStatus !== "blocked",
      guardianStatus: guardian.currentStatus,
    },
    passengers: trip.pax,
    specialRequirements: trip.specialRequirements,
  };
}

// ── Timer Alerts ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export type TimerAlertLevel = "none" | "amber" | "red" | "expired";

export interface TimerStatus {
  level: TimerAlertLevel;
  elapsedPercent: number;
  remainingMinutes: number;
}

/**
 * Check the timer status relative to the validity window.
 * - <70%: "none"
 * - 70-89%: "amber" — alert Phelipe via Mission Control
 * - 90-99%: "red" — urgent + prepare backup activation
 * - ≥100%: "expired" — follow substitution logic
 */
export function checkTimer(
  timerStarted: string,
  validUntil: string,
  now: Date = new Date(),
): TimerStatus {
  const startMs = new Date(timerStarted).getTime();
  const endMs = new Date(validUntil).getTime();
  const nowMs = now.getTime();

  const totalMs = endMs - startMs;
  if (totalMs <= 0) {
    return { level: "expired", elapsedPercent: 1, remainingMinutes: 0 };
  }

  const elapsedMs = nowMs - startMs;
  const elapsedPercent = elapsedMs / totalMs;
  const remainingMs = Math.max(0, endMs - nowMs);
  const remainingMinutes = Math.round(remainingMs / 60_000);

  if (elapsedPercent >= 1) {
    return { level: "expired", elapsedPercent: 1, remainingMinutes: 0 };
  }
  if (elapsedPercent >= QUOTE_VALIDITY.urgentAtPercent) {
    return { level: "red", elapsedPercent, remainingMinutes };
  }
  if (elapsedPercent >= QUOTE_VALIDITY.alertAtPercent) {
    return { level: "amber", elapsedPercent, remainingMinutes };
  }
  return { level: "none", elapsedPercent, remainingMinutes };
}

// ── Backup Activation Logic ──────────────────────────────────────────────

export type BackupAction =
  | { action: "auto_present"; reason: string }
  | { action: "pause_for_approval"; reason: string }
  | { action: "cancel_deal"; reason: string }
  | { action: "no_backup"; reason: string };

/**
 * Determine what to do when the primary falls through.
 *
 * Checks client.substitutionPref:
 * - auto_within_10pct: auto-present backup if backup price within 10% of primary
 * - always_approve: pause pipeline, call client for approval
 * - cancel_if_unavailable: close deal as failed
 * - null (new client): default to pause_for_approval
 */
export function determineBackupAction(
  ctx: DealContext,
  backupOsaPrice: number | null,
): BackupAction {
  const { sourcing, client, quote } = ctx;

  if (!sourcing.backupOperator) {
    return { action: "no_backup", reason: "No backup operator was ranked" };
  }

  if (backupOsaPrice === null) {
    return { action: "pause_for_approval", reason: "Backup operator available but no price yet — call for quote" };
  }

  const pref: SubstitutionPref | null = client.substitutionPref;
  const primaryPrice = quote.osaPrice;

  switch (pref) {
    case "auto_within_10pct": {
      if (primaryPrice && backupOsaPrice <= primaryPrice * 1.10) {
        return {
          action: "auto_present",
          reason: `Backup price $${backupOsaPrice.toLocaleString()} within 10% of primary $${primaryPrice.toLocaleString()}. Auto-presenting.`,
        };
      }
      return {
        action: "pause_for_approval",
        reason: `Backup price $${backupOsaPrice.toLocaleString()} exceeds 10% threshold of primary $${primaryPrice?.toLocaleString() ?? "unknown"}. Needs client approval.`,
      };
    }

    case "always_approve":
      return {
        action: "pause_for_approval",
        reason: `Client preference: always approve substitutions. Presenting backup $${backupOsaPrice.toLocaleString()} for approval.`,
      };

    case "cancel_if_unavailable":
      return {
        action: "cancel_deal",
        reason: "Client preference: cancel if primary unavailable",
      };

    default:
      // No preference (new client) → default to human approval
      return {
        action: "pause_for_approval",
        reason: `New client, no substitution preference. Call client to present backup at $${backupOsaPrice.toLocaleString()}.`,
      };
  }
}

// ── Main Entry Point ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────

/**
 * Generate the scenario / presentation for the current deal.
 *
 * Returns the DealContext["presentation"] shape. The orchestrator writes
 * sentAt, timerStarted, and clientDecision into the context after calling this.
 */
export async function scenario(ctx: DealContext): Promise<ScenarioResult> {
  // Build the presentation — validates that all required data exists
  const presentation = buildPresentation(ctx);

  if (!presentation) {
    const missing = [
      !ctx.sourcing.selectedOperator && "no operator selected",
      !ctx.quote.osaPrice && "no OSA price",
      !ctx.quote.validUntil && "no validity window",
      !ctx.trip.departure && "no departure",
      !ctx.trip.arrival && "no arrival",
    ].filter(Boolean).join(", ");
    throw new ScenarioError(`Missing data for quote presentation: ${missing}`);
  }

  // Return the presentation tracking fields — content is in the presentation object
  return {
    presentation: {
      sentAt: null,           // orchestrator fills on send
      viewedAt: null,
      clientDecision: null,   // orchestrator sets to "pending"
      decisionAt: null,
      timerStarted: null,     // orchestrator fills on send
    },
  };
}

// Re-export ScenarioError from shared errors module
export { ScenarioError } from "../utils/errors.js";
// === src/capabilities/guardian.ts ===
/**
 * Guardian Capability — continuous validation at every pipeline stage.
 * Runs stage-specific checks. Protects client, operator, and OSA.
 *
 * Per spec: capabilities/guardian.md
 *
 * CRITICAL: Guardian NEVER auto-corrects. It flags. Humans decide.
 * CRITICAL: Guardian NEVER returns "passed" if any check is critical + failed.
 *
 * Bugs fixed from old simulator (~/osa/simulator/src/agents/guardian.ts):
 * - checkRange (line 224): was a no-op that always returned pass. Now uses
 *   haversine to compute actual distance and compare against aircraft range
 *   with 10% safety margin.
 * - Warning double-count (line 361): determineVerdict counted warnings from
 *   both score.warning AND flags — but flags are generated from the same
 *   checklist warnings. Now uses only the checks array directly.
 */

import type { DealContext, GuardianCheck, GuardianPass } from "../schemas/deal-context.js";
import type { GuardianResult } from "../orchestrator/index.js";
import { haversine } from "../utils/haversine.js";
import { MARGIN_POLICY, ESTIMATE_TOLERANCE, GUARDIAN_THRESHOLDS } from "../config.js";

// ── Reference Data ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Embedded from ~/osa/simulator/src/fixtures/aircraft-performance.json
// and ~/osa/simulator/src/fixtures/airports.json.
// In production these come from Aviapages API / operator CRM.

export interface AircraftPerf {
  type: string;
  category: string;
  range_nm: number;
  runway_required_ft: number;
  passenger_capacity: number;
}

export interface AirportData {
  icao: string;
  latitude: number;
  longitude: number;
  longest_runway_ft: number;
  customs_capable: boolean;
}

/** Known aircraft performance data — source of truth for Guardian checks. */
export const AIRCRAFT_PERF: Record<string, AircraftPerf> = {
  "Citation CJ3+":       { type: "Citation CJ3+",       category: "light",            range_nm: 2040, runway_required_ft: 3180, passenger_capacity: 7 },
  "Phenom 300E":         { type: "Phenom 300E",         category: "light",            range_nm: 2010, runway_required_ft: 3209, passenger_capacity: 8 },
  "Learjet 75 Liberty":  { type: "Learjet 75 Liberty",  category: "light",            range_nm: 2040, runway_required_ft: 4250, passenger_capacity: 7 },
  "King Air 350i":       { type: "King Air 350i",       category: "light",            range_nm: 1806, runway_required_ft: 3300, passenger_capacity: 9 },
  "Citation Latitude":   { type: "Citation Latitude",   category: "midsize",          range_nm: 2700, runway_required_ft: 3580, passenger_capacity: 9 },
  "Gulfstream G280":     { type: "Gulfstream G280",     category: "super_mid",        range_nm: 3600, runway_required_ft: 4750, passenger_capacity: 10 },
  "Challenger 300":      { type: "Challenger 300",      category: "super_mid",        range_nm: 3100, runway_required_ft: 4810, passenger_capacity: 9 },
  "Citation X+":         { type: "Citation X+",         category: "super_mid",        range_nm: 3070, runway_required_ft: 5250, passenger_capacity: 8 },
  "Gulfstream G550":     { type: "Gulfstream G550",     category: "heavy",            range_nm: 6750, runway_required_ft: 5910, passenger_capacity: 14 },
  "Global 7500":         { type: "Global 7500",         category: "ultra_long_range", range_nm: 7700, runway_required_ft: 5800, passenger_capacity: 17 },
};

/** Default performance by aircraft class — fallback when specific type unknown. */
const CLASS_DEFAULTS: Record<string, AircraftPerf> = {
  light:            { type: "light (default)",            category: "light",            range_nm: 1800, runway_required_ft: 3500, passenger_capacity: 7 },
  midsize:          { type: "midsize (default)",          category: "midsize",          range_nm: 2700, runway_required_ft: 4000, passenger_capacity: 9 },
  super_mid:        { type: "super_mid (default)",        category: "super_mid",        range_nm: 3200, runway_required_ft: 5000, passenger_capacity: 9 },
  heavy:            { type: "heavy (default)",            category: "heavy",            range_nm: 6000, runway_required_ft: 5500, passenger_capacity: 14 },
  ultra_long_range: { type: "ultra_long_range (default)", category: "ultra_long_range", range_nm: 7500, runway_required_ft: 5800, passenger_capacity: 17 },
};

const SAFETY_MARGINS = GUARDIAN_THRESHOLDS.safetyMargins;

// ── Lookup Helpers ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────

/**
 * Resolve aircraft performance from type string or aircraft class.
 * Tries exact match first, then class default.
 */
export function resolveAircraftPerf(
  aircraftType: string | null,
  aircraftClass: string | null,
): AircraftPerf | null {
  if (aircraftType && AIRCRAFT_PERF[aircraftType]) {
    return AIRCRAFT_PERF[aircraftType]!;
  }
  if (aircraftClass && CLASS_DEFAULTS[aircraftClass]) {
    return CLASS_DEFAULTS[aircraftClass]!;
  }
  return null;
}

// ── Check Constructors ───────────────────────────────────────────────────

function passCheck(name: string, message: string): GuardianCheck {
  return { name, passed: true, severity: "info", message };
}

function warnCheck(name: string, message: string): GuardianCheck {
  return { name, passed: true, severity: "warning", message };
}

function criticalFail(name: string, message: string): GuardianCheck {
  return { name, passed: false, severity: "critical", message };
}

// ── Stage-Specific Checks ────────────────────────────────────────────────

/**
 * After INTAKE: is the trip physically possible?
 * - Departure airport exists
 * - Arrival airport exists
 * - Runway sufficient at both airports
 * - Range feasible (haversine + safety margin)
 * - Pax within aircraft class capacity
 * - Date in the future
 */
export function checksAfterIntake(
  ctx: DealContext,
  airports: Map<string, AirportData>,
): GuardianCheck[] {
  const checks: GuardianCheck[] = [];
  const { trip } = ctx;

  // Departure airport
  const dep = airports.get(trip.departure);
  if (!trip.departure) {
    checks.push(criticalFail("departure_airport", "No departure airport specified"));
  } else if (!dep) {
    checks.push(warnCheck("departure_airport", `Departure ${trip.departure} not in airport database. Cannot validate runway or range.`));
  } else {
    checks.push(passCheck("departure_airport", `Departure ${trip.departure} found`));
  }

  // Arrival airport
  const arr = airports.get(trip.arrival);
  if (!trip.arrival) {
    checks.push(criticalFail("arrival_airport", "No arrival airport specified"));
  } else if (!arr) {
    checks.push(warnCheck("arrival_airport", `Arrival ${trip.arrival} not in airport database. Cannot validate runway or range.`));
  } else {
    checks.push(passCheck("arrival_airport", `Arrival ${trip.arrival} found`));
  }

  // Resolve aircraft performance
  const perf = resolveAircraftPerf(trip.aircraftType, trip.aircraftClass);

  // Runway checks (only if we have both airport and aircraft data)
  if (perf) {
    const requiredFt = Math.ceil(perf.runway_required_ft * SAFETY_MARGINS.runway);

    if (dep) {
      if (dep.longest_runway_ft < requiredFt) {
        checks.push(criticalFail(
          "runway_departure",
          `${trip.departure} runway ${dep.longest_runway_ft}ft too short. ${perf.type} needs ${requiredFt}ft (${perf.runway_required_ft}ft + 10% margin).`,
        ));
      } else {
        checks.push(passCheck(
          "runway_departure",
          `${trip.departure} runway ${dep.longest_runway_ft}ft adequate for ${perf.type} (needs ${requiredFt}ft)`,
        ));
      }
    }

    if (arr) {
      if (arr.longest_runway_ft < requiredFt) {
        checks.push(criticalFail(
          "runway_arrival",
          `${trip.arrival} runway ${arr.longest_runway_ft}ft too short. ${perf.type} needs ${requiredFt}ft (${perf.runway_required_ft}ft + 10% margin).`,
        ));
      } else {
        checks.push(passCheck(
          "runway_arrival",
          `${trip.arrival} runway ${arr.longest_runway_ft}ft adequate for ${perf.type} (needs ${requiredFt}ft)`,
        ));
      }
    }

    // Range check — FIX for old bug at line 224.
    // Old implementation was a no-op that always returned pass.
    // Now: compute haversine distance, compare with aircraft range minus 10% safety margin.
    if (dep && arr) {
      const distanceNm = haversine(dep.latitude, dep.longitude, arr.latitude, arr.longitude);
      const safeRange = perf.range_nm / SAFETY_MARGINS.range; // usable range after IFR reserve

      if (distanceNm > safeRange) {
        checks.push(criticalFail(
          "range",
          `Route distance ${Math.round(distanceNm)}nm exceeds ${perf.type} safe range ${Math.round(safeRange)}nm (max ${perf.range_nm}nm minus 10% IFR reserve)`,
        ));
      } else {
        const margin = ((safeRange - distanceNm) / safeRange * 100).toFixed(0);
        checks.push(passCheck(
          "range",
          `Route distance ${Math.round(distanceNm)}nm within ${perf.type} safe range ${Math.round(safeRange)}nm (${margin}% margin)`,
        ));
      }
    }

    // Pax capacity
    if (trip.pax > 0) {
      if (trip.pax > perf.passenger_capacity) {
        checks.push(criticalFail(
          "pax_capacity",
          `${trip.pax} passengers exceeds ${perf.type} capacity of ${perf.passenger_capacity}`,
        ));
      } else if (trip.pax === perf.passenger_capacity) {
        checks.push(warnCheck(
          "pax_capacity",
          `At max capacity (${trip.pax}/${perf.passenger_capacity}). No room for additional passengers.`,
        ));
      } else {
        checks.push(passCheck(
          "pax_capacity",
          `${trip.pax} passengers, ${perf.type} capacity ${perf.passenger_capacity}. ${perf.passenger_capacity - trip.pax} seats margin.`,
        ));
      }
    }
  } else if (trip.aircraftType || trip.aircraftClass) {
    checks.push(warnCheck("aircraft_data", `Unknown aircraft "${trip.aircraftType ?? trip.aircraftClass}". Cannot validate runway, range, or capacity.`));
  }

  // Date in the future
  if (trip.date) {
    const tripDate = new Date(trip.date);
    const now = new Date();
    now.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0);
    if (tripDate < now) {
      checks.push(criticalFail("date_future", `Trip date ${trip.date} is in the past`));
    } else {
      checks.push(passCheck("date_future", `Trip date ${trip.date} is valid`));
    }
  } else {
    checks.push(criticalFail("date_future", "No trip date specified"));
  }

  return checks;
}

/**
 * After ESTIMATE: does client budget overlap the price range?
 */
export function checksAfterEstimate(ctx: DealContext): GuardianCheck[] {
  const checks: GuardianCheck[] = [];
  const { client, estimate } = ctx;

  if (!estimate.priceRange) {
    checks.push(warnCheck("estimate_available", "No price estimate available. Cannot validate budget overlap."));
    return checks;
  }

  checks.push(passCheck("estimate_available", `Estimate range $${estimate.priceRange.low.toLocaleString()}-$${estimate.priceRange.high.toLocaleString()}`));

  // Suspiciously low estimate (possible API error)
  if (estimate.priceRange.low < GUARDIAN_THRESHOLDS.estimateSanityFloor) {
    checks.push(warnCheck("estimate_sanity", `Estimate floor $${estimate.priceRange.low} unusually low. Possible API error.`));
  }

  if (!client.budget) {
    checks.push(warnCheck("budget_overlap", "Client budget not set. Cannot check overlap."));
    return checks;
  }

  // Budget must overlap estimate range
  // Client max below estimate low = budget mismatch
  if (client.budget.max < estimate.priceRange.low) {
    checks.push(criticalFail(
      "budget_overlap",
      `Client budget ceiling $${client.budget.max.toLocaleString()} is below estimate floor $${estimate.priceRange.low.toLocaleString()}`,
    ));
  } else if (client.budget.min > estimate.priceRange.high) {
    // Client min above estimate high — unusual but not blocking
    checks.push(warnCheck(
      "budget_overlap",
      `Client minimum $${client.budget.min.toLocaleString()} above estimate ceiling $${estimate.priceRange.high.toLocaleString()}. Client expects premium options.`,
    ));
  } else {
    checks.push(passCheck(
      "budget_overlap",
      `Client budget $${client.budget.min.toLocaleString()}-$${client.budget.max.toLocaleString()} overlaps estimate $${estimate.priceRange.low.toLocaleString()}-$${estimate.priceRange.high.toLocaleString()}`,
    ));
  }

  return checks;
}

/**
 * After SOURCING: is the selected operator safe and certified?
 */
export function checksAfterSourcing(
  ctx: DealContext,
  operatorCerts?: Map<string, OperatorCertData>,
): GuardianCheck[] {
  const checks: GuardianCheck[] = [];
  const { sourcing } = ctx;

  if (!sourcing.selectedOperator) {
    checks.push(criticalFail("operator_selected", "No operator selected"));
    return checks;
  }

  const op = sourcing.selectedOperator;
  checks.push(passCheck("operator_selected", `Selected operator: ${op.operatorName} (${op.baseAirport})`));

  // Check certifications if data available
  const cert = operatorCerts?.get(op.operatorId);
  if (cert) {
    // Active AOC / Part 135
    if (cert.part135Active) {
      checks.push(passCheck("part135_cert", `${op.operatorName} Part 135 certificate active`));
    } else {
      checks.push(criticalFail("part135_cert", `${op.operatorName} Part 135 certificate NOT active`));
    }

    // Insurance current
    if (cert.insuranceCurrent) {
      checks.push(passCheck("insurance", `${op.operatorName} insurance confirmed current`));
    } else if (cert.insuranceCurrent === false) {
      checks.push(criticalFail("insurance", `${op.operatorName} insurance expired or not confirmed`));
    } else {
      checks.push(warnCheck("insurance", `${op.operatorName} insurance status unverified`));
    }

    // Safety rating
    if (cert.argusRating === "platinum" || cert.argusRating === "gold") {
      checks.push(passCheck("safety_rating", `${op.operatorName} ARGUS ${cert.argusRating}`));
    } else if (cert.wyvernRating === "wingman" || cert.wyvernRating === "approved") {
      checks.push(passCheck("safety_rating", `${op.operatorName} Wyvern ${cert.wyvernRating}`));
    } else if (cert.argusRating === "silver") {
      checks.push(warnCheck("safety_rating", `${op.operatorName} ARGUS Silver. Below preferred minimum.`));
    } else {
      checks.push(warnCheck("safety_rating", `${op.operatorName} no recognized safety rating (ARGUS/Wyvern)`));
    }
  } else {
    // No cert data — flag as warning, don't block (day 1-10 cold start)
    checks.push(warnCheck("part135_cert", `Certification data not available for ${op.operatorName}. Verify manually.`));
    checks.push(warnCheck("insurance", `Insurance data not available for ${op.operatorName}. Verify manually.`));
  }

  // Aircraft type match
  if (ctx.trip.aircraftType && op.aircraftType !== ctx.trip.aircraftType) {
    checks.push(warnCheck(
      "aircraft_match",
      `Client requested ${ctx.trip.aircraftType}, operator offering ${op.aircraftType}`,
    ));
  }

  return checks;
}

/**
 * After QUOTE: is the operator quote within reasonable range of estimate?
 */
export function checksAfterQuote(ctx: DealContext): GuardianCheck[] {
  const checks: GuardianCheck[] = [];
  const { quote, estimate } = ctx;

  if (!quote.operatorPrice) {
    checks.push(warnCheck("quote_available", "No operator quote entered yet"));
    return checks;
  }

  checks.push(passCheck("quote_available", `Operator quote: $${quote.operatorPrice.toLocaleString()}`));

  // Quote vs estimate sanity (25% tolerance per spec)
  if (estimate.priceRange) {
    const estimateMedian = (estimate.priceRange.low + estimate.priceRange.high) / 2;
    const delta = Math.abs(quote.operatorPrice - estimateMedian) / estimateMedian;

    if (delta > ESTIMATE_TOLERANCE.warningThreshold) {
      checks.push(warnCheck(
        "quote_vs_estimate",
        `Operator quote $${quote.operatorPrice.toLocaleString()} is ${(delta * 100).toFixed(0)}% off estimate median $${Math.round(estimateMedian).toLocaleString()} (threshold: ${ESTIMATE_TOLERANCE.warningThreshold * 100}%)`,
      ));
    } else {
      checks.push(passCheck(
        "quote_vs_estimate",
        `Operator quote within ${(delta * 100).toFixed(0)}% of estimate median (threshold: ${ESTIMATE_TOLERANCE.warningThreshold * 100}%)`,
      ));
    }
  }

  // Margin check (8-15% per spec, >50% is critical — pipeline halt)
  if (quote.margin !== null) {
    if (quote.margin > 0.50) {
      checks.push(criticalFail(
        "margin_policy",
        `Margin ${(quote.margin * 100).toFixed(1)}% exceeds 50% hard limit — pipeline halted`,
      ));
    } else if (quote.margin < MARGIN_POLICY.min) {
      checks.push(warnCheck(
        "margin_policy",
        `Margin ${(quote.margin * 100).toFixed(1)}% below minimum ${MARGIN_POLICY.min * 100}%`,
      ));
    } else if (quote.margin > MARGIN_POLICY.max) {
      checks.push(warnCheck(
        "margin_policy",
        `Margin ${(quote.margin * 100).toFixed(1)}% above maximum ${MARGIN_POLICY.max * 100}%`,
      ));
    } else {
      checks.push(passCheck(
        "margin_policy",
        `Margin ${(quote.margin * 100).toFixed(1)}% within policy (${MARGIN_POLICY.min * 100}-${MARGIN_POLICY.max * 100}%)`,
      ));
    }
  }

  // Quote validity window
  if (quote.validUntil) {
    const validMs = new Date(quote.validUntil).getTime() - Date.now();
    const validMin = Math.round(validMs / 60_000);
    if (validMin < GUARDIAN_THRESHOLDS.quoteValidityMinMinutes) {
      checks.push(warnCheck("quote_validity", `Quote validity only ${validMin} minutes. Under ${GUARDIAN_THRESHOLDS.quoteValidityMinMinutes}-minute minimum.`));
    } else {
      checks.push(passCheck("quote_validity", `Quote valid for ${validMin} minutes`));
    }
  }

  return checks;
}

/**
 * Before PRESENTATION: final sanity — everything still valid.
 */
export function checksBeforePresentation(ctx: DealContext): GuardianCheck[] {
  const checks: GuardianCheck[] = [];

  // All required data populated
  if (!ctx.sourcing.selectedOperator) {
    checks.push(criticalFail("pre_present_operator", "No operator selected. Cannot present quote."));
  }
  if (!ctx.quote.osaPrice) {
    checks.push(criticalFail("pre_present_price", "No OSA price calculated. Cannot present quote."));
  }
  if (!ctx.quote.operatorPrice) {
    checks.push(criticalFail("pre_present_quote", "No operator quote. Cannot present to client."));
  }

  // Everything present
  if (ctx.sourcing.selectedOperator && ctx.quote.osaPrice && ctx.quote.operatorPrice) {
    checks.push(passCheck("pre_present_complete", "All required deal data present for presentation"));
  }

  return checks;
}

// ── Operator Certification Data ──────────────────────────────────────────

export interface OperatorCertData {
  operatorId: string;
  part135Active: boolean;
  insuranceCurrent: boolean | null;
  argusRating: "platinum" | "gold" | "silver" | "registered" | null;
  wyvernRating: "wingman" | "approved" | null;
}

// ── Main Guardian Entry Point ────────────────────────────────────────────

export interface GuardianOptions {
  airports?: Map<string, AirportData>;
  operatorCerts?: Map<string, OperatorCertData>;
}

export async function guardian(
  stage: string,
  ctx: DealContext,
  options: GuardianOptions = {},
): Promise<GuardianResult> {
  const { airports = new Map(), operatorCerts } = options;

  let checks: GuardianCheck[];

  switch (stage) {
    case "intake":
      checks = checksAfterIntake(ctx, airports);
      break;
    case "estimated":
      checks = checksAfterEstimate(ctx);
      break;
    case "sourced":
      checks = checksAfterSourcing(ctx, operatorCerts);
      break;
    case "quoted":
      checks = checksAfterQuote(ctx);
      break;
    case "presented":
      checks = checksBeforePresentation(ctx);
      break;
    default:
      checks = [passCheck("unknown_stage", `No specific checks for stage: ${stage}`)];
  }

  // Determine pass/fail — FIX for old warning double-count bug (line 361).
  // Old code counted score.warning + flags.filter(warning) which double-counted
  // because flags were generated from the same warnings. Now we use a single
  // source: the checks array itself.
  const hasCriticalFailure = checks.some((c) => !c.passed && c.severity === "critical");
  const passed = !hasCriticalFailure;

  const pass: GuardianPass = {
    stage,
    passed,
    checks,
    timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
  };

  return { pass };
}
// === src/capabilities/transcript-processor.ts ===
/**
 * Transcript Processor — real-time entity extraction from voice calls.
 * Runs on each transcript chunk AND post-call on the full transcript.
 * Per spec: communication-layer.md FR-5, Sprint 4.
 */

import type { TranscriptChunk, DetectedEntity } from "../schemas/call.js";
import { AVIATION_VOCABULARY } from "../integrations/deepgram.js";

// ── Known aircraft types for detection ───────────────────────────────────

const AIRCRAFT_TYPES = new Set(
  AVIATION_VOCABULARY.filter((v) =>
    /^(phenom|citation|challenger|gulfstream|global|king air|learjet)/i.test(v),
  ).map((v) => v.toLowerCase()),
);

const ICAO_CODES = new Set(
  AVIATION_VOCABULARY.filter((v) => /^[A-Z]{4}$/.test(v)),
);

// ── Phonetic tail number mapping ─────────────────────────────────────────

const PHONETIC: Record<string, string> = {
  alpha: "A", bravo: "B", charlie: "C", delta: "D", echo: "E",
  foxtrot: "F", golf: "G", hotel: "H", india: "I", juliet: "J",
  kilo: "K", lima: "L", mike: "M", november: "N", oscar: "O",
  papa: "P", quebec: "Q", romeo: "R", sierra: "S", tango: "T",
  uniform: "U", victor: "V", whiskey: "W", xray: "X", yankee: "Y", zulu: "Z",
};

const SPOKEN_NUMBERS: Record<string, string> = {
  zero: "0", one: "1", two: "2", three: "3", four: "4",
  five: "5", six: "6", seven: "7", eight: "8", nine: "9",
  niner: "9", hundred: "00",
};

// ── Price patterns (shared logic with message-router extractPrice) ───────

/**
 * Extract a dollar amount from text.
 * Supports: "$28,000", "$28k", "28 thousand", "twenty-eight five" (= $28,500).
 */
export function extractPrice(text: string): number | null {
  const lower = text.toLowerCase();

  // "$XXk"
  const kMatch = lower.match(/\$\s*(\d+)\s*k\b/);
  if (kMatch) return parseInt(kMatch[1]!, 10) * 1000;

  // "XX thousand"
  const thousandMatch = lower.match(/(\d+)\s*thousand/);
  if (thousandMatch) return parseInt(thousandMatch[1]!, 10) * 1000;

  // "$XX,XXX" or "$XXXXX"
  const dollarMatch = lower.match(/\$\s*([\d,]+(?:\.\d+)?)/);
  if (dollarMatch) {
    const val = parseFloat(dollarMatch[1]!.replace(/,/g, ""));
    if (val > 100) return val;
  }

  // Spoken: "twenty-eight five" = $28,500
  const spokenMatch = lower.match(/(?:twenty|thirty|forty|fifty|sixty|seventy|eighty|ninety)[\s-](?:one|two|three|four|five|six|seven|eight|nine)\s*(?:five|hundred)?/);
  if (spokenMatch) {
    const parsed = parseSpokenPrice(spokenMatch[0]);
    if (parsed) return parsed;
  }

  return null;
}

function parseSpokenPrice(spoken: string): number | null {
  const tens: Record<string, number> = { twenty: 20, thirty: 30, forty: 40, fifty: 50, sixty: 60, seventy: 70, eighty: 80, ninety: 90 };
  const ones: Record<string, number> = { one: 1, two: 2, three: 3, four: 4, five: 5, six: 6, seven: 7, eight: 8, nine: 9 };
  const parts = spoken.toLowerCase().replace(/-/g, " ").split(/\s+/);
  if (parts.length < 2) return null;
  const t = tens[parts[0]!];
  const o = ones[parts[1]!];
  if (t == null || o == null) return null;
  const base = t + o;
  // "twenty-eight five" = $28,500. "twenty-eight hundred" = $2,800
  if (parts[2] === "five") return base * 1000 + 500;
  if (parts[2] === "hundred") return base * 100;
  return base * 1000;
}

// ── Entity Detection ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────

/**
 * Process a single transcript chunk for entity detection.
 * Runs in real-time during the call.
 *
 * @param chunk - The transcript chunk to analyze.
 * @param _accumulated - All previous chunks (for context). Unused in v1.
 * @returns Array of newly detected entities.
 */
export function processChunk(
  chunk: TranscriptChunk,
  _accumulated: TranscriptChunk[] = [],
): DetectedEntity[] {
  return detectEntities(chunk.text, chunk.speaker, chunk.timestampMs, chunk.confidence);
}

/**
 * Post-call batch extraction from the full transcript.
 * More thorough than real-time because it has full context.
 *
 * @param chunks - All transcript chunks from the call.
 * @returns All detected entities across the full transcript.
 */
export function extractAllEntities(chunks: TranscriptChunk[]): DetectedEntity[] {
  const all: DetectedEntity[] = [];
  const fullText = chunks.map((c) => c.text).join(" ");

  // Run per-chunk detection
  for (const chunk of chunks) {
    all.push(...processChunk(chunk));
  }

  // Also run on concatenated text for cross-chunk patterns
  const crossChunk = detectEntities(fullText, "unknown", 0, 0.8);
  // Deduplicate by value
  const seen = new Set(all.map((e) => `${e.type}:${e.value}`));
  for (const e of crossChunk) {
    const key = `${e.type}:${e.value}`;
    if (!seen.has(key)) {
      all.push(e);
      seen.add(key);
    }
  }

  return all;
}

function detectEntities(
  text: string,
  speaker: string,
  timestampMs: number,
  baseConfidence: number,
): DetectedEntity[] {
  const entities: DetectedEntity[] = [];
  const speakerLabel = speaker === "broker" || speaker === "counterparty" || speaker === "unknown"
    ? speaker
    : (speaker === "0" ? "broker" : "counterparty");

  // Price
  const price = extractPrice(text);
  if (price) {
    entities.push({
      type: "price",
      value: `$${price.toLocaleString()}`,
      raw: text.match(/\$[\d,k]+|\d+\s*(?:thousand|k\b)|(?:twenty|thirty|forty|fifty|sixty|seventy|eighty|ninety)[\s-]\w+/i)?.[0] ?? text,
      speaker: speakerLabel,
      confidence: baseConfidence,
      timestampMs,
    });
  }

  // Aircraft type
  const lower = text.toLowerCase();
  for (const acType of AIRCRAFT_TYPES) {
    if (lower.includes(acType)) {
      entities.push({
        type: "aircraft_type",
        value: acType.split(" ").map((w) => w.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + w.slice(1)).join(" "),
        raw: acType,
        speaker: speakerLabel,
        confidence: baseConfidence,
        timestampMs,
      });
      break;
    }
  }

  // Airport ICAO codes
  for (const icao of ICAO_CODES) {
    if (text.includes(icao)) {
      entities.push({
        type: "airport",
        value: icao,
        raw: icao,
        speaker: speakerLabel,
        confidence: baseConfidence,
        timestampMs,
      });
    }
  }

  // Tail number: "november three hundred echo lima" → N300EL, or direct "N300EL"
  const tailDirect = text.match(/\bN\d{1,5}[A-Z]{0,2}\b/);
  if (tailDirect) {
    entities.push({ type: "tail_number", value: tailDirect[0], raw: tailDirect[0], speaker: speakerLabel, confidence: baseConfidence, timestampMs });
  } else {
    const tailParsed = parsePhoneticTail(text);
    if (tailParsed) {
      entities.push({ type: "tail_number", value: tailParsed, raw: text, speaker: speakerLabel, confidence: baseConfidence * 0.8, timestampMs });
    }
  }

  // Availability
  if (/\b(?:not available|unavailable|sold|booked out)\b/i.test(text)) {
    entities.push({ type: "availability", value: "false", raw: text.match(/not available|unavailable|sold|booked out/i)?.[0] ?? "", speaker: speakerLabel, confidence: baseConfidence, timestampMs });
  } else if (/\b(?:(?:is |it's )?available|can do|can confirm)\b/i.test(text)) {
    entities.push({ type: "availability", value: "true", raw: text.match(/available|can do|can confirm/i)?.[0] ?? "", speaker: speakerLabel, confidence: baseConfidence, timestampMs });
  }

  // Hold duration
  const holdMatch = text.match(/hold\s+(?:it\s+)?(?:for\s+)?(\w+)\s*(hours?|minutes?|min)/i);
  if (holdMatch) {
    const num = SPOKEN_NUMBERS[holdMatch[1]!.toLowerCase()] ?? holdMatch[1]!;
    const unit = holdMatch[2]!.startsWith("h") ? 60 : 1;
    entities.push({ type: "hold_duration", value: `${parseInt(num, 10) * unit}min`, raw: holdMatch[0], speaker: speakerLabel, confidence: baseConfidence, timestampMs });
  }

  // Negotiation signals
  if (/\b(?:best I can do|firm|final offer|take it or leave)\b/i.test(text)) {
    entities.push({ type: "negotiation_signal", value: "firm", raw: text.match(/best I can do|firm|final offer/i)?.[0] ?? "", speaker: speakerLabel, confidence: baseConfidence, timestampMs });
  } else if (/\b(?:I (?:might|could|can) (?:be able to|do)|maybe|possibly|let me see)\b/i.test(text)) {
    entities.push({ type: "negotiation_signal", value: "flexible", raw: text.match(/might be able|could do|can do|maybe|possibly/i)?.[0] ?? "", speaker: speakerLabel, confidence: baseConfidence, timestampMs });
  } else if (/\b(?:let me check|give me a (?:minute|moment|sec)|hold on)\b/i.test(text)) {
    entities.push({ type: "negotiation_signal", value: "checking", raw: text.match(/let me check|give me a/i)?.[0] ?? "", speaker: speakerLabel, confidence: baseConfidence, timestampMs });
  }

  return entities;
}

// ── Phonetic tail number parser ──────────────────────────────────────────

function parsePhoneticTail(text: string): string | null {
  const words = text.toLowerCase().split(/\s+/);
  const novIdx = words.indexOf("november");
  if (novIdx < 0) return null;

  let tail = "N";
  for (let i = novIdx + 1; i < words.length && i < novIdx + 6; i++) {
    const w = words[i]!;
    if (PHONETIC[w]) tail += PHONETIC[w];
    else if (SPOKEN_NUMBERS[w]) tail += SPOKEN_NUMBERS[w];
    else if (/^\d$/.test(w)) tail += w;
    else break;
  }
  return tail.length >= 3 ? tail : null;
}

// ── Summary generation (post-call) ───────────────────────────────────────

export interface SummaryOptions {
  /** Injectable LLM call for testing. */
  summarize?: (transcript: string, entities: DetectedEntity[]) => Promise<string>;
}

/**
 * Generate a post-call summary from transcript + detected entities.
 * Uses Claude Haiku for summarization (or injectable mock).
 *
 * @param chunks - All transcript chunks.
 * @param entities - All detected entities.
 * @param options - Injectable summarizer for testing.
 * @returns Concise text summary.
 */
export async function generateSummary(
  chunks: TranscriptChunk[],
  entities: DetectedEntity[],
  options?: SummaryOptions,
): Promise<string> {
  const fullText = chunks.map((c) => `[${c.speaker}] ${c.text}`).join("\n");

  if (options?.summarize) {
    return options.summarize(fullText, entities);
  }

  // Default: build a structured summary without LLM
  const prices = entities.filter((e) => e.type === "price").map((e) => e.value);
  const aircraft = entities.filter((e) => e.type === "aircraft_type").map((e) => e.value);
  const availability = entities.find((e) => e.type === "availability");

  const parts: string[] = [];
  if (aircraft.length > 0) parts.push(`Aircraft discussed: ${aircraft.join(", ")}`);
  if (prices.length > 0) parts.push(`Prices mentioned: ${prices.join(", ")}`);
  if (availability) parts.push(`Availability: ${availability.value === "true" ? "confirmed" : "not available"}`);
  parts.push(`Duration: ${chunks.length} transcript segments`);

  return parts.join(". ") + ".";
}
// === src/capabilities/draft-evaluator.ts ===
/**
 * Draft Evaluator — evaluator-optimizer pattern for outbound messages.
 * Per spec: communication-layer.md FR-9, section 2.5.
 * Source: Anthropic "Building Effective Agents" — evaluator-optimizer workflow.
 *
 * Flow: Haiku evaluates → if issues → Sonnet revises → return result.
 */

import type { Message } from "../schemas/message.js";
import type { DealContext } from "../schemas/deal-context.js";
import type { EditPattern } from "../memory/edit-memory.js";

// ── Types ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export interface EvaluationResult {
  draft: string;
  wasRevised: boolean;
  issues: string[];
  originalBeforeRevision: string | null;
}

/** Injectable evaluator function (LLM call). Defaults to pass-through in tests. */
export type EvaluatorFn = (draft: string, context: EvaluatorContext) => Promise<{ issues: string[]; pass: boolean }>;

/** Injectable reviser function (LLM call). */
export type ReviserFn = (original: string, issues: string[], context: EvaluatorContext) => Promise<string>;

export interface EvaluatorContext {
  dealSummary: string;
  clientType: string;
  recipientType: string;
  editExamples: EditPattern[];
}

// ── Evaluation ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

/**
 * Summarize a deal context for the evaluator (keeps token budget small).
 */
export function summarizeDealForEval(deal: DealContext): string {
  const parts: string[] = [];
  parts.push(`Route: ${deal.trip.departure} → ${deal.trip.arrival}`);
  parts.push(`Date: ${deal.trip.date}`);
  parts.push(`Pax: ${deal.trip.pax}`);
  if (deal.trip.aircraftClass) parts.push(`Aircraft class: ${deal.trip.aircraftClass}`);
  if (deal.estimate.priceRange) {
    parts.push(`Estimate: $${deal.estimate.priceRange.low.toLocaleString()}-$${deal.estimate.priceRange.high.toLocaleString()}`);
  }
  if (deal.quote.osaPrice) parts.push(`OSA price: $${deal.quote.osaPrice.toLocaleString()}`);
  if (deal.sourcing.selectedOperator) parts.push(`Operator: ${deal.sourcing.selectedOperator.operatorName}`);
  return parts.join(". ");
}

/**
 * Get the recipient type from the message and deal context.
 */
function getRecipientType(draft: Message, deal: DealContext): string {
  const to = draft.to?.toLowerCase() ?? "";
  const clientContact = deal.client.contact?.toLowerCase() ?? "";
  if (clientContact && to.includes(clientContact)) return "client";
  return "operator";
}

export interface EvaluateDraftOptions {
  /** Injectable evaluator (Haiku). Uses code-based checks if not provided. */
  evaluator?: EvaluatorFn;
  /** Injectable reviser (Sonnet). Required if evaluator is provided. */
  reviser?: ReviserFn;
  /** Edit history for broker style context. */
  editHistory?: EditPattern[];
}

/**
 * Evaluate a draft message and optionally revise it.
 * Two-pass: Haiku evaluates, Sonnet revises if issues found.
 *
 * @param draft - The outbound message draft to evaluate.
 * @param deal - Current deal context for accuracy checks.
 * @param options - Injectable LLM functions and edit history.
 * @returns Evaluation result with original/revised draft and issues.
 */
export async function evaluateDraft(
  draft: Message,
  deal: DealContext,
  options: EvaluateDraftOptions = {},
): Promise<EvaluationResult> {
  const context: EvaluatorContext = {
    dealSummary: summarizeDealForEval(deal),
    clientType: deal.client.type,
    recipientType: getRecipientType(draft, deal),
    editExamples: options.editHistory ?? [],
  };

  // ── Code-based checks (always run, no LLM needed) ─────────────────

  const codeIssues = runCodeChecks(draft, deal);

  // ── LLM evaluation (if evaluator provided) ─────────────────────────

  let llmIssues: string[] = [];
  if (options.evaluator) {
    const evaluation = await options.evaluator(draft.body, context);
    llmIssues = evaluation.issues;
  }

  const allIssues = [...codeIssues, ...llmIssues];

  // No issues — pass through unchanged
  if (allIssues.length === 0) {
    return { draft: draft.body, wasRevised: false, issues: [], originalBeforeRevision: null };
  }

  // Issues found — revise if reviser available
  if (options.reviser) {
    const revised = await options.reviser(draft.body, allIssues, context);
    return { draft: revised, wasRevised: true, issues: allIssues, originalBeforeRevision: draft.body };
  }

  // No reviser — return with issues flagged but draft unchanged
  return { draft: draft.body, wasRevised: false, issues: allIssues, originalBeforeRevision: null };
}

// ── Code-based evaluation checks ─────────────────────────────────────────

function runCodeChecks(draft: Message, deal: DealContext): string[] {
  const issues: string[] = [];
  const body = draft.body.toLowerCase();

  // Check for unverifiable claims
  if (body.includes("wifi") && !deal.trip.specialRequirements.includes("wifi")) {
    issues.push("Draft mentions WiFi but no amenity data in deal context.");
  }
  if (body.includes("bed") && !deal.trip.specialRequirements.includes("bed")) {
    issues.push("Draft mentions bed/sleeping but no cabin config data.");
  }
  if (body.includes("catering included") && !deal.trip.specialRequirements.includes("catering")) {
    issues.push("Draft claims catering included but not in deal requirements.");
  }

  // Check pricing consistency
  if (deal.quote.osaPrice) {
    const priceStr = `$${deal.quote.osaPrice.toLocaleString()}`;
    if (body.includes("$") && !body.includes(priceStr.toLowerCase().replace(/,/g, "")) && !body.includes(priceStr.toLowerCase())) {
      // Draft mentions a price but it doesn't match OSA price — flag for review
      const draftPrice = body.match(/\$[\d,]+/);
      if (draftPrice) {
        const draftVal = parseFloat(draftPrice[0].replace(/[$,]/g, ""));
        if (Math.abs(draftVal - deal.quote.osaPrice) > 100) {
          issues.push(`Draft price ${draftPrice[0]} doesn't match OSA price ${priceStr}.`);
        }
      }
    }
  }

  return issues;
}
// === src/capabilities/quote.ts ===
/**
 * Quote Capability — two-phase pricing pipeline.
 *
 * Phase 1 (estimate): Call Aviapages Price Calculator API, apply historical
 * delta correction from DealStore memory, write estimate to DealContext.
 * Guardian runs after to check budget overlap.
 *
 * Phase 2 (real quote): Accept operator price input, calculate OSA price
 * with margin from config, write quote to DealContext. Guardian runs after
 * to check quote vs estimate sanity and margin policy.
 *
 * Per spec: capabilities/quote.md
 */

import type { DealContext } from "../schemas/deal-context.js";
import type { EstimateResult, QuoteResult } from "../orchestrator/index.js";
import type { AviapagesClient, PriceEstimateResult } from "../integrations/aviapages.js";
import type { DealStore } from "../memory/deal-store.js";
import { MARGIN_POLICY, QUOTE_VALIDITY } from "../config.js";

// ── Phase 1: Estimate ────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export interface EstimateOptions {
  /** Aviapages client — null on cold start or if API unavailable. */
  aviapages: AviapagesClient | null;
  /** DealStore for historical delta correction. */
  dealStore?: DealStore | null;
}

/**
 * Phase 1: Generate a price estimate.
 *
 * 1. Call Aviapages Price Calculator API
 * 2. Apply historical delta correction from memory
 * 3. Write to DealContext estimate section
 *
 * If Aviapages is unavailable, returns estimate with source="manual" and null range.
 */
export async function estimate(
  ctx: DealContext,
  options: EstimateOptions,
): Promise<EstimateResult> {
  const { aviapages, dealStore = null } = options;
  const { trip } = ctx;

  // Call Aviapages
  let apiResult: PriceEstimateResult | null = null;
  if (aviapages && trip.departure && trip.arrival) {
    apiResult = await aviapages.getPriceEstimate({
      departure: trip.departure,
      arrival: trip.arrival,
      aircraftType: trip.aircraftType ?? undefined,
      date: trip.date,
      pax: trip.pax,
    });
  }

  if (!apiResult) {
    // API unavailable — return empty estimate
    return {
      estimate: {
        source: "manual",
        flightTime: null,
        priceRange: null,
        fuelBurn: null,
        positioning: null,
        calculatedAt: null,
      },
    };
  }

  // Apply historical delta correction
  let adjustedLow = apiResult.low;
  let adjustedHigh = apiResult.high;

  if (dealStore && trip.departure && trip.arrival) {
    const delta = dealStore.getPricingDelta(trip.departure, trip.arrival);
    if (delta !== null) {
      // delta is a fraction: +0.12 means Aviapages underestimates by 12%
      adjustedLow = Math.round(apiResult.low * (1 + delta));
      adjustedHigh = Math.round(apiResult.high * (1 + delta));
    }
  }

  return {
    estimate: {
      source: "aviapages",
      flightTime: apiResult.flightTime > 0 ? apiResult.flightTime : null,
      priceRange: { low: adjustedLow, high: adjustedHigh },
      fuelBurn: apiResult.fuelBurn > 0 ? apiResult.fuelBurn : null,
      positioning: null,
      calculatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    },
  };
}

// ── Phase 2: Real Quote ──────────────────────────────────────────────────

export interface QuoteInput {
  /** Operator's quoted price. */
  operatorPrice: number;
  /** Target margin as decimal (0.08 = 8%). Uses config default if not provided. */
  margin?: number;
  /** Quote validity in minutes. Uses config default if not provided. */
  validityMinutes?: number;
  /** Aircraft details from operator. */
  aircraft?: { type: string; tail: string; year: number } | null;
}

/**
 * Phase 2: Process a real operator quote.
 *
 * 1. Accept operator price
 * 2. Calculate OSA price = operatorPrice / (1 - margin)
 * 3. Set validity window
 * 4. Write to DealContext quote section
 */
export async function quote(
  _ctx: DealContext,
  input: QuoteInput,
): Promise<QuoteResult> {
  const rawMargin = input.margin ?? MARGIN_POLICY.target;
  // Hard bounds: prevent division by zero (margin=1.0), negative prices, and Infinity
  const margin = Math.max(0.01, Math.min(rawMargin, 0.50));
  const validityMinutes = input.validityMinutes ?? QUOTE_VALIDITY.defaultMinutes;

  // OSA price formula per spec: operatorPrice / (1 - margin%)
  // This ensures the margin is the percentage of the final price, not a markup on cost.
  // Example: operator $28,000 at 8% margin → $28,000 / 0.92 = $30,434.78
  const osaPrice = Math.round(input.operatorPrice / (1 - margin));

  const now = new Date();
  const validUntil = new Date(now.getTime() + validityMinutes * 60_000);

  return {
    quote: {
      operatorPrice: input.operatorPrice,
      osaPrice,
      margin,
      validUntil: validUntil.toISOString(),
      aircraft: input.aircraft ?? null,
      quotedAt: now.toISOString(),
    },
  };
}

// ── Helpers ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

/**
 * Check if client budget fits the estimate range.
 * Returns null if fit, or a reason string if budget mismatch.
 * Used by the orchestrator before advancing past the estimate stage.
 */
export function checkBudgetFit(
  budget: { min: number; max: number } | null,
  priceRange: { low: number; high: number } | null,
): string | null {
  if (!budget || !priceRange) return null; // can't check — proceed
  if (budget.max < priceRange.low) {
    return `Client budget ceiling $${budget.max.toLocaleString()} is below estimate floor $${priceRange.low.toLocaleString()}`;
  }
  return null;
}

/**
 * Calculate the estimate-vs-actual delta for learning loop.
 * Returns a fraction: +0.12 means actual was 12% above estimate median.
 */
export function calcEstimateDelta(
  estimateRange: { low: number; high: number },
  actualPrice: number,
): number {
  const median = (estimateRange.low + estimateRange.high) / 2;
  if (median === 0) return 0;
  return (actualPrice - median) / median;
}
