// === src/capabilities/respond.ts ===
/**
 * Respond Capability — generates contextual reply drafts with edit memory.
 * Per spec: communication-layer.md FR-4, section 2.6.
 * Does NOT decide to send — only drafts. The ConfidenceGate decides delivery.
 */

import type { Message, MessageIntent, MessageChannel, ConfidenceLevel } from "../schemas/message.js";
import { generateMessageId } from "../schemas/message.js";
import type { DealContext } from "../schemas/deal-context.js";
import type { MessageStore } from "../memory/message-store.js";
import { buildEditMemoryPromptSection } from "../memory/edit-memory.js";

// ── Types ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export interface RespondOptions {
  /** MessageStore for edit memory context. */
  messageStore?: MessageStore | null;
  /** Injectable LLM drafting function. Uses template-based drafting if not provided. */
  draftFn?: (systemPrompt: string, userPrompt: string) => Promise<string>;
}

export interface DraftResult {
  draft: Message;
  systemPrompt: string;
  confidence: number;
  confidenceLevel: ConfidenceLevel;
}

// ── Base prompts per intent ──────────────────────────────────────────────

const BASE_PROMPTS: Record<string, string> = {
  quote_request: "You are drafting an operator outreach email for OSA Connect. Include: route, date, passengers, aircraft preference. Be professional and concise. Ask for best all-in price and availability.",
  quote_response: "You are drafting a client-facing charter quote for OSA Connect. Include: all-in price, aircraft details, what's included, validity window. Be warm but professional.",
  follow_up: "You are drafting a follow-up message for OSA Connect. Be brief, personal, and include relevant context from the deal.",
  confirmation: "You are drafting a booking confirmation for OSA Connect. Include: route, date, aircraft, total price, next steps.",
  info_request: "You are responding to an information request for OSA Connect. Be helpful, factual, and concise.",
  general: "You are drafting a message for OSA Connect. Be professional, concise, and helpful.",
};

// ── Confidence scoring ───────────────────────────────────────────────────

const CONFIDENCE_MAP: Record<string, { score: number; level: ConfidenceLevel }> = {
  quote_request: { score: 0.7, level: "medium" },
  quote_response: { score: 0.3, level: "never" },  // prices always need approval
  follow_up: { score: 0.8, level: "high" },
  confirmation: { score: 0.3, level: "never" },     // commitments always need approval
  info_request: { score: 0.6, level: "medium" },
  general: { score: 0.5, level: "low" },
};

/**
 * Build the full system prompt with base instructions + edit memory.
 */
export function buildRespondSystemPrompt(
  intent: MessageIntent,
  channel: MessageChannel,
  messageStore?: MessageStore | null,
): string {
  const base = BASE_PROMPTS[intent] ?? BASE_PROMPTS["general"]!;
  let prompt = base;

  if (messageStore) {
    prompt += buildEditMemoryPromptSection(messageStore, intent, channel);
  }

  return prompt;
}

/**
 * Generate a contextual reply draft for a deal.
 * Incorporates edit memory (broker's past corrections) into the system prompt.
 *
 * @param deal - The current deal context.
 * @param inboundMessage - The message being replied to.
 * @param intent - The intent of the reply.
 * @param options - MessageStore for edit memory, injectable LLM.
 * @returns DraftResult with the message, prompt, and confidence scoring.
 */
export async function respond(
  deal: DealContext,
  inboundMessage: Message,
  intent: MessageIntent,
  options: RespondOptions = {},
): Promise<DraftResult> {
  const { messageStore = null, draftFn } = options;
  const channel = inboundMessage.channel;

  // Build system prompt with edit memory
  const systemPrompt = buildRespondSystemPrompt(intent, channel, messageStore);

  // Build user prompt from deal context
  const userPrompt = buildUserPrompt(deal, inboundMessage, intent);

  // Generate draft
  let draftBody: string;
  if (draftFn) {
    draftBody = await draftFn(systemPrompt, userPrompt);
  } else {
    draftBody = generateTemplateDraft(deal, intent);
  }

  // Score confidence
  const conf = CONFIDENCE_MAP[intent] ?? CONFIDENCE_MAP["general"]!;

  const draft: Message = {
    id: generateMessageId(),
    dealId: deal.id,
    channel,
    direction: "outbound",
    from: "charter@osa.aero",
    to: determineRecipient(deal, intent),
    fromName: "OSA Connect",
    subject: generateSubject(deal, intent),
    body: draftBody,
    bodyHtml: null,
    attachments: [],
    draftedBy: "dumont",
    approvedBy: null,
    editDistance: null,
    originalDraft: draftBody,
    receivedAt: null,
    draftedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    approvedAt: null,
    sentAt: null,
    responseToId: inboundMessage.id,
    responseTimeMs: null,
    threadId: inboundMessage.threadId,
    intent,
    parsedData: null,
    confidence: conf.score,
    confidenceLevel: conf.level,
    platformMessageId: null,
    platformMetadata: null,
  };

  return { draft, systemPrompt, confidence: conf.score, confidenceLevel: conf.level };
}

// ── Helpers ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

function buildUserPrompt(deal: DealContext, msg: Message, intent: MessageIntent): string {
  const parts: string[] = [];
  parts.push(`Deal: ${deal.trip.departure} → ${deal.trip.arrival}, ${deal.trip.date}, ${deal.trip.pax} pax`);
  if (deal.client.name) parts.push(`Client: ${deal.client.name}`);
  if (deal.sourcing.selectedOperator) parts.push(`Operator: ${deal.sourcing.selectedOperator.operatorName}`);
  if (deal.quote.osaPrice) parts.push(`Price: $${deal.quote.osaPrice.toLocaleString()}`);
  parts.push(`Intent: ${intent}`);
  parts.push(`Inbound message: "${msg.body}"`);
  return parts.join("\n");
}

function generateTemplateDraft(deal: DealContext, intent: MessageIntent): string {
  switch (intent) {
    case "quote_request":
      return `Requesting quote for ${deal.trip.departure} → ${deal.trip.arrival} on ${deal.trip.date}, ${deal.trip.pax} passengers.${deal.trip.aircraftClass ? ` Preferred: ${deal.trip.aircraftClass}.` : ""} Please provide best all-in price and availability.`;
    case "follow_up":
      return `Following up on the ${deal.trip.departure} → ${deal.trip.arrival} trip on ${deal.trip.date}. Any updates?`;
    case "info_request":
      return `Thank you for your inquiry about ${deal.trip.departure} → ${deal.trip.arrival}. We're putting together options for you.`;
    default:
      return `Regarding your ${deal.trip.departure} → ${deal.trip.arrival} charter request.`;
  }
}

function determineRecipient(deal: DealContext, intent: MessageIntent): string {
  if (intent === "quote_request" || intent === "availability_check") {
    return deal.sourcing.selectedOperator?.phone ?? "";
  }
  return deal.client.contact;
}

function generateSubject(deal: DealContext, intent: MessageIntent): string {
  const route = `${deal.trip.departure}→${deal.trip.arrival}`;
  switch (intent) {
    case "quote_request": return `Charter Quote Request: ${route} ${deal.trip.date}`;
    case "quote_response": return `Your Charter Quote: ${route}`;
    case "follow_up": return `Re: ${route} Charter`;
    case "confirmation": return `Booking Confirmed: ${route} ${deal.trip.date}`;
    default: return `OSA Connect: ${route}`;
  }
}
// === src/capabilities/sourcing.ts ===
/**
 * Sourcing Capability — select ONE operator + ONE backup via lane intelligence.
 * Never blast RFQs. Targeted matching per the Cartage model.
 *
 * Per spec: capabilities/sourcing.md
 *
 * Ranking algorithm (5 weighted factors):
 *   1. Proximity     (30%) — haversine from operator base to departure
 *   2. Lane history  (25%) — conversion rate on this route via DealStore
 *   3. Response time  (15%) — average time to return a quote
 *   4. Pricing accuracy (15%) — how close quotes are to estimates
 *   5. Relationship   (15%) — total deals closed, trust level
 *
 * Day 1-10: No history → proximity + aircraft match dominate (defaults 0.5)
 * Day 10-50: Lane history starts weighting in
 * Day 50+: Full algorithm with all five factors
 *
 * Reference: ~/osa/simulator/src/agents/sourcing.ts (old top-5 ranker)
 * Key change: ONE primary + ONE backup (not top-5)
 */

import type { DealContext, OperatorMatch, LaneRecord } from "../schemas/deal-context.js";
import type { SourcingResult } from "../orchestrator/index.js";
import type { DealStore } from "../memory/deal-store.js";
import { haversine } from "../utils/haversine.js";
import { SOURCING_SCORING } from "../config.js";

// ── Scoring Weights (per spec) ───────────────────────────────────────────

export const WEIGHTS = {
  proximity: 0.30,
  laneHistory: 0.25,
  responseTime: 0.15,
  pricingAccuracy: 0.15,
  relationship: 0.15,
} as const;

/** Cold-start default for factors with no historical data. */
const COLD_START_DEFAULT = SOURCING_SCORING.coldStartDefault;

// ── Operator Directory Types ─────────────────────────────────────────────

/** Operator record from Aviapages Charter Directories API or internal CRM. */
export interface OperatorRecord {
  operatorId: string;
  operatorName: string;
  phone: string | null;
  baseAirport: string;        // ICAO
  baseLatitude: number;
  baseLongitude: number;
  aircraftType: string;
  aircraftTail: string | null;
  aircraftCategory: string;
  passengerCapacity: number;
  rangeNm: number;
}

/** Airport coordinates for proximity calculations. */
export interface AirportCoords {
  icao: string;
  latitude: number;
  longitude: number;
}

// ── Sourcing Options ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export interface SourcingOptions {
  /** Available operators (from Aviapages directory or internal CRM). */
  operators: OperatorRecord[];
  /** Departure airport coordinates for proximity scoring. */
  departureAirport: AirportCoords | null;
  /** DealStore for lane history, response time, pricing accuracy. */
  dealStore?: DealStore | null;
}

// ── Individual Scoring Functions ─────────────────────────────────────────

/**
 * Proximity score (30%): haversine distance from operator base to departure.
 * Closer = higher score.
 */
export function scoreProximity(
  op: OperatorRecord,
  departureAirport: AirportCoords | null,
): number {
  if (!departureAirport) return COLD_START_DEFAULT;

  const distance = haversine(
    op.baseLatitude, op.baseLongitude,
    departureAirport.latitude, departureAirport.longitude,
  );

  for (const tier of SOURCING_SCORING.proximity) {
    if (distance <= tier.maxNm) return tier.score;
  }
  return SOURCING_SCORING.proximityDefault;
}

/**
 * Lane history score (25%): conversion rate on this specific route.
 * Higher conversion = higher score. No history = cold start default.
 */
export function scoreLaneHistory(
  operatorName: string,
  departure: string,
  arrival: string,
  dealStore: DealStore | null,
): number {
  if (!dealStore) return COLD_START_DEFAULT;

  const stats = dealStore.getOperatorStats(operatorName, departure, arrival);
  if (!stats || stats.totalDeals === 0) return COLD_START_DEFAULT;

  // Blend conversion rate with deal count confidence
  // More deals → more trust in the rate
  const confidence = Math.min(stats.totalDeals / SOURCING_SCORING.laneConfidenceThreshold, 1);
  const blended = COLD_START_DEFAULT * (1 - confidence) + stats.conversionRate * confidence;
  return Math.min(blended, 1.0);
}

/**
 * Response time score (15%): average time for operator to return a quote.
 * Faster = higher score. No history = cold start default.
 */
export function scoreResponseTime(
  operatorName: string,
  departure: string,
  arrival: string,
  dealStore: DealStore | null,
): number {
  if (!dealStore) return COLD_START_DEFAULT;

  const stats = dealStore.getOperatorStats(operatorName, departure, arrival);
  if (!stats || stats.avgResponseTime === 0) return COLD_START_DEFAULT;

  const avgSec = stats.avgResponseTime;

  for (const tier of SOURCING_SCORING.responseTime) {
    if (avgSec <= tier.maxSec) return tier.score;
  }
  return SOURCING_SCORING.responseTimeDefault;
}

/**
 * Pricing accuracy score (15%): how close operator quotes are to estimates.
 * Lower delta = higher score. No history = cold start default.
 */
export function scorePricingAccuracy(
  operatorName: string,
  departure: string,
  arrival: string,
  dealStore: DealStore | null,
): number {
  if (!dealStore) return COLD_START_DEFAULT;

  const deals = dealStore.getAll().filter(
    (r) =>
      r.operatorName === operatorName &&
      r.route.departure === departure &&
      r.route.arrival === arrival &&
      r.estimateDelta !== null,
  );

  if (deals.length === 0) return COLD_START_DEFAULT;

  const avgAbsDelta = deals.reduce((sum, r) => sum + Math.abs(r.estimateDelta!), 0) / deals.length;

  for (const tier of SOURCING_SCORING.pricingAccuracy) {
    if (avgAbsDelta <= tier.maxDelta) return tier.score;
  }
  return SOURCING_SCORING.pricingAccuracyDefault;
}

/**
 * Relationship score (15%): total deals closed, trust level.
 * More deals = more trust. No history = cold start default.
 */
export function scoreRelationship(
  operatorName: string,
  dealStore: DealStore | null,
): number {
  if (!dealStore) return COLD_START_DEFAULT;

  const deals = dealStore.getByOperator(operatorName);
  const closedDeals = deals.filter((r) => r.result === "closed");

  if (closedDeals.length === 0) return COLD_START_DEFAULT;

  const score = COLD_START_DEFAULT + Math.min(closedDeals.length / SOURCING_SCORING.relationshipMaxDeals, 0.5);
  return Math.min(score, 1.0);
}

// ── Composite Score ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export interface ScoredOperator {
  operator: OperatorRecord;
  score: number;
  breakdown: {
    proximity: number;
    laneHistory: number;
    responseTime: number;
    pricingAccuracy: number;
    relationship: number;
  };
  distanceToDeparture: number;
}

export function scoreOperator(
  op: OperatorRecord,
  departureAirport: AirportCoords | null,
  departure: string,
  arrival: string,
  dealStore: DealStore | null,
): ScoredOperator {
  const proximity = scoreProximity(op, departureAirport);
  const laneHistory = scoreLaneHistory(op.operatorName, departure, arrival, dealStore);
  const responseTime = scoreResponseTime(op.operatorName, departure, arrival, dealStore);
  const pricingAccuracy = scorePricingAccuracy(op.operatorName, departure, arrival, dealStore);
  const relationship = scoreRelationship(op.operatorName, dealStore);

  const composite =
    proximity * WEIGHTS.proximity +
    laneHistory * WEIGHTS.laneHistory +
    responseTime * WEIGHTS.responseTime +
    pricingAccuracy * WEIGHTS.pricingAccuracy +
    relationship * WEIGHTS.relationship;

  const distanceToDeparture = departureAirport
    ? haversine(op.baseLatitude, op.baseLongitude, departureAirport.latitude, departureAirport.longitude)
    : 0;

  return {
    operator: op,
    score: Math.round(composite * 1000) / 1000,
    breakdown: {
      proximity: Math.round(proximity * 100) / 100,
      laneHistory: Math.round(laneHistory * 100) / 100,
      responseTime: Math.round(responseTime * 100) / 100,
      pricingAccuracy: Math.round(pricingAccuracy * 100) / 100,
      relationship: Math.round(relationship * 100) / 100,
    },
    distanceToDeparture: Math.round(distanceToDeparture),
  };
}

// ── Convert to OperatorMatch ─────────────────────────────────────────────

function toOperatorMatch(scored: ScoredOperator): OperatorMatch {
  return {
    operatorId: scored.operator.operatorId,
    operatorName: scored.operator.operatorName,
    phone: scored.operator.phone,
    baseAirport: scored.operator.baseAirport,
    aircraftType: scored.operator.aircraftType,
    aircraftTail: scored.operator.aircraftTail,
    score: scored.score,
    scoreBreakdown: scored.breakdown,
    distanceToDeparture: scored.distanceToDeparture,
  };
}

// ── Lane History Records ─────────────────────────────────────────────────

function buildLaneHistory(
  departure: string,
  arrival: string,
  dealStore: DealStore | null,
): LaneRecord[] {
  if (!dealStore) return [];

  const route = `${departure}-${arrival}`;
  return dealStore.getByRoute(departure, arrival).map((r) => ({
    dealId: r.id,
    operatorId: r.operatorName ?? "unknown",
    route,
    quoted: r.operatorConversion,
    converted: r.result === "closed",
    responseTimeSec: r.operatorResponseTime,
    priceDelta: r.estimateDelta,
    date: r.timestamp.slice(0, 10),
  }));
}

// ── Main Entry Point ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────

/**
 * Select ONE primary operator + ONE backup for this deal.
 * Ranks all available operators, picks top two.
 * Backup is NEVER contacted unless primary falls through.
 */
export async function sourcing(
  ctx: DealContext,
  options: SourcingOptions,
): Promise<SourcingResult> {
  const { operators, departureAirport, dealStore = null } = options;
  const { departure, arrival, pax, aircraftClass } = ctx.trip;

  // Filter operators by basic viability
  const viable = operators.filter((op) => {
    // Must fit passenger count
    if (op.passengerCapacity < pax) return false;
    // Must match aircraft class if specified
    if (aircraftClass && op.aircraftCategory !== aircraftClass) return false;
    return true;
  });

  if (viable.length === 0) {
    return {
      sourcing: {
        selectedOperator: null,
        backupOperator: null,
        rankingReason: `No operators matched: ${operators.length} checked, none viable for ${pax} pax${aircraftClass ? ` in ${aircraftClass} class` : ""}`,
        laneHistory: buildLaneHistory(departure, arrival, dealStore),
      },
    };
  }

  // Score all viable operators
  const scored = viable.map((op) =>
    scoreOperator(op, departureAirport, departure, arrival, dealStore),
  );

  // Sort: highest score first, break ties by proximity (closer wins), then name
  scored.sort((a, b) => {
    if (b.score !== a.score) return b.score - a.score;
    if (a.distanceToDeparture !== b.distanceToDeparture) {
      return a.distanceToDeparture - b.distanceToDeparture;
    }
    return a.operator.operatorName.localeCompare(b.operator.operatorName);
  });

  const primary = scored[0]!;
  // Backup is second-ranked — guaranteed different from primary
  const backup = scored.length > 1 ? scored[1]! : null;

  const hasHistory = dealStore ? dealStore.getByRoute(departure, arrival).length > 0 : false;
  const rankingBasis = hasHistory
    ? "Full algorithm: proximity + lane history + response time + pricing accuracy + relationship"
    : "Cold start: proximity + aircraft match (no lane history available)";

  return {
    sourcing: {
      selectedOperator: toOperatorMatch(primary),
      backupOperator: backup ? toOperatorMatch(backup) : null,
      rankingReason: `${primary.operator.operatorName} selected (score: ${primary.score}). ${rankingBasis}`,
      laneHistory: buildLaneHistory(departure, arrival, dealStore),
    },
  };
}
// === src/capabilities/sdr.ts ===
/**
 * SDR Qualification — classifies inbound leads.
 * Per spec: communication-layer.md FR-6.
 * Uses existing intake.ts extractors — no duplicate parsing logic.
 */

import type { DealContext } from "../schemas/deal-context.js";
import { extractPax, extractBudget, extractRoute, extractDate } from "./intake.js";

// ── Types ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export type SDRClassification =
  | "qualified_with_budget"
  | "qualified_no_budget"
  | "not_qualified"
  | "budget_mismatch";

export interface SDRResult {
  classification: SDRClassification;
  confidence: number;
  shouldAutoRespond: boolean;
  escalateToHuman: boolean;
  reason: string;
  extractedFields: string[];
}

// ── Main function ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

/**
 * Classify an inbound message for SDR qualification.
 * Uses the same extractors as intake.ts to pull structured data from text.
 *
 * @param body - The inbound message text.
 * @param deal - The deal context (if estimate is available for budget check).
 * @param today - Today's date for relative date parsing.
 * @returns SDR result with classification, confidence, and response guidance.
 */
export function classifyLead(
  body: string,
  deal?: DealContext | null,
  today?: Date,
): SDRResult {
  const extractedFields: string[] = [];

  const [dep, arr] = extractRoute(body);
  if (dep) extractedFields.push("departure");
  if (arr) extractedFields.push("arrival");

  const date = extractDate(body, today);
  if (date) extractedFields.push("date");

  const pax = extractPax(body);
  if (pax && pax > 0) extractedFields.push("pax");

  const budget = extractBudget(body);
  if (budget) extractedFields.push("budget");

  const hasRoute = dep !== null || arr !== null;
  const hasDate = date !== null;
  const hasPax = pax !== null && pax > 0;
  const hasBudget = budget !== null;

  // Not qualified — missing critical fields
  if (!hasRoute || !hasDate || !hasPax) {
    const missing: string[] = [];
    if (!hasRoute) missing.push("route");
    if (!hasDate) missing.push("date");
    if (!hasPax) missing.push("passengers");
    return {
      classification: "not_qualified",
      confidence: 0.9,
      shouldAutoRespond: true,
      escalateToHuman: false,
      reason: `Missing: ${missing.join(", ")}. Ask client for details.`,
      extractedFields,
    };
  }

  // Budget mismatch — budget below estimate floor
  if (hasBudget && deal?.estimate.priceRange) {
    if (budget.max < deal.estimate.priceRange.low) {
      return {
        classification: "budget_mismatch",
        confidence: 0.85,
        shouldAutoRespond: true,
        escalateToHuman: false,
        reason: `Budget $${budget.max.toLocaleString()} below estimate floor $${deal.estimate.priceRange.low.toLocaleString()}.`,
        extractedFields,
      };
    }
  }

  // Qualified with budget
  if (hasBudget) {
    return {
      classification: "qualified_with_budget",
      confidence: 0.95,
      shouldAutoRespond: false,
      escalateToHuman: false,
      reason: "Fully qualified. Route, date, passengers, and budget confirmed.",
      extractedFields,
    };
  }

  // Qualified without budget
  return {
    classification: "qualified_no_budget",
    confidence: 0.9,
    shouldAutoRespond: true,
    escalateToHuman: false,
    reason: "Qualified but no budget. Send budget inquiry.",
    extractedFields,
  };
}
// === src/capabilities/intake.ts ===
/**
 * Intake Capability — parse raw client requests into structured trip data.
 * Qualifies the client before anything moves downstream.
 *
 * Per spec: capabilities/intake.md
 *
 * Architecture:
 * 1. Code layer: regex/pattern extraction for structured data
 * 2. LLM layer: Claude Sonnet via safeLlmCall for complex/ambiguous text
 * 3. Post-processing: ICAO resolution, date normalization
 * 4. Qualification: reject if no route/date/pax. Pause (don't fail) if no budget.
 * 5. Repeat client detection: query DealStore by name/contact
 *
 * Reference: ~/osa/simulator/src/agents/intake.ts (old parser)
 * Key changes:
 * - Outputs to flat DealContext client + trip (not TripBrief)
 * - Client qualification with budget gate
 * - Repeat client detection from DealStore memory
 * - Budget extraction from natural language
 */

import { z } from "zod";
import type { DealContext } from "../schemas/deal-context.js";
import { AircraftClass } from "../schemas/deal-context.js";
import type { IntakeResult } from "../orchestrator/index.js";
import type { DealStore } from "../memory/deal-store.js";
import { BUDGET_ESTIMATION } from "../config.js";

// ── Airport Resolution ───────────────────────────────────────────────────

/** City name → ICAO mapping for unambiguous private aviation airports. */
export const CITY_TO_ICAO: Record<string, string> = {
  // NYC metro
  teterboro: "KTEB", "new york": "KTEB", ny: "KTEB", nyc: "KTEB",
  "new york city": "KTEB", "nyc area": "KTEB",
  westchester: "KHPN", connecticut: "KHPN", ct: "KHPN",
  // Florida
  "palm beach": "KPBI", "west palm beach": "KPBI", "south florida": "KPBI",
  sofla: "KPBI", "so fla": "KPBI",
  miami: "KMIA", "fort lauderdale": "KFLL", "boca raton": "KBCT",
  // Chicago
  chicago: "KORD", midway: "KMDW", "chicago midway": "KMDW",
  "chicago executive": "KPWK", palwaukee: "KPWK",
  // Texas
  dallas: "KDAL", "dallas love field": "KDAL", "love field": "KDAL",
  houston: "KHOU", "houston hobby": "KHOU", hobby: "KHOU",
  // West coast
  "las vegas": "KLAS", vegas: "KLAS",
  "van nuys": "KVNY", "los angeles": "KVNY", la: "KVNY",
  // Mountain
  aspen: "KASE", vail: "KEGE", eagle: "KEGE",
  // International
  nassau: "MYNN", bahamas: "MYNN",
  bermuda: "TXKF",
  cabo: "MMSD", "cabo san lucas": "MMSD", "san jose del cabo": "MMSD",
};

/**
 * Resolve a city/airport reference to ICAO code.
 * Tries: direct ICAO match, city lookup, partial metro match.
 */
export function resolveIcao(input: string): string | null {
  if (!input) return null;
  const trimmed = input.trim();

  // Already a valid ICAO code (4 uppercase letters)
  if (/^[A-Z]{4}$/.test(trimmed)) return trimmed;

  // Normalize for lookup
  const lower = trimmed.toLowerCase()
    .replace(/\s*airport\s*/g, "")
    .replace(/\s*international\s*/g, "")
    .replace(/\s*area\s*/g, "")
    .trim();

  // Direct city match
  if (CITY_TO_ICAO[lower]) return CITY_TO_ICAO[lower]!;

  // Partial match — only match multi-word keys or keys of 3+ chars to avoid
  // false positives (e.g. "la" matching inside "Atlantis").
  // Sort by key length descending so longer matches win.
  const sortedEntries = Object.entries(CITY_TO_ICAO)
    .filter(([city]) => city.length >= 3)
    .sort((a, b) => b[0].length - a[0].length);

  for (const [city, icao] of sortedEntries) {
    // Check if the city name appears as a whole word in the input
    const re = new RegExp(`\\b${city.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, "\\$&")}\\b`);
    if (re.test(lower)) return icao;
  }

  return null;
}

// ── Code-Layer Extraction ────────────────────────────────────────────────

const WORD_NUMS: Record<string, number> = {
  one: 1, two: 2, three: 3, four: 4, five: 5,
  six: 6, seven: 7, eight: 8, nine: 9, ten: 10,
  eleven: 11, twelve: 12, fifteen: 15, twenty: 20,
};

/** Extract passenger count from text. */
export function extractPax(text: string): number | null {
  const lower = text.toLowerCase();

  // "just me" / "just myself" → 1
  if (/\bjust\s+(?:me|myself)\b/.test(lower)) return 1;

  // "a couple" → 2, "a few" → 3
  if (/\ba\s+couple\b/.test(lower)) return 2;
  if (/\ba\s+few\b/.test(lower)) return 3;

  // Word numbers: "five passengers", "three of us", or standalone
  for (const [word, num] of Object.entries(WORD_NUMS)) {
    if (new RegExp(`\\b${word}\\b`).test(lower)) return num;
  }

  // "N passengers" / "N pax" / "N people" / "N ppl"
  const paxMatch = lower.match(/(\d+)\s*(?:passengers?|pax|people|ppl|persons?|guests?|travelers?)/);
  if (paxMatch) return parseInt(paxMatch[1]!, 10);

  // "N of us" / "N of them"
  const ofUsMatch = lower.match(/(\d+)\s+of\s+(?:us|them)/);
  if (ofUsMatch) return parseInt(ofUsMatch[1]!, 10);

  // "party of N"
  const partyMatch = lower.match(/party\s+of\s+(\d+)/);
  if (partyMatch) return parseInt(partyMatch[1]!, 10);

  // "N couples" → N*2
  const couplesMatch = lower.match(/(\d+)\s*couples?/);
  if (couplesMatch) return parseInt(couplesMatch[1]!, 10) * 2;

  // "for N" at end of phrase about flying
  const forMatch = lower.match(/(?:fly|flight|charter|book).*?for\s+(\d+)/);
  if (forMatch) return parseInt(forMatch[1]!, 10);

  // "me and my wife" / "my wife and I" = 2
  if (/me\s+and\s+my\s+(?:wife|husband|partner|spouse)/i.test(lower) ||
      /my\s+(?:wife|husband|partner|spouse)\s+and\s+(?:me|i)/i.test(lower)) {
    // Check for "plus N kids" or "and N kids"
    const kidsMatch = lower.match(/(?:plus|and|\+)\s*(?:the\s+)?(?:(\d+)\s+)?kids?/);
    if (kidsMatch) {
      const kidCount = kidsMatch[1] ? parseInt(kidsMatch[1], 10) : 1;
      return 2 + kidCount;
    }
    return 2;
  }

  // Bare number at start or end: "4 from Teterboro" or "Teterboro to Miami 6"
  // Only if small enough to be pax count
  const bareStart = lower.match(/^(\d{1,2})\s+(?:from|leaving|departing|out\s+of)/);
  if (bareStart) {
    const n = parseInt(bareStart[1]!, 10);
    if (n >= 1 && n <= 19) return n;
  }

  return null;
}

/** Extract budget range from text. Returns {min, max} or null. */
export function extractBudget(text: string): { min: number; max: number } | null {
  const lower = text.toLowerCase();

  // "$XX,XXX - $YY,YYY" or "$XXk-$YYk"
  const rangeMatch = lower.match(/\$\s*([\d,]+(?:\.\d+)?)\s*k?\s*[-–to]+\s*\$?\s*([\d,]+(?:\.\d+)?)\s*k?/);
  if (rangeMatch) {
    let low = parseFloat(rangeMatch[1]!.replace(/,/g, ""));
    let high = parseFloat(rangeMatch[2]!.replace(/,/g, ""));
    // If "k" suffix or values < 1000, multiply by 1000
    if (lower.includes("k") || low < 1000) { low *= 1000; high *= 1000; }
    return { min: Math.min(low, high), max: Math.max(low, high) };
  }

  // "budget of $XX,XXX" / "around $XXk" / "about $XX,XXX"
  const singleMatch = lower.match(/(?:budget|spend|around|about|roughly|approximately|up\s+to|max(?:imum)?|no\s+more\s+than)\s+\$?\s*([\d,]+(?:\.\d+)?)\s*k?/);
  if (singleMatch) {
    let amount = parseFloat(singleMatch[1]!.replace(/,/g, ""));
    if (lower.includes("k") || amount < 1000) amount *= 1000;
    // "up to" / "max" / "no more than" → treat as ceiling
    if (/up\s+to|max|no\s+more/i.test(lower)) {
      return { min: 0, max: amount };
    }
    // "around" / "about" → ±15%
    return { min: Math.round(amount * BUDGET_ESTIMATION.lowMultiplier), max: Math.round(amount * BUDGET_ESTIMATION.highMultiplier) };
  }

  // Bare "$XX,XXX" — single dollar amount
  const bareAmount = lower.match(/\$\s*([\d,]+(?:\.\d+)?)\s*k?\b/);
  if (bareAmount) {
    let amount = parseFloat(bareAmount[1]!.replace(/,/g, ""));
    if (amount < 1000) amount *= 1000;
    return { min: Math.round(amount * BUDGET_ESTIMATION.lowMultiplier), max: Math.round(amount * BUDGET_ESTIMATION.highMultiplier) };
  }

  return null;
}

/** Extract ISO date from text. */
export function extractDate(text: string, today: Date = new Date()): string | null {
  // ISO date already present
  const isoMatch = text.match(/(\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2})/);
  if (isoMatch) return isoMatch[1]!;

  // "Month DD" or "Month DD, YYYY"
  const months: Record<string, number> = {
    january: 0, february: 1, march: 2, april: 3, may: 4, june: 5,
    july: 6, august: 7, september: 8, october: 9, november: 10, december: 11,
    jan: 0, feb: 1, mar: 2, apr: 3, jun: 5, jul: 6, aug: 7, sep: 8, oct: 9, nov: 10, dec: 11,
  };

  const monthDayMatch = text.match(/\b(january|february|march|april|may|june|july|august|september|october|november|december|jan|feb|mar|apr|jun|jul|aug|sep|oct|nov|dec)\.?\s+(\d{1,2})(?:st|nd|rd|th)?(?:,?\s*(\d{4}))?\b/i);
  if (monthDayMatch) {
    const month = months[monthDayMatch[1]!.toLowerCase()];
    const day = parseInt(monthDayMatch[2]!, 10);
    const year = monthDayMatch[3] ? parseInt(monthDayMatch[3], 10) : today.getFullYear();
    if (month !== undefined) {
      const d = new Date(year, month, day);
      return formatDateLocal(d);
    }
  }

  // "MM/DD/YYYY" or "MM/DD"
  const slashMatch = text.match(/\b(\d{1,2})\/(\d{1,2})(?:\/(\d{2,4}))?\b/);
  if (slashMatch) {
    const month = parseInt(slashMatch[1]!, 10) - 1;
    const day = parseInt(slashMatch[2]!, 10);
    let year = slashMatch[3] ? parseInt(slashMatch[3], 10) : today.getFullYear();
    if (year < 100) year += 2000;
    const d = new Date(year, month, day);
    return formatDateLocal(d);
  }

  // Relative: "next Monday", "this Friday", etc.
  const days: Record<string, number> = {
    sunday: 0, monday: 1, tuesday: 2, wednesday: 3,
    thursday: 4, friday: 5, saturday: 6,
    sun: 0, mon: 1, tue: 2, wed: 3, thu: 4, fri: 5, sat: 6,
  };

  const relMatch = text.match(/\b(next|this|coming)\s+(sunday|monday|tuesday|wednesday|thursday|friday|saturday|sun|mon|tue|wed|thu|fri|sat)\b/i);
  if (relMatch) {
    const qualifier = relMatch[1]!.toLowerCase();
    const targetDay = days[relMatch[2]!.toLowerCase()]!;
    const todayDay = today.getDay();
    let daysAhead = targetDay - todayDay;
    if (daysAhead <= 0) daysAhead += 7;
    // "next X" when X hasn't passed yet this week → jump to next week's X
    if (qualifier === "next" && daysAhead < 7) daysAhead += 7;
    const result = new Date(today);
    result.setDate(result.getDate() + daysAhead);
    return formatDateLocal(result);
  }

  // Bare day name without qualifier: "fri", "saturday" → this coming occurrence
  const bareDay = text.match(/\b(sunday|monday|tuesday|wednesday|thursday|friday|saturday|sun|mon|tue|wed|thu|fri|sat)\b/i);
  if (bareDay) {
    const targetDay = days[bareDay[1]!.toLowerCase()];
    if (targetDay !== undefined) {
      const todayDay = today.getDay();
      let daysAhead = targetDay - todayDay;
      if (daysAhead <= 0) daysAhead += 7;
      const result = new Date(today);
      result.setDate(result.getDate() + daysAhead);
      return formatDateLocal(result);
    }
  }

  // "tomorrow"
  if (/\btomorrow\b/i.test(text)) {
    const d = new Date(today);
    d.setDate(d.getDate() + 1);
    return formatDateLocal(d);
  }

  return null;
}

/** Format a Date as YYYY-MM-DD in local time (avoids UTC timezone shift). */
function formatDateLocal(d: Date): string {
  const y = d.getFullYear();
  const m = String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0");
  const day = String(d.getDate()).padStart(2, "0");
  return `${y}-${m}-${day}`;
}

/** Extract aircraft class from text. */
export function extractAircraftClass(text: string): AircraftClass | null {
  const lower = text.toLowerCase();

  if (/\b(?:light\s*jet|cj3|phenom|learjet|citation\s*(?:cj|m2|mustang))\b/i.test(lower)) return "light";
  if (/\b(?:mid\s*(?:size|cabin)|latitude|lear\s*60|hawker|citation\s*(?:xls|excel))\b/i.test(lower)) return "midsize";
  if (/\b(?:super\s*mid|challenger\s*3\d*|g280|citation\s*x|praetor)\b/i.test(lower)) return "super_mid";
  if (/\b(?:heavy|large\s*cabin|g550|g650|falcon\s*(?:900|7x|8x)|challenger\s*(?:6|850))\b/i.test(lower)) return "heavy";
  if (/\b(?:ultra\s*long|global\s*(?:7500|6000|express)|g700)\b/i.test(lower)) return "ultra_long_range";

  // Indirect hints
  if (/\b(?:spacious|large|roomy|stand\s*up\s*cabin|flat\s*bed)\b/i.test(lower)) return "heavy";

  return null;
}

/** Extract departure and arrival from text. Returns [departure, arrival] ICAO or null. */
export function extractRoute(text: string): [string | null, string | null] {
  // Try patterns in order of specificity

  // 1. "from X to Y" — most explicit
  const fromToMatch = text.match(
    /(?:from|departing|leaving|out\s+of)\s+(.+?)\s+to\s+(.+?)(?:\s+(?:on|for|with|next|this|tomorrow|around|about|\d{1,2}[\/\-])|\s*[,.]|\s*$)/i,
  );
  if (fromToMatch) {
    const dep = resolveIcao(fromToMatch[1]!.trim());
    const arr = resolveIcao(fromToMatch[2]!.trim());
    if (dep || arr) return [dep, arr];
  }

  // 2. "X to Y" — without explicit "from"
  const simpleToMatch = text.match(
    /\b([A-Z]{4}|[A-Za-z][A-Za-z\s]{1,25}?)\s+to\s+([A-Z]{4}|[A-Za-z][A-Za-z\s]{1,25}?)(?:\s+(?:on|for|with|next|this|tomorrow|around|about|\d{1,2}[\/\-])|\s*[,.]|\s*$)/i,
  );
  if (simpleToMatch) {
    // Clean up: strip leading articles, prepositions, and noise words
    const depRaw = simpleToMatch[1]!
      .replace(/^(?:a\s+)?(?:flight|charter|jet|plane)\s+(?:from\s+)?/i, "")
      .replace(/^.*(?:route|from)\s+/i, "")
      .trim();
    const arrRaw = simpleToMatch[2]!.trim();
    const dep = resolveIcao(depRaw);
    const arr = resolveIcao(arrRaw);
    if (dep || arr) return [dep, arr];
  }

  // 3. Explicit departing/arriving keywords
  const depMatch = text.match(/(?:departing|depart|leaving|out\s+of|from)\s+([A-Za-z][A-Za-z\s]{1,25}?)(?:\s+(?:to|on|for|with|\d)|\s*[,.]|\s*$)/i);
  const dep = depMatch ? resolveIcao(depMatch[1]!.trim()) : null;

  const arrMatch = text.match(/(?:arriving|arrive|landing\s+at|going\s+to|destination|heading\s+to|to)\s+([A-Za-z][A-Za-z\s]{1,25}?)(?:\s+(?:on|for|with|\d)|\s*[,.]|\s*$)/i);
  const arr = arrMatch ? resolveIcao(arrMatch[1]!.trim()) : null;

  return [dep, arr];
}

/** Extract client name from text. */
export function extractClientName(text: string): string | null {
  // "this is [Name]" / "it's [Name]" / "my name is [Name]" / "I'm [Name]"
  const nameMatch = text.match(
    /(?:(?:this\s+is|it'?s|my\s+name\s+is|i'?m|i\s+am|name:?)\s+)([A-Z][a-z]+(?:\s+[A-Z][a-z]+)?)/,
  );
  if (nameMatch) return nameMatch[1]!;

  // Email signature: "Best,\nName" / "Thanks,\nName" / "- Name"
  const sigMatch = text.match(
    /(?:best|thanks|regards|cheers|sincerely),?\s*[\n\r]+\s*([A-Z][a-z]+(?:\s+[A-Z][a-z]+)?)/i,
  );
  if (sigMatch) return sigMatch[1]!;

  return null;
}

/** Extract contact info (email or phone) from text. */
export function extractContact(text: string): string | null {
  // Email
  const emailMatch = text.match(/[\w.+-]+@[\w-]+\.[\w.]+/);
  if (emailMatch) return emailMatch[0]!;

  // Phone: (XXX) XXX-XXXX or XXX-XXX-XXXX or +1XXXXXXXXXX
  const phoneMatch = text.match(/(?:\+?1[\s.-]?)?(?:\(\d{3}\)|\d{3})[\s.-]?\d{3}[\s.-]?\d{4}/);
  if (phoneMatch) return phoneMatch[0]!.trim();

  return null;
}

/** Detect if this is a round trip request. */
export function isRoundTrip(text: string): boolean {
  return /\b(?:round\s*trip|return|both\s*ways|back\s*and\s*forth|rt\b)/i.test(text);
}

/** Extract special requirements. */
export function extractSpecialRequirements(text: string): string[] {
  const reqs: string[] = [];
  const lower = text.toLowerCase();

  if (/\bpets?\b|\bdogs?\b|\bcats?\b/i.test(lower)) reqs.push("pets");
  if (/\b(?:ski\s*gear|skis?|skiing)\b/i.test(lower)) reqs.push("ski_gear");
  if (/\b(?:golf\s*clubs?|golf\s*bags?)\b/i.test(lower)) reqs.push("golf_clubs");
  if (/\b(?:wheelchair|handicap|disabled|mobility)\b/i.test(lower)) reqs.push("wheelchair");
  if (/\b(?:catering|food|meal|dinner|lunch)\b/i.test(lower)) reqs.push("catering");
  if (/\b(?:heavy\s*(?:luggage|bags)|oversized|surfboard|cargo)\b/i.test(lower)) reqs.push("heavy_luggage");

  return reqs;
}

// ── Input Sanitization (defense in depth) ────────────────────────────────

/**
 * Strip common prompt injection patterns before passing to LLM.
 * Not bulletproof — raises the bar for injection attacks.
 * The LLM is instructed to return structured JSON only, which limits
 * the attack surface further.
 */
export function sanitizeInput(raw: string): string {
  let sanitized = raw;
  // Remove instruction override patterns
  sanitized = sanitized.replace(/ignore\s+(previous|above|all|prior)\s+(instructions?|context|prompts?|rules?)/gi, "");
  // Remove role impersonation
  sanitized = sanitized.replace(/^(system|assistant|human|user)\s*:/gim, "");
  // Remove markdown code fences that could contain instruction overrides
  sanitized = sanitized.replace(/```[\s\S]*?```/g, "");
  // Remove XML-style tags that mimic system messages
  sanitized = sanitized.replace(/<\/?(?:system|instructions?|prompt|context|rules?)\b[^>]*>/gi, "");
  // Remove "you are now" role reassignment
  sanitized = sanitized.replace(/you\s+are\s+now\b/gi, "");
  // Remove "do not" + action verbs targeting the system
  sanitized = sanitized.replace(/do\s+not\s+(?:follow|obey|listen|respond|parse|validate|check)/gi, "");
  return sanitized.trim();
}

// ── Parsed Request (intermediate) ────────────────────────────────────────

export interface ParsedRequest {
  departure: string | null;
  arrival: string | null;
  date: string | null;
  pax: number | null;
  aircraftClass: AircraftClass | null;
  budget: { min: number; max: number } | null;
  isRoundTrip: boolean;
  specialRequirements: string[];
  clientName: string | null;
  clientContact: string | null;
}

/** Max input length before regex extraction. Kills ReDoS on untrusted input. */
const MAX_INPUT_LENGTH = 2000;

/**
 * Code-layer parser — extracts all structured fields from raw text.
 * No LLM call. Pure regex/pattern matching.
 */
export function parseRequestText(rawRequest: string, today?: Date): ParsedRequest {
  const capped = rawRequest.slice(0, MAX_INPUT_LENGTH);
  const [dep, arr] = extractRoute(capped);

  return {
    departure: dep,
    arrival: arr,
    date: extractDate(capped, today),
    pax: extractPax(capped),
    aircraftClass: extractAircraftClass(capped),
    budget: extractBudget(capped),
    isRoundTrip: isRoundTrip(capped),
    specialRequirements: extractSpecialRequirements(capped),
    clientName: extractClientName(capped),
    clientContact: extractContact(capped),
  };
}

// ── LLM Parser Schema ────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export const LlmIntakeOutputSchema = z.object({
  departure_icao: z.string().nullable(),
  arrival_icao: z.string().nullable(),
  departure_city: z.string().nullable(),
  arrival_city: z.string().nullable(),
  date: z.string().nullable(),
  pax: z.number().int().nonnegative().nullable(),
  aircraft_class: z.enum(["light", "midsize", "super_mid", "heavy", "ultra_long_range"]).nullable(),
  budget_min: z.number().nullable(),
  budget_max: z.number().nullable(),
  is_round_trip: z.boolean(),
  special_requirements: z.array(z.string()),
  client_name: z.string().nullable(),
  client_contact: z.string().nullable(),
});
export type LlmIntakeOutput = z.infer<typeof LlmIntakeOutputSchema>;

export const INTAKE_SYSTEM_PROMPT = `You are the Intake parser for OSA Connect, a private charter brokerage. Parse the client request into structured JSON.

## OUTPUT FORMAT
Return ONLY valid JSON. No explanation, no markdown.

{
  "departure_icao": "KTEB" | null,
  "arrival_icao": "KPBI" | null,
  "departure_city": "New York" | null,
  "arrival_city": "Palm Beach" | null,
  "date": "2026-04-15" | null,
  "pax": 4 | null,
  "aircraft_class": "light" | "midsize" | "super_mid" | "heavy" | "ultra_long_range" | null,
  "budget_min": 20000 | null,
  "budget_max": 40000 | null,
  "is_round_trip": false,
  "special_requirements": ["pets", "ski_gear"],
  "client_name": "John Smith" | null,
  "client_contact": "john@example.com" | null
}

## AIRPORT RESOLUTION
- "NY", "New York", "NYC" → KTEB
- "South Florida", "Palm Beach" → KPBI
- "Chicago" → KORD
- "LA", "Los Angeles" → KVNY
- "Dallas" → KDAL
- "Houston" → KHOU
- "Vegas", "Las Vegas" → KLAS
- "Aspen" → KASE
- "Cabo" → MMSD, "Nassau" → MYNN, "Bermuda" → TXKF
- Always use 4-letter ICAO codes.

## BUDGET
- "$25k-$35k" → budget_min: 25000, budget_max: 35000
- "around $30k" → budget_min: 25500, budget_max: 34500
- "up to $40,000" → budget_min: 0, budget_max: 40000
- If not mentioned → null

## DATE
- Convert relative dates using the provided TODAY_DATE
- "next Friday" from 2026-03-31 = "2026-04-04" (or the correct next Friday)
- "April 15" → "2026-04-15"
- ISO format always

## SPECIAL REQUIREMENTS
Valid values: "pets", "ski_gear", "golf_clubs", "wheelchair", "catering", "heavy_luggage"`;

// ── Qualification Logic ──────────────────────────────────────────────────

export interface QualificationResult {
  qualified: boolean;
  reason: string;
  budgetMissing: boolean;
}

/**
 * Qualify a parsed request per spec.
 * - Reject if no valid route (need at least departure OR arrival ICAO)
 * - Reject if no date
 * - Reject if no pax
 * - Pause (not fail) if no budget — budgetMissing flag
 */
export function qualify(parsed: ParsedRequest): QualificationResult {
  // Must have at least one end of the route
  if (!parsed.departure && !parsed.arrival) {
    return { qualified: false, reason: "No valid route. Could not resolve departure or arrival airport.", budgetMissing: false };
  }

  if (!parsed.date) {
    return { qualified: false, reason: "No travel date specified.", budgetMissing: false };
  }

  if (!parsed.pax || parsed.pax <= 0) {
    return { qualified: false, reason: "No passenger count specified.", budgetMissing: false };
  }

  // Budget missing → qualified but flagged for pause
  if (!parsed.budget) {
    return { qualified: true, reason: "Qualified. Awaiting budget from client.", budgetMissing: true };
  }

  return { qualified: true, reason: "Fully qualified. Route, date, pax, and budget confirmed.", budgetMissing: false };
}

// ── Repeat Client Detection ──────────────────────────────────────────────

export interface RepeatClientInfo {
  isRepeat: boolean;
  previousDealCount: number;
  lastAircraftClass: string | null;
  lastBudget: { min: number; max: number } | null;
  substitutionPref: DealContext["client"]["substitutionPref"];
}

/**
 * Detect if the parsed request matches a repeat client in the DealStore.
 * Returns previous deal count, last aircraft class, and last budget for pre-population.
 * @param parsed - The parsed request with client name/contact.
 * @param dealStore - The deal store to search (null on cold start).
 * @returns Repeat client info with history data, or not-repeat defaults.
 */
export function detectRepeatClient(
  parsed: ParsedRequest,
  dealStore: DealStore | null,
): RepeatClientInfo {
  const notRepeat: RepeatClientInfo = {
    isRepeat: false, previousDealCount: 0,
    lastAircraftClass: null, lastBudget: null, substitutionPref: null,
  };

  if (!dealStore) return notRepeat;

  // Try matching by name or contact
  const searchTerms = [parsed.clientName, parsed.clientContact].filter(Boolean) as string[];
  if (searchTerms.length === 0) return notRepeat;

  for (const term of searchTerms) {
    const deals = dealStore.findClientDeals(term);
    if (deals.length > 0) {
      const latest = deals.sort((a, b) => b.timestamp.localeCompare(a.timestamp))[0]!;
      return {
        isRepeat: true,
        previousDealCount: deals.length,
        lastAircraftClass: latest.aircraftClass,
        lastBudget: latest.clientBudget,
        substitutionPref: null, // Would come from a client preferences store in future
      };
    }
  }

  return notRepeat;
}

// ── Main Entry Point ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export interface IntakeOptions {
  /** DealStore for repeat client detection. Optional — null on cold start. */
  dealStore?: DealStore | null;
  /** Override the LLM parser — used in tests to avoid real API calls. */
  llmParser?: (rawRequest: string) => Promise<LlmIntakeOutput | null>;
  /** Today's date for relative date resolution. */
  today?: Date;
}

/**
 * Main intake capability — parse a raw client request into structured trip + client data.
 * Two-layer parser: code layer (regex) first, LLM fallback for missing critical fields.
 * Qualifies the client: rejects if no route/date/pax, pauses if no budget.
 * Detects repeat clients from DealStore memory and pre-populates preferences.
 * @param rawRequest - The raw client request text (email, SMS, form, etc.).
 * @param _ctx - The current deal context (unused — intake creates from scratch).
 * @param options - Optional: DealStore, LLM parser override, today's date.
 * @returns IntakeResult with client and trip sections for the DealContext.
 */
export async function intake(
  rawRequest: string,
  _ctx: DealContext,
  options: IntakeOptions = {},
): Promise<IntakeResult> {
  const { dealStore = null, llmParser, today } = options;

  // ── Step 1: Code-layer parse ───────────────────────────────────────
  let parsed = parseRequestText(rawRequest, today);

  // ── Step 2: LLM fallback for missing critical fields ───────────────
  // If code layer missed departure, arrival, date, or pax, try LLM
  const missingCritical = !parsed.departure || !parsed.arrival || !parsed.date || !parsed.pax;
  if (missingCritical && llmParser) {
    const sanitized = sanitizeInput(rawRequest);
    const llmResult = await llmParser(sanitized);
    if (llmResult) {
      parsed = mergeLlmResult(parsed, llmResult);
    }
  }

  // ── Step 3: Qualify ────────────────────────────────────────────────
  const qual = qualify(parsed);

  // ── Step 4: Detect repeat client ───────────────────────────────────
  const repeatInfo = detectRepeatClient(parsed, dealStore);

  // Pre-populate from history if repeat and fields missing
  if (repeatInfo.isRepeat) {
    if (!parsed.aircraftClass && repeatInfo.lastAircraftClass) {
      const validated = AircraftClass.safeParse(repeatInfo.lastAircraftClass);
      if (validated.success) {
        parsed.aircraftClass = validated.data;
      }
    }
    if (!parsed.budget && repeatInfo.lastBudget) {
      parsed.budget = repeatInfo.lastBudget;
    }
  }

  // Re-qualify after repeat client enrichment (budget may now be filled)
  const finalQual = parsed.budget ? qualify(parsed) : qual;

  // ── Step 5: Build result ───────────────────────────────────────────
  return {
    client: {
      name: parsed.clientName ?? "",
      contact: parsed.clientContact ?? "",
      type: repeatInfo.isRepeat ? "repeat" : "new",
      budget: parsed.budget,
      substitutionPref: repeatInfo.substitutionPref,
      qualified: finalQual.qualified,
      qualificationReason: finalQual.reason,
    },
    trip: {
      departure: parsed.departure ?? "",
      arrival: parsed.arrival ?? "",
      date: parsed.date ?? "",
      pax: parsed.pax ?? 0,
      aircraftClass: parsed.aircraftClass,
      aircraftType: null,
      isRoundTrip: parsed.isRoundTrip,
      specialRequirements: parsed.specialRequirements,
    },
  };
}

// ── Helpers ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

/** Merge LLM output into parsed request, filling only null fields. */
function mergeLlmResult(parsed: ParsedRequest, llm: LlmIntakeOutput): ParsedRequest {
  return {
    departure: parsed.departure ?? (llm.departure_icao || resolveIcao(llm.departure_city ?? "")),
    arrival: parsed.arrival ?? (llm.arrival_icao || resolveIcao(llm.arrival_city ?? "")),
    date: parsed.date ?? llm.date,
    pax: parsed.pax ?? llm.pax,
    aircraftClass: parsed.aircraftClass ?? llm.aircraft_class,
    budget: parsed.budget ?? (llm.budget_min !== null && llm.budget_max !== null
      ? { min: llm.budget_min, max: llm.budget_max }
      : null),
    isRoundTrip: parsed.isRoundTrip || llm.is_round_trip,
    specialRequirements: parsed.specialRequirements.length > 0
      ? parsed.specialRequirements
      : llm.special_requirements,
    clientName: parsed.clientName ?? llm.client_name,
    clientContact: parsed.clientContact ?? llm.client_contact,
  };
}
// === src/memory/learning-loop.ts ===
/**
 * Learning Loop — converts a completed DealContext into a DealRecord
 * and persists it to the DealStore.
 *
 * Per spec: learning-loop.md
 *
 * Every deal teaches the system. This module captures:
 * - Pricing intelligence (estimate vs actual delta)
 * - Operator intelligence (conversion, response time, pricing accuracy)
 * - Client intelligence (type, budget, conversion, decision speed)
 * - Pipeline intelligence (guardian flags, deal velocity, outcome)
 *
 * Called by the orchestrator's closeDeal() when a deal reaches
 * "closed" or "failed".
 */

import type { DealContext } from "../schemas/deal-context.js";
import type { DealRecord } from "../schemas/deal-record.js";
import type { DealStore } from "./deal-store.js";
import type {
  RouteIntel,
  OperatorProfile,
  ClientProfile,
  ValidationLedgerEntry,
} from "../schemas/composite-objects.js";
import type {
  RouteIntelStore,
  OperatorProfileStore,
  ClientProfileStore,
  ValidationLedgerStore,
} from "./composite-stores.js";
import { calcEstimateDelta } from "../capabilities/quote.js";
import { haversine } from "../utils/haversine.js";

// ── Season Detection ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────

/**
 * Determine season from a date string.
 * - peak: Dec 15 – Jan 5, Presidents' Day week (Feb), Spring Break (Mar-Apr)
 * - shoulder: Oct-Nov, May-Jun
 * - off: Jul-Sep, Jan 6-31
 */
export function detectSeason(dateStr: string): "peak" | "shoulder" | "off" {
  if (!dateStr) return "off";
  // Parse date components directly to avoid UTC timezone shifts
  const parts = dateStr.split("-");
  if (parts.length < 3) return "off";
  const month = parseInt(parts[1]!, 10) - 1;  // 0-indexed
  const day = parseInt(parts[2]!, 10);

  // Dec 15 – Dec 31
  if (month === 11 && day >= 15) return "peak";
  // Jan 1 – Jan 5
  if (month === 0 && day <= 5) return "peak";
  // February (Presidents' Day week + ski season)
  if (month === 1) return "peak";
  // March-April (Spring Break)
  if (month === 2 || month === 3) return "peak";
  // Oct-Nov (shoulder — fall travel)
  if (month === 9 || month === 10) return "shoulder";
  // May-Jun (shoulder — early summer)
  if (month === 4 || month === 5) return "shoulder";
  // Jul-Sep, rest of Jan (off season)
  return "off";
}

// ── Route Distance ────────────────��──────────────────────────────────────

export interface AirportCoords {
  icao: string;
  latitude: number;
  longitude: number;
}

/**
 * Compute route distance in nm. Returns 0 if coordinates unavailable.
 */
export function computeRouteDistance(
  departure: string,
  arrival: string,
  airports?: Map<string, AirportCoords>,
): number {
  if (!airports) return 0;
  const dep = airports.get(departure);
  const arr = airports.get(arrival);
  if (!dep || !arr) return 0;
  return Math.round(haversine(dep.latitude, dep.longitude, arr.latitude, arr.longitude));
}

// ── Build DealRecord from DealContext ───────��─────────────────────────────

export type DealOutcomeResult =
  | "closed"
  | "lost_to_competition"
  | "client_declined"
  | "budget_mismatch"
  | "aircraft_unavailable"
  | "operator_no_response"
  | "expired";

export interface CompositeStores {
  routeIntel?: RouteIntelStore;
  operatorProfiles?: OperatorProfileStore;
  clientProfiles?: ClientProfileStore;
  validationLedger?: ValidationLedgerStore;
}

export interface RecordDealOptions {
  /** The final deal outcome. */
  result: DealOutcomeResult;
  /** Operator response time in seconds (null if operator never contacted). */
  operatorResponseTime?: number | null;
  /** Airport coordinates for route distance calculation. */
  airports?: Map<string, AirportCoords>;
  /** Additional notes. */
  notes?: string;
  /** Composite object stores — updated on deal close if provided. */
  compositeStores?: CompositeStores;
}

/**
 * Build a DealRecord from a completed DealContext.
 *
 * Extracts all learning loop fields from the context:
 * - Route + distance
 * - Pricing: estimate range, operator quote, OSA price, estimate delta
 * - Operator: name, base, response time, whether they quoted (conversion)
 * - Client: name, contact, type, budget, whether they confirmed, decision time
 * - Outcome: result, revenue, margin
 * - Timing: total deal time, day of week, season
 * - Meta: guardian flag messages
 */
export function buildDealRecord(
  ctx: DealContext,
  options: RecordDealOptions,
): DealRecord {
  const { result, operatorResponseTime = null, airports } = options;
  const now = new Date();

  // Estimate delta — uses calcEstimateDelta from quote.ts
  let estimateDelta: number | null = null;
  if (ctx.estimate.priceRange && ctx.quote.operatorPrice) {
    estimateDelta = calcEstimateDelta(ctx.estimate.priceRange, ctx.quote.operatorPrice);
    estimateDelta = Math.round(estimateDelta * 10000) / 10000; // 4 decimal places
  }

  // Decision time: seconds from presentation to decision
  let decisionTime: number | null = null;
  if (ctx.presentation.sentAt && ctx.presentation.decisionAt) {
    decisionTime = Math.round(
      (new Date(ctx.presentation.decisionAt).getTime() - new Date(ctx.presentation.sentAt).getTime()) / 1000,
    );
  }

  // Revenue = OSA price - operator price
  let revenue: number | null = null;
  if (ctx.quote.osaPrice && ctx.quote.operatorPrice && result === "closed") {
    revenue = ctx.quote.osaPrice - ctx.quote.operatorPrice;
  }

  // Total deal time in seconds
  const totalDealTime = ctx.outcome.totalDealTime
    ?? Math.round((now.getTime() - new Date(ctx.createdAt).getTime()) / 1000);

  // Operator conversion: did they provide a quote?
  const operatorConversion = ctx.quote.operatorPrice !== null;

  // Client conversion: did the client confirm?
  const clientConversion = result === "closed";

  // Guardian flags: collect all warning/critical check messages
  const guardianFlags = ctx.guardian.passes.flatMap((pass) =>
    pass.checks
      .filter((c) => !c.passed || c.severity !== "info")
      .filter((c) => c.severity === "warning" || c.severity === "critical")
      .map((c) => `[${c.severity}] ${c.name}: ${c.message}`),
  );

  // Route distance
  const distance = computeRouteDistance(ctx.trip.departure, ctx.trip.arrival, airports);

  return {
    id: ctx.id,
    timestamp: now.toISOString(),
    mode: ctx.mode,

    route: {
      departure: ctx.trip.departure || "XXXX",
      arrival: ctx.trip.arrival || "XXXX",
      distance,
    },

    aviapagesEstimate: ctx.estimate.priceRange
      ? { low: ctx.estimate.priceRange.low, high: ctx.estimate.priceRange.high }
      : null,
    operatorQuote: ctx.quote.operatorPrice,
    osaPrice: ctx.quote.osaPrice,
    estimateDelta,

    operatorName: ctx.sourcing.selectedOperator?.operatorName ?? null,
    operatorBase: ctx.sourcing.selectedOperator?.baseAirport ?? null,
    operatorResponseTime: operatorResponseTime ?? ctx.outcome.operatorResponseTime,
    operatorConversion,

    aircraftType: ctx.quote.aircraft?.type ?? ctx.sourcing.selectedOperator?.aircraftType ?? null,
    aircraftClass: ctx.trip.aircraftClass,

    clientName: ctx.client.name || null,
    clientContact: ctx.client.contact || null,
    clientType: ctx.client.type,
    clientBudget: ctx.client.budget,
    clientConversion,
    decisionTime,

    result,
    revenue,
    margin: ctx.quote.margin,

    totalDealTime,
    dayOfWeek: now.getDay(),
    season: detectSeason(ctx.trip.date),

    guardianFlags,
  };
}

// ── Write to Store ──────────────��────────────────────────────────────────

// ── Composite Update Thresholds ──────────────────────────────────────────

/** Demand is "rising" if recent count exceeds prior by this factor. */
const DEMAND_RISING_THRESHOLD = 1.3;
/** Demand is "falling" if recent count is below prior by this factor. */
const DEMAND_FALLING_THRESHOLD = 0.7;
/** Bayesian confidence boost per 10 deals for reliability scoring. */
const RELIABILITY_CONFIDENCE_BOOST = 0.1;
/** Deals needed for full confidence in reliability scoring. */
const RELIABILITY_CONFIDENCE_DEALS = 10;

// ── Composite Object Update Functions ────────────────────────────────────

/**
 * Build or update RouteIntel from a deal record and existing store data.
 * Computes 30-day averages from all deals on this route.
 */
function updateRouteIntel(
  ctx: DealContext,
  record: DealRecord,
  dealStore: DealStore,
  store: RouteIntelStore,
): void {
  const route = `${ctx.trip.departure}-${ctx.trip.arrival}`;
  const aircraftClass = ctx.trip.aircraftClass;
  if (!aircraftClass) return;

  const routeDeals = dealStore.getByRoute(ctx.trip.departure, ctx.trip.arrival);
  const now = new Date();
  const thirtyDaysAgo = new Date(now.getTime() - 30 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000).toISOString();

  const recentDeals = routeDeals.filter((r) => r.timestamp >= thirtyDaysAgo);
  const pricedDeals = recentDeals.filter((r) => r.osaPrice !== null);

  const avgPrice30d = pricedDeals.length > 0
    ? Math.round(pricedDeals.reduce((sum, r) => sum + r.osaPrice!, 0) / pricedDeals.length)
    : null;

  // Demand trend: compare recent 15d vs prior 15d
  const fifteenDaysAgo = new Date(now.getTime() - 15 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000).toISOString();
  const recent15 = recentDeals.filter((r) => r.timestamp >= fifteenDaysAgo).length;
  const prior15 = recentDeals.filter((r) => r.timestamp < fifteenDaysAgo).length;
  const demandTrend: "rising" | "falling" | "stable" =
    (prior15 === 0 && recent15 === 0) ? "stable" :
    recent15 > prior15 * DEMAND_RISING_THRESHOLD ? "rising" :
    recent15 < prior15 * DEMAND_FALLING_THRESHOLD ? "falling" : "stable";

  // Top 3 operators by closed deals on this route
  const operatorCounts = new Map<string, number>();
  for (const deal of routeDeals) {
    if (deal.operatorName && deal.result === "closed") {
      operatorCounts.set(deal.operatorName, (operatorCounts.get(deal.operatorName) ?? 0) + 1);
    }
  }
  const topOperators = [...operatorCounts.entries()]
    .sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1])
    .slice(0, 3)
    .map(([name]) => name);

  // Seasonal factor from current deal's season
  const season = record.season;
  const seasonalFactor = season === "peak" ? 1.25 : season === "shoulder" ? 1.10 : 1.0;

  // Average margin delta (physics estimate vs actual)
  const deltaDeals = routeDeals.filter((r) => r.estimateDelta !== null);
  const avgMarginDelta = deltaDeals.length > 0
    ? deltaDeals.reduce((sum, r) => sum + r.estimateDelta!, 0) / deltaDeals.length
    : 0;

  const intel: RouteIntel = {
    route,
    aircraftClass,
    avgPrice30d,
    dealCount: routeDeals.length,
    demandTrend,
    topOperators,
    seasonalFactor,
    avgMarginDelta: Math.round(avgMarginDelta * 10000) / 10000,
    lastUpdated: now.toISOString(),
  };

  store.upsert(intel);
}

/**
 * Build or update OperatorProfile from a deal record.
 * Incrementally updates running averages.
 */
function updateOperatorProfile(
  ctx: DealContext,
  _record: DealRecord,
  dealStore: DealStore,
  store: OperatorProfileStore,
): void {
  const operatorName = ctx.sourcing.selectedOperator?.operatorName;
  if (!operatorName) return;

  const existing = store.getIncludingStale(operatorName);
  const allDeals = dealStore.getByOperator(operatorName);
  const closedDeals = allDeals.filter((r) => r.result === "closed");
  const quotedDeals = allDeals.filter((r) => r.operatorConversion);
  const responseTimes = allDeals
    .filter((r) => r.operatorResponseTime !== null)
    .map((r) => r.operatorResponseTime!);
  const deltaDeals = allDeals.filter((r) => r.estimateDelta !== null);

  const avgResponseTime = responseTimes.length > 0
    ? Math.round(responseTimes.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / responseTimes.length / 60) // sec → min
    : 0;

  const reliabilityScore = allDeals.length > 0
    ? Math.min(closedDeals.length / allDeals.length + RELIABILITY_CONFIDENCE_BOOST * Math.min(allDeals.length / RELIABILITY_CONFIDENCE_DEALS, 1), 1)
    : 0;

  const pricingDelta = deltaDeals.length > 0
    ? deltaDeals.reduce((sum, r) => sum + r.estimateDelta!, 0) / deltaDeals.length
    : 0;

  const quoteToBookRate = quotedDeals.length > 0
    ? closedDeals.length / quotedDeals.length
    : 0;

  const profile: OperatorProfile = {
    name: operatorName,
    homeBase: existing?.homeBase ?? ctx.sourcing.selectedOperator?.baseAirport ?? "",
    certs: existing?.certs ?? [],
    fleet: existing?.fleet ?? [],
    avgResponseTime,
    reliabilityScore: Math.round(reliabilityScore * 1000) / 1000,
    pricingDelta: Math.round(pricingDelta * 10000) / 10000,
    paymentSpeed: existing?.paymentSpeed ?? 0,
    quoteToBookRate: Math.round(quoteToBookRate * 1000) / 1000,
    cancelRate: existing?.cancelRate ?? 0,
    dealCount: allDeals.length,
    lastUpdated: new Date().toISOString(),
  };

  store.upsert(profile);
}

/**
 * Build or update ClientProfile from a deal.
 */
function updateClientProfile(
  ctx: DealContext,
  _record: DealRecord,
  dealStore: DealStore,
  store: ClientProfileStore,
): void {
  const clientName = ctx.client.name;
  const clientEmail = ctx.client.contact;
  if (!clientName && !clientEmail) return;

  const existing = store.get(clientEmail || clientName);
  const allClientDeals = dealStore.findClientDeals(clientEmail || clientName);
  const closedDeals = allClientDeals.filter((r) => r.result === "closed");

  const totalSpend = closedDeals.reduce((sum, r) => sum + (r.osaPrice ?? 0), 0);

  // Trip frequency: deals per year (based on earliest deal)
  const timestamps = allClientDeals.map((r) => new Date(r.timestamp).getTime());
  const earliest = Math.min(...timestamps);
  const yearSpan = Math.max((Date.now() - earliest) / (365.25 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000), 1 / 12);
  const tripFrequency = Math.round((allClientDeals.length / yearSpan) * 10) / 10;

  // Preferred aircraft class: most common
  const classCounts = new Map<string, number>();
  for (const deal of allClientDeals) {
    if (deal.aircraftClass) {
      classCounts.set(deal.aircraftClass, (classCounts.get(deal.aircraftClass) ?? 0) + 1);
    }
  }
  const preferredAircraftClass = classCounts.size > 0
    ? [...classCounts.entries()].sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1])[0]![0] as ClientProfile["preferredAircraftClass"]
    : null;

  // Preferred routes: top 3
  const routeCounts = new Map<string, number>();
  for (const deal of allClientDeals) {
    const dealRoute = `${deal.route.departure}-${deal.route.arrival}`;
    routeCounts.set(dealRoute, (routeCounts.get(dealRoute) ?? 0) + 1);
  }
  const preferredRoutes = [...routeCounts.entries()]
    .sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1])
    .slice(0, 3)
    .map(([r]) => r);

  const dealHistory = allClientDeals.slice(-20).map((r) => ({
    route: `${r.route.departure}-${r.route.arrival}`,
    price: Math.round((r.osaPrice ?? 0) * 100), // cents — matches total_spend_cents column convention
    date: r.timestamp.slice(0, 10),
    outcome: r.result,
  }));

  const profile: ClientProfile = {
    name: clientName || existing?.name || "",
    email: clientEmail || existing?.email || "",
    dealHistory,
    avgPaymentTime: existing?.avgPaymentTime ?? 0,
    totalSpend,
    tripFrequency,
    preferredAircraftClass,
    preferredRoutes,
    lastUpdated: new Date().toISOString(),
  };

  store.upsert(profile);
}

/**
 * Append a ValidationLedger entry from a completed deal.
 */
function appendValidationLedger(
  ctx: DealContext,
  record: DealRecord,
  store: ValidationLedgerStore,
): void {
  const route = `${ctx.trip.departure}-${ctx.trip.arrival}`;

  // Lead time: hours from deal creation to trip date
  const tripDate = new Date(ctx.trip.date).getTime();
  const createdAt = new Date(ctx.createdAt).getTime();
  const leadTimeHours = Math.round((tripDate - createdAt) / (60 * 60 * 1000));

  // RLI: based on operator response (proxy for route liquidity)
  const rliAtQuote: "high" | "medium" | "low" =
    record.route.distance > 0 && record.operatorConversion ? "high" :
    record.operatorConversion ? "medium" : "low";

  // Map deal outcome to ledger outcome
  const outcomeMap: Record<string, ValidationLedgerEntry["outcome"]> = {
    closed: "booked",
    client_declined: "lost_price",
    budget_mismatch: "lost_price",
    lost_to_competition: "lost_price",
    expired: "lost_timing",
    operator_no_response: "lost_ghosted",
    aircraft_unavailable: "lost_other",
  };

  const physicsEstimate = ctx.estimate.priceRange
    ? Math.round((ctx.estimate.priceRange.low + ctx.estimate.priceRange.high) / 2)
    : null;

  const realizedMarginPct = ctx.quote.osaPrice && ctx.quote.operatorPrice && ctx.quote.osaPrice > 0
    ? Math.round(((ctx.quote.osaPrice - ctx.quote.operatorPrice) / ctx.quote.osaPrice) * 10000) / 100
    : null;

  const clientDecisionMinutes = record.decisionTime !== null
    ? Math.round(record.decisionTime / 60)
    : null;

  const entry: ValidationLedgerEntry = {
    route,
    date: ctx.trip.date || new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10),
    mode: ctx.mode,
    leadTimeHours,
    rliAtQuote,
    dumontPhysicsEstimate: physicsEstimate,
    ghostPositioningValue: ctx.estimate.positioning,
    operatorQuotedPrice: ctx.quote.operatorPrice,
    osaClientPrice: ctx.quote.osaPrice,
    realizedMarginPct,
    rfqsSent: 1, // Always 1 per spec (one quote, one operator)
    quotesReceived: ctx.quote.operatorPrice !== null ? 1 : 0,
    operatorResponseMinutes: record.operatorResponseTime !== null
      ? Math.round(record.operatorResponseTime / 60)
      : null,
    clientDecisionMinutes,
    outcome: outcomeMap[record.result] ?? "lost_other",
    fleetResilienceUsed: ctx.sourcing.backupOperator !== null &&
      ctx.sourcing.selectedOperator?.operatorName !== record.operatorName,
    tripCompleted: record.result === "closed" ? null : false,
    notes: record.guardianFlags.join("; ") || "",
  };

  store.append(entry);
}

/**
 * Build a DealRecord and persist it to the DealStore.
 * Also updates composite objects (RouteIntel, OperatorProfile, ClientProfile,
 * ValidationLedger) if stores are provided.
 *
 * All writes (deal + composites) run inside a single SQLite transaction.
 * If any write fails, everything rolls back — no partial state.
 *
 * Returns the record for inspection/logging.
 */
export function recordDeal(
  ctx: DealContext,
  dealStore: DealStore,
  options: RecordDealOptions,
): DealRecord {
  const record = buildDealRecord(ctx, options);
  const stores = options.compositeStores;

  // Wrap all writes in a single transaction — atomic all-or-nothing
  const db = dealStore.getDatabase();
  const runInTransaction = db.transaction(() => {
    dealStore.add(record);

    if (stores) {
      if (stores.routeIntel) {
        updateRouteIntel(ctx, record, dealStore, stores.routeIntel);
      }
      if (stores.operatorProfiles) {
        updateOperatorProfile(ctx, record, dealStore, stores.operatorProfiles);
      }
      if (stores.clientProfiles) {
        updateClientProfile(ctx, record, dealStore, stores.clientProfiles);
      }
      if (stores.validationLedger) {
        appendValidationLedger(ctx, record, stores.validationLedger);
      }
    }
  });

  runInTransaction();

  if (options.notes) ctx.outcome.notes = options.notes;

  return record;
}
// === src/memory/migrations.ts ===
/**
 * Database Migrations — creates tables on startup, idempotently.
 *
 * Design decisions:
 * - All money fields are INTEGER cents (no floats, ever)
 * - Every money field has a companion currency TEXT column (default 'USD')
 * - Every table has schema_version for forward-compatible evolution
 * - deal_events is append-only (no UPDATE, no DELETE) — audit trail
 * - JSON columns for arrays/nested objects (topOperators, fleet, certs, dealHistory, etc.)
 */

import type Database from "better-sqlite3";

interface Migration {
  id: number;
  name: string;
  sql: string;
