/**
 * Run all pending migrations. Idempotent — skips already-applied migrations.
 */
export function runMigrations(db: Database.Database): void {
  // Migration 1 creates the tracking table — always run it first
  db.exec(MIGRATIONS[0]!.sql);

  const applied = new Set(
    db.prepare("SELECT id FROM _migrations").all().map((r) => (r as { id: number }).id),
  );

  for (const migration of MIGRATIONS) {
    if (applied.has(migration.id)) continue;
    db.exec(migration.sql);
    db.prepare("INSERT INTO _migrations (id, name) VALUES (?, ?)").run(
      migration.id,
      migration.name,
    );
  }
}
// === src/memory/autonomy-store.ts ===
/**
 * Autonomy Store — SQLite-backed per-template approval tracking.
 * Layer: memory
 *
 * Tracks how many times a draft type was approved with zero edits.
 * After threshold (default 10), Dumont earns autonomy to auto-send.
 * Uses UNIQUE(channel, intent, recipient_type) with INSERT OR REPLACE for upserts.
 */

import type Database from "better-sqlite3";
import type { AutonomyRule } from "../schemas/channel-config.js";
import type { MessageChannel, MessageIntent } from "../schemas/message.js";
import { getDb } from "./database.js";

const DEFAULT_AUTO_SEND_AFTER = 10;
const DEFAULT_MAX_DELAY_SECONDS = 30;

// ── Typed Row Interface ──────────────────────────────────────────────────

interface AutonomyRuleRow {
  id: number;
  channel: string;
  intent: string;
  recipient_type: string;
  auto_send_enabled: number;
  auto_send_after: number;
  current_approvals: number;
  max_delay_seconds: number;
}

// ── Row ↔ AutonomyRule conversion ────────────────────────────────────────

function rowToRule(row: AutonomyRuleRow): AutonomyRule {
  return {
    channel: row.channel as MessageChannel,
    intent: row.intent as MessageIntent,
    recipientType: row.recipient_type as "client" | "operator",
    autoSendEnabled: Boolean(row.auto_send_enabled),
    autoSendAfter: row.auto_send_after,
    currentApprovals: row.current_approvals,
    maxDelaySeconds: row.max_delay_seconds,
  };
}

// ── AutonomyStore ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export class AutonomyStore {
  private readonly db: Database.Database;

  constructor(pathOrDb?: string | Database.Database) {
    if (typeof pathOrDb === "object" && pathOrDb !== null) {
      this.db = pathOrDb;
    } else {
      this.db = getDb(pathOrDb);
    }
  }

  /** Expose the database instance for transactions across stores. */
  getDatabase(): Database.Database { return this.db; }

  /**
   * Get or create the autonomy rule for a channel+intent+recipientType.
   * Creates a conservative default if none exists.
   */
  getRule(channel: MessageChannel, intent: MessageIntent, recipientType: "client" | "operator"): AutonomyRule {
    const row = this.db.prepare(
      "SELECT * FROM autonomy_rules WHERE channel = ? AND intent = ? AND recipient_type = ?",
    ).get(channel, intent, recipientType) as AutonomyRuleRow | undefined;

    if (row) return rowToRule(row);

    // Create default rule
    this.db.prepare(`
      INSERT INTO autonomy_rules (channel, intent, recipient_type, auto_send_enabled, auto_send_after, current_approvals, max_delay_seconds)
      VALUES (?, ?, ?, 0, ?, 0, ?)
    `).run(channel, intent, recipientType, DEFAULT_AUTO_SEND_AFTER, DEFAULT_MAX_DELAY_SECONDS);

    return {
      channel, intent, recipientType,
      autoSendEnabled: false,
      autoSendAfter: DEFAULT_AUTO_SEND_AFTER,
      currentApprovals: 0,
      maxDelaySeconds: DEFAULT_MAX_DELAY_SECONDS,
    };
  }

  /**
   * Record a draft approval. If editDistance === 0, increment toward autonomy.
   * If editDistance > 0, reset counter and revoke autonomy.
   *
   * @returns Whether autonomy was just graduated (newly earned).
   */
  recordApproval(
    channel: MessageChannel,
    intent: MessageIntent,
    recipientType: "client" | "operator",
    editDistance: number,
  ): boolean {
    // Ensure rule exists
    const rule = this.getRule(channel, intent, recipientType);

    if (editDistance === 0) {
      const newApprovals = rule.currentApprovals + 1;
      const shouldGraduate = newApprovals >= rule.autoSendAfter && !rule.autoSendEnabled;

      this.db.prepare(`
        UPDATE autonomy_rules
        SET current_approvals = ?, auto_send_enabled = ?
        WHERE channel = ? AND intent = ? AND recipient_type = ?
      `).run(
        newApprovals,
        shouldGraduate ? 1 : (rule.autoSendEnabled ? 1 : 0),
        channel, intent, recipientType,
      );

      return shouldGraduate;
    } else {
      // Reset on edit
      this.db.prepare(`
        UPDATE autonomy_rules
        SET current_approvals = 0, auto_send_enabled = 0
        WHERE channel = ? AND intent = ? AND recipient_type = ?
      `).run(channel, intent, recipientType);

      return false;
    }
  }

  /** Check if a template has earned autonomy. */
  isAutonomous(channel: MessageChannel, intent: MessageIntent, recipientType: "client" | "operator"): boolean {
    const rule = this.getRule(channel, intent, recipientType);
    return rule.autoSendEnabled && rule.currentApprovals >= rule.autoSendAfter;
  }

  /** Manually revoke autonomy for a template. */
  revokeAutonomy(channel: MessageChannel, intent: MessageIntent, recipientType: "client" | "operator"): void {
    // Ensure rule exists first
    this.getRule(channel, intent, recipientType);

    this.db.prepare(`
      UPDATE autonomy_rules
      SET auto_send_enabled = 0, current_approvals = 0
      WHERE channel = ? AND intent = ? AND recipient_type = ?
    `).run(channel, intent, recipientType);
  }
}
// === src/memory/webhook-dedup.ts ===
/**
 * Webhook Deduplication — prevents duplicate processing from provider retries.
 *
 * Webhook providers (Vapi, Resend) retry if they don't get a 200 within ~10s.
 * If an LLM call takes 15s, the webhook retries and we process twice.
 *
 * Pattern: check idempotency key → return 200 immediately → process async.
 * Keys expire after 24 hours.
 */

import type Database from "better-sqlite3";
import { getDb } from "./database.js";

const CLEANUP_INTERVAL_MS = 60 * 60 * 1000; // 1 hour
const TTL_HOURS = 24;

export class WebhookDedup {
  private readonly db: Database.Database;

  constructor(pathOrDb?: string | Database.Database) {
    this.db = typeof pathOrDb === "object" && pathOrDb !== null ? pathOrDb : getDb(pathOrDb);
    this.startCleanup();
  }

  /**
   * Check if a webhook has already been processed.
   * If not seen, marks it as processed and returns false.
   * If already seen, returns true (caller should skip processing).
   */
  isDuplicate(key: string): boolean {
    const existing = this.db.prepare(
      "SELECT 1 FROM processed_webhooks WHERE idempotency_key = ?",
    ).get(key) as { "1": number } | undefined;

    if (existing) return true;

    this.db.prepare(
      "INSERT OR IGNORE INTO processed_webhooks (idempotency_key) VALUES (?)",
    ).run(key);

    return false;
  }

  /** Remove entries older than 24 hours. */
  cleanup(): void {
    this.db.prepare(
      `DELETE FROM processed_webhooks WHERE processed_at < datetime('now', '-${TTL_HOURS} hours')`,
    ).run();
  }

  private cleanupTimer: ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null = null;

  private startCleanup(): void {
    this.cleanupTimer = setInterval(() => this.cleanup(), CLEANUP_INTERVAL_MS);
    this.cleanupTimer.unref();
  }

  /** Stop the cleanup timer (for testing). */
  stop(): void {
    if (this.cleanupTimer) {
      clearInterval(this.cleanupTimer);
      this.cleanupTimer = null;
    }
  }
}
// === src/memory/deal-store.ts ===
/**
 * Deal Store — SQLite-backed storage for deal records.
 * Layer: memory
 *
 * All money stored as integer cents. Converted to dollars at the public API boundary.
 * Indexed on route, operator, client, and timestamp.
 */

import type Database from "better-sqlite3";
import type { DealRecord } from "../schemas/deal-record.js";
import { getDb } from "./database.js";

// ── Dollar ↔ Cents helpers ──────────────────────────────────────────────

function toCents(dollars: number | null): number | null {
  return dollars !== null ? Math.round(dollars * 100) : null;
}

function toDollars(cents: number | null): number | null {
  return cents !== null ? cents / 100 : null;
}

// ── Typed Row Interface ──────────────────────────────────────────────────

interface DealRow {
  id: string;
  timestamp: string;
  mode: string;
  route_departure: string;
  route_arrival: string;
  route_distance: number;
  estimate_low_cents: number | null;
  estimate_high_cents: number | null;
  operator_quote_cents: number | null;
  osa_price_cents: number | null;
  estimate_delta: number | null;
  operator_name: string | null;
  operator_base: string | null;
  operator_response_time: number | null;
  operator_conversion: number;
  aircraft_type: string | null;
  aircraft_class: string | null;
  client_name: string | null;
  client_contact: string | null;
  client_type: string;
  client_budget_min_cents: number | null;
  client_budget_max_cents: number | null;
  client_conversion: number;
  decision_time: number | null;
  result: string;
  revenue_cents: number | null;
  margin: number | null;
  total_deal_time: number | null;
  day_of_week: number;
  season: string;
  guardian_flags: string;
}

// ── Row ↔ DealRecord conversion ─────────────────────────────────────────

function rowToRecord(row: DealRow): DealRecord {
  return {
    id: row.id,
    timestamp: row.timestamp,
    mode: row.mode as DealRecord["mode"],
    route: {
      departure: row.route_departure,
      arrival: row.route_arrival,
      distance: row.route_distance,
    },
    aviapagesEstimate: row.estimate_low_cents !== null
      ? { low: toDollars(row.estimate_low_cents)!, high: toDollars(row.estimate_high_cents)! }
      : null,
    operatorQuote: toDollars(row.operator_quote_cents),
    osaPrice: toDollars(row.osa_price_cents),
    estimateDelta: row.estimate_delta,
    operatorName: row.operator_name,
    operatorBase: row.operator_base,
    operatorResponseTime: row.operator_response_time,
    operatorConversion: Boolean(row.operator_conversion),
    aircraftType: row.aircraft_type,
    aircraftClass: row.aircraft_class,
    clientName: row.client_name,
    clientContact: row.client_contact,
    clientType: row.client_type as DealRecord["clientType"],
    clientBudget: row.client_budget_min_cents !== null
      ? { min: toDollars(row.client_budget_min_cents)!, max: toDollars(row.client_budget_max_cents)! }
      : null,
    clientConversion: Boolean(row.client_conversion),
    decisionTime: row.decision_time,
    result: row.result as DealRecord["result"],
    revenue: toDollars(row.revenue_cents),
    margin: row.margin,
    totalDealTime: row.total_deal_time,
    dayOfWeek: row.day_of_week,
    season: row.season as DealRecord["season"],
    guardianFlags: JSON.parse(row.guardian_flags || "[]"),
  };
}

// ── DealStore ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export class DealStore {
  private readonly db: Database.Database;

  constructor(pathOrDb?: string | Database.Database) {
    if (typeof pathOrDb === "object" && pathOrDb !== null) {
      this.db = pathOrDb;
    } else {
      this.db = getDb(pathOrDb);
    }
  }

  /** Expose the database instance for transactions across stores. */
  getDatabase(): Database.Database { return this.db; }

  add(record: DealRecord): void {
    this.db.prepare(`
      INSERT OR REPLACE INTO deals (
        id, timestamp, mode,
        route_departure, route_arrival, route_distance,
        estimate_low_cents, estimate_high_cents,
        operator_quote_cents, osa_price_cents, estimate_delta,
        operator_name, operator_base, operator_response_time, operator_conversion,
        aircraft_type, aircraft_class,
        client_name, client_contact, client_type,
        client_budget_min_cents, client_budget_max_cents,
        client_conversion, decision_time,
        result, revenue_cents, margin,
        total_deal_time, day_of_week, season,
        guardian_flags
      ) VALUES (
        ?, ?, ?,
        ?, ?, ?,
        ?, ?,
        ?, ?, ?,
        ?, ?, ?, ?,
        ?, ?,
        ?, ?, ?,
        ?, ?,
        ?, ?,
        ?, ?, ?,
        ?, ?, ?,
        ?
      )
    `).run(
      record.id, record.timestamp, record.mode,
      record.route.departure, record.route.arrival, record.route.distance,
      record.aviapagesEstimate ? toCents(record.aviapagesEstimate.low) : null,
      record.aviapagesEstimate ? toCents(record.aviapagesEstimate.high) : null,
      toCents(record.operatorQuote), toCents(record.osaPrice), record.estimateDelta,
      record.operatorName, record.operatorBase, record.operatorResponseTime,
      record.operatorConversion ? 1 : 0,
      record.aircraftType, record.aircraftClass,
      record.clientName, record.clientContact, record.clientType,
      record.clientBudget ? toCents(record.clientBudget.min) : null,
      record.clientBudget ? toCents(record.clientBudget.max) : null,
      record.clientConversion ? 1 : 0, record.decisionTime,
      record.result, toCents(record.revenue), record.margin,
      record.totalDealTime, record.dayOfWeek, record.season,
      JSON.stringify(record.guardianFlags),
    );
  }

  getAll(): DealRecord[] {
    return this.db.prepare("SELECT * FROM deals ORDER BY timestamp").all().map((r) => rowToRecord(r as DealRow));
  }

  getByRoute(departure: string, arrival: string): DealRecord[] {
    return this.db.prepare(
      "SELECT * FROM deals WHERE route_departure = ? AND route_arrival = ? ORDER BY timestamp",
    ).all(departure, arrival).map((r) => rowToRecord(r as DealRow));
  }

  getByOperator(operatorName: string): DealRecord[] {
    return this.db.prepare(
      "SELECT * FROM deals WHERE operator_name = ? ORDER BY timestamp",
    ).all(operatorName).map((r) => rowToRecord(r as DealRow));
  }

  getByClient(clientType: "new" | "repeat"): DealRecord[] {
    return this.db.prepare(
      "SELECT * FROM deals WHERE client_type = ? ORDER BY timestamp",
    ).all(clientType).map((r) => rowToRecord(r as DealRow));
  }

  /**
   * Find past deals by client name or contact (case-insensitive match).
   * Email matches are exact; name matches use LIKE for substring.
   */
  findClientDeals(nameOrContact: string): DealRecord[] {
    if (!nameOrContact) return [];
    const needle = nameOrContact.trim();
    // If it looks like an email, do exact match on contact
    if (needle.includes("@")) {
      return this.db.prepare(
        "SELECT * FROM deals WHERE client_contact = ? COLLATE NOCASE ORDER BY timestamp",
      ).all(needle).map((r) => rowToRecord(r as DealRow));
    }
    // Otherwise search by name or contact substring (escape LIKE metacharacters)
    const escaped = needle.replace(/[%_\\]/g, (c) => `\\${c}`);
    const like = `%${escaped}%`;
    return this.db.prepare(
      "SELECT * FROM deals WHERE client_name LIKE ? ESCAPE '\\' COLLATE NOCASE OR client_contact LIKE ? ESCAPE '\\' COLLATE NOCASE ORDER BY timestamp",
    ).all(like, like).map((r) => rowToRecord(r as DealRow));
  }

  getLatestClientDeal(nameOrContact: string): DealRecord | null {
    const deals = this.findClientDeals(nameOrContact);
    if (deals.length === 0) return null;
    return deals.sort((a, b) => b.timestamp.localeCompare(a.timestamp))[0]!;
  }

  getPricingDelta(departure: string, arrival: string): number | null {
    const row = this.db.prepare(
      "SELECT AVG(estimate_delta) as avg_delta FROM deals WHERE route_departure = ? AND route_arrival = ? AND estimate_delta IS NOT NULL",
    ).get(departure, arrival) as { avg_delta: number | null } | undefined;
    return row?.avg_delta ?? null;
  }

  getOperatorStats(operatorName: string, departure: string, arrival: string) {
    const rows = this.db.prepare(
      "SELECT * FROM deals WHERE operator_name = ? AND route_departure = ? AND route_arrival = ?",
    ).all(operatorName, departure, arrival) as DealRow[];
    if (rows.length === 0) return null;

    const records = rows.map(rowToRecord);
    const conversions = records.filter((r) => r.operatorConversion).length;
    const withResponse = records.filter((r) => r.operatorResponseTime !== null);
    const avgResponseTime = withResponse.length > 0
      ? withResponse.reduce((acc, r) => acc + (r.operatorResponseTime ?? 0), 0) / withResponse.length
      : 0;

    return {
      totalDeals: records.length,
      conversionRate: conversions / records.length,
      avgResponseTime,
    };
  }

  get count(): number {
    const row = this.db.prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) as cnt FROM deals").get() as { cnt: number } | undefined;
    return row?.cnt ?? 0;
  }
}
// === src/memory/database.ts ===
/**
 * Database singleton — SQLite via better-sqlite3.
 *
 * Opens ./data/dumont.db (or path from DUMONT_DB_PATH env var).
 * WAL mode for read performance. Foreign keys enabled.
 * Runs migrations on first access.
 */

import Database from "better-sqlite3";
import path from "node:path";
import { runMigrations } from "./migrations.js";

let db: Database.Database | null = null;

/**
 * Get the database instance. Creates and migrates on first call.
 * @param dbPath - Override path (used in tests for temp databases).
 */
export function getDb(dbPath?: string): Database.Database {
  if (db && !dbPath) return db;

  const resolvedPath = dbPath ?? process.env.DUMONT_DB_PATH ?? path.resolve("./data/dumont.db");

  const instance = new Database(resolvedPath);
  instance.pragma("journal_mode = WAL");
  instance.pragma("foreign_keys = ON");

  runMigrations(instance);

  // Only cache the singleton for the default path
  if (!dbPath) {
    db = instance;
  }

  return instance;
}

/**
 * Close the singleton database connection.
 * Call on process shutdown or in tests for cleanup.
 */
export function closeDb(): void {
  if (db) {
    db.close();
    db = null;
  }
}

/**
 * Create an isolated in-memory database for testing.
 * Fully migrated, independent from the singleton.
 */
export function createTestDb(): Database.Database {
  return getDb(":memory:");
}

// === src/memory/deal-event-log.ts ===
/**
 * Deal Event Log — append-only audit trail for every deal lifecycle event.
 *
 * Immutable. No UPDATE, no DELETE. This is the financial compliance layer.
 * Every stage transition, Guardian pass/block, and deal close writes an event.
 *
 * Powers:
 * - Audit trail (Layer 4: what was the price when Guardian approved it?)
 * - Time-series intelligence (Layer 5: deal replay for training data)
 * - Dispute resolution (prove what happened and when)
 */

import type Database from "better-sqlite3";
import { getDb } from "./database.js";

export interface DealEvent {
  id: number;
  dealId: string;
  stage: string;
  eventType: string;
  payload: Record<string, unknown> | null;
  createdAt: string;
  schemaVersion: number;
}

export type DealEventType =
  | "stage_entered"
  | "guardian_pass"
  | "guardian_block"
  | "estimate_generated"
  | "pricewall_cleared"
  | "quote_received"
  | "deal_closed"
  | "deal_lost";

interface DealEventRow {
  id: number;
  deal_id: string;
  stage: string;
  event_type: string;
  payload: string | null;
  created_at: string;
  schema_version: number;
}

export class DealEventLog {
  private readonly db: Database.Database;

  constructor(pathOrDb?: string | Database.Database) {
    this.db = typeof pathOrDb === "object" && pathOrDb !== null ? pathOrDb : getDb(pathOrDb);
  }

  /** Append an immutable event. */
  appendEvent(
    dealId: string,
    stage: string,
    eventType: DealEventType | string,
    payload?: Record<string, unknown>,
  ): void {
    this.db.prepare(`
      INSERT INTO deal_events (deal_id, stage, event_type, payload)
      VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)
    `).run(
      dealId,
      stage,
      eventType,
      payload ? JSON.stringify(payload) : null,
    );
  }

  /** Get all events for a deal, ordered by creation time. */
  getEvents(dealId: string): DealEvent[] {
    return this.db.prepare(
      "SELECT * FROM deal_events WHERE deal_id = ? ORDER BY id",
    ).all(dealId).map((r) => this.rowToEvent(r as DealEventRow));
  }

  /** Get events of a specific type across all deals. */
  getEventsByType(eventType: string, limit: number = 100): DealEvent[] {
    return this.db.prepare(
      "SELECT * FROM deal_events WHERE event_type = ? ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT ?",
    ).all(eventType, limit).map((r) => this.rowToEvent(r as DealEventRow));
  }

  /** Count events for a deal. */
  getEventCount(dealId: string): number {
    const row = this.db.prepare(
      "SELECT COUNT(*) as cnt FROM deal_events WHERE deal_id = ?",
    ).get(dealId) as { cnt: number } | undefined;
    return row?.cnt ?? 0;
  }

  /** Total event count across all deals. */
  get count(): number {
    const row = this.db.prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) as cnt FROM deal_events").get() as { cnt: number } | undefined;
    return row?.cnt ?? 0;
  }

  private rowToEvent(row: DealEventRow): DealEvent {
    return {
      id: row.id,
      dealId: row.deal_id,
      stage: row.stage,
      eventType: row.event_type,
      payload: row.payload ? JSON.parse(row.payload) : null,
      createdAt: row.created_at,
      schemaVersion: row.schema_version,
    };
  }
}
// === src/memory/edit-memory.ts ===
/**
 * Edit Memory — learns the broker's voice from past corrections.
 * Per spec: communication-layer.md FR-10, section 2.6.
 * Queries MessageStore for edited drafts. No separate persistence.
 */

import type { MessageStore } from "./message-store.js";
import type { MessageIntent, MessageChannel } from "../schemas/message.js";

/** A single edit pattern: what Dumont wrote vs what the broker sent. */
export interface EditPattern {
  dumontWrote: string;
  brokerChangedTo: string;
  wordsChanged: number;
  context: string;
}

/**
 * Get recent edit patterns for a specific intent + channel combination.
 * Returns the broker's corrections sorted by most recent first.
 *
 * @param store - MessageStore to query.
 * @param intent - Message intent filter (e.g. "follow_up").
 * @param channel - Channel filter (e.g. "email").
 * @param limit - Max patterns to return (default 10, caps token budget).
 * @returns Array of edit patterns, most recent first.
 */
export function getEditHistory(
  store: MessageStore,
  intent: MessageIntent,
  channel: MessageChannel,
  limit: number = 10,
): EditPattern[] {
  const all = store.getAll()
    .filter((m) =>
      m.draftedBy === "dumont" &&
      m.direction === "outbound" &&
      m.intent === intent &&
      m.channel === channel &&
      m.editDistance !== null &&
      m.editDistance > 0 &&
      m.originalDraft !== null,
    )
    .sort((a, b) => (b.sentAt ?? "").localeCompare(a.sentAt ?? ""))
    .slice(0, limit);

  return all.map((m) => ({
    dumontWrote: m.originalDraft!,
    brokerChangedTo: m.body,
    wordsChanged: m.editDistance!,
    context: `${m.intent} / ${m.channel}`,
  }));
}

/**
 * Build the style-learning section for the respond system prompt.
 * Injects edit examples when available, omits the section on cold start.
 *
 * @param store - MessageStore to query.
 * @param intent - Message intent.
 * @param channel - Channel.
 * @returns Style learning prompt section, or empty string if no history.
 */
export function buildEditMemoryPromptSection(
  store: MessageStore,
  intent: MessageIntent,
  channel: MessageChannel,
): string {
  const history = getEditHistory(store, intent, channel);
  if (history.length === 0) return "";

  let section = "\n\n--- STYLE LEARNING ---\n";
  section += "Below are your recent drafts of this type that the broker edited before sending. ";
  section += "Study the differences and write in the broker's voice.\n\n";

  for (const ex of history) {
    section += `YOUR DRAFT: ${ex.dumontWrote}\n`;
    section += `BROKER SENT: ${ex.brokerChangedTo}\n`;
    section += "---\n";
  }

  return section;
}
// === src/memory/message-store.ts ===
/**
 * Message Store — SQLite-backed persistence for all messages.
 * Same pattern as DealStore. Used by thread matcher for lookups.
 * Per spec: communication-layer.md FR-8 (Data Capture).
 *
 * Migration from JSON: public API is identical.
 */

import type Database from "better-sqlite3";
import type { Message } from "../schemas/message.js";
import { getDb } from "./database.js";

// ── Typed Row Interface ──────────────────────────────────────────────────

interface MessageRow {
  id: string;
  deal_id: string | null;
  channel: string;
  direction: string;
  sender: string;
  recipient: string | null;
  from_name: string | null;
  subject: string | null;
  body: string;
  body_html: string | null;
  attachments: string;
  drafted_by: string | null;
  approved_by: string | null;
  edit_distance: number | null;
  original_draft: string | null;
  received_at: string | null;
  drafted_at: string | null;
  approved_at: string | null;
  sent_at: string | null;
  response_to_id: string | null;
  response_time_ms: number | null;
  thread_id: string | null;
  intent: string | null;
  parsed_data: string | null;
  confidence: number | null;
  confidence_level: string | null;
  platform_message_id: string | null;
  platform_metadata: string | null;
}

// ── Row ↔ Message conversion ─────────────────────────────────────────

function rowToMessage(row: MessageRow): Message {
  return {
    id: row.id,
    dealId: row.deal_id,
    channel: row.channel as Message["channel"],
    direction: row.direction as Message["direction"],
    from: row.sender,
    to: row.recipient,
    fromName: row.from_name,
    subject: row.subject,
    body: row.body,
    bodyHtml: row.body_html,
    attachments: JSON.parse(row.attachments),
    draftedBy: row.drafted_by as Message["draftedBy"],
    approvedBy: row.approved_by,
    editDistance: row.edit_distance,
    originalDraft: row.original_draft,
    receivedAt: row.received_at,
    draftedAt: row.drafted_at,
    approvedAt: row.approved_at,
    sentAt: row.sent_at,
    responseToId: row.response_to_id,
    responseTimeMs: row.response_time_ms,
    threadId: row.thread_id,
    intent: row.intent as Message["intent"],
    parsedData: row.parsed_data ? JSON.parse(row.parsed_data) : null,
    confidence: row.confidence,
    confidenceLevel: row.confidence_level as Message["confidenceLevel"],
    platformMessageId: row.platform_message_id,
    platformMetadata: row.platform_metadata ? JSON.parse(row.platform_metadata) : null,
  };
}

// ── MessageStore ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export class MessageStore {
  private readonly db: Database.Database;

  constructor(pathOrDb?: string | Database.Database) {
    if (typeof pathOrDb === "object" && pathOrDb !== null) {
      this.db = pathOrDb;
    } else {
      this.db = getDb(pathOrDb);
    }
  }

  /** Expose the database instance for transactions across stores. */
  getDatabase(): Database.Database { return this.db; }

  /** Persist a message. */
  saveMessage(message: Message): void {
    this.db.prepare(`
      INSERT OR REPLACE INTO messages (
        id, deal_id, channel, direction, sender, recipient,
        from_name, subject, body, body_html, attachments,
        drafted_by, approved_by, edit_distance, original_draft,
        received_at, drafted_at, approved_at, sent_at,
        response_to_id, response_time_ms, thread_id,
        intent, parsed_data, confidence, confidence_level,
        platform_message_id, platform_metadata
      ) VALUES (
        ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?,
        ?, ?, ?, ?, ?,
        ?, ?, ?, ?,
        ?, ?, ?, ?,
        ?, ?, ?,
        ?, ?, ?, ?,
        ?, ?
      )
    `).run(
      message.id, message.dealId, message.channel, message.direction, message.from, message.to,
      message.fromName, message.subject, message.body, message.bodyHtml, JSON.stringify(message.attachments),
      message.draftedBy, message.approvedBy, message.editDistance, message.originalDraft,
      message.receivedAt, message.draftedAt, message.approvedAt, message.sentAt,
      message.responseToId, message.responseTimeMs, message.threadId,
      message.intent, message.parsedData ? JSON.stringify(message.parsedData) : null,
      message.confidence, message.confidenceLevel,
      message.platformMessageId, message.platformMetadata ? JSON.stringify(message.platformMetadata) : null,
    );
  }

  /** Get all messages for a deal. */
  getByDealId(dealId: string): Message[] {
    return this.db.prepare(
      "SELECT * FROM messages WHERE deal_id = ?",
    ).all(dealId).map((r) => rowToMessage(r as MessageRow));
  }

  /** Get a single message by its ID. */
  getByMessageId(id: string): Message | null {
    const row = this.db.prepare(
      "SELECT * FROM messages WHERE id = ?",
    ).get(id) as MessageRow | undefined;
    return row ? rowToMessage(row) : null;
  }

  /** Get messages by sender contact (phone or email). */
  getByContact(contact: string, limit?: number): Message[] {
    const needle = contact.toLowerCase();
    // Use SQL LIKE for substring matching, same behavior as the old filter
    const rows = this.db.prepare(
      "SELECT * FROM messages WHERE LOWER(sender) = ? OR LOWER(sender) LIKE ?",
    ).all(needle, `%${needle}%`) as MessageRow[];
    const results = rows.map(rowToMessage);
    return limit ? results.slice(-limit) : results;
  }

  /** Get all messages (for edit memory queries). */
  getAll(): Message[] {
    return this.db.prepare("SELECT * FROM messages").all().map((r) => rowToMessage(r as MessageRow));
  }

  /** Total message count. */
  get count(): number {
    const row = this.db.prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) as cnt FROM messages").get() as { cnt: number } | undefined;
    return row?.cnt ?? 0;
  }
}
// === src/memory/call-store.ts ===
/**
 * Call Store — SQLite-backed persistence for call records.
 * Same pattern as DealStore and MessageStore.
 * Per spec: communication-layer.md FR-5.
 *
 * Migration from JSON: public API is identical.
 */

import type Database from "better-sqlite3";
import type { Call } from "../schemas/call.js";
import { getDb } from "./database.js";

// ── Typed Row Interface ──────────────────────────────────────────────────

interface CallRow {
  call_id: string;
  deal_id: string;
  direction: string;
  participants: string;
  started_at: string;
  ended_at: string | null;
  duration_sec: number | null;
  transcript: string;
  detected_entities: string;
  summary: string | null;
  recording_url: string | null;
  recording_expires_at: string | null;
}

// ── Row ↔ Call conversion ────────────────────────────────────────────────

function rowToCall(row: CallRow): Call {
  return {
    callId: row.call_id,
    dealId: row.deal_id,
    direction: row.direction as Call["direction"],
    participants: JSON.parse(row.participants),
    startedAt: row.started_at,
    endedAt: row.ended_at,
    durationSec: row.duration_sec,
    transcript: JSON.parse(row.transcript),
    detectedEntities: JSON.parse(row.detected_entities),
    summary: row.summary,
    recordingUrl: row.recording_url,
    recordingExpiresAt: row.recording_expires_at,
  };
}

// ── CallStore ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export class CallStore {
  private readonly db: Database.Database;

  constructor(pathOrDb?: string | Database.Database) {
    if (typeof pathOrDb === "object" && pathOrDb !== null) {
      this.db = pathOrDb;
    } else {
      this.db = getDb(pathOrDb);
    }
  }

  /** Expose the database instance for transactions across stores. */
  getDatabase(): Database.Database { return this.db; }

  /** Persist a call record. */
  saveCall(call: Call): void {
    this.db.prepare(`
      INSERT OR REPLACE INTO calls (
        call_id, deal_id, direction, participants,
        started_at, ended_at, duration_sec,
        transcript, detected_entities, summary,
        recording_url, recording_expires_at
      ) VALUES (
        ?, ?, ?, ?,
        ?, ?, ?,
        ?, ?, ?,
        ?, ?
      )
    `).run(
      call.callId, call.dealId, call.direction, JSON.stringify(call.participants),
      call.startedAt, call.endedAt, call.durationSec,
      JSON.stringify(call.transcript), JSON.stringify(call.detectedEntities), call.summary,
      call.recordingUrl, call.recordingExpiresAt,
    );
  }

  /** Get all calls for a deal. */
  getByDealId(dealId: string): Call[] {
    return this.db.prepare(
      "SELECT * FROM calls WHERE deal_id = ?",
    ).all(dealId).map((r) => rowToCall(r as CallRow));
  }

  /** Get a single call by ID. */
  getById(callId: string): Call | null {
    const row = this.db.prepare(
      "SELECT * FROM calls WHERE call_id = ?",
    ).get(callId) as CallRow | undefined;
    return row ? rowToCall(row) : null;
  }

  /** Total call count. */
  get count(): number {
    const row = this.db.prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) as cnt FROM calls").get() as { cnt: number } | undefined;
    return row?.cnt ?? 0;
  }
}
// === src/memory/composite-stores.ts ===
/**
 * Composite Object Stores — SQLite-backed persistence for pre-aggregated
 * intelligence objects.
 *
 * TTL rules:
 * - RouteIntel: 24 hours (stale data re-computed from DealStore on next access)
 * - OperatorProfile: 24 hours
 * - ClientProfile: no TTL (updated on every deal close)
 * - ValidationLedger: append-only, permanent, no TTL
 *
 * All money stored as INTEGER cents. Converted at boundary.
 */

import type Database from "better-sqlite3";
import type {
  RouteIntel,
  OperatorProfile,
  ClientProfile,
  ValidationLedgerEntry,
} from "../schemas/composite-objects.js";
import { getDb } from "./database.js";

// ── TTL Config ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

const TTL_MS = 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000; // 24 hours

function isStale(lastUpdated: string): boolean {
  return Date.now() - new Date(lastUpdated).getTime() > TTL_MS;
}

// ── SQLite Row Interfaces ───────────────────────────────────────────────

interface RouteIntelRow {
  route: string;
  aircraft_class: string;
  avg_price_30d_cents: number | null;
  deal_count: number;
  demand_trend: string;
  top_operators: string;
  seasonal_factor: number;
  avg_margin_delta: number;
  last_updated: string;
}

interface OperatorProfileRow {
  name: string;
  home_base: string;
  certs: string;
  fleet: string;
  avg_response_time: number;
  reliability_score: number;
  pricing_delta: number;
  payment_speed: number;
  quote_to_book_rate: number;
  cancel_rate: number;
  deal_count: number;
  last_updated: string;
}

interface ClientProfileRow {
  rowid: number;
  name: string;
  email: string;
  deal_history: string;
  avg_payment_time: number;
  total_spend_cents: number;
  trip_frequency: number;
  preferred_aircraft_class: string | null;
  preferred_routes: string;
  last_updated: string;
}

interface ValidationLedgerRow {
  id: number;
  route: string;
  date: string;
  mode: string;
  lead_time_hours: number;
  rli_at_quote: string;
  dumont_physics_estimate_cents: number | null;
  ghost_positioning_value: number | null;
  operator_quoted_price_cents: number | null;
  osa_client_price_cents: number | null;
  realized_margin_pct: number | null;
  rfqs_sent: number;
  quotes_received: number;
  operator_response_minutes: number | null;
  client_decision_minutes: number | null;
  outcome: string;
  fleet_resilience_used: number;
  trip_completed: number | null;
  notes: string;
}

interface CountRow {
  cnt: number;
}

// ── Dollar ↔ Cents helpers ──────────────────────────────────────────────

function toCents(dollars: number | null): number | null {
  return dollars !== null ? Math.round(dollars * 100) : null;
}

function toDollars(cents: number | null): number | null {
  return cents !== null ? cents / 100 : null;
}

// ── RouteIntelStore ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export class RouteIntelStore {
  private readonly db: Database.Database;

  constructor(pathOrDb?: string | Database.Database) {
    this.db = typeof pathOrDb === "object" && pathOrDb !== null ? pathOrDb : getDb(pathOrDb);
  }

  private rowToRouteIntel(row: RouteIntelRow): RouteIntel {
    return {
      route: row.route,
      aircraftClass: row.aircraft_class as RouteIntel["aircraftClass"],
      avgPrice30d: toDollars(row.avg_price_30d_cents),
      dealCount: row.deal_count,
      demandTrend: row.demand_trend as RouteIntel["demandTrend"],
      topOperators: JSON.parse(row.top_operators || "[]"),
      seasonalFactor: row.seasonal_factor,
      avgMarginDelta: row.avg_margin_delta,
      lastUpdated: row.last_updated,
    };
  }

  get(route: string, aircraftClass: string): RouteIntel | null {
    const row = this.db.prepare(
      "SELECT * FROM route_intel WHERE route = ? AND aircraft_class = ?",
    ).get(route, aircraftClass) as RouteIntelRow | undefined;
    if (!row) return null;
    const intel = this.rowToRouteIntel(row);
    if (isStale(intel.lastUpdated)) return null;
    return intel;
  }

  getIncludingStale(route: string, aircraftClass: string): RouteIntel | null {
    const row = this.db.prepare(
      "SELECT * FROM route_intel WHERE route = ? AND aircraft_class = ?",
    ).get(route, aircraftClass) as RouteIntelRow | undefined;
    return row ? this.rowToRouteIntel(row) : null;
  }

  upsert(intel: RouteIntel): void {
    this.db.prepare(`
      INSERT OR REPLACE INTO route_intel (
        route, aircraft_class, avg_price_30d_cents, deal_count,
        demand_trend, top_operators, seasonal_factor, avg_margin_delta, last_updated
      ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
    `).run(
      intel.route, intel.aircraftClass, toCents(intel.avgPrice30d), intel.dealCount,
      intel.demandTrend, JSON.stringify(intel.topOperators), intel.seasonalFactor,
      intel.avgMarginDelta, intel.lastUpdated,
    );
  }

  invalidate(route: string, aircraftClass: string): void {
    this.db.prepare(
      "UPDATE route_intel SET last_updated = ? WHERE route = ? AND aircraft_class = ?",
    ).run(new Date(0).toISOString(), route, aircraftClass);
  }

  getAll(): RouteIntel[] {
    return this.db.prepare("SELECT * FROM route_intel").all().map((r) => this.rowToRouteIntel(r as RouteIntelRow));
  }

  get count(): number {
    const row = this.db.prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) as cnt FROM route_intel").get() as CountRow | undefined;
    return row?.cnt ?? 0;
  }
}

// ── OperatorProfileStore ────────────────────────────────────────────────

export class OperatorProfileStore {
  private readonly db: Database.Database;

  constructor(pathOrDb?: string | Database.Database) {
    this.db = typeof pathOrDb === "object" && pathOrDb !== null ? pathOrDb : getDb(pathOrDb);
  }

  private rowToProfile(row: OperatorProfileRow): OperatorProfile {
    return {
      name: row.name,
      homeBase: row.home_base,
      certs: JSON.parse(row.certs || "[]"),
      fleet: JSON.parse(row.fleet || "[]"),
      avgResponseTime: row.avg_response_time,
      reliabilityScore: row.reliability_score,
      pricingDelta: row.pricing_delta,
      paymentSpeed: row.payment_speed,
      quoteToBookRate: row.quote_to_book_rate,
      cancelRate: row.cancel_rate,
      dealCount: row.deal_count,
      lastUpdated: row.last_updated,
    };
  }

  get(name: string): OperatorProfile | null {
    const row = this.db.prepare("SELECT * FROM operator_profiles WHERE name = ?").get(name) as OperatorProfileRow | undefined;
    if (!row) return null;
    const profile = this.rowToProfile(row);
    if (isStale(profile.lastUpdated)) return null;
    return profile;
  }

  getIncludingStale(name: string): OperatorProfile | null {
    const row = this.db.prepare("SELECT * FROM operator_profiles WHERE name = ?").get(name) as OperatorProfileRow | undefined;
    return row ? this.rowToProfile(row) : null;
  }

  getMany(names: string[]): OperatorProfile[] {
    return names
      .map((n) => this.get(n))
      .filter((p): p is OperatorProfile => p !== null);
  }

  upsert(profile: OperatorProfile): void {
    this.db.prepare(`
      INSERT OR REPLACE INTO operator_profiles (
        name, home_base, certs, fleet,
        avg_response_time, reliability_score, pricing_delta, payment_speed,
        quote_to_book_rate, cancel_rate, deal_count, last_updated
      ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
    `).run(
      profile.name, profile.homeBase, JSON.stringify(profile.certs), JSON.stringify(profile.fleet),
      profile.avgResponseTime, profile.reliabilityScore, profile.pricingDelta, profile.paymentSpeed,
      profile.quoteToBookRate, profile.cancelRate, profile.dealCount, profile.lastUpdated,
    );
  }

  getAll(): OperatorProfile[] {
    return this.db.prepare("SELECT * FROM operator_profiles").all().map((r) => this.rowToProfile(r as OperatorProfileRow));
  }

  get count(): number {
    const row = this.db.prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) as cnt FROM operator_profiles").get() as CountRow | undefined;
    return row?.cnt ?? 0;
  }
}

// ── ClientProfileStore ──────────────────────────────────────────────────

export class ClientProfileStore {
  private readonly db: Database.Database;

  constructor(pathOrDb?: string | Database.Database) {
    this.db = typeof pathOrDb === "object" && pathOrDb !== null ? pathOrDb : getDb(pathOrDb);
  }

  private rowToProfile(row: ClientProfileRow): ClientProfile {
    return {
      name: row.name,
      email: row.email,
      dealHistory: JSON.parse(row.deal_history || "[]"),
      avgPaymentTime: row.avg_payment_time,
      totalSpend: toDollars(row.total_spend_cents) ?? 0,
      tripFrequency: row.trip_frequency,
      preferredAircraftClass: row.preferred_aircraft_class as ClientProfile["preferredAircraftClass"],
      preferredRoutes: JSON.parse(row.preferred_routes || "[]"),
      lastUpdated: row.last_updated,
    };
  }

  get(nameOrEmail: string): ClientProfile | null {
    if (!nameOrEmail) return null;
    const needle = nameOrEmail.toLowerCase().trim();
    const row = this.db.prepare(
      "SELECT * FROM client_profiles WHERE LOWER(name) = ? OR LOWER(email) = ?",
    ).get(needle, needle) as ClientProfileRow | undefined;
    return row ? this.rowToProfile(row) : null;
  }

  upsert(profile: ClientProfile): void {
    // Try to find existing by email (primary) or name (secondary)
    let existing: { rowid: number } | undefined = undefined;
    if (profile.email) {
      existing = this.db.prepare(
        "SELECT rowid FROM client_profiles WHERE LOWER(email) = ?",
      ).get(profile.email.toLowerCase()) as { rowid: number } | undefined;
    }
    if (!existing && profile.name) {
      existing = this.db.prepare(
        "SELECT rowid FROM client_profiles WHERE LOWER(name) = ?",
      ).get(profile.name.toLowerCase()) as { rowid: number } | undefined;
    }

    if (existing) {
      this.db.prepare(`
        UPDATE client_profiles SET
          name = ?, email = ?, deal_history = ?, avg_payment_time = ?,
          total_spend_cents = ?, trip_frequency = ?, preferred_aircraft_class = ?,
          preferred_routes = ?, last_updated = ?
        WHERE rowid = ?
      `).run(
        profile.name, profile.email, JSON.stringify(profile.dealHistory),
        profile.avgPaymentTime, toCents(profile.totalSpend),
        profile.tripFrequency, profile.preferredAircraftClass,
        JSON.stringify(profile.preferredRoutes), profile.lastUpdated,
        existing.rowid,
      );
    } else {
      this.db.prepare(`
        INSERT INTO client_profiles (
          name, email, deal_history, avg_payment_time,
          total_spend_cents, trip_frequency, preferred_aircraft_class,
          preferred_routes, last_updated
        ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
      `).run(
        profile.name, profile.email, JSON.stringify(profile.dealHistory),
        profile.avgPaymentTime, toCents(profile.totalSpend),
        profile.tripFrequency, profile.preferredAircraftClass,
        JSON.stringify(profile.preferredRoutes), profile.lastUpdated,
      );
    }
  }

  getAll(): ClientProfile[] {
    return this.db.prepare("SELECT * FROM client_profiles").all().map((r) => this.rowToProfile(r as ClientProfileRow));
  }

  get count(): number {
    const row = this.db.prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) as cnt FROM client_profiles").get() as CountRow | undefined;
    return row?.cnt ?? 0;
  }
}

// ── ValidationLedgerStore ───────────────────────────────────────────────

export class ValidationLedgerStore {
  private readonly db: Database.Database;

  constructor(pathOrDb?: string | Database.Database) {
    this.db = typeof pathOrDb === "object" && pathOrDb !== null ? pathOrDb : getDb(pathOrDb);
  }

  private rowToEntry(row: ValidationLedgerRow): ValidationLedgerEntry {
    return {
      route: row.route,
      date: row.date,
      mode: row.mode as ValidationLedgerEntry["mode"],
      leadTimeHours: row.lead_time_hours,
      rliAtQuote: row.rli_at_quote as ValidationLedgerEntry["rliAtQuote"],
      dumontPhysicsEstimate: toDollars(row.dumont_physics_estimate_cents),
      ghostPositioningValue: row.ghost_positioning_value,
      operatorQuotedPrice: toDollars(row.operator_quoted_price_cents),
      osaClientPrice: toDollars(row.osa_client_price_cents),
      realizedMarginPct: row.realized_margin_pct,
      rfqsSent: row.rfqs_sent,
      quotesReceived: row.quotes_received,
      operatorResponseMinutes: row.operator_response_minutes,
      clientDecisionMinutes: row.client_decision_minutes,
      outcome: row.outcome as ValidationLedgerEntry["outcome"],
      fleetResilienceUsed: Boolean(row.fleet_resilience_used),
      tripCompleted: row.trip_completed === null ? null : Boolean(row.trip_completed),
      notes: row.notes,
    };
  }

  append(entry: ValidationLedgerEntry): void {
    this.db.prepare(`
      INSERT INTO validation_ledger (
        route, date, mode, lead_time_hours, rli_at_quote,
        dumont_physics_estimate_cents, ghost_positioning_value,
        operator_quoted_price_cents, osa_client_price_cents,
        realized_margin_pct, rfqs_sent, quotes_received,
        operator_response_minutes, client_decision_minutes,
        outcome, fleet_resilience_used, trip_completed, notes
      ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
    `).run(
      entry.route, entry.date, entry.mode, entry.leadTimeHours, entry.rliAtQuote,
      toCents(entry.dumontPhysicsEstimate), entry.ghostPositioningValue,
      toCents(entry.operatorQuotedPrice), toCents(entry.osaClientPrice),
      entry.realizedMarginPct, entry.rfqsSent, entry.quotesReceived,
      entry.operatorResponseMinutes, entry.clientDecisionMinutes,
      entry.outcome, entry.fleetResilienceUsed ? 1 : 0,
      entry.tripCompleted === null ? null : (entry.tripCompleted ? 1 : 0),
      entry.notes,
    );
  }

  getByRoute(route: string): ValidationLedgerEntry[] {
    return this.db.prepare(
      "SELECT * FROM validation_ledger WHERE route = ? ORDER BY date",
    ).all(route).map((r) => this.rowToEntry(r as ValidationLedgerRow));
  }

  getBooked(): ValidationLedgerEntry[] {
    return this.db.prepare(
      "SELECT * FROM validation_ledger WHERE outcome = 'booked' ORDER BY date",
    ).all().map((r) => this.rowToEntry(r as ValidationLedgerRow));
  }

  getByDateRange(from: string, to: string): ValidationLedgerEntry[] {
    return this.db.prepare(
      "SELECT * FROM validation_ledger WHERE date >= ? AND date <= ? ORDER BY date",
    ).all(from, to).map((r) => this.rowToEntry(r as ValidationLedgerRow));
  }

  getAll(): ValidationLedgerEntry[] {
    return this.db.prepare("SELECT * FROM validation_ledger ORDER BY id").all()
      .map((r) => this.rowToEntry(r as ValidationLedgerRow));
  }

  get count(): number {
    const row = this.db.prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) as cnt FROM validation_ledger").get() as CountRow | undefined;
    return row?.cnt ?? 0;
  }
}
// === src/server/middleware.ts ===
/**
 * Shared Express middleware — auth, validation, async error handling.
 * Layer: server
 * Status: extracted for new endpoints. Not yet wired into existing routes.
 *
 * Adding a new endpoint should be a 10-line file, not a 40-line file.
 * These middleware extract the boilerplate.
 */

import type { Request, Response, NextFunction } from "express";
import { z } from "zod";

// ── Auth Middleware ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────

const DUMONT_API_KEY = process.env.DUMONT_API_KEY;
const VAPI_SECRET = process.env.VAPI_SECRET;
const IS_PROD = process.env.NODE_ENV === "production";

/**
 * Bearer token auth middleware. Checks DUMONT_API_KEY or VAPI_SECRET.
 * In dev without keys configured, requests pass through with a warning.
 * In production, unauthenticated requests get 401.
 */
export function authMiddleware(req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction): void {
  const token = req.headers.authorization?.replace("Bearer ", "");
  if (token && (token === DUMONT_API_KEY || token === VAPI_SECRET)) {
    next();
    return;
  }

  if (!DUMONT_API_KEY && !VAPI_SECRET) {
    if (IS_PROD) {
      res.status(401).json({ error: "Authentication required in production" });
      return;
    }
    next(); // Dev only
    return;
  }

  res.status(401).json({ error: "unauthorized" });
}

// ── Body Validation Middleware ───────────────────────────────────────────

/**
 * Zod-validates req.body against a schema. Returns 400 with error details on failure.
 * Usage: router.post("/endpoint", validateBody(MySchema), handler)
 */
export function validateBody<T>(schema: z.ZodType<T>) {
  return (req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction): void => {
    const result = schema.safeParse(req.body);
    if (!result.success) {
      res.status(400).json({
        error: "Validation failed",
        details: result.error.issues.map((i) => ({ path: i.path.join("."), message: i.message })),
      });
      return;
    }
    req.body = result.data;
    next();
  };
}

// ── Async Handler Wrapper ───────────────────────────────────────────────

/**
 * Wraps an async route handler with try/catch → next(err).
 * Usage: router.get("/endpoint", asyncHandler(async (req, res) => { ... }))
 */
export function asyncHandler(
  fn: (req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction) => Promise<void>,
) {
  return (req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction): void => {
    fn(req, res, next).catch(next);
  };
}
// === src/server/dumont-brain.ts ===
/**
 * Dumont Brain — conversational engine.
 * Extracts trip fields, qualifies the caller, runs estimate + sourcing,
 * and returns what Dumont should say next.
 * Stateless per-request; state is held in an in-memory session map keyed by call_id.
 */

import {
  extractRoute, extractPax, extractDate, extractBudget,
  extractAircraftClass, resolveIcao,
} from "../capabilities/intake.js";
import { classifyLead } from "../capabilities/sdr.js";
import { mockAviapagesEstimate, OPERATORS, AIRPORTS } from "../fixtures/sim-data.js";
import { scoreOperator, type ScoredOperator } from "../capabilities/sourcing.js";

// ── Types ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

interface ExtractedFields {
  departure: string | null;
  arrival: string | null;
  date: string | null;
  pax: number | null;
  budget: { min: number; max: number } | null;
  aircraftClass: string | null;
}

export interface ConversationInput {
  call_id: string;
  transcript: string;
  last_utterance: string;
  extracted: Record<string, unknown> | null;
}

export interface DumontResponse {
  response: string;
  action: "continue" | "transfer" | "end";
  transfer_to?: string;
  deal_data: Record<string, unknown>;
}

// ── Session State ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

interface ConversationState {
  fields: ExtractedFields;
  turnCount: number;
  qualified: boolean;
  estimateReturned: boolean;
  lastAccess: number;
}

const SESSION_TTL_MS = 60 * 60 * 1000; // 1 hour
const MAX_SESSIONS = 1000;
const sessions = new Map<string, ConversationState>();

// Evict stale sessions every 10 minutes
setInterval(() => {
  const cutoff = Date.now() - SESSION_TTL_MS;
  for (const [id, state] of sessions) {
    if (state.lastAccess < cutoff) sessions.delete(id);
  }
}, 10 * 60 * 1000).unref();

function getOrCreate(callId: string): ConversationState {
  let state = sessions.get(callId);
  if (!state) {
    // LRU eviction: if at capacity, delete the oldest session
    if (sessions.size >= MAX_SESSIONS) {
      let oldestKey: string | null = null;
      let oldestAccess = Infinity;
      for (const [key, s] of sessions) {
        if (s.lastAccess < oldestAccess) {
          oldestAccess = s.lastAccess;
          oldestKey = key;
        }
      }
      if (oldestKey) sessions.delete(oldestKey);
    }
    state = {
      fields: { departure: null, arrival: null, date: null, pax: null, budget: null, aircraftClass: null },
      turnCount: 0,
      qualified: false,
      estimateReturned: false,
      lastAccess: Date.now(),
    };
    sessions.set(callId, state);
  }
  state.lastAccess = Date.now();
  return state;
}

// ── Helpers ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

const BROKER_PHONE = process.env.BROKER_PHONE ?? "+1-312-555-0100";

const ICAO_TO_CITY: Record<string, string> = {
  KTEB: "Teterboro", KPBI: "Palm Beach", KASE: "Aspen",
  KORD: "Chicago", KMDW: "Midway", KPWK: "Chicago Executive",
  KMIA: "Miami", KFLL: "Fort Lauderdale", KVNY: "Van Nuys",
  KLAS: "Las Vegas", KDAL: "Dallas", KHOU: "Houston",
  KHPN: "Westchester", KEGE: "Vail", MYNN: "Nassau",
  MMSD: "Cabo", TXKF: "Bermuda",
};

function cityName(icao: string): string {
  return ICAO_TO_CITY[icao] ?? icao;
}

function formatPrice(n: number): string {
  if (n >= 1000) return `${Math.round(n / 1000)} thousand`;
  return n.toLocaleString();
}

function formatDateNatural(iso: string | null): string {
  if (!iso) return "your requested date";
  const months = ["January", "February", "March", "April", "May", "June",
    "July", "August", "September", "October", "November", "December"];
  const parts = iso.split("-");
  if (parts.length !== 3) return iso;
  const month = months[parseInt(parts[1]!, 10) - 1] ?? parts[1];
  const day = parseInt(parts[2]!, 10);
  return `${month} ${day}`;
}

function classToName(cls: string | null): string {
  switch (cls) {
    case "light": return "Phenom 300E";
    case "midsize": return "Citation Latitude";
    case "super_mid": return "Challenger 300";
    case "heavy": return "Gulfstream G550";
    case "ultra_long_range": return "Global 7500";
    default: return "light jet";
  }
}

function buildContinue(text: string, fields: ExtractedFields): DumontResponse {
  return {
    response: text,
    action: "continue",
    deal_data: {
      departure: fields.departure,
      arrival: fields.arrival,
      date: fields.date,
      pax: fields.pax,
    },
  };
}

// ── Core Logic ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export function processConversation(input: ConversationInput): DumontResponse {
  const { call_id, transcript, last_utterance, extracted } = input;

  const state = getOrCreate(call_id);
  state.turnCount++;

  const fields = state.fields;

  // Extract from last utterance
  if (last_utterance) {
    const [dep, arr] = extractRoute(last_utterance);
    if (dep) fields.departure = dep;
    if (arr) fields.arrival = arr;

    const date = extractDate(last_utterance);
    if (date) fields.date = date;

    const pax = extractPax(last_utterance);
    if (pax) fields.pax = pax;

    const budget = extractBudget(last_utterance);
    if (budget) fields.budget = budget;

    const ac = extractAircraftClass(last_utterance);
    if (ac) fields.aircraftClass = ac;
  }

  // Merge pre-extracted fields (from adapters that do their own extraction)
  if (extracted) {
    if (extracted.departure && !fields.departure) {
      fields.departure = resolveIcao(extracted.departure as string) ?? fields.departure;
    }
    if (extracted.arrival && !fields.arrival) {
      fields.arrival = resolveIcao(extracted.arrival as string) ?? fields.arrival;
    }
    if (extracted.date && !fields.date) {
      fields.date = extractDate(extracted.date as string) ?? fields.date;
    }
    if (extracted.pax && !fields.pax) {
      fields.pax = typeof extracted.pax === "number" ? extracted.pax : null;
    }
  }

  // Greeting (empty transcript)
  if (!transcript && !last_utterance) {
    return {
      response: "Thank you for calling Orange Sunshine Aviation. This is Dumont, your charter assistant. This call may be recorded for quality. How can I help you today?",
      action: "continue",
      deal_data: {},
    };
  }

  // Not charter related
  const sdr = classifyLead(transcript || last_utterance);
  if (state.turnCount >= 2 && sdr.classification === "not_qualified" && sdr.extractedFields.length === 0) {
    const hasAnything = fields.departure || fields.arrival || fields.date || fields.pax;
    if (!hasAnything) {
      return {
        response: "I appreciate the call, but we specialize in private charter flights. Is there a trip I can help you arrange?",
        action: "continue",
        deal_data: {},
      };
    }
  }

  // Ask for missing fields
  if (!fields.departure) return buildContinue("Where will you be flying from?", fields);
  if (!fields.arrival) return buildContinue("And where are you headed?", fields);
  if (!fields.date) return buildContinue("When do you need to fly?", fields);
  if (!fields.pax) return buildContinue("How many passengers will be traveling?", fields);

  // All info present — run estimate + sourcing
  if (state.estimateReturned) {
    return buildContinue("Phelipe will have your exact quote shortly. Is there anything else I can help with?", fields);
  }

  state.qualified = true;
  state.estimateReturned = true;

  if (!fields.aircraftClass) {
    if (fields.pax <= 7) fields.aircraftClass = "light";
    else if (fields.pax <= 9) fields.aircraftClass = "super_mid";
    else fields.aircraftClass = "heavy";
  }

  const est = mockAviapagesEstimate(fields.departure, fields.arrival, fields.aircraftClass);

  const depAirport = AIRPORTS.get(fields.departure) ?? null;
  const viable = OPERATORS.filter((op) => {
    if (op.passengerCapacity < (fields.pax ?? 1)) return false;
    if (fields.aircraftClass && op.aircraftCategory !== fields.aircraftClass) return false;
    return true;
  });

  let topOperator: ScoredOperator | null = null;
  if (viable.length > 0 && depAirport) {
    const scored = viable.map((op) =>
      scoreOperator(op, depAirport, fields.departure!, fields.arrival!, null),
    );
    scored.sort((a, b) => b.score - a.score);
    topOperator = scored[0]!;
  }

  const depCity = cityName(fields.departure);
  const arrCity = cityName(fields.arrival);
  const dateStr = formatDateNatural(fields.date);
  const acName = topOperator?.operator.aircraftType ?? classToName(fields.aircraftClass);
  const opName = topOperator?.operator.operatorName ?? null;

  let spokenResponse: string;
  if (est) {
    const low = formatPrice(est.low);
    const high = formatPrice(est.high);
    spokenResponse = `For ${depCity} to ${arrCity} on ${dateStr} with ${fields.pax} passengers, you're looking at a ${acName}. Roughly ${low} to ${high} all-in.`;
    if (opName) spokenResponse += ` ${opName} runs that route regularly.`;
    spokenResponse += " Let me connect you with Phelipe, he'll have your exact quote within minutes.";
  } else {
    spokenResponse = `I have your trip from ${depCity} to ${arrCity} on ${dateStr} with ${fields.pax} passengers. Let me connect you with Phelipe to get you a precise quote on a ${acName}.`;
  }

  const dealData: Record<string, unknown> = {
    departure: fields.departure,
    arrival: fields.arrival,
    date: fields.date,
    pax: fields.pax,
    aircraft_class: fields.aircraftClass,
    aircraft_name: acName,
    qualified: true,
  };

  if (est) {
    dealData.estimate_low = est.low;
    dealData.estimate_high = est.high;
  }
  if (opName) {
    dealData.top_operator = opName;
  }

  return {
    response: spokenResponse,
    action: "transfer",
    transfer_to: BROKER_PHONE,
    deal_data: dealData,
  };
}
// === src/server/claude-sdr.ts ===
/**
 * Claude SDR — natural language layer over Dumont's extraction engine.
 * Haiku generates the spoken response; dumont-brain handles field extraction.
 */

import Anthropic from "@anthropic-ai/sdk";
import type { DumontResponse } from "./dumont-brain.js";

const client = new Anthropic();

const SYSTEM_PROMPT = `You are Dumont, the charter assistant at Orange Sunshine Aviation. You're on the phone with someone interested in a private flight. Think of yourself as a friendly, sharp concierge at a premium FBO — not a call center agent reading a script.

CURRENT STATE:
{state_json}

YOUR GOAL:
Collect four things naturally through conversation: where they're leaving from, where they're going, when, and how many people. That's it. Don't rush. Don't interrogate. Let the conversation breathe.

HOW REAL CHARTER BROKERS TALK:
- They combine questions: "Love it. Where are you coming from and how many are flying?"
- They acknowledge what was said before asking the next thing: "Chicago to Miami, great route."
- They're casual but professional: "Perfect" not "Acknowledged"
- They use first names when given: "Got it, Mike."
- Short sentences. Phone call, not email.
- They mirror the client's energy — relaxed client, relaxed tone. Urgent client, faster pace.
- They say things like "Let me get a couple details and we'll get you taken care of"
- They never say "certainly" or "absolutely" or "I understand" robotically

WHEN ALL FOUR DETAILS ARE COLLECTED:
Say something like: "Perfect, I've got everything I need — [departure] to [arrival] on [date] for [pax]. Phelipe, our broker, is actually up in the air right now finishing a flight. He'll give you a call as soon as he lands with your options and pricing. Usually within the hour. Sound good?"

RULES:
- Never make up prices, aircraft, or availability
- Never say "How can I assist you today" — too robotic
- If they ask about pricing: "Phelipe will have exact numbers for you — it depends on the aircraft and timing but he'll walk you through everything"
- If they ask what OSA does: "We arrange private flights. No lines, no hassle, door to door."
- If asked about safety: "Every operator we work with holds an FAA Part 135 certificate — we don't cut corners on that"
- Keep responses to 1-2 sentences. This is a phone call.
- Sound like a human who happens to be really good at this job
- Calls may be recorded for quality

NEVER SAY:
- "Certainly" / "Absolutely" / "I understand your needs"
- "Let me assist you with that"
- "Thank you for choosing Orange Sunshine Aviation"
- "Is there anything else I can help you with?"
- "Your call is important to us"`;

function buildSystemPrompt(result: DumontResponse): string {
  const state: Record<string, unknown> = {};
  const d = result.deal_data;
  if (d.departure) state.departure = d.departure;
  if (d.arrival) state.arrival = d.arrival;
  if (d.date) state.date = d.date;
  if (d.pax) state.pax = d.pax;
  const stateStr = Object.keys(state).length > 0
    ? JSON.stringify(state, null, 2)
    : "No details collected yet.";
  return SYSTEM_PROMPT.replace("{state_json}", stateStr);
}

export type MessageParam = { role: "user" | "assistant"; content: string };

/**
 * Stream Claude's response as an async iterator of text chunks.
 */
export async function* streamClaudeResponse(
  messages: MessageParam[],
  result: DumontResponse,
): AsyncGenerator<string> {
  const stream = client.messages.stream({
    model: "claude-haiku-4-5-20251001",
    max_tokens: 150,
    system: buildSystemPrompt(result),
    messages,
  });

  for await (const event of stream) {
    if (event.type === "content_block_delta" && event.delta.type === "text_delta") {
      yield event.delta.text;
    }
  }
}

/**
 * Non-streaming: get the full response as a string.
 */
export async function getClaudeResponse(
  messages: MessageParam[],
  result: DumontResponse,
): Promise<string> {
  const resp = await client.messages.create({
    model: "claude-haiku-4-5-20251001",
    max_tokens: 150,
    system: buildSystemPrompt(result),
    messages,
  });

  const block = resp.content[0];
  return block?.type === "text" ? block.text : result.response;
}
// === src/server/index.ts ===
/**
 * Dumont Agent — Express server.
 * Voice AI via Vapi /chat/completions endpoint.
 * Port 3001.
 */

import express from "express";
import type { Request, Response, NextFunction } from "express";
import cors from "cors";
import { conversationRouter } from "./routes/conversation.js";
import { healthRouter } from "./routes/health.js";
import { vapiRouter } from "./routes/vapi-llm.js";
import { closeDb } from "../memory/database.js";

// ── Env Validation ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────

const IS_PROD = process.env.NODE_ENV === "production";

if (!process.env.ANTHROPIC_API_KEY) {
  console.error("[dumont] FATAL: ANTHROPIC_API_KEY is required");
  process.exit(1);
}

if (!process.env.VAPI_SECRET) {
  console.warn("[security] VAPI_SECRET not set — /chat/completions is unauthenticated" + (IS_PROD ? " (PRODUCTION)" : " (dev)"));
}


// ── CORS ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

const ALLOWED_ORIGINS = process.env.ALLOWED_ORIGINS
  ? process.env.ALLOWED_ORIGINS.split(",").map((o) => o.trim())
  : IS_PROD
    ? [] // In production, require explicit ALLOWED_ORIGINS
    : ["http://localhost:5174", "http://localhost:3001"]; // Dev: dashboard + server

const app = express();
const PORT = parseInt(process.env.PORT ?? "3001", 10);

app.use(cors({
  origin: (origin, callback) => {
    // Allow requests with no origin (server-to-server, curl, Vapi webhooks)
    if (!origin) return callback(null, true);
    if (ALLOWED_ORIGINS.includes(origin)) return callback(null, true);
    callback(new Error(`CORS: origin ${origin} not allowed`));
  },
}));
app.use(express.json());

app.use(conversationRouter);
app.use(healthRouter);
app.use(vapiRouter);

// ── Global Error Handler ────────────────────────────────────────────────

app.use((err: Error, _req: Request, res: Response, _next: NextFunction) => {
  console.error(`[dumont] Unhandled error: ${err.message}`);
  if (!res.headersSent) {
    res.status(500).json({ error: "Internal server error" });
  }
});

// ── Start + Graceful Shutdown ───────────────────────────────────────────

const server = app.listen(PORT, "0.0.0.0", () => {
  console.log(`[dumont] Server running on port ${PORT}`);
});

function shutdown() {
  console.log("[dumont] Shutting down gracefully...");
  server.close(() => {
    closeDb();
    process.exit(0);
  });
  // Force exit after 5s if connections don't close
  setTimeout(() => {
    closeDb();
    process.exit(1);
  }, 5000).unref();
}

process.on("SIGTERM", shutdown);
process.on("SIGINT", shutdown);

export { app };
// === src/server/routes/vapi-llm.ts ===
/**
 * POST /chat/completions — OpenAI-compatible LLM endpoint for Vapi.
 * Vapi sends transcript → Dumont extracts fields → Claude generates spoken response → SSE back to Vapi.
 */

import { Router, type Request, type Response } from "express";
import { randomUUID, createHash } from "crypto";
import { processConversation } from "../dumont-brain.js";
import { streamClaudeResponse, getClaudeResponse, type MessageParam } from "../claude-sdr.js";
import { WebhookDedup } from "../../memory/webhook-dedup.js";

export const vapiRouter = Router();

// Webhook dedup — prevents duplicate processing from Vapi retries
const dedup = new WebhookDedup();

// ── Call ID resolution ──────────────────────────────────────────────────

const CALL_ID_TTL_MS = 60 * 60 * 1000; // 1 hour
const callIdMap = new Map<string, { id: string; ts: number }>();

// Evict stale call ID mappings every 10 minutes
setInterval(() => {
  const cutoff = Date.now() - CALL_ID_TTL_MS;
  for (const [fp, entry] of callIdMap) {
    if (entry.ts < cutoff) callIdMap.delete(fp);
  }
}, 10 * 60 * 1000).unref();

function deriveCallId(req: Request, messages: Array<{ role: string; content: string }>): string {
  const vapiHeader = req.headers["x-vapi-call-id"] as string | undefined;
  if (vapiHeader) return vapiHeader;
  if (req.body?.call_id) return req.body.call_id as string;

  const systemMsg = messages.find((m) => m.role === "system");
  const firstUser = messages.find((m) => m.role === "user");
  const fingerprint = createHash("md5")
    .update((systemMsg?.content ?? "") + "|" + (firstUser?.content ?? ""))
    .digest("hex")
    .slice(0, 16);

  const existing = callIdMap.get(fingerprint);
  if (existing) { existing.ts = Date.now(); return existing.id; }
  const newId = `vapi_${randomUUID().slice(0, 12)}`;
  callIdMap.set(fingerprint, { id: newId, ts: Date.now() });
  return newId;
}

function buildTranscript(messages: Array<{ role: string; content: string }>): string {
  return messages
    .filter((m) => m.role !== "system")
    .map((m) => `${m.role === "user" ? "Caller" : "Dumont"}: ${m.content}`)
    .join("\n");
}

function toClaudeMessages(messages: Array<{ role: string; content: string }>): MessageParam[] {
  return messages
    .filter((m) => m.role === "user" || m.role === "assistant")
    .map((m) => ({ role: m.role as "user" | "assistant", content: m.content }));
}

// ── SSE helpers ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

function sseChunk(id: string, ts: number, delta: Record<string, unknown>, finish?: string) {
  return JSON.stringify({
    id, object: "chat.completion.chunk", created: ts, model: "dumont",
    choices: [{ index: 0, delta, finish_reason: finish ?? null }],
  });
}

function transferToolCall(destination: string) {
  return [{
    index: 0,
    id: `call_${randomUUID().slice(0, 8)}`,
    type: "function",
    function: { name: "transferCall", arguments: JSON.stringify({ destination }) },
  }];
}

// ── Auth ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

const VAPI_SECRET = process.env.VAPI_SECRET;
const IS_PROD = process.env.NODE_ENV === "production";

function checkAuth(req: Request, res: Response): boolean {
  if (!VAPI_SECRET) {
    if (IS_PROD) {
      res.status(401).json({ error: "VAPI_SECRET required in production" });
      return false;
    }
    return true; // Dev only — allow without secret
  }
  const header = req.headers.authorization;
  if (header === `Bearer ${VAPI_SECRET}`) return true;
  res.status(401).json({ error: "unauthorized" });
  return false;
}

// ── Route ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

vapiRouter.post("/chat/completions", async (req: Request, res: Response) => {
  if (!checkAuth(req, res)) return;

  const { messages = [], stream = false } = req.body ?? {};

  if (!Array.isArray(messages) || messages.length === 0) {
    res.status(400).json({ error: "messages array required" });
    return;
  }

  // Idempotency: deduplicate webhook retries
  const bodyHash = createHash("sha256").update(JSON.stringify(req.body)).digest("hex").slice(0, 16);
  if (dedup.isDuplicate(bodyHash)) {
    res.status(200).json({ id: `chatcmpl-dedup`, choices: [{ message: { role: "assistant", content: "" } }] });
    return;
  }

  const callId = deriveCallId(req, messages);
  const turnCount = messages.filter((m: { role: string }) => m.role === "user").length;
  console.log("[vapi] call_id:", callId, "turn:", turnCount);

  const lastUser = [...messages].reverse().find((m: { role: string }) => m.role === "user");
  const result = processConversation({
    call_id: callId,
    transcript: buildTranscript(messages),
    last_utterance: lastUser?.content ?? "",
    extracted: null,
  });

  const completionId = `chatcmpl-${randomUUID().slice(0, 12)}`;
  const ts = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);
  const claudeMessages = toClaudeMessages(messages);

  // ── Non-streaming ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
  if (!stream) {
    let content: string;
    try {
      content = await getClaudeResponse(claudeMessages, result);
    } catch (err) {
      console.error("[vapi] Claude fallback:", (err as Error).message);
      content = result.response;
    }

    const message: Record<string, unknown> = { role: "assistant", content };
    if (result.action === "transfer" && result.transfer_to) {
      message.tool_calls = transferToolCall(result.transfer_to);
    }
    res.json({
      id: completionId, object: "chat.completion", created: ts, model: "dumont",
      choices: [{ index: 0, message, finish_reason: "stop" }],
      usage: { prompt_tokens: 0, completion_tokens: 0, total_tokens: 0 },
    });
    return;
  }

  // ── Streaming ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  res.setHeader("Content-Type", "text/event-stream");
  res.setHeader("Cache-Control", "no-cache");
  res.setHeader("Connection", "keep-alive");
  res.flushHeaders();

  const send = (data: string) => res.write(`data: ${data}\n\n`);
  send(sseChunk(completionId, ts, { role: "assistant" }));

  try {
    for await (const text of streamClaudeResponse(claudeMessages, result)) {
      send(sseChunk(completionId, ts, { content: text }));
    }
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("[vapi] Claude stream fallback:", (err as Error).message);
    const words = result.response.split(/(\s+)/);
    for (const w of words) {
      if (w) send(sseChunk(completionId, ts, { content: w }));
    }
  }

  if (result.action === "transfer" && result.transfer_to) {
    send(sseChunk(completionId, ts, { tool_calls: transferToolCall(result.transfer_to) }));
  }

  send(sseChunk(completionId, ts, {}, "stop"));
  res.write("data: [DONE]\n\n");
  res.end();
});
