// === src/server/routes/conversation.ts ===
/**
 * POST /api/dumont/respond — direct REST endpoint for curl testing.
 * Authenticated via DUMONT_API_KEY or VAPI_SECRET Bearer token.
 */

import { Router, type Request, type Response } from "express";
import { processConversation } from "../dumont-brain.js";

export const conversationRouter = Router();

const DUMONT_API_KEY = process.env.DUMONT_API_KEY;
const VAPI_SECRET = process.env.VAPI_SECRET;
const IS_PROD = process.env.NODE_ENV === "production";

function checkConversationAuth(req: Request, res: Response): boolean {
  // In production, require auth. No exceptions.
  const token = req.headers.authorization?.replace("Bearer ", "");
  if (token && (token === DUMONT_API_KEY || token === VAPI_SECRET)) return true;

  if (!DUMONT_API_KEY && !VAPI_SECRET) {
    if (IS_PROD) {
      res.status(401).json({ error: "Authentication required in production. Set DUMONT_API_KEY or VAPI_SECRET." });
      return false;
    }
    return true; // Dev only — allow without keys
  }

  res.status(401).json({ error: "unauthorized" });
  return false;
}

conversationRouter.post("/api/dumont/respond", (req: Request, res: Response) => {
  if (!checkConversationAuth(req, res)) return;
  const { call_id, transcript, last_utterance, extracted } = req.body ?? {};
  if (!call_id) { res.status(400).json({ error: "call_id required" }); return; }
  res.json(processConversation({ call_id, transcript: transcript ?? "", last_utterance: last_utterance ?? "", extracted: extracted ?? null }));
});
// === src/utils/errors.ts ===
/**
 * Typed error hierarchy for Dumont Agent.
 * Every error carries dealId, stage, and timestamp for structured logging
 * and debugging. The communication layer will route these to Mission Control.
 */

/** Base error for all Dumont Agent errors. */
export class DumontError extends Error {
  readonly dealId: string | null;
  readonly stage: string | null;
  readonly timestamp: string;

  constructor(message: string, options?: { dealId?: string; stage?: string }) {
    super(message);
    this.name = "DumontError";
    this.dealId = options?.dealId ?? null;
    this.stage = options?.stage ?? null;
    this.timestamp = new Date().toISOString();
  }
}

/** Thrown when intake parsing or qualification fails. */
export class IntakeError extends DumontError {
  constructor(message: string, options?: { dealId?: string }) {
    super(message, { ...options, stage: "intake" });
    this.name = "IntakeError";
  }
}

/** Thrown when Guardian blocks the pipeline. */
export class GuardianError extends DumontError {
  readonly blockReason: string;

  constructor(message: string, options: { dealId?: string; stage: string; blockReason: string }) {
    super(message, { dealId: options.dealId, stage: options.stage });
    this.name = "GuardianError";
    this.blockReason = options.blockReason;
  }
}

/** Thrown when estimate or quote processing fails. */
export class QuoteError extends DumontError {
  constructor(message: string, options?: { dealId?: string; stage?: string }) {
    super(message, { ...options, stage: options?.stage ?? "quoted" });
    this.name = "QuoteError";
  }
}

/** Thrown when operator sourcing fails. */
export class SourcingError extends DumontError {
  constructor(message: string, options?: { dealId?: string }) {
    super(message, { ...options, stage: "sourced" });
    this.name = "SourcingError";
  }
}

/** Thrown when the pipeline encounters an unrecoverable error. */
export class PipelineError extends DumontError {
  readonly outcome: string;

  constructor(message: string, options: { dealId?: string; stage?: string; outcome: string }) {
    super(message, { dealId: options.dealId, stage: options.stage });
    this.name = "PipelineError";
    this.outcome = options.outcome;
  }
}

/** Thrown when scenario/presentation building fails. */
export class ScenarioError extends DumontError {
  constructor(message: string, options?: { dealId?: string }) {
    super(message, { ...options, stage: "presented" });
    this.name = "ScenarioError";
  }
}
// === src/utils/recover-json.ts ===
/**
 * Attempts to recover valid JSON from malformed LLM output.
 * Three strategies: strip markdown fences, extract braces, Haiku fix.
 *
 * Migrated from ~/osa/simulator/src/utils/recover-json.ts
 */

import Anthropic from "@anthropic-ai/sdk";

export async function recoverJson(
  raw: string,
  apiClient?: Anthropic,
): Promise<unknown | null> {
  // Attempt 1: Strip markdown code fences
  const fenceStripped = raw
    .replace(/^```(?:json)?\s*/i, "")
    .replace(/\s*```\s*$/, "")
    .trim();
  try {
    return JSON.parse(fenceStripped);
  } catch {
    // continue
  }

  // Attempt 2: Extract first { to last }
  const start = raw.indexOf("{");
  const end = raw.lastIndexOf("}");
  if (start >= 0 && end > start) {
    try {
      return JSON.parse(raw.slice(start, end + 1));
    } catch {
      // continue
    }
  }

  // Attempt 3: Ask Haiku to fix it
  if (apiClient) {
    try {
      const response = await apiClient.messages.create({
        model: "claude-haiku-4-5-20251001",
        max_tokens: 4096,
        system:
          "Fix this invalid JSON. Return ONLY valid JSON, no explanation.",
        messages: [{ role: "user", content: raw }],
      });

      const text =
        response.content[0]?.type === "text" ? response.content[0].text : "";
      return JSON.parse(text.trim());
    } catch {
      // truly unrecoverable
    }
  }

  return null;
}
// === src/utils/safe-llm-call.ts ===
/**
 * Type-safe LLM call wrapper with retry, backoff, Zod validation, and token metering.
 *
 * Migrated from ~/osa/simulator/src/utils/safe-llm-call.ts
 * Updated model IDs to latest.
 *
 * Token metering:
 * - Captures response.usage from every Anthropic API call
 * - Right-sizes max_tokens per capability (not 4096 for everything)
 * - Estimates input tokens before call
 * - Tracks cumulative usage per deal with budget warning at 50K
 */

import Anthropic from "@anthropic-ai/sdk";
import { z } from "zod";
import { recoverJson } from "./recover-json.js";

// ── Token Metering ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export interface TokenUsageEntry {
  capability: string;
  dealId: string;
  model: string;
  inputTokens: number;
  outputTokens: number;
  estimatedInputTokens: number;
  timestamp: string;
}

/** Per-deal cumulative token budget tracker. */
const dealTokenBudgets = new Map<string, number>();

/** All token usage entries for inspection/export. */
const tokenUsageLog: TokenUsageEntry[] = [];

const DEAL_TOKEN_BUDGET = 50_000;
const MAX_TOKEN_LOG_ENTRIES = 1000;

/** Cheap input token estimate: ~4 chars per token on average. */
export function estimateInputTokens(systemPrompt: string, userMessage: string): number {
  return Math.ceil((systemPrompt.length + userMessage.length) / 4);
}

/** Record token usage and warn if deal exceeds budget. */
function recordTokenUsage(entry: TokenUsageEntry): void {
  tokenUsageLog.push(entry);
  // Ring buffer: cap at MAX_TOKEN_LOG_ENTRIES to prevent unbounded growth
  if (tokenUsageLog.length > MAX_TOKEN_LOG_ENTRIES) {
    tokenUsageLog.splice(0, tokenUsageLog.length - MAX_TOKEN_LOG_ENTRIES);
  }

  const current = dealTokenBudgets.get(entry.dealId) ?? 0;
  const total = current + entry.inputTokens + entry.outputTokens;
  dealTokenBudgets.set(entry.dealId, total);

  if (total > DEAL_TOKEN_BUDGET) {
    console.warn(
      `[token-budget] Deal ${entry.dealId} exceeded ${DEAL_TOKEN_BUDGET} tokens (now at ${total}). ` +
      `Last call: ${entry.capability} used ${entry.inputTokens}+${entry.outputTokens} tokens.`,
    );
  }
}

/** Evict a completed deal from the budget tracker (call on deal close). */
export function evictDealTokenBudget(dealId: string): void {
  dealTokenBudgets.delete(dealId);
}

/** Get cumulative token usage for a deal. */
export function getDealTokenUsage(dealId: string): number {
  return dealTokenBudgets.get(dealId) ?? 0;
}

/** Get full token usage log (for debugging/export). */
export function getTokenUsageLog(): TokenUsageEntry[] {
  return [...tokenUsageLog];
}

/** Reset token tracking (for testing). */
export function _resetTokenTracking(): void {
  dealTokenBudgets.clear();
  tokenUsageLog.length = 0;
}

// ── Right-sized output token limits per capability ──────────────────────

const EXPECTED_MAX_OUTPUT: Record<string, number> = {
  intake: 512,
  guardian: 512,
  estimate: 256,
  respond: 1024,
  evaluate: 512,
  revise: 1024,
  recovery: 512,
};

const DEFAULT_MAX_OUTPUT = 1024;

// ── LLM Call Interface ──────────────────────────────────────────────────

export interface LlmCallOptions {
  model: string;
  systemPrompt: string;
  userMessage: string;
  schema: z.ZodType;
  maxRetries?: number;
  baseTimeoutMs?: number;
  apiClient: Anthropic;
  temperature?: number;
  /** Capability name for token metering (e.g. "intake", "respond"). */
  capability?: string;
  /** Deal ID for per-deal token budget tracking. */
  dealId?: string;
  /** Override max output tokens. Uses capability-based default if not set. */
  expectedMaxOutput?: number;
}

export interface LlmCallError {
  type:
    | "llm_timeout"
    | "llm_api_error"
    | "llm_invalid_json"
    | "schema_validation_failed";
  message: string;
  retriesAttempted: number;
}

export type LlmCallResult<T> =
  | { ok: true; data: T; retriesUsed: number; usage?: TokenUsageEntry }
  | { ok: false; error: LlmCallError };

const DEFAULT_TIMEOUTS: Record<string, number> = {
  "claude-haiku-4-5-20251001": 30_000,
  "claude-sonnet-4-20250514": 45_000,
  "claude-opus-4-20250514": 60_000,
};

export async function safeLlmCall<T>(
  options: LlmCallOptions & { schema: z.ZodType<T> },
): Promise<LlmCallResult<T>> {
  const {
    model,
    systemPrompt,
    userMessage,
    schema,
    apiClient,
    temperature = 0,
    capability = "unknown",
    dealId = "unknown",
  } = options;
  const maxRetries = options.maxRetries ?? 3;
  let timeoutMs =
    options.baseTimeoutMs ?? DEFAULT_TIMEOUTS[model] ?? 45_000;

  // Right-size output tokens
  const maxTokens = options.expectedMaxOutput
    ?? EXPECTED_MAX_OUTPUT[capability]
    ?? DEFAULT_MAX_OUTPUT;

  // Pre-call input estimate
  const estimatedInput = estimateInputTokens(systemPrompt, userMessage);

  for (let attempt = 1; attempt <= maxRetries; attempt++) {
    try {
      const controller = new AbortController();
      const timer = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), timeoutMs);

      const response = await apiClient.messages.create(
        {
          model,
          max_tokens: maxTokens,
          system: systemPrompt,
          messages: [{ role: "user", content: userMessage }],
          temperature,
        },
        { signal: controller.signal },
      );

      clearTimeout(timer);

      // Capture token usage from response
      const usageEntry: TokenUsageEntry = {
        capability,
        dealId,
        model,
        inputTokens: response.usage?.input_tokens ?? 0,
        outputTokens: response.usage?.output_tokens ?? 0,
        estimatedInputTokens: estimatedInput,
        timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
      };
      recordTokenUsage(usageEntry);

      // Check for truncation — if max_tokens was hit, retry with doubled limit
      if (response.stop_reason === "max_tokens" && attempt < maxRetries) {
        continue;
      }

      const text =
        response.content[0]?.type === "text" ? response.content[0].text : "";

      let parsed: unknown;
      try {
        parsed = JSON.parse(text.trim());
      } catch {
        parsed = await recoverJson(text, apiClient);
        if (parsed === null) {
          if (attempt < maxRetries) continue;
          return {
            ok: false,
            error: {
              type: "llm_invalid_json",
              message: `Invalid JSON after ${attempt} attempts`,
              retriesAttempted: attempt,
            },
          };
        }
      }

      const result = schema.safeParse(parsed);
      if (result.success) {
        return { ok: true, data: result.data, retriesUsed: attempt - 1, usage: usageEntry };
      }

      if (attempt < maxRetries) continue;

      return {
        ok: false,
        error: {
          type: "schema_validation_failed",
          message: `Schema validation failed: ${result.error.message}`,
          retriesAttempted: attempt,
        },
      };
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      if (err instanceof Error && err.name === "AbortError") {
        if (attempt < maxRetries) {
          timeoutMs = Math.round(timeoutMs * 1.5);
          continue;
        }
        return {
          ok: false,
          error: {
            type: "llm_timeout",
            message: `Timed out after ${timeoutMs}ms on attempt ${attempt}`,
            retriesAttempted: attempt,
          },
        };
      }

      if (
        err instanceof Anthropic.RateLimitError ||
        (err instanceof Error && err.message.includes("rate_limit"))
      ) {
        await sleep(2 ** attempt * 1000);
        continue;
      }

      if (attempt < maxRetries) continue;

      return {
        ok: false,
        error: {
          type: "llm_api_error",
          message: err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err),
          retriesAttempted: attempt,
        },
      };
    }
  }

  return {
    ok: false,
    error: {
      type: "llm_api_error",
      message: "Exhausted all retries",
      retriesAttempted: maxRetries,
    },
  };
}

function sleep(ms: number): Promise<void> {
  return new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, ms));
}
// === src/utils/haversine.ts ===
/**
 * Haversine formula — great-circle distance between two points.
 * Returns distance in nautical miles.
 *
 * Migrated from ~/osa/simulator/src/utils/haversine.ts
 */

const EARTH_RADIUS_NM = 3440.065;

export function haversine(
  lat1: number,
  lon1: number,
  lat2: number,
  lon2: number,
): number {
  const toRad = (deg: number) => (deg * Math.PI) / 180;

  const dLat = toRad(lat2 - lat1);
  const dLon = toRad(lon2 - lon1);

  const a =
    Math.sin(dLat / 2) ** 2 +
    Math.cos(toRad(lat1)) * Math.cos(toRad(lat2)) * Math.sin(dLon / 2) ** 2;

  const c = 2 * Math.atan2(Math.sqrt(a), Math.sqrt(1 - a));

  return EARTH_RADIUS_NM * c;
}

/**

  fromName: z.string().nullable(),

  body: z.string(),
  bodyHtml: z.string().nullable(),

  draftedBy: z.enum(["dumont", "human"]).nullable(),
  approvedBy: z.string().nullable(),
  editDistance: z.number().int().nonnegative().nullable(),
  originalDraft: z.string().nullable(),

  receivedAt: z.string().datetime().nullable(),
  draftedAt: z.string().datetime().nullable(),

  confidence: z.number().min(0).max(1).nullable(),
  confidenceLevel: ConfidenceLevel.nullable(),

  platformMessageId: z.string().nullable(),
  platformMetadata: z.record(z.unknown()).nullable(),
});
export type Message = z.infer<typeof MessageSchema>;

/** Create a Message ID. */
export function generateMessageId(): string {
  const ts = Date.now().toString(36);
  const rand = Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 8);
  return `msg_${ts}_${rand}`;
}

/**
 * Composite Objects — pre-aggregated intelligence that accumulates from deals.
 *
 * These are NOT throwaway caches. They are permanent business objects that
 * power pricing, sourcing, and client personalization. They also serve as
 * the foundation for future payment infrastructure and financial products.

});

// === src/schemas/conversation.ts ===
/**
 * Conversation schema — unified thread for all messages in a deal.
 * Per spec: communication-layer.md FR-7 (Conversation Threading).
 */

import { z } from "zod";
import { MessageSchema, MessageChannel } from "./message.js";

export const AwaitingResponseFrom = z.enum(["client", "operator", "internal"]);
export type AwaitingResponseFrom = z.infer<typeof AwaitingResponseFrom>;

export const ConversationSchema = z.object({
  dealId: z.string(),
  messages: z.array(MessageSchema),
  touchpointCount: z.number().int().nonnegative(),
  avgResponseTimeMs: z.number().nonnegative().nullable(),
  channelsUsed: z.array(MessageChannel),
  clientContact: z.string().nullable(),
  operatorContact: z.string().nullable(),
  lastActivity: z.string().datetime().nullable(),
  awaitingResponseFrom: AwaitingResponseFrom.nullable(),
});
export type Conversation = z.infer<typeof ConversationSchema>;

/** Create an empty conversation for a deal. */
export function createConversation(dealId: string): Conversation {
  return {
    dealId,
    messages: [],
    touchpointCount: 0,
    avgResponseTimeMs: null,
    channelsUsed: [],
    clientContact: null,
    operatorContact: null,
    lastActivity: null,
    awaitingResponseFrom: null,
  };
}

/** Add a message to a conversation, updating metrics. */
export function addMessageToConversation(
  conv: Conversation,
  msg: z.infer<typeof MessageSchema>,
): void {
  conv.messages.push(msg);
  conv.touchpointCount = conv.messages.length;
  conv.lastActivity = msg.receivedAt ?? msg.sentAt ?? new Date().toISOString();

  // Track channels used
  if (!conv.channelsUsed.includes(msg.channel)) {
    conv.channelsUsed.push(msg.channel);
  }

  // Compute avg response time from inbound/outbound pairs
  const responseTimes = conv.messages
    .filter((m) => m.responseTimeMs !== null && m.responseTimeMs !== undefined)
    .map((m) => m.responseTimeMs!);
  if (responseTimes.length > 0) {
    conv.avgResponseTimeMs = responseTimes.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / responseTimes.length;
  }
}

  "expired",

  operatorResponseTime: z.number().nullable(),

  aircraftClass: z.string().nullable(),

// === src/schemas/deal-context.ts ===
/**
 * Deal Context — single source of truth for every deal in the pipeline.
 *
 * This is the new Dumont Agent schema, rebuilt from the orchestrator spec.
 * The old simulator had 5 independent agent sections; this schema follows
 * the spec's unified DealContext interface with Guardian as continuous
 * validation, not a final checkpoint.
 *
 * Key changes from simulator:
 * - Stages match the new pipeline: intake → qualified → estimated → sourced → quoted → presented → confirmed → closed → failed
 * - Client section includes budget + qualification (new)
 * - Guardian is an array of passes (runs at every stage), not a single section
 * - Estimate section uses Aviapages API (new)
 * - Sourcing selects ONE operator + ONE backup (was top-3)
 * - Quote captures real operator price + OSA margin (was formula-based)
 * - Presentation tracks client decision + timer (new)
 * - Outcome captures learning loop data (new)
 */

export type QuoteSection = z.infer<typeof QuoteSectionSchema>;

export const PresentationSchema = z.object({
  sentAt: z.string().datetime().nullable(),
  viewedAt: z.string().datetime().nullable(),
  clientDecision: ClientDecision.nullable(),
  decisionAt: z.string().datetime().nullable(),
  timerStarted: z.string().datetime().nullable(),
});
export type Presentation = z.infer<typeof PresentationSchema>;

export const OutcomeSchema = z.object({
  result: DealOutcome.nullable(),
  estimateVsActual: z.number().nullable(),       // percentage delta
  operatorResponseTime: z.number().nullable(),   // seconds
  totalDealTime: z.number().nullable(),          // seconds
  revenue: z.number().nullable(),
  notes: z.string().nullable(),
});
export type Outcome = z.infer<typeof OutcomeSchema>;

// ── Deal Context ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export const DealContextSchema = z.object({
  // Identity
  id: z.string(),
  createdAt: z.string().datetime(),
  updatedAt: z.string().datetime(),
  mode: DealMode,

  // Stage tracking
  stage: DealStage,
  stageHistory: z.array(StageHistoryEntrySchema),

  // Sections
  client: ClientSchema,
  trip: TripSchema,
  guardian: GuardianSectionSchema,
  estimate: EstimateSchema,
  sourcing: SourcingSectionSchema,
  quote: QuoteSectionSchema,
  presentation: PresentationSchema,
  outcome: OutcomeSchema,
});
export type DealContext = z.infer<typeof DealContextSchema>;
// === src/integrations/twilio.ts ===
/**
 * Twilio conference bridge client — 3-way calls for voice copilot.
 * Per spec: communication-layer.md FR-5, section 2.3.
 * Credentials via env: TWILIO_ACCOUNT_SID, TWILIO_AUTH_TOKEN.
 */

/** Injectable fetch for testing. */
export type FetchFn = (url: string, init: RequestInit) => Promise<{
  ok: boolean;
  status: number;
  json: () => Promise<unknown>;
}>;

// ── Types ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export interface TwilioConference {
  conferenceSid: string;
  friendlyName: string;
  status: string;
}

export interface TwilioParticipant {
  callSid: string;
  label: string;
  muted: boolean;
}

export interface TwilioStreamConfig {
  streamSid: string;
  url: string;
}

export interface ParticipantOptions {
  label: string;
  muted?: boolean;
  announceUrl?: string;
}

// ── Client ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export interface TwilioConfig {
  accountSid: string;
  authToken: string;
  baseUrl?: string;
}

export class TwilioClient {
  private readonly accountSid: string;
  private readonly authToken: string;
  private readonly baseUrl: string;
  private readonly fetchFn: FetchFn;

  constructor(config: TwilioConfig, fetchFn?: FetchFn) {
    this.accountSid = config.accountSid;
    this.authToken = config.authToken;
    this.baseUrl = config.baseUrl ?? `https://api.twilio.com/2010-04-01/Accounts/${config.accountSid}`;
    this.fetchFn = fetchFn ?? (globalThis.fetch as unknown as FetchFn);
  }

  private authHeader(): string {
    return `Basic ${Buffer.from(`${this.accountSid}:${this.authToken}`).toString("base64")}`;
  }

  /**
   * Create a Twilio conference bridge with recording enabled.
   * @param dealId - Deal ID used as the conference friendly name.
   * @returns Conference SID and metadata.
   */
  async createConference(dealId: string): Promise<TwilioConference> {
    const url = `${this.baseUrl}/Conferences.json`;
    const resp = await this.fetchFn(url, {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "Authorization": this.authHeader(), "Content-Type": "application/x-www-form-urlencoded" },
      body: `FriendlyName=${encodeURIComponent(dealId)}&Record=true&RecordingChannels=dual`,
    });
    const data = await resp.json() as Record<string, unknown>;
    return {
      conferenceSid: String(data["sid"] ?? ""),
      friendlyName: dealId,
      status: String(data["status"] ?? "init"),
    };
  }

  /**
   * Add a participant to an existing conference.
   * @param conferenceSid - The conference to join.
   * @param phone - Phone number to dial.
   * @param options - Label, muted flag, consent disclosure URL.
   * @returns Participant call SID and metadata.
   */
  async addParticipant(conferenceSid: string, phone: string, options: ParticipantOptions): Promise<TwilioParticipant> {
    const url = `${this.baseUrl}/Conferences/${conferenceSid}/Participants.json`;
    let body = `To=${encodeURIComponent(phone)}&Label=${encodeURIComponent(options.label)}`;
    if (options.muted) body += "&Muted=true";
    if (options.announceUrl) body += `&AnnounceUrl=${encodeURIComponent(options.announceUrl)}`;

    const resp = await this.fetchFn(url, {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "Authorization": this.authHeader(), "Content-Type": "application/x-www-form-urlencoded" },
      body,
    });
    const data = await resp.json() as Record<string, unknown>;
    return {
      callSid: String(data["call_sid"] ?? ""),
      label: options.label,
      muted: options.muted ?? false,
    };
  }

  /**
   * Start a media stream from the conference to a WebSocket URL (for transcription).
   * @param conferenceSid - The conference to stream.
   * @param streamUrl - WebSocket URL to send audio to.
   * @returns Stream SID.
   */
  async startStream(conferenceSid: string, streamUrl: string): Promise<TwilioStreamConfig> {
    const url = `${this.baseUrl}/Conferences/${conferenceSid}/Streams.json`;
    const resp = await this.fetchFn(url, {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "Authorization": this.authHeader(), "Content-Type": "application/x-www-form-urlencoded" },
      body: `Url=${encodeURIComponent(streamUrl)}&Track=both_tracks`,
    });
    const data = await resp.json() as Record<string, unknown>;
    return { streamSid: String(data["sid"] ?? ""), url: streamUrl };
  }

  /**
   * End a conference and all participants.
   * @param conferenceSid - The conference to terminate.
   */
  async endConference(conferenceSid: string): Promise<void> {
    const url = `${this.baseUrl}/Conferences/${conferenceSid}.json`;
    await this.fetchFn(url, {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "Authorization": this.authHeader(), "Content-Type": "application/x-www-form-urlencoded" },
      body: "Status=completed",
    });
  }
}
// === src/integrations/resend.ts ===
/**
 * Resend API client — email send/receive for operator RFQs and client quotes.
 * Per spec: communication-layer.md Sprint 3.
 * Credentials via env: RESEND_API_KEY, RESEND_WEBHOOK_SECRET, RESEND_FROM_ADDRESS.
 */

import { createHmac } from "node:crypto";

/** Fetch function type — injectable for testing. */
export type FetchFn = (url: string, init: RequestInit) => Promise<{
  ok: boolean;
  status: number;
  json: () => Promise<unknown>;
}>;

// ── Types ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export interface ResendSendOptions {
  from?: string;
  replyTo?: string;
  headers?: Record<string, string>;
  attachments?: Array<{ filename: string; content: string }>;
}

export interface ResendSendResult {
  success: boolean;
  messageId: string | null;
  error: string | null;
}

/** Resend inbound email webhook payload. */
export interface ResendInboundWebhook {
  type: "email.received";
  data: {
    id: string;
    from: string;
    from_name?: string;
    to: string[];
    subject: string;
    text?: string;
    html?: string;
    headers?: Record<string, string>;
    attachments?: Array<{ filename: string; content_type: string; url: string }>;
    created_at: string;
  };
}

// ── Client ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export interface ResendConfig {
  apiKey: string;
  webhookSecret?: string;
  fromAddress?: string;
  baseUrl?: string;
}

export class ResendClient {
  readonly baseUrl: string;
  private readonly apiKey: string;
  private readonly webhookSecret: string | null;
  readonly fromAddress: string;
  private readonly fetchFn: FetchFn;

  constructor(config: ResendConfig, fetchFn?: FetchFn) {
    this.apiKey = config.apiKey;
    this.webhookSecret = config.webhookSecret ?? null;
    this.fromAddress = config.fromAddress ?? "charter@osa.aero";
    this.baseUrl = config.baseUrl ?? "https://api.resend.com";
    this.fetchFn = fetchFn ?? (globalThis.fetch as unknown as FetchFn);
  }

  /**
   * Send an email via Resend API.
   * @param to - Recipient email address.
   * @param subject - Email subject line.
   * @param html - HTML body content.
   * @param options - Reply-to, headers (In-Reply-To), attachments.
   * @returns Send result with Resend message ID.
   */
  async sendEmail(
    to: string,
    subject: string,
    html: string,
    options?: ResendSendOptions,
  ): Promise<ResendSendResult> {
    const url = `${this.baseUrl}/emails`;
    try {
      const resp = await this.fetchFn(url, {
        method: "POST",
        headers: {
          "Authorization": `Bearer ${this.apiKey}`,
          "Content-Type": "application/json",
        },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          from: options?.from ?? this.fromAddress,
          to: [to],
          subject,
          html,
          reply_to: options?.replyTo,
          headers: options?.headers,
          attachments: options?.attachments,
        }),
      });

      if (!resp.ok) {
        return { success: false, messageId: null, error: `Resend API returned ${resp.status}` };
      }

      const data = await resp.json() as Record<string, unknown>;
      return {
        success: true,
        messageId: (data["id"] as string) ?? null,
        error: null,
      };
    } catch (err) {
      return {
        success: false,
        messageId: null,
        error: err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err),
      };
    }
  }

  /**
   * Verify a Resend webhook signature.
   * @param payload - Raw JSON body string.
   * @param signature - Value from svix-signature header.
   * @returns True if valid.
   */
  verifyWebhook(payload: string, signature: string): boolean {
    if (!this.webhookSecret) {
      console.warn("[security] Resend webhook secret not configured — rejecting unverified request");
      return false;
    }
    const expected = createHmac("sha256", this.webhookSecret)
      .update(payload)
      .digest("hex");
    return expected === signature;
  }
}
// === src/integrations/aviapages.ts ===
/**
 * Aviapages API Integration — price calculator, flight calculator, directories.
 *
 * Endpoints per spec:
 * - POST /price_calculator/  → charter cost estimate
 * - POST /flight_calculator/ → flight time + route + fuel
 * - GET  /aircraft/{id}      → aircraft specs
 *
 * HTTP calls are stubbed for now — the full request/response flow is built
 * so that real API integration only requires filling in fetch bodies.
 * Tests inject a mock via the `fetchFn` option.
 */

export interface AviapagesConfig {
  apiKey: string;
  baseUrl?: string;
}

export interface PriceEstimateParams {
  departure: string;   // ICAO
  arrival: string;     // ICAO
  aircraftType?: string;
  date: string;        // ISO date
  pax: number;
}

export interface PriceEstimateResult {
  low: number;
  high: number;
  flightTime: number;  // minutes
  fuelBurn: number;    // gallons
}

export interface FlightCalcParams {
  departure: string;
  arrival: string;
  aircraftType: string;
}

export interface FlightCalcResult {
  flightTime: number;  // minutes
  distance: number;    // nm
  fuelBurn: number;    // gallons
}

/**
 * Function type matching the subset of fetch we need.
 * Inject a mock in tests; production uses global fetch.
 */
export type FetchFn = (url: string, init: RequestInit) => Promise<{
  ok: boolean;
  status: number;
  json: () => Promise<unknown>;
}>;

export class AviapagesClient {
  readonly apiKey: string;
  readonly baseUrl: string;
  private readonly fetchFn: FetchFn;

  constructor(config: AviapagesConfig, fetchFn?: FetchFn) {
    this.apiKey = config.apiKey;
    this.baseUrl = config.baseUrl ?? "https://dir.aviapages.com/api";
    this.fetchFn = fetchFn ?? (globalThis.fetch as unknown as FetchFn);
  }

  /**
   * Get a charter price estimate from the Aviapages Price Calculator API.
   * @param params - Departure/arrival ICAO, aircraft type, date, pax count.
   * @returns Price range with flight time and fuel burn, or null on API failure.
   */
  async getPriceEstimate(params: PriceEstimateParams): Promise<PriceEstimateResult | null> {
    const url = `${this.baseUrl}/price_calculator/`;
    try {
      const resp = await this.fetchFn(url, {
        method: "POST",
        headers: {
          "Authorization": `Token ${this.apiKey}`,
          "Content-Type": "application/json",
        },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          departure: params.departure,
          arrival: params.arrival,
          aircraft_type: params.aircraftType ?? undefined,
          date: params.date,
          pax: params.pax,
        }),
      });

      if (!resp.ok) return null;

      const data = await resp.json() as Record<string, unknown>;

      // Map Aviapages response to our interface
      return {
        low: Number(data["price_low"] ?? data["low"] ?? 0),
        high: Number(data["price_high"] ?? data["high"] ?? 0),
        flightTime: Number(data["flight_time"] ?? data["flightTime"] ?? 0),
        fuelBurn: Number(data["fuel_burn"] ?? data["fuelBurn"] ?? 0),
      };
    } catch (err) {
      console.warn(`[aviapages:getPriceEstimate] ${err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err)}`);
      return null;
    }
  }

  /**
   * Get flight time, distance, and fuel burn from the Aviapages Flight Calculator API.
   * @param params - Departure/arrival ICAO and aircraft type.
   * @returns Flight calculation result, or null on API failure.
   */
  async getFlightCalculation(params: FlightCalcParams): Promise<FlightCalcResult | null> {
    const url = `${this.baseUrl}/flight_calculator/`;
    try {
      const resp = await this.fetchFn(url, {
        method: "POST",
        headers: {
          "Authorization": `Token ${this.apiKey}`,
          "Content-Type": "application/json",
        },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          departure: params.departure,
          arrival: params.arrival,
          aircraft_type: params.aircraftType,
        }),
      });

      if (!resp.ok) return null;

      const data = await resp.json() as Record<string, unknown>;

      return {
        flightTime: Number(data["flight_time"] ?? data["flightTime"] ?? 0),
        distance: Number(data["distance"] ?? 0),
        fuelBurn: Number(data["fuel_burn"] ?? data["fuelBurn"] ?? 0),
      };
    } catch (err) {
      console.warn(`[aviapages:getFlightCalc] ${err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err)}`);
      return null;
    }
  }
}
// === src/integrations/deepgram.ts ===
/**
 * Deepgram streaming transcription client — Nova-2 with diarization.
 * Per spec: communication-layer.md FR-5, Sprint 4.
 * Credentials via env: DEEPGRAM_API_KEY.
 */

// ── Aviation Vocabulary ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Seeded with known terms. Grows as more calls are processed.

export const AVIATION_VOCABULARY: string[] = [
  // ICAO airport codes
  "KTEB", "KPBI", "KASE", "KPWK", "KORD", "KMDW", "KMIA", "KFLL",
  "KVNY", "KLAS", "KDAL", "KHOU", "KHPN", "KEGE", "KLAX", "KJFK",
  "MYNN", "MMSD", "TXKF",
  // Aircraft types
  "Phenom 300E", "Phenom 300", "Citation CJ3", "Citation CJ3+",
  "Citation Latitude", "Citation X", "Citation X+",
  "Challenger 300", "Challenger 350", "Challenger 650",
  "Gulfstream G280", "Gulfstream G550", "Gulfstream G650",
  "Global 7500", "Global 6000", "Global Express",
  "King Air 350", "King Air 350i", "Learjet 75",
  // Pricing language
  "all-in", "plus fees", "repositioning", "empty leg",
  "twenty-eight five", "thirty-two thousand",
  "per hour", "block rate",
  // Aviation terms
  "Part 135", "ARGUS", "Wyvern", "FBO", "tail number",
];

// ── Types ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export interface DeepgramConfig {
  apiKey: string;
  model?: string;
  language?: string;
  customVocabulary?: string[];
}

/** Callback for each transcript chunk. */
export type TranscriptCallback = (chunk: {
  speaker: number;
  text: string;
  startMs: number;
  endMs: number;
  confidence: number;
  isFinal: boolean;
}) => void;

/** Injectable WebSocket factory for testing. No real connections in tests. */
export type WebSocketFactory = (url: string) => MockableWebSocket;

export interface MockableWebSocket {
  send(data: unknown): void;
  close(): void;
  onmessage: ((event: { data: string }) => void) | null;
  onclose: (() => void) | null;
  onerror: ((err: unknown) => void) | null;
  readyState: number;
}

// ── Client ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export class DeepgramClient {
  readonly apiKey: string;
  private readonly model: string;
  private readonly language: string;
  private readonly vocabulary: string[];
  private readonly wsFactory: WebSocketFactory | null;
  private ws: MockableWebSocket | null = null;
  private callback: TranscriptCallback | null = null;

  constructor(config: DeepgramConfig, wsFactory?: WebSocketFactory) {
    this.apiKey = config.apiKey;
    this.model = config.model ?? "nova-2";
    this.language = config.language ?? "en";
    this.vocabulary = [...AVIATION_VOCABULARY, ...(config.customVocabulary ?? [])];
    this.wsFactory = wsFactory ?? null;
  }

  /**
   * Open a streaming transcription session with Deepgram.
   * Uses Nova-2 with diarization and custom aviation vocabulary.
   * @returns The session config for reference.
   */
  createStreamingSession(): { model: string; language: string; vocabularySize: number } {
    const params = new URLSearchParams({
      model: this.model,
      language: this.language,
      diarize: "true",
      punctuate: "true",
      interim_results: "true",
      keywords: this.vocabulary.slice(0, 100).join(","),
    });

    const url = `wss://api.deepgram.com/v1/listen?${params.toString()}`;

    if (this.wsFactory) {
      this.ws = this.wsFactory(url);
      this.ws.onmessage = (event) => this.handleMessage(event.data);
    }

    return { model: this.model, language: this.language, vocabularySize: this.vocabulary.length };
  }

  /**
   * Register a callback for transcript chunks.
   * @param cb - Called on each transcript chunk with speaker, text, timing, confidence.
   */
  onTranscript(cb: TranscriptCallback): void {
    this.callback = cb;
  }

  /** Send raw audio data to the Deepgram session. */
  sendAudio(data: unknown): void {
    this.ws?.send(data);
  }

  /** Close the streaming session. */
  close(): void {
    this.ws?.close();
    this.ws = null;
  }

  /** Parse a Deepgram WebSocket message and fire the callback. */
  private handleMessage(raw: string): void {
    if (!this.callback) return;
    try {
      const msg = JSON.parse(raw) as DeepgramResponse;
      const alt = msg.channel?.alternatives?.[0];
      if (!alt?.transcript) return;

      this.callback({
        speaker: msg.channel?.alternatives?.[0]?.words?.[0]?.speaker ?? 0,
        text: alt.transcript,
        startMs: Math.round((msg.start ?? 0) * 1000),
        endMs: Math.round(((msg.start ?? 0) + (msg.duration ?? 0)) * 1000),
        confidence: alt.confidence ?? 0,
        isFinal: msg.is_final ?? false,
      });
    } catch (err) {
      console.warn(`[deepgram:onMessage] Malformed WebSocket message: ${err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err)}`);
    }
  }
}

// ── Deepgram response shape (subset) ─────────────────────────────────────

interface DeepgramResponse {
  channel?: {
    alternatives?: Array<{
      transcript: string;
      confidence: number;
      words?: Array<{ speaker: number }>;
    }>;
  };
  start?: number;
  duration?: number;
  is_final?: boolean;
}
// === src/templates/render.ts ===
/**
 * Template renderer — interpolates {{variable}} placeholders.
 * Loads HTML templates from the templates/email/ directory.
 */

import { readFileSync } from "node:fs";
import { resolve, dirname } from "node:path";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";

const __dirname = dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));

const TEMPLATE_CACHE = new Map<string, string>();

/**
 * Load and cache an email template by name.
 * @param name - Template name (e.g. "operator-rfq", "client-quote").
 * @returns Raw HTML string with {{placeholders}}.
 */
function loadTemplate(name: string): string {
  const cached = TEMPLATE_CACHE.get(name);
  if (cached) return cached;

  const path = resolve(__dirname, "email", `${name}.html`);
  const content = readFileSync(path, "utf-8");
  TEMPLATE_CACHE.set(name, content);
  return content;
}

/**
 * Render a template with data interpolation.
 * Replaces all {{key}} placeholders with values from the data object.
 * Missing keys are replaced with empty string.
 *
 * @param templateName - Template to render (e.g. "operator-rfq").
 * @param data - Key-value pairs for interpolation.
 * @returns Rendered HTML string.
 */
export function renderTemplate(templateName: string, data: Record<string, string | number>): string {
  let html = loadTemplate(templateName);
  for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(data)) {
    const escaped = key.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, "\\$&");
    html = html.replace(new RegExp(`\\{\\{${escaped}\\}\\}`, "g"), String(value));
  }
  // Remove any remaining unreplaced placeholders
  html = html.replace(/\{\{[^}]+\}\}/g, "");
  return html;
}

/** Clear the template cache (for testing). */
export function _clearTemplateCache(): void {
  TEMPLATE_CACHE.clear();
}

/**
 * Mock Aviapages pricing lookup. Returns estimate for a route + class combo.
 * Falls back to generic distance-based pricing if no specific data.
 */
export function mockAviapagesEstimate(
  departure: string,
  arrival: string,
  aircraftClass: string | null,
): PriceEstimateResult | null {
  const routeKey = `${departure}-${arrival}`;
  const reverseKey = `${arrival}-${departure}`;
  const routeData = ROUTE_PRICING[routeKey] ?? ROUTE_PRICING[reverseKey];

  if (routeData) {
    // Try exact class match, then any available
    const cls = aircraftClass ?? "light";
    const match = routeData[cls] ?? Object.values(routeData)[0];
    if (match) return match;
  }

  // Fallback: distance-based generic pricing
  const dep = AIRPORTS.get(departure);
  const arr = AIRPORTS.get(arrival);
  if (!dep || !arr) return null;

  const dist = haversineDist(dep.latitude, dep.longitude, arr.latitude, arr.longitude);
  const perNm = aircraftClass === "heavy" ? 40 : aircraftClass === "super_mid" ? 30 : aircraftClass === "midsize" ? 25 : 18;
  const base = Math.round(dist * perNm);

  return {
    low: Math.round(base * 0.85),
    high: Math.round(base * 1.15),
    flightTime: Math.round(dist / 7), // ~420 ktas average
    fuelBurn: Math.round(dist * 0.3),
  };
}
