// === src/run-sim.ts ===
/**
 * Scenario Runner — reads scenario JSON files, feeds each through the full
 * Dumont Agent pipeline, and reports pass/fail with stage-by-stage results.
 *
 * Usage: npm run sim
 *    or: npx tsx src/run-sim.ts
 *    or: npx tsx src/run-sim.ts --scenario S01
 *
 * Target: beat the old simulator's 72% pass rate.
 */

import { readFileSync } from "node:fs";
import { resolve, dirname } from "node:path";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";

import { createDealContext, updateDealContext } from "./orchestrator/deal-context-manager.js";
import { advanceStage } from "./orchestrator/stage-transitions.js";
import { closeDeal } from "./orchestrator/index.js";
import type { DealContext } from "./schemas/deal-context.js";

import { intake } from "./capabilities/intake.js";
import { guardian } from "./capabilities/guardian.js";
import { estimate } from "./capabilities/quote.js";
import { quote } from "./capabilities/quote.js";
import { sourcing } from "./capabilities/sourcing.js";
import { scenario } from "./capabilities/scenario.js";

import { DealStore } from "./memory/deal-store.js";
import { AviapagesClient } from "./integrations/aviapages.js";
import type { FetchFn } from "./integrations/aviapages.js";

import {
  AIRPORTS, OPERATORS, OPERATOR_CERTS, mockAviapagesEstimate,
} from "./fixtures/sim-data.js";

// ── Types ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

interface ScenarioFile {
  meta: { version: string; target_pass_rate: number; total_scenarios: number };
  scenarios: Scenario[];
}

interface Scenario {
  id: string;
  category: string;
  title: string;
  request: string;
  expected: {
    departure: string | null;
    arrival: string | null;
    pax: number | null;
    aircraftClass: string | null;
    shouldClose: boolean;
    failReason?: string;
  };
}

type StageResult = { stage: string; status: "pass" | "fail" | "skip"; detail: string; durationMs: number };

interface ScenarioResult {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  category: string;
  passed: boolean;
  stages: StageResult[];
  finalStage: string;
  osaPrice: number | null;
  failReason: string | null;
  wallClockMs: number;
}

// ── Mock Aviapages Client ────────────────────────────────────────────────

function createMockAviapages(): AviapagesClient {
  const mockFetch: FetchFn = async (_url, init) => {
    const body = JSON.parse(init.body as string);
    const dep = body.departure as string;
    const arr = body.arrival as string;
    const cls = body.aircraft_class as string | undefined;

    const result = mockAviapagesEstimate(dep, arr, cls ?? null);
    if (!result) {
      return { ok: false, status: 404, json: async () => ({}) };
    }
    return {
      ok: true, status: 200,
      json: async () => ({
        price_low: result.low, price_high: result.high,
        flight_time: result.flightTime, fuel_burn: result.fuelBurn,
      }),
    };
  };
  return new AviapagesClient({ apiKey: "sim-mock" }, mockFetch);
}

// ── Run Single Scenario ──────────────────────────────────────────────────

async function runScenario(
  sc: Scenario,
  dealStore: DealStore,
  aviapages: AviapagesClient,
): Promise<ScenarioResult> {
  const startTime = Date.now();
  const stages: StageResult[] = [];
  let failReason: string | null = null;

  const ctx = createDealContext("sim");

  function stageOk(stage: string, detail: string, start: number) {
    stages.push({ stage, status: "pass", detail, durationMs: Date.now() - start });
  }
  function stageFail(stage: string, detail: string, start: number) {
    stages.push({ stage, status: "fail", detail, durationMs: Date.now() - start });
    failReason = detail;
  }

  try {
    // ── 1. INTAKE ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
    let t = Date.now();
    // Use local-time March 31 (not UTC) to avoid timezone shift issues
    const intakeResult = await intake(sc.request, ctx, {
      today: new Date(2026, 2, 31), // month is 0-indexed: 2 = March
      dealStore,
    });
    updateDealContext(ctx, { client: intakeResult.client, trip: intakeResult.trip });

    if (!ctx.client.qualified) {
      stageFail("INTAKE", `Not qualified: ${ctx.client.qualificationReason}`, t);
      if (sc.expected.failReason === "qualification") {
        return buildResult(sc, true, stages, ctx, startTime, "Expected qualification failure");
      }
      return buildResult(sc, false, stages, ctx, startTime, failReason);
    }
    stageOk("INTAKE", `${ctx.trip.departure}→${ctx.trip.arrival} ${ctx.trip.pax}pax`, t);

    // ── 2. GUARDIAN: intake ────────────────────────────────────────
    t = Date.now();
    const gIntake = await guardian("intake", ctx, { airports: AIRPORTS, operatorCerts: OPERATOR_CERTS });
    ctx.guardian.passes.push(gIntake.pass);
    if (!gIntake.pass.passed) {
      stageFail("GUARDIAN:INT", gIntake.pass.checks.filter((c) => !c.passed).map((c) => c.message).join("; "), t);
      if (sc.expected.failReason === "pax") {
        return buildResult(sc, true, stages, ctx, startTime, "Expected pax/range failure");
      }
      return buildResult(sc, false, stages, ctx, startTime, failReason);
    }
    stageOk("GUARDIAN:INT", `${gIntake.pass.checks.length} checks clear`, t);

    advanceStage(ctx, "qualified");

    // Budget gate
    if (!ctx.client.budget) {
      stages.push({ stage: "BUDGET", status: "fail", detail: "No budget — pipeline paused", durationMs: 0 });
      if (sc.expected.failReason === "no_budget") {
        return buildResult(sc, true, stages, ctx, startTime, "Expected no-budget pause");
      }
      return buildResult(sc, false, stages, ctx, startTime, "No budget provided");
    }

    // ── 3. ESTIMATE ───────────────────────────────────────────────
    t = Date.now();
    advanceStage(ctx, "estimated");
    const estResult = await estimate(ctx, { aviapages, dealStore });
    updateDealContext(ctx, { estimate: estResult.estimate });

    if (!ctx.estimate.priceRange) {
      stageFail("ESTIMATE", "No price estimate returned", t);
      return buildResult(sc, false, stages, ctx, startTime, failReason);
    }
    stageOk("ESTIMATE", `$${ctx.estimate.priceRange.low.toLocaleString()}–$${ctx.estimate.priceRange.high.toLocaleString()}`, t);

    // ── 4. GUARDIAN: estimate ─────────────────────────────────────
    t = Date.now();
    const gEst = await guardian("estimated", ctx, { airports: AIRPORTS });
    ctx.guardian.passes.push(gEst.pass);
    if (!gEst.pass.passed) {
      stageFail("GUARDIAN:EST", gEst.pass.checks.filter((c) => !c.passed).map((c) => c.message).join("; "), t);
      if (sc.expected.failReason === "budget") {
        return buildResult(sc, true, stages, ctx, startTime, "Expected budget mismatch");
      }
      return buildResult(sc, false, stages, ctx, startTime, failReason);
    }
    stageOk("GUARDIAN:EST", "Budget overlaps estimate", t);

    // ── 5. SOURCING ───────────────────────────────────────────────
    t = Date.now();
    advanceStage(ctx, "sourced");
    const depAirport = AIRPORTS.get(ctx.trip.departure) ?? null;
    const srcResult = await sourcing(ctx, {
      operators: OPERATORS,
      departureAirport: depAirport,
      dealStore,
    });
    updateDealContext(ctx, { sourcing: srcResult.sourcing });

    if (!ctx.sourcing.selectedOperator) {
      stageFail("SOURCING", "No operator matched", t);
      if (sc.expected.failReason === "pax") {
        return buildResult(sc, true, stages, ctx, startTime, "Expected no-match (pax too high)");
      }
      return buildResult(sc, false, stages, ctx, startTime, failReason);
    }
    stageOk("SOURCING", `${ctx.sourcing.selectedOperator.operatorName} (${ctx.sourcing.selectedOperator.aircraftType})`, t);

    // ── 6. GUARDIAN: sourced ──────────────────────────────────────
    t = Date.now();
    const gSrc = await guardian("sourced", ctx, { airports: AIRPORTS, operatorCerts: OPERATOR_CERTS });
    ctx.guardian.passes.push(gSrc.pass);
    stageOk("GUARDIAN:SRC", `${gSrc.pass.passed ? "Clear" : "Warning"}`, t);

    // ── 7. QUOTE (sim: use estimate median as operator price) ────
    t = Date.now();
    advanceStage(ctx, "quoted");
    const estimateMedian = ctx.estimate.priceRange
      ? Math.round((ctx.estimate.priceRange.low + ctx.estimate.priceRange.high) / 2)
      : 30000;
    const quoteResult = await quote(ctx, {
      operatorPrice: estimateMedian,
      aircraft: ctx.quote.aircraft ?? (ctx.sourcing.selectedOperator ? {
        type: ctx.sourcing.selectedOperator.aircraftType,
        tail: ctx.sourcing.selectedOperator.aircraftTail ?? "N/A",
        year: 2022,
      } : undefined) ?? undefined,
    });
    updateDealContext(ctx, { quote: quoteResult.quote });
    stageOk("QUOTE", `Op: $${estimateMedian.toLocaleString()} → OSA: $${ctx.quote.osaPrice?.toLocaleString()}`, t);

    // ── 8. GUARDIAN: quoted ───────────────────────────────────────
    t = Date.now();
    const gQuote = await guardian("quoted", ctx);
    ctx.guardian.passes.push(gQuote.pass);
    stageOk("GUARDIAN:QOT", `${gQuote.pass.passed ? "Clear" : "Warning"}`, t);

    // ── 9. SCENARIO (presentation) ───────────────────────────────
    t = Date.now();
    advanceStage(ctx, "presented");
    try {
      await scenario(ctx);
      ctx.presentation.sentAt = new Date().toISOString();
      ctx.presentation.timerStarted = new Date().toISOString();
      ctx.presentation.clientDecision = "pending";
      stageOk("SCENARIO", "Presentation built", t);
    } catch (err) {
      stageFail("SCENARIO", err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err), t);
      return buildResult(sc, false, stages, ctx, startTime, failReason);
    }

    // ── 10. CLOSE DEAL ───────────────────────────────────────────
    t = Date.now();
    ctx.presentation.clientDecision = "confirmed";
    ctx.presentation.decisionAt = new Date().toISOString();
    closeDeal(ctx, {
      result: "closed",
      dealStore,
      airports: AIRPORTS,
      operatorResponseTime: 1800,
    });
    stageOk("CLOSE", `Revenue: $${ctx.outcome.revenue?.toLocaleString() ?? 0}`, t);

    return buildResult(sc, true, stages, ctx, startTime, null);

  } catch (err) {
    const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err);
    stages.push({ stage: "ERROR", status: "fail", detail: msg, durationMs: Date.now() - startTime });
    return buildResult(sc, false, stages, ctx, startTime, msg);
  }
}

function buildResult(
  sc: Scenario,
  passed: boolean,
  stages: StageResult[],
  ctx: DealContext,
  startTime: number,
  failReason: string | null,
): ScenarioResult {
  // Auto-score: check expected vs actual
  let autoPass = passed;
  const exp = sc.expected;

  if (exp.shouldClose && ctx.stage !== "closed") autoPass = false;
  if (!exp.shouldClose && ctx.stage === "closed") autoPass = false;

  // Check route parsing (only if expected non-null)
  if (exp.departure && ctx.trip.departure !== exp.departure) autoPass = false;
  if (exp.arrival && ctx.trip.arrival !== exp.arrival) autoPass = false;
  if (exp.pax !== null && ctx.trip.pax !== exp.pax) autoPass = false;

  return {
    id: sc.id,
    title: sc.title,
    category: sc.category,
    passed: autoPass,
    stages,
    finalStage: ctx.stage,
    osaPrice: ctx.quote.osaPrice,
    failReason: autoPass ? null : failReason,
    wallClockMs: Date.now() - startTime,
  };
}

// ── Main ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

async function main() {
  const __dirname = dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
  const scenariosPath = resolve(__dirname, "..", "scenarios", "scenarios.json");
  const file: ScenarioFile = JSON.parse(readFileSync(scenariosPath, "utf-8"));

  // Parse --scenario flag
  const args = process.argv.slice(2);
  const singleId = args.indexOf("--scenario") >= 0 ? args[args.indexOf("--scenario") + 1] : null;

  const scenarios = singleId
    ? file.scenarios.filter((s) => s.id === singleId)
    : file.scenarios;

  if (scenarios.length === 0) {
    console.error(`No scenarios found${singleId ? ` matching ${singleId}` : ""}`);
    process.exit(1);
  }

  const dealStore = new DealStore(resolve(__dirname, "..", "data", "sim-deals.db"));
  const aviapages = createMockAviapages();

  const hr = "=".repeat(72);
  console.log(`\n  ${hr}`);
  console.log(`  DUMONT AGENT — Scenario Runner`);
  console.log(`  ${scenarios.length} scenarios · target ${file.meta.target_pass_rate}% pass rate`);
  console.log(`  ${hr}\n`);

  const results: ScenarioResult[] = [];

  for (const sc of scenarios) {
    const result = await runScenario(sc, dealStore, aviapages);
    results.push(result);

    const icon = result.passed ? "\x1b[32mPASS\x1b[0m" : "\x1b[31mFAIL\x1b[0m";
    const price = result.osaPrice ? `$${result.osaPrice.toLocaleString()}` : "—";
    const stageStr = result.stages
      .map((s) => s.status === "pass" ? `\x1b[32m${s.stage}\x1b[0m` : s.status === "fail" ? `\x1b[31m${s.stage}\x1b[0m` : `\x1b[90m${s.stage}\x1b[0m`)
      .join(" → ");

    console.log(`  [${result.id}] ${icon}  ${result.title}`);
    console.log(`         ${stageStr}`);
    if (result.osaPrice) {
      console.log(`         Price: ${price}  Time: ${result.wallClockMs}ms`);
    }
    if (result.failReason && !result.passed) {
      console.log(`         \x1b[31mReason: ${result.failReason}\x1b[0m`);
    }
    console.log();
  }

  // ── Summary ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  const passed = results.filter((r) => r.passed).length;
  const failed = results.filter((r) => !r.passed).length;
  const passRate = Math.round((passed / results.length) * 100);
  const target = file.meta.target_pass_rate;
  const beatTarget = passRate >= target;

  console.log(`  ${hr}`);
  console.log(`  RESULTS: ${passed} pass / ${failed} fail  (${passRate}% pass rate)`);
  console.log(`  TARGET:  ${target}%  ${beatTarget ? "\x1b[32m— TARGET MET\x1b[0m" : "\x1b[31m— BELOW TARGET\x1b[0m"}`);
  console.log();

  // Category breakdown
  const cats: Record<string, { pass: number; total: number }> = {};
  for (const r of results) {
    if (!cats[r.category]) cats[r.category] = { pass: 0, total: 0 };
    cats[r.category]!.total++;
    if (r.passed) cats[r.category]!.pass++;
  }
  console.log("  Category Breakdown:");
  console.log("  " + "-".repeat(50));
  for (const [cat, stats] of Object.entries(cats).sort((a, b) => a[0].localeCompare(b[0]))) {
    const rate = Math.round((stats.pass / stats.total) * 100);
    const bar = "\x1b[32m" + "█".repeat(Math.round(rate / 5)) + "\x1b[90m" + "░".repeat(20 - Math.round(rate / 5)) + "\x1b[0m";
    console.log(`  ${cat.padEnd(25)} ${bar} ${rate}% (${stats.pass}/${stats.total})`);
  }

  // Deal store stats
  console.log(`\n  Learning Loop: ${dealStore.count} deals recorded`);
  console.log(`  ${hr}\n`);

  process.exit(beatTarget ? 0 : 1);
}

main().catch((err) => {
  console.error("Scenario runner failed:", err);
  process.exit(1);
});
// === src/orchestrator/context-factory.ts ===
/**
 * ContextFactory — builds stage-specific context slices from DealContext.
 *
 * Replaces the pattern of passing structuredClone(entireDealContext) to every
 * capability. Each stage gets ONLY what it needs — typed, strict, no optional
 * catchall fields.
 *
 * Rules:
 * - Pure TypeScript. No LLM calls.
 * - Guardian passes older than the last 3 are trimmed.
 * - laneHistory does not leak past sourcing.
 * - RouteIntel and OperatorProfile are injected from pre-aggregated stores.
 */

import type { DealContext, GuardianPass } from "../schemas/deal-context.js";
import type { RouteIntel, OperatorProfile, ClientProfile } from "../schemas/composite-objects.js";
import type { RouteIntelStore } from "../memory/composite-stores.js";
import type { OperatorProfileStore } from "../memory/composite-stores.js";
import type { ClientProfileStore } from "../memory/composite-stores.js";

// ── Stage Context Types ─────────────────────────────────────────────────

export interface IntakeContext {
  rawMessage: string;
  clientHistory: ClientProfile | null;
}

export interface GuardianContext {
  stage: string;
  trip: {
    departure: string;
    arrival: string;
    date: string;
    pax: number;
    aircraftClass: string | null;
    aircraftType: string | null;
    isRoundTrip: boolean;
    specialRequirements: string[];
  };
  recentGuardianPasses: GuardianPass[];
  currentStatus: string;
  blockReason: string | null;
  clientBudget: { min: number; max: number } | null;
  estimate: {
    priceRange: { low: number; high: number } | null;
    flightTime: number | null;
  } | null;
  selectedOperator: {
    operatorName: string;
    baseAirport: string;
    aircraftType: string;
  } | null;
  quote: {
    operatorPrice: number | null;
    osaPrice: number | null;
    margin: number | null;
    validUntil: string | null;
  } | null;
}

export interface EstimateContext {
  trip: {
    departure: string;
    arrival: string;
    date: string;
    pax: number;
    aircraftClass: string | null;
    aircraftType: string | null;
  };
  routeIntel: RouteIntel | null;
}

export interface PriceWallContext {
  trip: {
    departure: string;
    arrival: string;
    date: string;
    pax: number;
  };
  physicsEstimate: { low: number; high: number } | null;
  clientBudget: { min: number; max: number } | null;
}

export interface SourcingContext {
  trip: {
    departure: string;
    arrival: string;
    date: string;
    pax: number;
    aircraftClass: string | null;
  };
  routeIntel: RouteIntel | null;
  operatorProfiles: OperatorProfile[];
  budgetCeiling: number | null;
}

export interface QuoteContext {
  trip: {
    departure: string;
    arrival: string;
    date: string;
    pax: number;
    aircraftClass: string | null;
  };
  routeIntel: RouteIntel | null;
  selectedOperator: {
    operatorName: string;
    baseAirport: string;
    aircraftType: string;
    score: number;
  } | null;
  clientBudget: { min: number; max: number } | null;
  ghostPositioningValue: number | null;
}

export interface PresentContext {
  route: string;
  clientName: string;
  osaPrice: number | null;
  trip: {
    departure: string;
    arrival: string;
    date: string;
    pax: number;
    aircraftClass: string | null;
  };
  aircraft: {
    type: string;
    tail: string;
    year: number;
  } | null;
}

export interface RespondContext {
  route: string;
  clientName: string;
  osaPrice: number | null;
  intent: string;
  dealStage: string;
}

// ── Store Dependencies ──────────────────────────────────────────────────

export interface ContextFactoryStores {
  routeIntel?: RouteIntelStore;
  operatorProfiles?: OperatorProfileStore;
  clientProfiles?: ClientProfileStore;
}

// ── Trim Helpers ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

const MAX_RECENT_GUARDIAN_PASSES = 3;

function trimGuardianPasses(passes: GuardianPass[]): GuardianPass[] {
  if (passes.length <= MAX_RECENT_GUARDIAN_PASSES) return passes;
  return passes.slice(-MAX_RECENT_GUARDIAN_PASSES);
}

function routeKey(departure: string, arrival: string): string {
  return `${departure}-${arrival}`;
}

// ── Factory Functions ───────────────────────────────────────────────────

export function buildIntakeContext(
  rawMessage: string,
  stores?: ContextFactoryStores,
  clientNameOrEmail?: string,
): IntakeContext {
  const clientHistory = clientNameOrEmail && stores?.clientProfiles
    ? stores.clientProfiles.get(clientNameOrEmail)
    : null;
  return { rawMessage, clientHistory };
}

export function buildGuardianContext(
  stage: string,
  ctx: DealContext,
): GuardianContext {
  return {
    stage,
    trip: { ...ctx.trip },
    recentGuardianPasses: trimGuardianPasses(ctx.guardian.passes),
    currentStatus: ctx.guardian.currentStatus,
    blockReason: ctx.guardian.blockReason,
    clientBudget: ctx.client.budget ? { ...ctx.client.budget } : null,
    estimate: ctx.estimate.priceRange
      ? { priceRange: { ...ctx.estimate.priceRange }, flightTime: ctx.estimate.flightTime }
      : null,
    selectedOperator: ctx.sourcing.selectedOperator
      ? {
          operatorName: ctx.sourcing.selectedOperator.operatorName,
          baseAirport: ctx.sourcing.selectedOperator.baseAirport,
          aircraftType: ctx.sourcing.selectedOperator.aircraftType,
        }
      : null,
    quote: ctx.quote.operatorPrice !== null
      ? {
          operatorPrice: ctx.quote.operatorPrice,
          osaPrice: ctx.quote.osaPrice,
          margin: ctx.quote.margin,
          validUntil: ctx.quote.validUntil,
        }
      : null,
  };
}

export function buildEstimateContext(
  ctx: DealContext,
  stores?: ContextFactoryStores,
): EstimateContext {
  const route = routeKey(ctx.trip.departure, ctx.trip.arrival);
  const routeIntel = ctx.trip.aircraftClass && stores?.routeIntel
    ? stores.routeIntel.get(route, ctx.trip.aircraftClass)
    : null;
  return {
    trip: {
      departure: ctx.trip.departure,
      arrival: ctx.trip.arrival,
      date: ctx.trip.date,
      pax: ctx.trip.pax,
      aircraftClass: ctx.trip.aircraftClass,
      aircraftType: ctx.trip.aircraftType,
    },
    routeIntel,
  };
}

export function buildPriceWallContext(ctx: DealContext): PriceWallContext {
  return {
    trip: {
      departure: ctx.trip.departure,
      arrival: ctx.trip.arrival,
      date: ctx.trip.date,
      pax: ctx.trip.pax,
    },
    physicsEstimate: ctx.estimate.priceRange ? { ...ctx.estimate.priceRange } : null,
    clientBudget: ctx.client.budget ? { ...ctx.client.budget } : null,
  };
}

export function buildSourcingContext(
  ctx: DealContext,
  stores?: ContextFactoryStores,
): SourcingContext {
  const route = routeKey(ctx.trip.departure, ctx.trip.arrival);
  const routeIntel = ctx.trip.aircraftClass && stores?.routeIntel
    ? stores.routeIntel.get(route, ctx.trip.aircraftClass)
    : null;

  const operatorProfiles = routeIntel?.topOperators && stores?.operatorProfiles
    ? stores.operatorProfiles.getMany(routeIntel.topOperators)
    : [];

  return {
    trip: {
      departure: ctx.trip.departure,
      arrival: ctx.trip.arrival,
      date: ctx.trip.date,
      pax: ctx.trip.pax,
      aircraftClass: ctx.trip.aircraftClass,
    },
    routeIntel,
    operatorProfiles,
    budgetCeiling: ctx.client.budget?.max ?? null,
  };
}

export function buildQuoteContext(
  ctx: DealContext,
  stores?: ContextFactoryStores,
): QuoteContext {
  const route = routeKey(ctx.trip.departure, ctx.trip.arrival);
  const routeIntel = ctx.trip.aircraftClass && stores?.routeIntel
    ? stores.routeIntel.get(route, ctx.trip.aircraftClass)
    : null;

  const ghostPositioningValue = ctx.sourcing.selectedOperator
    ? ctx.sourcing.selectedOperator.distanceToDeparture
    : null;

  return {
    trip: {
      departure: ctx.trip.departure,
      arrival: ctx.trip.arrival,
      date: ctx.trip.date,
      pax: ctx.trip.pax,
      aircraftClass: ctx.trip.aircraftClass,
    },
    routeIntel,
    selectedOperator: ctx.sourcing.selectedOperator
      ? {
          operatorName: ctx.sourcing.selectedOperator.operatorName,
          baseAirport: ctx.sourcing.selectedOperator.baseAirport,
          aircraftType: ctx.sourcing.selectedOperator.aircraftType,
          score: ctx.sourcing.selectedOperator.score,
        }
      : null,
    clientBudget: ctx.client.budget ? { ...ctx.client.budget } : null,
    ghostPositioningValue,
  };
}

export function buildPresentContext(ctx: DealContext): PresentContext {
  return {
    route: routeKey(ctx.trip.departure, ctx.trip.arrival),
    clientName: ctx.client.name,
    osaPrice: ctx.quote.osaPrice,
    trip: {
      departure: ctx.trip.departure,
      arrival: ctx.trip.arrival,
      date: ctx.trip.date,
      pax: ctx.trip.pax,
      aircraftClass: ctx.trip.aircraftClass,
    },
    aircraft: ctx.quote.aircraft ? { ...ctx.quote.aircraft } : null,
  };
}

export function buildRespondContext(
  ctx: DealContext,
  intent: string,
): RespondContext {
  return {
    route: routeKey(ctx.trip.departure, ctx.trip.arrival),
    clientName: ctx.client.name,
    osaPrice: ctx.quote.osaPrice,
    intent,
    dealStage: ctx.stage,
  };
}
// === src/orchestrator/deal-context-manager.ts ===
/**
 * Deal Context Manager — creates and mutates deal contexts.
 *
 * Capabilities never mutate the context directly. They return data;
 * the orchestrator uses this manager to merge it in.
 *
 * Evolved from ~/osa/simulator/src/orchestrator/deal-context-manager.ts
 * Key change: DealContext is a flat object matching the spec, not agent-sectioned.
 */

import type { DealContext, DealMode } from "../schemas/deal-context.js";
import { ulid } from "ulid";

function generateDealId(mode: DealMode): string {
  const prefix = mode === "sim" ? "SIM" : "LIVE";
  return `${prefix}_${ulid()}`;
}

/**
 * Create a fresh DealContext with all sections at default/empty state.
 */
export function createDealContext(mode: DealMode): DealContext {
  const now = new Date().toISOString();

  return {
    id: generateDealId(mode),
    createdAt: now,
    updatedAt: now,
    mode,

    stage: "intake",
    stageHistory: [{ stage: "intake", timestamp: now, duration: null }],

    client: {
      name: "",
      contact: "",
      type: "new",
      budget: null,
      substitutionPref: null,
      qualified: false,
      qualificationReason: null,
    },

    trip: {
      departure: "",
      arrival: "",
      date: "",
      pax: 0,
      aircraftClass: null,
      aircraftType: null,
      isRoundTrip: false,
      specialRequirements: [],
    },

    guardian: {
      passes: [],
      currentStatus: "clear",
      blockReason: null,
    },

    estimate: {
      source: "aviapages",
      flightTime: null,
      priceRange: null,
      fuelBurn: null,
      positioning: null,
      calculatedAt: null,
    },

    sourcing: {
      selectedOperator: null,
      backupOperator: null,
      rankingReason: "",
      laneHistory: [],
    },

    quote: {
      operatorPrice: null,
      osaPrice: null,
      margin: null,
      validUntil: null,
      aircraft: null,
      quotedAt: null,
    },

    presentation: {
      sentAt: null,
      viewedAt: null,
      clientDecision: null,
      decisionAt: null,
      timerStarted: null,
    },

    outcome: {
      result: null,
      estimateVsActual: null,
      operatorResponseTime: null,
      totalDealTime: null,
      revenue: null,
      notes: null,
    },
  };
}

/**
 * Update specific fields of the deal context via shallow merge per section.
 * Only the orchestrator should call this — capabilities return data, they don't mutate.
 * @param ctx - The deal context to update (mutated in place).
 * @param updates - Partial section updates to merge.
 */
export function updateDealContext(
  ctx: DealContext,
  updates: Partial<Pick<DealContext, "client" | "trip" | "guardian" | "estimate" | "sourcing" | "quote" | "presentation" | "outcome">>,
): void {
  for (const [section, data] of Object.entries(updates)) {
    const key = section as keyof typeof updates;
    if (data && typeof data === "object" && ctx[key] && typeof ctx[key] === "object") {
      Object.assign(ctx[key], data);
    }
  }
  ctx.updatedAt = new Date().toISOString();
}

/**
 * Returns a deep-cloned snapshot. Capabilities receive this — mutations
 * to the snapshot don't affect the original.
 */
export function snapshotContext(ctx: DealContext): DealContext {
  return structuredClone(ctx);
}

/** No-op — kept for backwards compatibility with tests. ULIDs are globally unique. */
export function _resetDealCounter(): void {
  // ULIDs don't need resetting — they're globally unique by design.
}
// === src/orchestrator/stage-transitions.ts ===
/**
 * Stage Transitions — enforces valid pipeline stage progression.
 *
 * Forward transitions: intake → qualified → estimated → sourced → quoted → presented → confirmed → closed
 * Any stage can transition to "failed".
 *
 * Regressions (v2.1): backward transitions when real-world deal flow changes.
 * Per spec: communication-layer.md section 2.7 (Dynamic Planning).
 * Pattern: Anthropic "Building Effective Agents" — dynamic planning.
 */

import type { DealContext, DealStage } from "../schemas/deal-context.js";
import { PipelineError } from "../utils/errors.js";

const VALID_TRANSITIONS: Record<string, Set<string>> = {
  intake:           new Set(["qualified", "failed"]),
  qualified:        new Set(["estimated", "failed"]),
  estimated:        new Set(["sourced", "failed"]),
  sourced:          new Set(["quoted", "failed"]),
  quoted:           new Set(["presented", "awaiting_review", "failed"]),
  awaiting_review:  new Set(["presented", "failed"]),
  presented:        new Set(["confirmed", "failed"]),
  confirmed:        new Set(["closed", "failed"]),
};

/**
 * Check if a stage transition is valid without throwing.
 * @param from - Current stage.
 * @param to - Target stage.
 * @returns True if the transition is allowed.
 */
export function isValidTransition(from: DealStage, to: DealStage): boolean {
  if (to === "failed") return true;
  const allowed = VALID_TRANSITIONS[from];
  return allowed !== undefined && allowed.has(to);
}

/**
 * Advance the deal context to a new pipeline stage.
 * Closes the current stage history entry with duration, opens a new one.
 * @param ctx - The deal context to advance (mutated in place).
 * @param to - The target stage.
 * @throws {InvalidTransitionError} If the transition is not allowed.
 */
export function advanceStage(ctx: DealContext, to: DealStage): void {
  const from = ctx.stage;
  if (!isValidTransition(from, to)) {
    throw new InvalidTransitionError(from, to);
  }

  const now = new Date().toISOString();

  // Close current stage entry with duration
  const current = ctx.stageHistory[ctx.stageHistory.length - 1];
  if (current && current.duration === null) {
    current.duration = Date.now() - new Date(current.timestamp).getTime();
  }

  ctx.stage = to;
  ctx.stageHistory.push({ stage: to, timestamp: now, duration: null });
  ctx.updatedAt = now;
}

/**
 * Get all valid target stages from a given stage.
 * @param from - The current stage.
 * @returns Array of valid target stages.
 */
export function getValidTargets(from: DealStage): DealStage[] {
  const specific = VALID_TRANSITIONS[from];
  if (!specific) return ["failed"];
  return [...specific] as DealStage[];
}

export class InvalidTransitionError extends Error {
  public readonly from: DealStage;
  public readonly to: DealStage;

  constructor(from: DealStage, to: DealStage) {
    const targets = getValidTargets(from).join(", ");
    super(`Invalid stage transition: "${from}" → "${to}". Valid targets: ${targets}`);
    this.name = "InvalidTransitionError";
    this.from = from;
    this.to = to;
  }
}

// ── Stage Regression (Dynamic Planning) ──────────────────────────────────
// Per spec: communication-layer.md section 2.7
// Pipeline goes backwards when deal conditions change.

/**
 * Allowed backward transitions.
 * Key: "from:to", value: description of when this regression applies.
 * NOT allowed: any regression past intake, from closed, or from failed.
 */
const ALLOWED_REGRESSIONS: Record<string, string> = {
  "presented:estimated": "Client wants different aircraft class or cheaper option",
  "presented:sourced": "Client rejects specific operator",
  "quoted:sourced": "Quote expired, need fresh operator",
  "sourced:estimated": "Operator not available, changes aircraft scope",
};

/** Terminal stages that cannot regress. */
const TERMINAL_STAGES = new Set<string>(["closed", "failed"]);

/** Stages that cannot be regression targets (too far back — start a new deal). */
const NO_REGRESS_TARGETS = new Set<string>(["intake", "qualified"]);

/**
 * Check if a backward stage transition (regression) is allowed.
 * @param from - Current stage.
 * @param to - Target stage to regress to.
 * @returns True if the regression is in the allowed set.
 */
export function isAllowedRegression(from: DealStage, to: DealStage): boolean {
  if (TERMINAL_STAGES.has(from)) return false;
  if (NO_REGRESS_TARGETS.has(to)) return false;
  return `${from}:${to}` in ALLOWED_REGRESSIONS;
}

/** Empty shapes for clearing pipeline sections on regression. */
const EMPTY_SOURCING: DealContext["sourcing"] = {
  selectedOperator: null,
  backupOperator: null,
  rankingReason: "",
  laneHistory: [],
};

const EMPTY_QUOTE: DealContext["quote"] = {
  operatorPrice: null,
  osaPrice: null,
  margin: null,
  validUntil: null,
  aircraft: null,
  quotedAt: null,
};

const EMPTY_PRESENTATION: DealContext["presentation"] = {
  sentAt: null,
  viewedAt: null,
  clientDecision: null,
  decisionAt: null,
  timerStarted: null,
};

/**
 * Regress the deal to an earlier pipeline stage.
 * Clears invalidated downstream data so the pipeline can re-run from the target stage.
 *
 * Allowed regressions:
 * - presented → estimated (client wants different aircraft)
 * - presented → sourced (client rejects operator)
 * - quoted → sourced (quote expired)
 * - sourced → estimated (operator unavailable, scope change)
 *
 * @param ctx - The deal context to regress (mutated in place).
 * @param to - The target stage to regress to.
 * @param reason - Human-readable reason for the regression (logged to history).
 * @throws {PipelineError} If the regression is not allowed.
 */
export function regressStage(ctx: DealContext, to: DealStage, reason: string): void {
  const from = ctx.stage;

  if (!isAllowedRegression(from, to)) {
    throw new PipelineError(
      `Cannot regress from "${from}" to "${to}". Allowed regressions: ${Object.keys(ALLOWED_REGRESSIONS).join(", ")}`,
      { dealId: ctx.id, stage: from, outcome: "regression_blocked" },
    );
  }

  const now = new Date().toISOString();

  // Close current stage entry
  const current = ctx.stageHistory[ctx.stageHistory.length - 1];
  if (current && current.duration === null) {
    current.duration = Date.now() - new Date(current.timestamp).getTime();
  }

  // Log regression as a typed history entry
  ctx.stageHistory.push({
    stage: to,
    timestamp: now,
    duration: 0,
    regression: { from, to, reason },
  });

  // Store reason in outcome notes (append, don't overwrite)
  const regressionNote = `Regression ${from}→${to}: ${reason}`;
  ctx.outcome.notes = ctx.outcome.notes
    ? `${ctx.outcome.notes}; ${regressionNote}`
    : regressionNote;

  // Clear invalidated data based on target stage
  switch (to) {
    case "estimated":
      // Re-estimating: clear sourcing + quote + presentation
      ctx.sourcing = { ...EMPTY_SOURCING };
      ctx.quote = { ...EMPTY_QUOTE };
      ctx.presentation = { ...EMPTY_PRESENTATION };
      break;

    case "sourced":
      // Re-sourcing: clear quote + presentation (estimate stays)
      ctx.quote = { ...EMPTY_QUOTE };
      ctx.presentation = { ...EMPTY_PRESENTATION };
      break;
  }

  // Set the new stage
  ctx.stage = to;
  ctx.stageHistory.push({ stage: to, timestamp: now, duration: null });
  ctx.updatedAt = now;
}
// === src/orchestrator/index.ts ===
/**
 * Orchestrator — the PIC of every deal.
 *
 * Single pipeline controller that invokes capabilities in sequence,
 * runs Guardian validation at every stage, and writes outcomes to memory.
 *
 * Per spec: orchestrator.md
 *
 * Pipeline:
 *   1. INTAKE    — parse request + qualify client
 *   2. ESTIMATE  — Aviapages price estimate + budget gate
 *   3. SOURCE    — select ONE operator + backup
 *   4. QUOTE     — human-in-the-loop real quote (day 1-50)
 *   5. PRESENT   — client-facing OSA quote
 *   6. CONFIRM   — client decision
 *   7. LEARN     — write to memory
 *
 * Guardian runs after every stage (1-5). If blocked, pipeline stops.
 */

import type { DealContext, DealMode, GuardianPass } from "../schemas/deal-context.js";
import {
  createDealContext,
  updateDealContext,
  snapshotContext,
} from "./deal-context-manager.js";
import { advanceStage } from "./stage-transitions.js";
import type { ContextFactoryStores } from "./context-factory.js";

// ── Capability Signatures ────────────────────────────────────────────────
//
// Pattern for adding a new capability:
// 1. Create src/capabilities/your-capability.ts with a function matching the shape below
// 2. Add it to the Capabilities interface
// 3. Export from src/capabilities/index.ts
// 4. Wire it into the pipeline in runPipeline() below
//
// Capabilities receive a DealContext snapshot (immutable) and return section data.
// The orchestrator merges results via updateDealContext(). Capabilities NEVER
// mutate the context directly.

export interface IntakeResult {
  client: DealContext["client"];
  trip: DealContext["trip"];
}

export interface EstimateResult {
  estimate: DealContext["estimate"];
}

export interface SourcingResult {
  sourcing: DealContext["sourcing"];
}

export interface QuoteResult {
  quote: DealContext["quote"];
}

export interface ScenarioResult {
  presentation: DealContext["presentation"];
}

export interface GuardianResult {
  pass: GuardianPass;
}

export interface Capabilities {
  intake: (rawRequest: string, ctx: DealContext) => Promise<IntakeResult>;
  estimate: (ctx: DealContext) => Promise<EstimateResult>;
  sourcing: (ctx: DealContext) => Promise<SourcingResult>;
  quote: (ctx: DealContext) => Promise<QuoteResult>;
  scenario: (ctx: DealContext) => Promise<ScenarioResult>;
  guardian: (stage: string, ctx: DealContext) => Promise<GuardianResult>;
}

// ── Pipeline Result ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export type PipelineOutcome =
  | "presented"           // happy path — quote sent to client
  | "awaiting_quote"      // paused at step 4 — waiting for human
  | "awaiting_review"     // paused — Guardian warnings need human approval before presenting
  | "failed_intake"
  | "failed_qualification"
  | "failed_budget"
  | "failed_guardian"
  | "failed_sourcing"
  | "failed_error";

export interface PipelineResult {
  outcome: PipelineOutcome;
  dealContext: DealContext;
  wallClockMs: number;
  failReason?: string;
}

export interface PipelineOptions {
  mode: DealMode;
  capabilities: Capabilities;
  /** Composite object stores for context-aware pipeline stages. */
  stores?: ContextFactoryStores;
}

// ── Pre/Post Hooks (ruvnet pattern) ──────────────────────────────────────

export type HookFn = (ctx: DealContext, stage: string) => void | Promise<void>;

export interface PipelineHooks {
  onStageEnter?: HookFn;
  onStageExit?: HookFn;
  onGuardianPass?: (ctx: DealContext, pass: GuardianPass) => void | Promise<void>;
  onPipelineComplete?: (result: PipelineResult) => void | Promise<void>;
}

// ── Main Pipeline ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export async function runPipeline(
  rawRequest: string,
  options: PipelineOptions,
  hooks?: PipelineHooks,
): Promise<PipelineResult> {
  const startTime = Date.now();
  const { mode, capabilities } = options;
  const ctx = createDealContext(mode);

  const fail = (outcome: PipelineOutcome, reason: string): PipelineResult => {
    ctx.stage = "failed";
    ctx.outcome.notes = reason;
    const result: PipelineResult = {
      outcome,
      dealContext: ctx,
      wallClockMs: Date.now() - startTime,
      failReason: reason,
    };
    hooks?.onPipelineComplete?.(result);
    return result;
  };

  // Helper: run Guardian and check if pipeline should stop
  async function runGuardian(stage: string): Promise<boolean> {
    await hooks?.onStageEnter?.(ctx, `guardian:${stage}`);

    const guardianResult = await capabilities.guardian(stage, snapshotContext(ctx));
    const pass = guardianResult.pass;

    ctx.guardian.passes.push(pass);
    if (!pass.passed) {
      ctx.guardian.currentStatus = "blocked";
      ctx.guardian.blockReason = pass.checks
        .filter((c) => !c.passed && c.severity === "critical")
        .map((c) => c.message)
        .join("; ");
    } else {
      const hasWarnings = pass.checks.some((c) => !c.passed && c.severity === "warning");
      ctx.guardian.currentStatus = hasWarnings ? "warning" : "clear";
    }
    ctx.updatedAt = new Date().toISOString();

    await hooks?.onGuardianPass?.(ctx, pass);
    await hooks?.onStageExit?.(ctx, `guardian:${stage}`);

    return ctx.guardian.currentStatus === "blocked";
  }

  try {
    // ── 1. INTAKE ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    await hooks?.onStageEnter?.(ctx, "intake");

    const intakeResult = await capabilities.intake(rawRequest, snapshotContext(ctx));
    updateDealContext(ctx, { client: intakeResult.client, trip: intakeResult.trip });

    await hooks?.onStageExit?.(ctx, "intake");

    // Guardian: is trip physically possible?
    const blockedAfterIntake = await runGuardian("intake");
    if (blockedAfterIntake) {
      return fail("failed_guardian", `Guardian blocked at intake: ${ctx.guardian.blockReason}`);
    }

    // Qualification gate
    if (!ctx.client.qualified) {
      return fail("failed_qualification", ctx.client.qualificationReason ?? "Client not qualified");
    }

    advanceStage(ctx, "qualified");

    // Budget gate — pipeline pauses if no budget (spec says ask client, not fail)
    if (!ctx.client.budget) {
      return fail("failed_budget", "No budget provided — pipeline paused for client input");
    }

    // ── 2. ESTIMATE ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    advanceStage(ctx, "estimated");
    await hooks?.onStageEnter?.(ctx, "estimated");

    const estimateResult = await capabilities.estimate(snapshotContext(ctx));
    updateDealContext(ctx, { estimate: estimateResult.estimate });

    await hooks?.onStageExit?.(ctx, "estimated");

    // Guardian: does budget overlap estimate?
    const blockedAfterEstimate = await runGuardian("estimated");
    if (blockedAfterEstimate) {
      return fail("failed_budget", `Budget mismatch: ${ctx.guardian.blockReason}`);
    }

    // ── 3. SOURCE ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    advanceStage(ctx, "sourced");
    await hooks?.onStageEnter?.(ctx, "sourced");

    const sourcingResult = await capabilities.sourcing(snapshotContext(ctx));
    updateDealContext(ctx, { sourcing: sourcingResult.sourcing });

    await hooks?.onStageExit?.(ctx, "sourced");

    // Guardian: is operator certified?
    const blockedAfterSourcing = await runGuardian("sourced");
    if (blockedAfterSourcing) {
      return fail("failed_guardian", `Guardian blocked at sourcing: ${ctx.guardian.blockReason}`);
    }

    if (!ctx.sourcing.selectedOperator) {
      return fail("failed_sourcing", "No operator matched for this route");
    }

    // ── 4. QUOTE (human step, day 1-50) ────────────────────────────────
    // In SIM mode, the quote capability fills this from fixture data.
    // In LIVE mode, this pauses for human input.
    advanceStage(ctx, "quoted");
    await hooks?.onStageEnter?.(ctx, "quoted");

    if (mode === "live") {
      // Pipeline pauses here. Phelipe calls the operator, enters the quote.
      // The quote capability in live mode signals what to do.
      const quoteResult = await capabilities.quote(snapshotContext(ctx));
      updateDealContext(ctx, { quote: quoteResult.quote });

      if (!ctx.quote.operatorPrice) {
        return {
          outcome: "awaiting_quote",
          dealContext: ctx,
          wallClockMs: Date.now() - startTime,
        };
      }
    } else {
      const quoteResult = await capabilities.quote(snapshotContext(ctx));
      updateDealContext(ctx, { quote: quoteResult.quote });
    }

    await hooks?.onStageExit?.(ctx, "quoted");

    // Guardian: is quote within range?
    const blockedAfterQuote = await runGuardian("quoted");
    if (blockedAfterQuote) {
      return fail("failed_guardian", `Guardian blocked at quote: ${ctx.guardian.blockReason}`);
    }

    // If Guardian has warnings (not blocked, but flagged), pause for human review.
    // No deal with warnings reaches a client without human eyes.
    if (ctx.guardian.currentStatus === "warning") {
      advanceStage(ctx, "awaiting_review");
      const warningReasons = ctx.guardian.passes
        .flatMap((p) => p.checks.filter((c) => !c.passed && c.severity === "warning").map((c) => c.message));
      ctx.outcome.notes = ctx.outcome.notes
        ? `${ctx.outcome.notes}; Guardian warnings: ${warningReasons.join("; ")}`
        : `Guardian warnings: ${warningReasons.join("; ")}`;

      return {
        outcome: "awaiting_review",
        dealContext: ctx,
        wallClockMs: Date.now() - startTime,
      };
    }

    // ── 5. PRESENT ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    advanceStage(ctx, "presented");
    await hooks?.onStageEnter?.(ctx, "presented");

    const scenarioResult = await capabilities.scenario(snapshotContext(ctx));
    updateDealContext(ctx, { presentation: scenarioResult.presentation });
    ctx.presentation.sentAt = new Date().toISOString();
    ctx.presentation.timerStarted = new Date().toISOString();
    ctx.presentation.clientDecision = "pending";

    await hooks?.onStageExit?.(ctx, "presented");

    // Guardian: final sanity before presentation
    await runGuardian("presented");

    // ── Pipeline reaches presentation — remaining stages are async ────
    ctx.outcome.totalDealTime = (Date.now() - startTime) / 1000;

    const result: PipelineResult = {
      outcome: "presented",
      dealContext: ctx,
      wallClockMs: Date.now() - startTime,
    };

    await hooks?.onPipelineComplete?.(result);
    return result;

  } catch (err) {
    console.error(`[pipeline] Unhandled error in deal ${ctx.id}: ${err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err)}`);
    return fail("failed_error", err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err));
  }
}

// ── Close Deal (Learning Loop) ──────────────��────────────────────────────

import type { DealStore } from "../memory/deal-store.js";
import type { DealRecord } from "../schemas/deal-record.js";
import type { DealOutcomeResult, CompositeStores } from "../memory/learning-loop.js";
import type { AirportCoords } from "../memory/learning-loop.js";
import { recordDeal } from "../memory/learning-loop.js";
import { calcEstimateDelta } from "../capabilities/quote.js";
import { evictDealTokenBudget } from "../utils/safe-llm-call.js";

export interface CloseDealOptions {
  /** The deal outcome result. */
  result: DealOutcomeResult;
  /** DealStore to persist the record. */
  dealStore: DealStore;
  /** Operator response time in seconds. */
  operatorResponseTime?: number | null;
  /** Airport coordinates for distance calculation. */
  airports?: Map<string, AirportCoords>;
  /** Additional notes. */
  notes?: string;
  /** Pipeline hooks. */
  hooks?: PipelineHooks;
  /** Composite object stores — updated on deal close if provided. */
  compositeStores?: CompositeStores;
}

/**
 * Close a deal — transitions stage, writes to learning loop, fires hooks.
 *
 * Called when:
 * - Client confirms → result = "closed", stage = confirmed → closed
 * - Client declines → result = "client_declined", stage → failed
 * - Quote expires → result = "expired", stage → failed
 * - Any other terminal outcome
 *
 * Per spec: "ALWAYS log deal outcomes to memory (even failed/rejected deals)"
 */
export function closeDeal(
  ctx: DealContext,
  options: CloseDealOptions,
): DealRecord {
  const { result, dealStore, operatorResponseTime, airports, notes, hooks, compositeStores } = options;

  const isClosed = result === "closed";

  // Update outcome section
  ctx.outcome.result = result;
  if (operatorResponseTime != null) {
    ctx.outcome.operatorResponseTime = operatorResponseTime;
  }
  if (notes) {
    ctx.outcome.notes = notes;
  }

  // Calculate revenue for closed deals
  if (isClosed && ctx.quote.osaPrice && ctx.quote.operatorPrice) {
    ctx.outcome.revenue = ctx.quote.osaPrice - ctx.quote.operatorPrice;
  }

  // Compute total deal time
  if (!ctx.outcome.totalDealTime) {
    ctx.outcome.totalDealTime = Math.round(
      (Date.now() - new Date(ctx.createdAt).getTime()) / 1000,
    );
  }

  // Update presentation decision tracking
  if (ctx.presentation.sentAt && !ctx.presentation.decisionAt) {
    ctx.presentation.decisionAt = new Date().toISOString();
  }
  if (isClosed) {
    ctx.presentation.clientDecision = "confirmed";
  } else if (result === "client_declined" || result === "lost_to_competition") {
    ctx.presentation.clientDecision = "declined";
  } else if (result === "expired") {
    ctx.presentation.clientDecision = "expired";
  }

  // Transition stage
  if (isClosed && ctx.stage === "presented") {
    advanceStage(ctx, "confirmed");
    advanceStage(ctx, "closed");
  } else if (isClosed && ctx.stage === "confirmed") {
    advanceStage(ctx, "closed");
  } else if (!isClosed && ctx.stage !== "failed") {
    advanceStage(ctx, "failed");
  }

  ctx.updatedAt = new Date().toISOString();

  // Write to learning loop + update composite objects
  const record = recordDeal(ctx, dealStore, {
    result,
    operatorResponseTime,
    airports,
    notes,
    compositeStores,
  });

  // Evict deal from token budget tracker (prevents memory leak)
  evictDealTokenBudget(ctx.id);

  // Compute estimate delta and store in outcome
  if (ctx.estimate.priceRange && ctx.quote.operatorPrice) {
    ctx.outcome.estimateVsActual = calcEstimateDelta(ctx.estimate.priceRange, ctx.quote.operatorPrice);
  }

  // Fire completion hook
  if (hooks?.onPipelineComplete) {
    const pipelineResult: PipelineResult = {
      outcome: isClosed ? "presented" : "failed_error",
      dealContext: ctx,
      wallClockMs: (ctx.outcome.totalDealTime ?? 0) * 1000,
    };
    hooks.onPipelineComplete(pipelineResult);
  }

  return record;
}

// Re-exports

export { advanceStage, isValidTransition, InvalidTransitionError, regressStage, isAllowedRegression } from "./stage-transitions.js";
export {
  buildIntakeContext,
  buildGuardianContext,
  buildEstimateContext,
  buildPriceWallContext,
  buildSourcingContext,
  buildQuoteContext,
  buildPresentContext,
  buildRespondContext,
} from "./context-factory.js";

  IntakeContext,
  GuardianContext,
  EstimateContext,
  PriceWallContext,
  SourcingContext,
  QuoteContext,
  PresentContext,
  RespondContext,
  ContextFactoryStores,
} from "./context-factory.js";
// === src/channels/web-form.ts ===
/**
 * Web Form Channel Adapter — handles "Get a Quote" website submissions.
 * Inbound only. Always creates new deal. Responses go via email/SMS.
 * Per spec: communication-layer.md Sprint 3, FR-3.
 */

import type { Message, MessageChannel } from "../schemas/message.js";
import { generateMessageId } from "../schemas/message.js";
import type { ChannelAdapter, DeliveryResult } from "./adapter.js";

/** Expected form POST fields. */
export interface WebFormPayload {
  name: string;
  email: string;
  phone?: string;
  departure?: string;
  arrival?: string;
  date?: string;
  pax?: number | string;
  budget?: string;
  notes?: string;
}

export class WebFormAdapter implements ChannelAdapter {
  readonly channel: MessageChannel = "web_form";

  /**
   * Parse a website form POST body into a Message.
   * Maps structured form fields into both body (for intake parser) and parsedData.
   */
  receive(rawPayload: unknown): Message {
    const form = rawPayload as WebFormPayload;

    // Build body text from form fields for the intake parser
    const parts: string[] = [];
    if (form.departure && form.arrival) {
      parts.push(`Flight from ${form.departure} to ${form.arrival}`);
    }
    if (form.date) parts.push(`on ${form.date}`);
    if (form.pax) parts.push(`for ${form.pax} passengers`);
    if (form.budget) parts.push(`Budget: ${form.budget}`);
    if (form.notes) parts.push(form.notes);
    const body = parts.join(". ") || `Quote request from ${form.name}`;

    const parsedData: Record<string, unknown> = {};
    if (form.name) parsedData["clientName"] = form.name;
    if (form.email) parsedData["clientEmail"] = form.email;
    if (form.phone) parsedData["clientPhone"] = form.phone;
    if (form.departure) parsedData["departure"] = form.departure;
    if (form.arrival) parsedData["arrival"] = form.arrival;
    if (form.date) parsedData["date"] = form.date;
    if (form.pax) parsedData["pax"] = typeof form.pax === "string" ? parseInt(form.pax, 10) : form.pax;
    if (form.budget) parsedData["budget"] = form.budget;
    if (form.notes) parsedData["notes"] = form.notes;

    return {
      id: generateMessageId(),
      dealId: null,
      channel: "web_form",
      direction: "inbound",
      from: form.email,
      to: null,
      fromName: form.name,
      subject: null,
      body,
      bodyHtml: null,
      attachments: [],
      draftedBy: null,
      approvedBy: null,
      editDistance: null,
      originalDraft: null,
      receivedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      draftedAt: null,
      approvedAt: null,
      sentAt: null,
      responseToId: null,
      responseTimeMs: null,
      threadId: null,
      intent: "quote_request",
      parsedData: Object.keys(parsedData).length > 0 ? parsedData : null,
      confidence: null,
      confidenceLevel: null,
      platformMessageId: null,
      platformMetadata: null,
    };
  }

  /**
   * No-op. Web form responses go via email or Bland AI text.
   */
  async deliver(_message: Message): Promise<DeliveryResult> {
    return { success: true, platformMessageId: null, error: null };
  }

  supportsRealtime(): boolean {
    return false;
  }
}
// === src/channels/email.ts ===
/**
 * Email Channel Adapter — Resend-based email for operator RFQs and client quotes.
 * Per spec: communication-layer.md Sprint 3, FR-1, FR-3.
 */

import type { Message, MessageChannel } from "../schemas/message.js";
import { generateMessageId } from "../schemas/message.js";
import type { ChannelAdapter, DeliveryResult } from "./adapter.js";
import type { ResendClient, ResendInboundWebhook } from "../integrations/resend.js";
import { renderTemplate } from "../templates/render.js";

// ── Adapter ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export class EmailAdapter implements ChannelAdapter {
  readonly channel: MessageChannel = "email";
  private readonly client: ResendClient;

  constructor(client: ResendClient) {
    this.client = client;
  }

  /**
   * Parse a Resend inbound email webhook into a Message.
   * Extracts threading headers (In-Reply-To, References) for thread matching.
   */
  receive(rawPayload: unknown): Message {
    const payload = rawPayload as ResendInboundWebhook;
    const data = payload.data;
    const headers = data.headers ?? {};

    const inReplyTo = headers["In-Reply-To"] ?? headers["in-reply-to"] ?? null;
    const references = headers["References"] ?? headers["references"] ?? null;

    const attachments = (data.attachments ?? []).map((a) => ({
      url: a.url,
      mimeType: a.content_type,
      filename: a.filename,
      sizeBytes: null,
    }));

    return {
      id: generateMessageId(),
      dealId: null,
      channel: "email",
      direction: "inbound",
      from: data.from,
      to: data.to[0] ?? null,
      fromName: data.from_name ?? null,
      subject: data.subject,
      body: data.text ?? stripHtml(data.html ?? ""),
      bodyHtml: data.html ?? null,
      attachments,
      draftedBy: null,
      approvedBy: null,
      editDistance: null,
      originalDraft: null,
      receivedAt: data.created_at,
      draftedAt: null,
      approvedAt: null,
      sentAt: null,
      responseToId: inReplyTo,
      responseTimeMs: null,
      threadId: references ?? inReplyTo ?? null,
      intent: null,
      parsedData: null,
      confidence: null,
      confidenceLevel: null,
      platformMessageId: data.id,
      platformMetadata: null,
    };
  }

  /**
   * Send an outbound email via Resend with OSA-branded template.
   * Template is selected based on message intent.
   * Sets In-Reply-To header for threading if responseToId is present.
   */
  async deliver(message: Message): Promise<DeliveryResult> {
    if (!message.to) {
      return { success: false, platformMessageId: null, error: "No recipient email address." };
    }

    const html = this.renderForIntent(message);
    const headers: Record<string, string> = {};
    if (message.responseToId) {
      headers["In-Reply-To"] = message.responseToId;
      headers["References"] = message.responseToId;
    }

    const result = await this.client.sendEmail(
      message.to,
      message.subject ?? "OSA Connect",
      html,
      { headers: Object.keys(headers).length > 0 ? headers : undefined },
    );

    return {
      success: result.success,
      platformMessageId: result.messageId,
      error: result.error,
    };
  }

  supportsRealtime(): boolean {
    return false;
  }

  // ── Template selection ─────────────────────────────────────────────

  private renderForIntent(message: Message): string {
    const data = (message.parsedData ?? {}) as Record<string, string | number>;
    const templateData: Record<string, string | number> = {
      ...data,
      body: message.body,
      recipientName: String(data["recipientName"] ?? ""),
    };

    switch (message.intent) {
      case "quote_request":
      case "availability_check":
        return renderTemplate("operator-rfq", templateData);
      case "quote_response":
        return renderTemplate("client-quote", templateData);
      case "confirmation":
        return renderTemplate("confirmation", templateData);
      default:
        return renderTemplate("follow-up", templateData);
    }
  }
}

// ── Helpers ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

/** Strip HTML tags to get plain text. */
function stripHtml(html: string): string {
  return html.replace(/<[^>]+>/g, "").replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
}
// === src/channels/voice.ts ===
/**
 * Voice Copilot Channel Adapter — Twilio conference + Deepgram transcription.
 * Dumont listens while the broker negotiates. Extracts entities in real-time.
 * Per spec: communication-layer.md FR-5, section 2.3.
 */

import type { Message, MessageChannel } from "../schemas/message.js";
import { generateMessageId } from "../schemas/message.js";
import type { ChannelAdapter, DeliveryResult } from "./adapter.js";
import type { Call } from "../schemas/call.js";
import type { DealContext } from "../schemas/deal-context.js";

// ── Call Brief ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export interface CallBrief {
  dealId: string;
  route: string;
  date: string;
  pax: number;
  estimateRange: string;
  operatorName: string;
  talkingPoints: string[];
}

/**
 * Generate a call brief from DealContext.
 * Gives the broker context before dialing the operator.
 */
export function generateCallBrief(deal: DealContext): CallBrief {
  const points: string[] = [];
  if (deal.estimate.priceRange) {
    points.push(`Estimate: $${deal.estimate.priceRange.low.toLocaleString()}-$${deal.estimate.priceRange.high.toLocaleString()}`);
  }
  if (deal.client.budget) {
    points.push(`Client budget ceiling: $${deal.client.budget.max.toLocaleString()}`);
  }
  if (deal.trip.aircraftClass) {
    points.push(`Aircraft class: ${deal.trip.aircraftClass}`);
  }
  points.push(`Target margin: 8-10%`);

  return {
    dealId: deal.id,
    route: `${deal.trip.departure} → ${deal.trip.arrival}`,
    date: deal.trip.date,
    pax: deal.trip.pax,
    estimateRange: deal.estimate.priceRange
      ? `$${deal.estimate.priceRange.low.toLocaleString()}-$${deal.estimate.priceRange.high.toLocaleString()}`
      : "No estimate",
    operatorName: deal.sourcing.selectedOperator?.operatorName ?? "Unknown",
    talkingPoints: points,
  };
}

// ── Adapter ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export class VoiceCopilotAdapter implements ChannelAdapter {
  readonly channel: MessageChannel = "voice_copilot";

  /**
   * Convert post-call data into a Message.
   * Called after a call ends with the full transcript and summary.
   */
  receive(rawPayload: unknown): Message {
    const call = rawPayload as Call;
    const body = call.summary ?? call.transcript.map((c) => `[${c.speaker}] ${c.text}`).join("\n");

    return {
      id: generateMessageId(),
      dealId: call.dealId,
      channel: "voice_copilot",
      direction: "inbound",
      from: call.participants.find((p) => p.role === "operator")?.phone ?? "",
      to: call.participants.find((p) => p.role === "broker")?.phone ?? "",
      fromName: call.participants.find((p) => p.role === "operator")?.name ?? null,
      subject: null,
      body,
      bodyHtml: null,
      attachments: [],
      draftedBy: null,
      approvedBy: null,
      editDistance: null,
      originalDraft: null,
      receivedAt: call.endedAt ?? new Date().toISOString(),
      draftedAt: null,
      approvedAt: null,
      sentAt: null,
      responseToId: null,
      responseTimeMs: null,
      threadId: call.callId,
      intent: null,
      parsedData: {
        callDuration: call.durationSec,
        entityCount: call.detectedEntities.length,
        recordingUrl: call.recordingUrl,
      },
      confidence: null,
      confidenceLevel: null,
      platformMessageId: call.callId,
      platformMetadata: null,
    };
  }

  /** No-op. Voice copilot doesn't send messages — it listens. */
  async deliver(_message: Message): Promise<DeliveryResult> {
    return { success: true, platformMessageId: null, error: null };
  }

  /** Voice copilot supports real-time streaming (WebSocket transcription). */
  supportsRealtime(): boolean {
    return true;
  }
}
// === src/channels/adapter.ts ===
/**
 * ChannelAdapter interface — the contract every channel must implement.
 * Adapters are dumb pipes: normalize inbound, deliver outbound.
 * Per spec: communication-layer.md FR-3.
 */

import type { Message, MessageChannel } from "../schemas/message.js";

export interface DeliveryResult {
  success: boolean;
  platformMessageId: string | null;
  error: string | null;
}

export interface ChannelAdapter {
  /** Which channel this adapter handles. */
  readonly channel: MessageChannel;

  /**
   * Normalize a platform-specific inbound payload into a Message.
   * @param rawPayload - The raw webhook/event payload from the platform.
   * @returns A normalized Message object.
   */
  receive(rawPayload: unknown): Message;

  /**
   * Deliver an outbound Message via the platform's API.
   * @param message - The Message to send.
   * @returns Delivery result with platform message ID or error.
   */
  deliver(message: Message): Promise<DeliveryResult>;

  /** Whether this channel supports real-time streaming (voice, WebSocket). */
  supportsRealtime(): boolean;
}
// === src/channels/index.ts ===
export type { ChannelAdapter, DeliveryResult } from "./adapter.js";
export { EmailAdapter } from "./email.js";
export { WebFormAdapter } from "./web-form.js";
export { VoiceCopilotAdapter } from "./voice.js";
// === src/router/thread-matcher.ts ===
/**
 * Thread Matcher — matches inbound messages to existing deals.
 * Per spec: communication-layer.md FR-2, FR-7, section 2.1 step 1.
 *
 * Strategy (in priority order):
 * 1. responseToId chain (email In-Reply-To → find deal by message ID)
 * 2. Phone number match to active deal
 * 3. Email address match + recency (48 hours)
 * 4. threadId match (email thread, conversation SID)
 * Most recent deal wins ties.
 */

import type { Message } from "../schemas/message.js";
import type { Conversation } from "../schemas/conversation.js";
import type { DealContext } from "../schemas/deal-context.js";

/** Active deal with its conversation, managed by the router. */
export interface ActiveDeal {
  deal: DealContext;
  conversation: Conversation;
}

const TERMINAL_STAGES = new Set(["closed", "failed"]);
const RECENCY_WINDOW_MS = 48 * 60 * 60 * 1000; // 48 hours

/**
 * Match an inbound message to an existing active deal.
 * Returns the matched ActiveDeal or null if no match found.
 *
 * @param msg - The inbound message to match.
 * @param activeDeals - All currently tracked deals with conversations.
 * @returns The best-matching deal, or null for a new deal.
 */
export function matchThread(
  msg: Message,
  activeDeals: ActiveDeal[],
): ActiveDeal | null {
  // Filter to non-terminal deals for matching
  const live = activeDeals.filter((d) => !TERMINAL_STAGES.has(d.deal.stage));

  // Strategy 1: responseToId chain — most precise
  if (msg.responseToId) {
    const match = findByMessageId(msg.responseToId, activeDeals);
    if (match) return match;
  }

  // Strategy 2: Phone number match
  const byPhone = findByContact(msg.from, live);
  if (byPhone) return byPhone;

  // Strategy 3: Email match with recency window
  const byEmail = findByContactWithRecency(msg.from, live, RECENCY_WINDOW_MS);
  if (byEmail) return byEmail;

  // Strategy 4: threadId match
  if (msg.threadId) {
    const byThread = findByThreadId(msg.threadId, activeDeals);
    if (byThread) return byThread;
  }

  return null;
}

// ── Strategy implementations ─────────────────────────────────────────────

/**
 * Find a deal by searching all conversations for a message with the given ID.
 * Searches ALL deals (including closed) for reply chain integrity.
 */
function findByMessageId(messageId: string, deals: ActiveDeal[]): ActiveDeal | null {
  for (const ad of deals) {
    if (ad.conversation.messages.some((m) => m.id === messageId)) {
      // If the matched deal is closed, return null (router will create new deal)
      if (TERMINAL_STAGES.has(ad.deal.stage)) return null;
      return ad;
    }
  }
  return null;
}

/**
 * Find the most recently updated deal where the contact matches.
 * Contact can be a phone number or email.
 */
function findByContact(contact: string, deals: ActiveDeal[]): ActiveDeal | null {
  const needle = contact.toLowerCase();
  const matches = deals.filter((ad) => {
    const clientContact = ad.deal.client.contact?.toLowerCase() ?? "";
    const operatorPhone = ad.deal.sourcing.selectedOperator?.phone?.toLowerCase() ?? "";
    return (
      (clientContact && (clientContact === needle || clientContact.includes(needle) || needle.includes(clientContact))) ||
      (operatorPhone && (operatorPhone === needle || operatorPhone.includes(needle)))
    );
  });

  if (matches.length === 0) return null;
  // Most recent deal wins
  return mostRecent(matches);
}

/**
 * Find by contact with a recency window — only matches deals updated within N ms.
 */
function findByContactWithRecency(
  contact: string,
  deals: ActiveDeal[],
  windowMs: number,
): ActiveDeal | null {
  const now = Date.now();
  const recent = deals.filter((ad) => {
    const updated = new Date(ad.deal.updatedAt).getTime();
    return now - updated < windowMs;
  });
  return findByContact(contact, recent);
}

/**
 * Find a deal whose conversation contains messages with a matching threadId.
 */
function findByThreadId(threadId: string, deals: ActiveDeal[]): ActiveDeal | null {
  const matches = deals.filter((ad) =>
    ad.conversation.messages.some((m) => m.threadId === threadId),
  );
  if (matches.length === 0) return null;
  const live = matches.filter((ad) => !TERMINAL_STAGES.has(ad.deal.stage));
  return live.length > 0 ? mostRecent(live) : null;
}

/** Pick the most recently updated deal from a list. */
function mostRecent(deals: ActiveDeal[]): ActiveDeal {
  return deals.sort((a, b) => b.deal.updatedAt.localeCompare(a.deal.updatedAt))[0]!;
}
// === src/router/message-router.ts ===
/**
 * Message Router — the air traffic controller for all inbound messages.
 * Per spec: communication-layer.md FR-2, section 2.1.
 *
 * Flow:
 * 1. Match to existing deal via ThreadMatcher (or create new via intake)
 * 2. Append to conversation
 * 3. Run extractors on EVERY inbound (update pax, budget, etc.)
 * 4. Classify intent
 * 5. Advance stage on operator quote or client confirmation
 */

import type { Message, MessageIntent } from "../schemas/message.js";
import type { Conversation } from "../schemas/conversation.js";
import { createConversation, addMessageToConversation } from "../schemas/conversation.js";
import type { DealContext } from "../schemas/deal-context.js";
import { matchThread } from "./thread-matcher.js";
import type { ActiveDeal } from "./thread-matcher.js";
import { classifySender, classifyIntent } from "./intent-classifier.js";
import type { SenderRole } from "./intent-classifier.js";
import { extractPax, extractBudget, extractRoute, extractAircraftClass } from "../capabilities/intake.js";
import { z } from "zod";

// ── Operator Quote Extraction Schema ────────────────────────────────────

/** Schema for LLM-confirmed operator quote extraction. */
export const OperatorQuoteSchema = z.object({
  quotedPrice: z.number().positive(),
  currency: z.string().default("USD"),
  validUntil: z.string().optional(),
  notes: z.string().optional(),
});
export type OperatorQuote = z.infer<typeof OperatorQuoteSchema>;

/** Injectable LLM function for confirming operator quotes. */
export type QuoteExtractorFn = (messageBody: string) => Promise<OperatorQuote | null>;

// ── Router Result ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export interface RouteResult {
  /** The matched or newly created deal. */
  deal: DealContext;
  /** The deal's conversation. */
  conversation: Conversation;
  /** Whether a new deal was created. */
  isNewDeal: boolean;
  /** Classified intent of the message. */
  intent: MessageIntent;
  /** Sender's role relative to the deal. */
  senderRole: SenderRole;
  /** Fields updated from extractor re-run. */
  updatedFields: string[];
  /** Action to take (for orchestrator). */
  action: RouteAction;
}

export type RouteAction =
  | { type: "none" }
  | { type: "advance_to_quoted"; operatorPrice: number }
  | { type: "close_deal"; result: "closed" }
  | { type: "close_deal"; result: "client_declined" }
  | { type: "activate_backup" }
  | { type: "flag_for_human"; reason: string }
  | { type: "create_new_deal" };

// ── Router Options ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export interface RouterOptions {
  /**
   * Create a new DealContext from the message body.
   * In production this calls intake(). Injectable for testing.
   */
  createDeal: (messageBody: string) => Promise<DealContext>;
  /**
   * LLM-confirmed quote extraction. If provided, operator quote_response
   * messages are verified by the LLM before any price enters the pipeline.
   * The regex extractPrice is used as a fast pre-check; the LLM confirms.
   */
  quoteExtractor?: QuoteExtractorFn;
}

// ── Main Router ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

/**
 * Route an inbound message to the correct deal and determine the action.
 *
 * @param msg - The inbound message.
 * @param activeDeals - All tracked deals with their conversations.
 * @param options - Injectable deal creation function.
 * @returns RouteResult with deal, intent, and action.
 */
export async function routeMessage(
  msg: Message,
  activeDeals: ActiveDeal[],
  options: RouterOptions,
): Promise<RouteResult> {
  // ── Step 1: Thread matching ────────────────────────────────────────
  let matched = matchThread(msg, activeDeals);
  let isNewDeal = false;

  if (!matched) {
    // Create new deal from message
    const deal = await options.createDeal(msg.body);
    const conversation = createConversation(deal.id);
    matched = { deal, conversation };
    isNewDeal = true;
  }

  const { deal, conversation } = matched;

  // Link message to deal
  msg.dealId = deal.id;

  // ── Step 2: Append to conversation ─────────────────────────────────
  addMessageToConversation(conversation, msg);

  // ── Step 3: Run extractors on EVERY inbound ────────────────────────
  const updatedFields: string[] = [];
  if (msg.direction === "inbound") {
    const patch = runExtractors(msg.body, deal);
    // Apply patch through proper channel — not direct mutation
    if (patch.trip) Object.assign(deal.trip, patch.trip);
    if (patch.client) Object.assign(deal.client, patch.client);
    if (patch.fields.length > 0) deal.updatedAt = new Date().toISOString();
    updatedFields.push(...patch.fields);
  }

  // ── Step 4: Classify intent ────────────────────────────────────────
  const senderRole = classifySender(msg, deal);
  const intent = classifyIntent(msg, deal, senderRole);
  msg.intent = intent;

  // ── Step 5: Determine action ───────────────────────────────────────
  const action = await determineAction(intent, senderRole, deal, msg, options.quoteExtractor);

  return {
    deal,
    conversation,
    isNewDeal,
    intent,
    senderRole,
    updatedFields,
    action,
  };
}

// ── Extractors (re-run on every inbound) ─────────────────────────────────

/**
 * Extract patch of new data from a message body.
 * Returns a patch object + list of fields that would be updated.
 * Does NOT mutate the deal — caller applies the patch via updateDealContext.
 */
export interface ExtractorPatch {
  trip?: Partial<DealContext["trip"]>;
  client?: Partial<DealContext["client"]>;
  fields: string[];
}

function runExtractors(body: string, deal: DealContext): ExtractorPatch {
  const patch: ExtractorPatch = { fields: [] };
  const tripPatch: Partial<DealContext["trip"]> = {};
  const clientPatch: Partial<DealContext["client"]> = {};

  // Pax update
  const pax = extractPax(body);
  if (pax && pax !== deal.trip.pax && pax > 0) {
    tripPatch.pax = pax;
    patch.fields.push("pax");
  }

  // Budget (only fill if currently null)
  if (!deal.client.budget) {
    const budget = extractBudget(body);
    if (budget) {
      clientPatch.budget = budget;
      patch.fields.push("budget");
    }
  }

  // Route (only fill missing endpoints)
  const [dep, arr] = extractRoute(body);
  if (dep && !deal.trip.departure) {
    tripPatch.departure = dep;
    patch.fields.push("departure");
  }
  if (arr && !deal.trip.arrival) {
    tripPatch.arrival = arr;
    patch.fields.push("arrival");
  }

  // Aircraft class (only fill if null)
  if (!deal.trip.aircraftClass) {
    const cls = extractAircraftClass(body);
    if (cls) {
      tripPatch.aircraftClass = cls;
      patch.fields.push("aircraftClass");
    }
  }

  if (Object.keys(tripPatch).length > 0) patch.trip = tripPatch;
  if (Object.keys(clientPatch).length > 0) patch.client = clientPatch;

  return patch;
}

// ── Action determination ─────────────────────────────────────────────────

async function determineAction(
  intent: MessageIntent,
  senderRole: SenderRole,
  deal: DealContext,
  msg: Message,
  quoteExtractor?: QuoteExtractorFn,
): Promise<RouteAction> {
  switch (intent) {
    case "quote_response": {
      if (senderRole === "operator") {
        // Fast pre-check: regex extraction
        const regexPrice = extractPrice(msg.body);
        if (regexPrice === null) return { type: "none" };

        // If LLM extractor is available, confirm the price before it enters the pipeline
        if (quoteExtractor) {
          const confirmed = await quoteExtractor(msg.body);
          if (confirmed) {
            // Cross-check: LLM price must be within 25% of regex price
            const deviation = Math.abs(confirmed.quotedPrice - regexPrice) / regexPrice;
            if (deviation > 0.25) {
              return {
                type: "flag_for_human",
                reason: `Price mismatch: regex found $${regexPrice.toLocaleString()}, LLM found $${confirmed.quotedPrice.toLocaleString()} (${(deviation * 100).toFixed(0)}% deviation). Manual review required.`,
              };
            }
            return { type: "advance_to_quoted", operatorPrice: confirmed.quotedPrice };
          }
          // LLM couldn't confirm — flag for human review
          return { type: "flag_for_human", reason: `Regex found $${regexPrice.toLocaleString()} but LLM could not confirm operator quote. Manual review required.` };
        }

        // No LLM extractor (tests, cold start) — use regex price
        return { type: "advance_to_quoted", operatorPrice: regexPrice };
      }
      return { type: "none" };
    }

    case "confirmation": {
      if (senderRole === "client" && deal.stage === "presented") {
        return { type: "close_deal", result: "closed" };
      }
      return { type: "none" };
    }

    case "decline": {
      if (senderRole === "client") {
        return { type: "close_deal", result: "client_declined" };
      }
      if (senderRole === "operator") {
        return { type: "activate_backup" };
      }
      return { type: "none" };
    }

    case "negotiation": {
      return { type: "flag_for_human", reason: "Negotiation detected. Requires human handling." };
    }

    default:
      return { type: "none" };
  }
}

// ── Price extraction ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────

/**
 * Extract a dollar amount from message body.
 * Supports "$28,000", "$28000", "$28k", "28 thousand".
 */
function extractPrice(body: string): number | null {
  // "$XXk" — check before bare dollar to avoid matching "$32" from "$32k"
  const kMatch = body.match(/\$\s*(\d+)\s*k\b/i);
  if (kMatch) {
    return parseInt(kMatch[1]!, 10) * 1000;
  }

  // "XX thousand"
  const thousandMatch = body.match(/(\d+)\s*thousand/i);
  if (thousandMatch) {
    return parseInt(thousandMatch[1]!, 10) * 1000;
  }

  // "$XX,XXX" or "$XXXXX"
  const dollarMatch = body.match(/\$\s*([\d,]+(?:\.\d+)?)/);
  if (dollarMatch) {
    const val = parseFloat(dollarMatch[1]!.replace(/,/g, ""));
    if (val > 0) return val;
  }

  return null;
}
// === src/router/confidence-gate.ts ===
/**
 * Confidence Gate — gatekeeper for outbound message autonomy.
 * Per spec: communication-layer.md section 2.2.
 * Pattern: Anthropic "Building Effective Agents" — human-in-the-loop with graduated autonomy.
 */

import type { Message } from "../schemas/message.js";
import type { DealContext } from "../schemas/deal-context.js";
import type { AutonomyStore } from "../memory/autonomy-store.js";

// ── Gate Decision ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export type GateDecision =
  | { action: "auto_send" }
  | { action: "delayed"; delaySec: number }
  | { action: "hold"; reason: string }
  | { action: "blocked"; reason: string };

// ── NEVER auto-send list (hard-coded, NOT configurable) ──────────────────

const NEVER_AUTO_SEND_INTENTS: Set<string> = new Set([
  "negotiation",
  "availability_check",
]);

/**
 * Check if a draft body contains dollar amounts.
 * Client-facing messages with prices ALWAYS require approval.
 */
function containsDollarAmount(body: string): boolean {
  return /\$\s*[\d,]+/.test(body);
}

/**
 * Determine the recipient type from the message and deal context.
 */
export function getRecipientType(
  draft: Message,
  deal: DealContext,
): "client" | "operator" {
  const to = draft.to?.toLowerCase() ?? "";
  const clientContact = deal.client.contact?.toLowerCase() ?? "";
  const operatorPhone = deal.sourcing.selectedOperator?.phone?.toLowerCase() ?? "";

  if (operatorPhone && to.includes(operatorPhone)) return "operator";
  if (clientContact && to.includes(clientContact)) return "client";

  // Default to client (safer — requires more scrutiny)
  return "client";
}

/**
 * Check if this is the first contact with a specific operator.
 * First-contact messages always require human approval.
 */
function isFirstContactWithOperator(
  draft: Message,
  deal: DealContext,
): boolean {
  if (getRecipientType(draft, deal) !== "operator") return false;
  // If the deal hasn't had any operator messages yet, it's first contact
  return deal.stage === "sourced" || deal.stage === "estimated";
}

/**
 * Evaluate a draft message and determine whether to auto-send, delay, or hold.
 * Per spec pseudocode section 2.2.
 *
 * @param draft - The outbound message to evaluate.
 * @param deal - The current deal context.
 * @param autonomyStore - Store for autonomy rules (null = always hold).
 * @returns Gate decision: auto_send, delayed, hold, or blocked.
 */
export function evaluateConfidence(
  draft: Message,
