  deal: DealContext,
  autonomyStore: AutonomyStore | null,
): GateDecision {
  const recipientType = getRecipientType(draft, deal);
  const intent = draft.intent;

  // ── NEVER auto-send checks (hard-coded) ────────────────────────────

  // Dollar amounts to clients
  if (containsDollarAmount(draft.body) && recipientType === "client") {
    return { action: "blocked", reason: "Client-facing message contains pricing. Always requires approval." };
  }

  // Negotiation, availability commitments
  if (intent && NEVER_AUTO_SEND_INTENTS.has(intent)) {
    return { action: "blocked", reason: `Intent "${intent}" is in the never-auto-send list.` };
  }

  // First contact with operator
  if (isFirstContactWithOperator(draft, deal)) {
    return { action: "blocked", reason: "First contact with operator. Always requires approval." };
  }

  // ── Check earned autonomy ──────────────────────────────────────────

  if (autonomyStore && intent) {
    const isAuto = autonomyStore.isAutonomous(
      draft.channel, intent, recipientType,
    );
    if (isAuto) {
      return { action: "auto_send" };
    }
  }

  // ── Confidence-based gating ────────────────────────────────────────

  if (draft.confidenceLevel === "medium") {
    const rule = autonomyStore?.getRule(draft.channel, intent ?? "general", recipientType);
    return { action: "delayed", delaySec: rule?.maxDelaySeconds ?? 30 };
  }

  // Everything else: hold for human approval
  return { action: "hold", reason: "Awaiting broker approval." };
}
// === src/router/intent-classifier.ts ===
/**
 * Intent Classifier — determines what a message means in deal context.
 * Pure code-layer classification (no LLM). Pattern-based like intake.ts.
 * Per spec: communication-layer.md section 2.1 step 4.
 */

import type { Message, MessageIntent } from "../schemas/message.js";
import type { DealContext } from "../schemas/deal-context.js";

/** Participant role relative to the deal. */
export type SenderRole = "client" | "operator" | "unknown";

/**
 * Determine if the sender is the client, the operator, or unknown.
 * Matches message.from against deal contacts.
 */
export function classifySender(msg: Message, deal: DealContext): SenderRole {
  const from = msg.from.toLowerCase();
  const clientContact = deal.client.contact?.toLowerCase() ?? "";
  const operatorPhone = deal.sourcing.selectedOperator?.phone?.toLowerCase() ?? "";
  const operatorName = deal.sourcing.selectedOperator?.operatorName?.toLowerCase() ?? "";

  if (clientContact && (from === clientContact || from.includes(clientContact) || clientContact.includes(from))) {
    return "client";
  }
  if (operatorPhone && (from === operatorPhone || from.includes(operatorPhone))) {
    return "operator";
  }
  if (operatorName && from.includes(operatorName)) {
    return "operator";
  }
  return "unknown";
}

/**
 * Classify the intent of a message based on its content and deal stage.
 * Returns one of the MessageIntent enum values.
 *
 * @param msg - The inbound message to classify.
 * @param deal - The current deal context.
 * @param senderRole - Who sent the message (client/operator/unknown).
 * @returns The classified intent.
 */
export function classifyIntent(
  msg: Message,
  deal: DealContext,
  senderRole: SenderRole,
): MessageIntent {
  const body = msg.body.toLowerCase();

  // ── Quote response (operator provides a price) ─────────────────────
  if (senderRole === "operator" && containsPrice(body)) {
    return "quote_response";
  }

  // ── Decline from operator (not available, sold, etc.) ───────────────
  if (senderRole === "operator" && isDecline(body)) return "decline";

  // ── Confirmation (client says yes) ─────────────────────────────────
  if (senderRole === "client" && deal.stage === "presented") {
    if (isConfirmation(body)) return "confirmation";
    if (isDecline(body)) return "decline";
  }

  // ── Decline (from client at any stage) ─────────────────────────────
  if (senderRole === "client" && isDecline(body)) return "decline";

  // ── Negotiation (price discussion, counteroffers) ──────────────────
  if (isNegotiation(body)) return "negotiation";

  // ── Availability check ─────────────────────────────────────────────
  if (senderRole === "operator" && isAvailabilityResponse(body)) {
    return "availability_check";
  }

  // ── Follow-up (continuing an existing conversation) ────────────────
  if (msg.responseToId && deal.stage !== "intake") {
    return "follow_up";
  }

  // ── Info request (asking questions) ────────────────────────────────
  if (isInfoRequest(body)) return "info_request";

  // ── Quote request (new client asking for pricing) ──────────────────
  if (senderRole === "client" || senderRole === "unknown") {
    if (isQuoteRequest(body)) return "quote_request";
  }

  return "general";
}

// ── Pattern matchers ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────

function containsPrice(body: string): boolean {
  return /\$\s*[\d,]+(?:\.\d+)?/.test(body) || /\b\d{2,6}\s*(?:k|thousand|all[- ]in)\b/i.test(body);
}

function isConfirmation(body: string): boolean {
  return /\b(?:let'?s\s+do\s+it|confirm|approved?|book\s+it|go\s+ahead|yes|sounds?\s+good|we'?re?\s+in|deal)\b/i.test(body);
}

function isDecline(body: string): boolean {
  return /\b(?:pass|decline|not\s+interested|not\s+available|unavailable|too\s+(?:expensive|much|high)|cancel|no\s+(?:thanks?|thank\s+you)|can'?t\s+do\s+(?:it|that)|sold)\b/i.test(body);
}

function isNegotiation(body: string): boolean {
  return /\b(?:counter|negotiate|lower|discount|best\s+price|can\s+you\s+do|what\s+about|too\s+expensive.*(?:lighter|smaller|cheaper))\b/i.test(body);
}

function isAvailabilityResponse(body: string): boolean {
  return /\b(?:available|not\s+available|unavailable|sold|booked|aircraft\s+(?:is|not)|can\s+(?:do|confirm)|schedule)\b/i.test(body);
}

function isInfoRequest(body: string): boolean {
  return /\b(?:how\s+much|what\s+(?:would|does)|can\s+you|do\s+you|is\s+(?:it|there)|tell\s+me)\b/i.test(body) || body.includes("?");
}

function isQuoteRequest(body: string): boolean {
  return /\b(?:charter|flight|fly|quote|price|cost|book|need\s+a?\s*(?:plane|jet|aircraft))\b/i.test(body);
}
// === tests/quote.test.ts ===
/**
 * Tests for Quote capability — two-phase pricing pipeline.
 *
 * Coverage:
 * 1. Phase 1 (estimate): successful Aviapages call, API failure fallback
 * 2. Historical delta correction from DealStore memory
 * 3. Budget mismatch detection
 * 4. Phase 2 (real quote): margin calculation, OSA price formula
 * 5. Quote vs estimate sanity check (via Guardian integration)
 * 6. Edge cases: no budget, no estimate, zero values
 */

import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach } from "vitest";
import {
  estimate,
  quote,
  checkBudgetFit,
  calcEstimateDelta,
} from "../src/capabilities/quote.js";
import { checksAfterEstimate, checksAfterQuote } from "../src/capabilities/guardian.js";
import { AviapagesClient } from "../src/integrations/aviapages.js";
import type { FetchFn, PriceEstimateResult } from "../src/integrations/aviapages.js";
import { DealStore } from "../src/memory/deal-store.js";
import type { DealRecord } from "../src/schemas/deal-record.js";
import type { DealContext } from "../src/schemas/deal-context.js";
import { createDealContext, updateDealContext, _resetDealCounter } from "../src/orchestrator/deal-context-manager.js";
import { MARGIN_POLICY, QUOTE_VALIDITY } from "../src/config.js";
import { createTestDb } from "../src/memory/database.js";

// ── Test Helpers ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

/** Create a mock Aviapages fetch that returns a fixed price estimate. */
function mockFetch(result: PriceEstimateResult): FetchFn {
  return async (_url: string, _init: RequestInit) => ({
    ok: true,
    status: 200,
    json: async () => ({
      price_low: result.low,
      price_high: result.high,
      flight_time: result.flightTime,
      fuel_burn: result.fuelBurn,
    }),
  });
}

/** Create a mock fetch that fails. */
function failingFetch(): FetchFn {
  return async () => ({ ok: false, status: 500, json: async () => ({}) });
}

/** Create a mock fetch that throws. */
function throwingFetch(): FetchFn {
  return async () => { throw new Error("Network error"); };
}

/** Create a deal context with trip and budget populated. */
function makeDealCtx(overrides?: {
  budgetMin?: number;
  budgetMax?: number;
  departure?: string;
  arrival?: string;
}): DealContext {
  const ctx = createDealContext("sim");
  updateDealContext(ctx, {
    trip: {
      departure: overrides?.departure ?? "KTEB",
      arrival: overrides?.arrival ?? "KPBI",
      date: "2026-04-15",
      pax: 4,
      aircraftClass: "light",
      aircraftType: "Phenom 300E",
      isRoundTrip: false,
      specialRequirements: [],
    },
    client: {
      name: "Test Client",
      contact: "test@example.com",
      type: "new",
      budget: { min: overrides?.budgetMin ?? 20000, max: overrides?.budgetMax ?? 40000 },
      substitutionPref: null,
      qualified: true,
      qualificationReason: "Fully qualified",
    },
  });
  return ctx;
}

/** Create a DealStore backed by an in-memory SQLite database with historical deals. */
function makeStoreWithDeals(records: DealRecord[]): { store: DealStore; cleanup: () => void } {
  const db = createTestDb();
  const store = new DealStore(db);
  for (const r of records) store.add(r);
  return { store, cleanup: () => db.close() };
}

/** Make a historical deal record for pricing delta tests. */
function makeDealRecord(overrides: Partial<DealRecord> = {}): DealRecord {
  return {
    id: "deal_hist_001",
    timestamp: "2026-03-01T10:00:00.000Z",
    mode: "live",
    route: { departure: "KTEB", arrival: "KPBI", distance: 950 },
    aviapagesEstimate: { low: 25000, high: 35000 },
    operatorQuote: 33600,  // +12% above median of 30000
    osaPrice: 36522,
    estimateDelta: 0.12,   // (33600 - 30000) / 30000
    operatorName: "Elite Aviation",
    operatorBase: "KTEB",
    operatorResponseTime: 3600,
    operatorConversion: true,
    aircraftType: "Phenom 300E",
    aircraftClass: "light",
    clientName: "Test",
    clientContact: "test@test.com",
    clientType: "new",
    clientBudget: { min: 25000, max: 40000 },
    clientConversion: true,
    decisionTime: 1800,
    result: "closed",
    revenue: 2922,
    margin: 0.08,
    totalDealTime: 7200,
    dayOfWeek: 2,
    season: "shoulder",
    guardianFlags: [],
    ...overrides,
  };
}

// ── Phase 1: Estimate ────────────────────────────────────────────────────

describe("estimate (Phase 1)", () => {
  beforeEach(() => _resetDealCounter());

  it("returns price range from successful Aviapages call", async () => {
    const apiResult: PriceEstimateResult = { low: 25000, high: 35000, flightTime: 150, fuelBurn: 280 };
    const client = new AviapagesClient({ apiKey: "test" }, mockFetch(apiResult));
    const ctx = makeDealCtx();

    const result = await estimate(ctx, { aviapages: client });

    expect(result.estimate.source).toBe("aviapages");
    expect(result.estimate.priceRange).not.toBeNull();
    expect(result.estimate.priceRange!.low).toBe(25000);
    expect(result.estimate.priceRange!.high).toBe(35000);
    expect(result.estimate.flightTime).toBe(150);
    expect(result.estimate.fuelBurn).toBe(280);
    expect(result.estimate.calculatedAt).not.toBeNull();
  });

  it("returns manual source when Aviapages unavailable", async () => {
    const ctx = makeDealCtx();
    const result = await estimate(ctx, { aviapages: null });

    expect(result.estimate.source).toBe("manual");
    expect(result.estimate.priceRange).toBeNull();
    expect(result.estimate.calculatedAt).toBeNull();
  });

  it("returns manual source when API returns error", async () => {
    const client = new AviapagesClient({ apiKey: "test" }, failingFetch());
    const ctx = makeDealCtx();

    const result = await estimate(ctx, { aviapages: client });

    expect(result.estimate.source).toBe("manual");
    expect(result.estimate.priceRange).toBeNull();
  });

  it("handles network errors gracefully", async () => {
    const client = new AviapagesClient({ apiKey: "test" }, throwingFetch());
    const ctx = makeDealCtx();

    const result = await estimate(ctx, { aviapages: client });

    expect(result.estimate.source).toBe("manual");
    expect(result.estimate.priceRange).toBeNull();
  });

  it("passes correct params to Aviapages API", async () => {
    let capturedBody: unknown;
    const captureFetch: FetchFn = async (_url, init) => {
      capturedBody = JSON.parse(init.body as string);
      return {
        ok: true,
        status: 200,
        json: async () => ({ price_low: 25000, price_high: 35000, flight_time: 150, fuel_burn: 280 }),
      };
    };
    const client = new AviapagesClient({ apiKey: "test-key" }, captureFetch);
    const ctx = makeDealCtx();

    await estimate(ctx, { aviapages: client });

    expect(capturedBody).toEqual({
      departure: "KTEB",
      arrival: "KPBI",
      aircraft_type: "Phenom 300E",
      date: "2026-04-15",
      pax: 4,
    });
  });

  it("handles zero flightTime and fuelBurn as null", async () => {
    const apiResult: PriceEstimateResult = { low: 25000, high: 35000, flightTime: 0, fuelBurn: 0 };
    const client = new AviapagesClient({ apiKey: "test" }, mockFetch(apiResult));
    const ctx = makeDealCtx();

    const result = await estimate(ctx, { aviapages: client });

    expect(result.estimate.flightTime).toBeNull();
    expect(result.estimate.fuelBurn).toBeNull();
  });
});

// ── Historical Delta Correction ──────────────────────────────────────────

describe("estimate with historical delta correction", () => {
  beforeEach(() => _resetDealCounter());

  it("adjusts estimate range using historical pricing delta", async () => {
    // Historical deals show Aviapages underestimates by 12% on KTEB→KPBI
    const records = [
      makeDealRecord({ estimateDelta: 0.10 }),
      makeDealRecord({ id: "deal_hist_002", estimateDelta: 0.14 }),
    ];
    // Average delta: (0.10 + 0.14) / 2 = 0.12
    const { store, cleanup } = makeStoreWithDeals(records);

    const apiResult: PriceEstimateResult = { low: 25000, high: 35000, flightTime: 150, fuelBurn: 280 };
    const client = new AviapagesClient({ apiKey: "test" }, mockFetch(apiResult));
    const ctx = makeDealCtx();

    const result = await estimate(ctx, { aviapages: client, dealStore: store });

    // Adjusted: 25000 * 1.12 = 28000, 35000 * 1.12 = 39200
    expect(result.estimate.priceRange!.low).toBe(28000);
    expect(result.estimate.priceRange!.high).toBe(39200);

    cleanup();
  });

  it("applies negative delta (Aviapages overestimates)", async () => {
    const records = [
      makeDealRecord({ estimateDelta: -0.08 }),
    ];
    const { store, cleanup } = makeStoreWithDeals(records);

    const apiResult: PriceEstimateResult = { low: 30000, high: 40000, flightTime: 150, fuelBurn: 280 };
    const client = new AviapagesClient({ apiKey: "test" }, mockFetch(apiResult));
    const ctx = makeDealCtx();

    const result = await estimate(ctx, { aviapages: client, dealStore: store });

    // Adjusted: 30000 * 0.92 = 27600, 40000 * 0.92 = 36800
    expect(result.estimate.priceRange!.low).toBe(27600);
    expect(result.estimate.priceRange!.high).toBe(36800);

    cleanup();
  });

  it("uses raw API result when no historical data exists", async () => {
    const { store, cleanup } = makeStoreWithDeals([]);

    const apiResult: PriceEstimateResult = { low: 25000, high: 35000, flightTime: 150, fuelBurn: 280 };
    const client = new AviapagesClient({ apiKey: "test" }, mockFetch(apiResult));
    const ctx = makeDealCtx();

    const result = await estimate(ctx, { aviapages: client, dealStore: store });

    expect(result.estimate.priceRange!.low).toBe(25000);
    expect(result.estimate.priceRange!.high).toBe(35000);

    cleanup();
  });

  it("uses raw API result when store has deals for different route", async () => {
    const records = [
      makeDealRecord({ route: { departure: "KORD", arrival: "KASE", distance: 1200 }, estimateDelta: 0.20 }),
    ];
    const { store, cleanup } = makeStoreWithDeals(records);

    const apiResult: PriceEstimateResult = { low: 25000, high: 35000, flightTime: 150, fuelBurn: 280 };
    const client = new AviapagesClient({ apiKey: "test" }, mockFetch(apiResult));
    const ctx = makeDealCtx(); // KTEB→KPBI

    const result = await estimate(ctx, { aviapages: client, dealStore: store });

    // No delta for KTEB→KPBI, so raw values
    expect(result.estimate.priceRange!.low).toBe(25000);
    expect(result.estimate.priceRange!.high).toBe(35000);

    cleanup();
  });
});

// ── Budget Mismatch ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────

describe("checkBudgetFit", () => {
  it("returns null when budget overlaps estimate", () => {
    expect(checkBudgetFit(
      { min: 20000, max: 40000 },
      { low: 25000, high: 35000 },
    )).toBeNull();
  });

  it("returns reason when budget max below estimate floor", () => {
    const reason = checkBudgetFit(
      { min: 10000, max: 15000 },
      { low: 25000, high: 35000 },
    );
    expect(reason).not.toBeNull();
    expect(reason).toContain("15,000");
    expect(reason).toContain("25,000");
  });

  it("returns null when budget is null (can't check)", () => {
    expect(checkBudgetFit(null, { low: 25000, high: 35000 })).toBeNull();
  });

  it("returns null when estimate is null (can't check)", () => {
    expect(checkBudgetFit({ min: 20000, max: 40000 }, null)).toBeNull();
  });

  it("returns null when budget min above estimate high (premium client)", () => {
    // Budget above range is unusual but not a mismatch — client wants premium
    expect(checkBudgetFit(
      { min: 50000, max: 80000 },
      { low: 25000, high: 35000 },
    )).toBeNull();
  });
});

describe("budget mismatch via Guardian", () => {
  beforeEach(() => _resetDealCounter());

  it("Guardian blocks when budget max below adjusted estimate floor", () => {
    const ctx = makeDealCtx({ budgetMin: 10000, budgetMax: 15000 });
    updateDealContext(ctx, {
      estimate: {
        source: "aviapages",
        flightTime: 150,
        priceRange: { low: 25000, high: 35000 },
        fuelBurn: 280,
        positioning: null,
        calculatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      },
    });

    const checks = checksAfterEstimate(ctx);
    const budgetCheck = checks.find((c) => c.name === "budget_overlap");
    expect(budgetCheck).toBeDefined();
    expect(budgetCheck!.passed).toBe(false);
    expect(budgetCheck!.severity).toBe("critical");
  });

  it("Guardian passes when budget overlaps estimate", () => {
    const ctx = makeDealCtx({ budgetMin: 20000, budgetMax: 40000 });
    updateDealContext(ctx, {
      estimate: {
        source: "aviapages",
        flightTime: 150,
        priceRange: { low: 25000, high: 35000 },
        fuelBurn: 280,
        positioning: null,
        calculatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      },
    });

    const checks = checksAfterEstimate(ctx);
    const budgetCheck = checks.find((c) => c.name === "budget_overlap");
    expect(budgetCheck!.passed).toBe(true);
  });
});

// ── Phase 2: Real Quote ──────────────────────────────────────────────────

describe("quote (Phase 2)", () => {
  beforeEach(() => _resetDealCounter());

  it("calculates OSA price with default margin (8%)", async () => {
    const ctx = makeDealCtx();
    const result = await quote(ctx, { operatorPrice: 28000 });

    // OSA price = 28000 / (1 - 0.08) = 28000 / 0.92 = 30434.78 → rounded to 30435
    expect(result.quote.operatorPrice).toBe(28000);
    expect(result.quote.osaPrice).toBe(Math.round(28000 / (1 - MARGIN_POLICY.target)));
    expect(result.quote.margin).toBe(MARGIN_POLICY.target);
  });

  it("calculates OSA price with custom margin", async () => {
    const ctx = makeDealCtx();
    const result = await quote(ctx, { operatorPrice: 28000, margin: 0.10 });

    // OSA price = 28000 / 0.90 = 31111.11 → 31111
    expect(result.quote.osaPrice).toBe(Math.round(28000 / 0.90));
    expect(result.quote.margin).toBe(0.10);
  });

  it("sets validity window from config default", async () => {
    const ctx = makeDealCtx();
    const before = Date.now();
    const result = await quote(ctx, { operatorPrice: 28000 });
    const after = Date.now();

    expect(result.quote.validUntil).not.toBeNull();
    const validTime = new Date(result.quote.validUntil!).getTime();
    const expectedMs = QUOTE_VALIDITY.defaultMinutes * 60_000;
    expect(validTime).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(before + expectedMs - 1000);
    expect(validTime).toBeLessThanOrEqual(after + expectedMs + 1000);
  });

  it("sets custom validity window", async () => {
    const ctx = makeDealCtx();
    const before = Date.now();
    const result = await quote(ctx, { operatorPrice: 28000, validityMinutes: 60 });

    const validTime = new Date(result.quote.validUntil!).getTime();
    expect(validTime).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(before + 60 * 60_000 - 1000);
  });

  it("includes aircraft details when provided", async () => {
    const ctx = makeDealCtx();
    const result = await quote(ctx, {
      operatorPrice: 28000,
      aircraft: { type: "Phenom 300E", tail: "N300TE", year: 2022 },
    });

    expect(result.quote.aircraft).toEqual({ type: "Phenom 300E", tail: "N300TE", year: 2022 });
  });

  it("sets aircraft to null when not provided", async () => {
    const ctx = makeDealCtx();
    const result = await quote(ctx, { operatorPrice: 28000 });

    expect(result.quote.aircraft).toBeNull();
  });

  it("sets quotedAt timestamp", async () => {
    const ctx = makeDealCtx();
    const before = new Date().toISOString();
    const result = await quote(ctx, { operatorPrice: 28000 });

    expect(result.quote.quotedAt).not.toBeNull();
    expect(result.quote.quotedAt! >= before).toBe(true);
  });
});

// ── Margin Calculations ──────────────────────────────────────────────────

describe("margin calculation edge cases", () => {
  beforeEach(() => _resetDealCounter());

  it("margin at policy minimum (6%)", async () => {
    const ctx = makeDealCtx();
    const result = await quote(ctx, { operatorPrice: 28000, margin: MARGIN_POLICY.min });

    // 28000 / 0.94 = 29787.23 → 29787
    expect(result.quote.osaPrice).toBe(Math.round(28000 / (1 - MARGIN_POLICY.min)));
    expect(result.quote.osaPrice!).toBeGreaterThan(28000);
  });

  it("margin at policy maximum (15%)", async () => {
    const ctx = makeDealCtx();
    const result = await quote(ctx, { operatorPrice: 28000, margin: MARGIN_POLICY.max });

    // 28000 / 0.85 = 32941.18 → 32941
    expect(result.quote.osaPrice).toBe(Math.round(28000 / (1 - MARGIN_POLICY.max)));
  });

  it("margin is percentage of final price, not markup on cost", async () => {
    const ctx = makeDealCtx();
    const result = await quote(ctx, { operatorPrice: 28000, margin: 0.10 });

    // Verify: (osaPrice - operatorPrice) / osaPrice ≈ 0.10
    const actualMargin = (result.quote.osaPrice! - result.quote.operatorPrice!) / result.quote.osaPrice!;
    expect(Math.abs(actualMargin - 0.10)).toBeLessThan(0.001);
  });
});

// ── Quote vs Estimate Sanity (via Guardian) ──────────────────────────────

describe("quote vs estimate sanity check", () => {
  beforeEach(() => _resetDealCounter());

  it("Guardian passes when quote within 25% of estimate median", () => {
    const ctx = makeDealCtx();
    updateDealContext(ctx, {
      estimate: {
        source: "aviapages",
        flightTime: 150,
        priceRange: { low: 25000, high: 35000 },
        fuelBurn: 280,
        positioning: null,
        calculatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      },
      quote: {
        operatorPrice: 28000,
        osaPrice: 30435,
        margin: 0.08,
        validUntil: new Date(Date.now() + 120 * 60_000).toISOString(),
        aircraft: null,
        quotedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      },
    });

    const checks = checksAfterQuote(ctx);
    const sanityCheck = checks.find((c) => c.name === "quote_vs_estimate");
    expect(sanityCheck).toBeDefined();
    expect(sanityCheck!.passed).toBe(true);
    // 28000 vs median 30000 = 6.7% off
    expect(sanityCheck!.message).toContain("within");
  });

  it("Guardian warns when quote more than 25% off estimate", () => {
    const ctx = makeDealCtx();
    updateDealContext(ctx, {
      estimate: {
        source: "aviapages",
        flightTime: 150,
        priceRange: { low: 25000, high: 35000 },
        fuelBurn: 280,
        positioning: null,
        calculatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      },
      quote: {
        operatorPrice: 42000, // 40% above median of 30000
        osaPrice: 45652,
        margin: 0.08,
        validUntil: new Date(Date.now() + 120 * 60_000).toISOString(),
        aircraft: null,
        quotedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      },
    });

    const checks = checksAfterQuote(ctx);
    const sanityCheck = checks.find((c) => c.name === "quote_vs_estimate");
    expect(sanityCheck!.severity).toBe("warning");
    expect(sanityCheck!.message).toContain("40%");
  });

  it("Guardian warns when margin below policy minimum", () => {
    const ctx = makeDealCtx();
    updateDealContext(ctx, {
      estimate: {
        source: "aviapages",
        flightTime: 150,
        priceRange: { low: 25000, high: 35000 },
        fuelBurn: 280,
        positioning: null,
        calculatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      },
      quote: {
        operatorPrice: 28000,
        osaPrice: 29120, // ~4% margin
        margin: 0.04,
        validUntil: new Date(Date.now() + 120 * 60_000).toISOString(),
        aircraft: null,
        quotedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      },
    });

    const checks = checksAfterQuote(ctx);
    const marginCheck = checks.find((c) => c.name === "margin_policy");
    expect(marginCheck!.severity).toBe("warning");
    expect(marginCheck!.message).toContain("below minimum");
  });

  it("Guardian warns when margin above policy maximum", () => {
    const ctx = makeDealCtx();
    updateDealContext(ctx, {
      estimate: {
        source: "aviapages",
        flightTime: 150,
        priceRange: { low: 25000, high: 35000 },
        fuelBurn: 280,
        positioning: null,
        calculatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      },
      quote: {
        operatorPrice: 28000,
        osaPrice: 37333, // ~25% margin
        margin: 0.25,
        validUntil: new Date(Date.now() + 120 * 60_000).toISOString(),
        aircraft: null,
        quotedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      },
    });

    const checks = checksAfterQuote(ctx);
    const marginCheck = checks.find((c) => c.name === "margin_policy");
    expect(marginCheck!.severity).toBe("warning");
    expect(marginCheck!.message).toContain("above maximum");
  });
});

// ── Learning Loop: Estimate Delta ────────────────────────────────────────

describe("calcEstimateDelta", () => {
  it("calculates positive delta (actual above estimate)", () => {
    const delta = calcEstimateDelta({ low: 25000, high: 35000 }, 33600);
    // median = 30000, delta = (33600 - 30000) / 30000 = 0.12
    expect(delta).toBeCloseTo(0.12, 2);
  });

  it("calculates negative delta (actual below estimate)", () => {
    const delta = calcEstimateDelta({ low: 25000, high: 35000 }, 27000);
    // median = 30000, delta = (27000 - 30000) / 30000 = -0.10
    expect(delta).toBeCloseTo(-0.10, 2);
  });

  it("returns 0 when actual equals estimate median", () => {
    const delta = calcEstimateDelta({ low: 25000, high: 35000 }, 30000);
    expect(delta).toBe(0);
  });

  it("handles zero median gracefully", () => {
    const delta = calcEstimateDelta({ low: 0, high: 0 }, 10000);
    expect(delta).toBe(0);
  });
});

// ── End-to-End: Estimate + Quote ─────────────────────────────────────────

describe("end-to-end: estimate then quote", () => {
  beforeEach(() => _resetDealCounter());

  it("full flow: estimate → budget check → quote → Guardian", async () => {
    // Step 1: Get estimate
    const apiResult: PriceEstimateResult = { low: 25000, high: 35000, flightTime: 150, fuelBurn: 280 };
    const client = new AviapagesClient({ apiKey: "test" }, mockFetch(apiResult));
    const ctx = makeDealCtx({ budgetMin: 25000, budgetMax: 40000 });

    const estResult = await estimate(ctx, { aviapages: client });
    updateDealContext(ctx, { estimate: estResult.estimate });

    // Step 2: Budget check
    const budgetIssue = checkBudgetFit(ctx.client.budget, ctx.estimate.priceRange);
    expect(budgetIssue).toBeNull(); // budget fits

    // Step 3: Guardian validates estimate
    const estChecks = checksAfterEstimate(ctx);
    const estFailed = estChecks.filter((c) => !c.passed && c.severity === "critical");
    expect(estFailed).toHaveLength(0);

    // Step 4: Real quote comes in
    const quoteResult = await quote(ctx, {
      operatorPrice: 28000,
      aircraft: { type: "Phenom 300E", tail: "N300TE", year: 2022 },
    });
    updateDealContext(ctx, { quote: quoteResult.quote });

    // Step 5: Guardian validates quote
    const quoteChecks = checksAfterQuote(ctx);
    const quoteFailed = quoteChecks.filter((c) => !c.passed && c.severity === "critical");
    expect(quoteFailed).toHaveLength(0);

    // Verify final state
    expect(ctx.estimate.priceRange!.low).toBe(25000);
    expect(ctx.estimate.priceRange!.high).toBe(35000);
    expect(ctx.quote.operatorPrice).toBe(28000);
    expect(ctx.quote.osaPrice).toBe(Math.round(28000 / 0.92));
    expect(ctx.quote.margin).toBe(0.08);

    // Learning loop delta
    const delta = calcEstimateDelta(ctx.estimate.priceRange!, ctx.quote.operatorPrice!);
    expect(delta).toBeCloseTo(-0.067, 2); // 28000 vs median 30000
  });

  it("budget mismatch stops pipeline before operator contact", async () => {
    const apiResult: PriceEstimateResult = { low: 50000, high: 70000, flightTime: 240, fuelBurn: 500 };
    const client = new AviapagesClient({ apiKey: "test" }, mockFetch(apiResult));
    const ctx = makeDealCtx({ budgetMin: 20000, budgetMax: 35000 });

    const estResult = await estimate(ctx, { aviapages: client });
    updateDealContext(ctx, { estimate: estResult.estimate });

    // Budget check fails
    const budgetIssue = checkBudgetFit(ctx.client.budget, ctx.estimate.priceRange);
    expect(budgetIssue).not.toBeNull();
    expect(budgetIssue).toContain("35,000");
    expect(budgetIssue).toContain("50,000");

    // Guardian also catches it
    const checks = checksAfterEstimate(ctx);
    const budgetCheck = checks.find((c) => c.name === "budget_overlap");
    expect(budgetCheck!.passed).toBe(false);
    expect(budgetCheck!.severity).toBe("critical");
  });
});
// === tests/token-metering.test.ts ===
/**
 * Tests for token metering utilities in safe-llm-call.ts.
 * Covers: estimateInputTokens, getDealTokenUsage, getTokenUsageLog,
 *         evictDealTokenBudget, _resetTokenTracking, log capping, budget warnings.
 */

import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach, vi } from "vitest";
import {
  estimateInputTokens,
  getDealTokenUsage,
  getTokenUsageLog,
  evictDealTokenBudget,
  _resetTokenTracking,
  type TokenUsageEntry,
} from "../src/utils/safe-llm-call.js";

/** Helper: build a TokenUsageEntry and push it via the internal recordTokenUsage
 *  path. Since recordTokenUsage is private, we use safeLlmCall — but that needs
 *  an Anthropic client. Instead, we exploit the fact that _resetTokenTracking
 *  clears the module-level arrays, and we directly test the exported readers.
 *
 *  For tests that need entries in the log/budget, we import the module internals
 *  by re-importing the module and accessing the non-exported function through
 *  a dynamic import workaround — OR we simply accept that some tests can only
 *  verify the "empty" baseline through the public API.
 *
 *  Strategy: We use vi.importActual + module-level access to call recordTokenUsage
 *  indirectly. The cleanest approach is to cast the module and call the private fn.
 */

// Access the private recordTokenUsage by importing the full module
// eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any
let mod: any;

beforeEach(async () => {
  _resetTokenTracking();
  // Dynamically import to get a reference that includes the private function
  mod = await import("../src/utils/safe-llm-call.js");
  mod._resetTokenTracking();
});

/** Helper to build a token usage entry. */
function makeEntry(overrides: Partial<TokenUsageEntry> = {}): TokenUsageEntry {
  return {
    capability: "intake",
    dealId: "DEAL-001",
    model: "claude-haiku-4-5-20251001",
    inputTokens: 100,
    outputTokens: 50,
    estimatedInputTokens: 90,
    timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
    ...overrides,
  };
}

/**
 * Since recordTokenUsage is not exported, we need a way to populate the internal
 * state. We access it through the module's internal scope by using a small trick:
 * we call the private function via the module namespace if available, otherwise
 * we test only the public API baseline behavior.
 *
 * For a cleaner approach, we add entries by directly manipulating the exported
 * functions that READ state (they can't write) — so we need the private fn.
 * We'll use Object.keys to find it or fall back to testing only public API.
 */

// The private recordTokenUsage is not on the module exports. We need another way.
// Let's use a workaround: import the module source and call it via eval — NO.
// Better: we test the public surface thoroughly. For budget/log tests that need
// populated state, we mock the module internals.

// Actually, the simplest approach: re-export recordTokenUsage from the module
// for testing, or accept we can test the public API thoroughly.
// Given constraints, let's test everything we can with the public API.

describe("estimateInputTokens", () => {
  it("returns correct estimate based on ~4 chars per token", () => {
    // 20 chars system + 20 chars user = 40 chars => 40/4 = 10 tokens
    const result = estimateInputTokens("a]".repeat(10), "b".repeat(20));
    expect(result).toBe(Math.ceil(40 / 4));
  });

  it("returns 0 for empty strings", () => {
    expect(estimateInputTokens("", "")).toBe(0);
  });

  it("handles long strings correctly", () => {
    const longSystem = "x".repeat(10_000);
    const longUser = "y".repeat(6_000);
    expect(estimateInputTokens(longSystem, longUser)).toBe(Math.ceil(16_000 / 4));
  });

  it("rounds up for non-divisible lengths", () => {
    // 5 chars total => ceil(5/4) = 2
    expect(estimateInputTokens("ab", "cde")).toBe(2);
  });

  it("handles single character input", () => {
    expect(estimateInputTokens("a", "")).toBe(1);
    expect(estimateInputTokens("", "b")).toBe(1);
  });
});

describe("getDealTokenUsage", () => {
  it("returns 0 for unknown deal", () => {
    expect(getDealTokenUsage("DEAL-NONEXISTENT")).toBe(0);
  });

  it("returns 0 after reset", () => {
    _resetTokenTracking();
    expect(getDealTokenUsage("DEAL-001")).toBe(0);
  });
});

describe("getTokenUsageLog", () => {
  it("returns empty array initially", () => {
    expect(getTokenUsageLog()).toEqual([]);
  });

  it("returns empty array after reset", () => {
    _resetTokenTracking();
    const log = getTokenUsageLog();
    expect(log).toEqual([]);
    expect(Array.isArray(log)).toBe(true);
  });

  it("returns a copy, not the internal array reference", () => {
    const log1 = getTokenUsageLog();
    const log2 = getTokenUsageLog();
    expect(log1).not.toBe(log2);
    expect(log1).toEqual(log2);
  });

  it("mutations on returned array do not affect internal state", () => {
    const log = getTokenUsageLog();
    log.push(makeEntry());
    expect(getTokenUsageLog()).toEqual([]);
  });
});

describe("evictDealTokenBudget", () => {
  it("does not throw for unknown deal", () => {
    expect(() => evictDealTokenBudget("DEAL-NONEXISTENT")).not.toThrow();
  });

  it("getDealTokenUsage returns 0 after eviction", () => {
    // Even without populating, eviction of unknown should still yield 0
    evictDealTokenBudget("DEAL-001");
    expect(getDealTokenUsage("DEAL-001")).toBe(0);
  });
});

describe("_resetTokenTracking", () => {
  it("clears all state without throwing", () => {
    expect(() => _resetTokenTracking()).not.toThrow();
    expect(getTokenUsageLog()).toEqual([]);
    expect(getDealTokenUsage("any-deal")).toBe(0);
  });

  it("can be called multiple times safely", () => {
    _resetTokenTracking();
    _resetTokenTracking();
    _resetTokenTracking();
    expect(getTokenUsageLog()).toEqual([]);
  });
});

describe("Token metering through safeLlmCall", () => {
  it("records token usage from a mocked Anthropic API call", async () => {
    const { safeLlmCall } = await import("../src/utils/safe-llm-call.js");
    const { z } = await import("zod");

    _resetTokenTracking();

    const mockClient = {
      messages: {
        create: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue({
          content: [{ type: "text", text: '{"result": "ok"}' }],
          usage: { input_tokens: 200, output_tokens: 80 },
        }),
      },
    };

    const schema = z.object({ result: z.string() });

    const result = await safeLlmCall({
      model: "claude-haiku-4-5-20251001",
      systemPrompt: "You are a test assistant.",
      userMessage: "Parse this.",
      schema,
      apiClient: mockClient as any,
      capability: "intake",
      dealId: "DEAL-TEST-001",
    });

    expect(result.ok).toBe(true);
    if (result.ok) {
      expect(result.usage).toBeDefined();
      expect(result.usage!.inputTokens).toBe(200);
      expect(result.usage!.outputTokens).toBe(80);
      expect(result.usage!.capability).toBe("intake");
      expect(result.usage!.dealId).toBe("DEAL-TEST-001");
    }

    // Verify log was populated
    const log = getTokenUsageLog();
    expect(log).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(log[0].inputTokens).toBe(200);
    expect(log[0].outputTokens).toBe(80);

    // Verify deal budget was updated
    expect(getDealTokenUsage("DEAL-TEST-001")).toBe(280);
  });

  it("accumulates token usage across multiple calls for same deal", async () => {
    const { safeLlmCall } = await import("../src/utils/safe-llm-call.js");
    const { z } = await import("zod");

    _resetTokenTracking();

    const mockClient = {
      messages: {
        create: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue({
          content: [{ type: "text", text: '{"v": 1}' }],
          usage: { input_tokens: 100, output_tokens: 50 },
        }),
      },
    };

    const schema = z.object({ v: z.number() });
    const opts = {
      model: "claude-haiku-4-5-20251001",
      systemPrompt: "sys",
      userMessage: "usr",
      schema,
      apiClient: mockClient as any,
      capability: "intake",
      dealId: "DEAL-MULTI",
    };

    await safeLlmCall(opts);
    await safeLlmCall(opts);
    await safeLlmCall(opts);

    expect(getDealTokenUsage("DEAL-MULTI")).toBe(450); // 3 * (100+50)
    expect(getTokenUsageLog()).toHaveLength(3);
  });

  it("fires console.warn when deal exceeds 50K token budget", async () => {
    const { safeLlmCall } = await import("../src/utils/safe-llm-call.js");
    const { z } = await import("zod");

    _resetTokenTracking();

    const warnSpy = vi.spyOn(console, "warn").mockImplementation(() => {});

    const mockClient = {
      messages: {
        create: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue({
          content: [{ type: "text", text: '{"ok": true}' }],
          usage: { input_tokens: 30_000, output_tokens: 25_000 },
        }),
      },
    };

    const schema = z.object({ ok: z.boolean() });

    await safeLlmCall({
      model: "claude-haiku-4-5-20251001",
      systemPrompt: "s",
      userMessage: "u",
      schema,
      apiClient: mockClient as any,
      capability: "estimate",
      dealId: "DEAL-OVER-BUDGET",
    });

    // 30000 + 25000 = 55000 > 50000
    expect(warnSpy).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect(warnSpy).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
      expect.stringContaining("DEAL-OVER-BUDGET"),
    );
    expect(warnSpy).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
      expect.stringContaining("exceeded"),
    );

    warnSpy.mockRestore();
  });

  it("evictDealTokenBudget resets deal usage after safeLlmCall", async () => {
    const { safeLlmCall } = await import("../src/utils/safe-llm-call.js");
    const { z } = await import("zod");

    _resetTokenTracking();

    const mockClient = {
      messages: {
        create: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue({
          content: [{ type: "text", text: '{"x": 1}' }],
          usage: { input_tokens: 500, output_tokens: 200 },
        }),
      },
    };

    await safeLlmCall({
      model: "claude-haiku-4-5-20251001",
      systemPrompt: "s",
      userMessage: "u",
      schema: z.object({ x: z.number() }),
      apiClient: mockClient as any,
      dealId: "DEAL-EVICT",
    });

    expect(getDealTokenUsage("DEAL-EVICT")).toBe(700);

    evictDealTokenBudget("DEAL-EVICT");
    expect(getDealTokenUsage("DEAL-EVICT")).toBe(0);
  });

  it("caps token log at MAX_TOKEN_LOG_ENTRIES (1000)", async () => {
    const { safeLlmCall } = await import("../src/utils/safe-llm-call.js");
    const { z } = await import("zod");

    _resetTokenTracking();

    const mockClient = {
      messages: {
        create: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue({
          content: [{ type: "text", text: '{"n": 1}' }],
          usage: { input_tokens: 10, output_tokens: 5 },
        }),
      },
    };

    const schema = z.object({ n: z.number() });
    const opts = {
      model: "claude-haiku-4-5-20251001",
      systemPrompt: "s",
      userMessage: "u",
      schema,
      apiClient: mockClient as any,
      capability: "intake",
      dealId: "DEAL-CAP",
    };

    // Make 1005 calls to exceed the 1000 cap
    for (let i = 0; i < 1005; i++) {
      await safeLlmCall(opts);
    }

    const log = getTokenUsageLog();
    expect(log.length).toBeLessThanOrEqual(1000);
    expect(log.length).toBe(1000);
  }, 30_000);

  it("records estimatedInputTokens in usage entry", async () => {
    const { safeLlmCall } = await import("../src/utils/safe-llm-call.js");
    const { z } = await import("zod");

    _resetTokenTracking();

    const systemPrompt = "You are helpful.";
    const userMessage = "Do something.";

    const mockClient = {
      messages: {
        create: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue({
          content: [{ type: "text", text: '{"done": true}' }],
          usage: { input_tokens: 50, output_tokens: 20 },
        }),
      },
    };

    const result = await safeLlmCall({
      model: "claude-haiku-4-5-20251001",
      systemPrompt,
      userMessage,
      schema: z.object({ done: z.boolean() }),
      apiClient: mockClient as any,
      dealId: "DEAL-EST",
    });

    expect(result.ok).toBe(true);
    if (result.ok) {
      const expected = estimateInputTokens(systemPrompt, userMessage);
      expect(result.usage!.estimatedInputTokens).toBe(expected);
    }
  });
});
// === tests/deal-context.test.ts ===
/**
 * Tests for Deal Context schema, manager, and stage transitions.
 * Build sequence step 1: verify the foundation before capabilities.
 */

import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach } from "vitest";
import {
  DealContextSchema,
  DealStage,
  type DealContext,
} from "../src/schemas/deal-context.js";
import {
  createDealContext,
  updateDealContext,
  snapshotContext,
  _resetDealCounter,
} from "../src/orchestrator/deal-context-manager.js";
import {
  advanceStage,
  isValidTransition,
  InvalidTransitionError,
} from "../src/orchestrator/stage-transitions.js";

describe("DealContext Schema", () => {
  beforeEach(() => _resetDealCounter());

  it("creates a valid deal context", () => {
    const ctx = createDealContext("sim");
    const result = DealContextSchema.safeParse(ctx);
    expect(result.success).toBe(true);
  });

  it("creates context with correct defaults", () => {
    const ctx = createDealContext("sim");
    expect(ctx.mode).toBe("sim");
    expect(ctx.stage).toBe("intake");
    expect(ctx.client.qualified).toBe(false);
    expect(ctx.guardian.currentStatus).toBe("clear");
    expect(ctx.guardian.passes).toHaveLength(0);
    expect(ctx.estimate.priceRange).toBeNull();
    expect(ctx.sourcing.selectedOperator).toBeNull();
    expect(ctx.quote.operatorPrice).toBeNull();
    expect(ctx.presentation.clientDecision).toBeNull();
    expect(ctx.outcome.result).toBeNull();
  });

  it("generates unique deal IDs with mode prefix", () => {
    const sim = createDealContext("sim");
    const live = createDealContext("live");
    expect(sim.id).toMatch(/^SIM_/);
    expect(live.id).toMatch(/^LIVE_/);
    expect(sim.id).not.toBe(live.id);
  });

  it("validates all DealStage values", () => {
    const stages = [
      "intake", "qualified", "estimated", "sourced",
      "quoted", "presented", "confirmed", "closed", "failed",
    ];
    for (const stage of stages) {
      expect(DealStage.safeParse(stage).success).toBe(true);
    }
    expect(DealStage.safeParse("invalid").success).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe("Deal Context Manager", () => {
  beforeEach(() => _resetDealCounter());

  it("updates context sections via shallow merge", () => {
    const ctx = createDealContext("sim");
    updateDealContext(ctx, {
      client: { name: "Test Client", contact: "test@example.com", qualified: true },
    });
    expect(ctx.client.name).toBe("Test Client");
    expect(ctx.client.type).toBe("new"); // unchanged
  });

  it("snapshot returns a deep clone", () => {
    const ctx = createDealContext("sim");
    const snap = snapshotContext(ctx);
    snap.client.name = "Modified";
    expect(ctx.client.name).toBe(""); // original unchanged
  });

  it("updates the updatedAt timestamp", () => {
    const ctx = createDealContext("sim");
    const before = ctx.updatedAt;
    // Small delay to ensure timestamp differs
    updateDealContext(ctx, { client: { name: "X" } });
    expect(ctx.updatedAt).toBeDefined();
  });
});

describe("Stage Transitions", () => {
  beforeEach(() => _resetDealCounter());

  it("allows the happy path", () => {
    const ctx = createDealContext("sim");
    advanceStage(ctx, "qualified");
    expect(ctx.stage).toBe("qualified");

    advanceStage(ctx, "estimated");
    expect(ctx.stage).toBe("estimated");

    advanceStage(ctx, "sourced");
    advanceStage(ctx, "quoted");
    advanceStage(ctx, "presented");
    advanceStage(ctx, "confirmed");
    advanceStage(ctx, "closed");

    expect(ctx.stage).toBe("closed");
    expect(ctx.stageHistory).toHaveLength(8); // intake + 7 transitions
  });

  it("allows any stage to transition to failed", () => {
    for (const stage of ["intake", "qualified", "estimated", "sourced", "quoted", "presented"] as const) {
      expect(isValidTransition(stage, "failed")).toBe(true);
    }
  });

  it("rejects invalid transitions", () => {
    const ctx = createDealContext("sim");
    expect(() => advanceStage(ctx, "closed")).toThrow(InvalidTransitionError);
    expect(() => advanceStage(ctx, "sourced")).toThrow(InvalidTransitionError);
  });

  it("tracks stage history with timestamps", () => {
    const ctx = createDealContext("sim");
    advanceStage(ctx, "qualified");

    expect(ctx.stageHistory).toHaveLength(2);
    expect(ctx.stageHistory[0]!.stage).toBe("intake");
    expect(ctx.stageHistory[0]!.duration).not.toBeNull(); // closed
    expect(ctx.stageHistory[1]!.stage).toBe("qualified");
    expect(ctx.stageHistory[1]!.duration).toBeNull(); // open
  });

  it("records duration on stage exit", () => {
    const ctx = createDealContext("sim");
    advanceStage(ctx, "qualified");
    const intakeEntry = ctx.stageHistory[0]!;
    expect(typeof intakeEntry.duration).toBe("number");
    expect(intakeEntry.duration!).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(0);
  });
});
// === tests/intake.test.ts ===
/**
 * Tests for Intake capability — request parsing, qualification, repeat detection.
 *
 * Test fixtures cover:
 * 1. Clean text request — all fields parseable
 * 2. Email format — multi-line with signature
 * 3. Ambiguous airport — city resolution
 * 4. Missing budget — qualified but budget null (pipeline pauses)
 * 5. Repeat client detection — from DealStore memory
 * 6. Edge cases — SMS shorthand, multiple formats, partial data
 *
 * No LLM calls — tests use code-layer parsing and mock LLM where needed.
 */

import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach } from "vitest";
import {
  parseRequestText,
  qualify,
  detectRepeatClient,
  resolveIcao,
  extractPax,
  extractBudget,
  extractDate,
  extractAircraftClass,
  extractRoute,
  extractClientName,
  extractContact,
  extractSpecialRequirements,
  isRoundTrip,
  intake,
} from "../src/capabilities/intake.js";
import type { ParsedRequest } from "../src/capabilities/intake.js";
import { createDealContext } from "../src/orchestrator/deal-context-manager.js";
import { _resetDealCounter } from "../src/orchestrator/deal-context-manager.js";
import { DealStore } from "../src/memory/deal-store.js";
import type { DealRecord } from "../src/schemas/deal-record.js";
import { createTestDb } from "../src/memory/database.js";

// ── Test Fixtures ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

const TODAY = new Date("2026-03-31T12:00:00Z");

const FIXTURES = {
  /** Clean text: all critical fields present and unambiguous. */
  cleanText: "I need a light jet from Teterboro to Palm Beach on April 15 for 4 passengers. Budget around $30,000.",

  /** Email format with signature, multi-line. */
  emailFormat: `Hi there,

I'd like to charter a flight from Chicago to Aspen on May 1st for 6 people.
We'll have ski gear. Looking to spend $25k-$35k.

Best,
Sarah Johnson
sarah@johnson.com`,

  /** Ambiguous airport — "NY" and "South Florida" need resolution. */
  ambiguousAirport: "Need to fly from NY to South Florida on April 20, 3 passengers",

  /** Missing budget — should qualify but flag budget as missing. */
  missingBudget: "Charter from KTEB to KPBI on April 10 for 5 passengers",

  /** Has departure but not arrival. */
  partialRoute: "I need to fly somewhere warm from Teterboro next Friday for 2",

  /** SMS shorthand. */
  smsShorthand: "4 from chicago to miami fri round trip",

  /** Vague / insufficient — should fail qualification. */
  vagueRequest: "What would it cost to fly somewhere nice?",

  /** Complex with pets and special requirements. */
  complexRequest: "Round trip from Van Nuys to Cabo for 6 passengers on June 1st, returning June 5th. We're bringing 2 dogs and golf clubs. Heavy jet preferred. Budget up to $80,000.",

  /** Repeat client referencing prior trip. */
  repeatClientRequest: "Hey it's John Smith again, same route as before but on May 10 for 4 passengers. Budget $30k.",
};

// ── Airport Resolution ───────────────────────────────────────────────────

describe("resolveIcao", () => {
  it("passes through valid ICAO codes", () => {
    expect(resolveIcao("KTEB")).toBe("KTEB");
    expect(resolveIcao("KPBI")).toBe("KPBI");
    expect(resolveIcao("MMSD")).toBe("MMSD");
  });

  it("resolves common city names", () => {
    expect(resolveIcao("Teterboro")).toBe("KTEB");
    expect(resolveIcao("Palm Beach")).toBe("KPBI");
    expect(resolveIcao("Aspen")).toBe("KASE");
    expect(resolveIcao("Chicago")).toBe("KORD");
  });

  it("resolves metro area shorthand", () => {
    expect(resolveIcao("NY")).toBe("KTEB");
    expect(resolveIcao("NYC")).toBe("KTEB");
    expect(resolveIcao("New York")).toBe("KTEB");
    expect(resolveIcao("LA")).toBe("KVNY");
    expect(resolveIcao("South Florida")).toBe("KPBI");
  });

  it("handles 'airport' and 'international' suffixes", () => {
    expect(resolveIcao("Miami International Airport")).toBe("KMIA");
    expect(resolveIcao("Las Vegas Airport")).toBe("KLAS");
  });

  it("returns null for unresolvable input", () => {
    expect(resolveIcao("")).toBeNull();
    expect(resolveIcao("Atlantis")).toBeNull();
  });

  it("resolves international destinations", () => {
    expect(resolveIcao("Cabo")).toBe("MMSD");
    expect(resolveIcao("Nassau")).toBe("MYNN");
    expect(resolveIcao("Bermuda")).toBe("TXKF");
  });
});

// ── Passenger Extraction ─────────────────────────────────────────────────

describe("extractPax", () => {
  it("extracts 'N passengers'", () => {
    expect(extractPax("for 4 passengers")).toBe(4);
    expect(extractPax("6 people")).toBe(6);
    expect(extractPax("3 pax")).toBe(3);
  });

  it("extracts 'party of N'", () => {
    expect(extractPax("party of 8")).toBe(8);
  });

  it("handles couples", () => {
    expect(extractPax("2 couples")).toBe(4);
  });

  it("handles 'me and my wife'", () => {
    expect(extractPax("me and my wife")).toBe(2);
    expect(extractPax("me and my wife plus 3 kids")).toBe(5);
  });

  it("returns null when no pax mentioned", () => {
    expect(extractPax("fly from NY to LA")).toBeNull();
  });
});

// ── Budget Extraction ────────────────────────────────────────────────────

describe("extractBudget", () => {
  it("extracts dollar range '$25k-$35k'", () => {
    const budget = extractBudget("Looking to spend $25k-$35k");
    expect(budget).not.toBeNull();
    expect(budget!.min).toBe(25000);
    expect(budget!.max).toBe(35000);
  });

  it("extracts 'around $30,000'", () => {
    const budget = extractBudget("Budget around $30,000");
    expect(budget).not.toBeNull();
    expect(budget!.min).toBe(25500); // 30000 * 0.85
    expect(budget!.max).toBe(34500); // 30000 * 1.15
  });

  it("extracts 'up to $80,000'", () => {
    const budget = extractBudget("Budget up to $80,000");
    expect(budget).not.toBeNull();
    expect(budget!.min).toBe(0);
    expect(budget!.max).toBe(80000);
  });

  it("returns null when no budget mentioned", () => {
    expect(extractBudget("fly from NY to LA")).toBeNull();
  });

  it("handles comma-separated amounts", () => {
    const budget = extractBudget("budget of $25,000");
    expect(budget).not.toBeNull();
    expect(budget!.min).toBeGreaterThan(20000);
    expect(budget!.max).toBeLessThan(30000);
  });
});

// ── Date Extraction ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────

describe("extractDate", () => {
  it("extracts ISO date", () => {
    expect(extractDate("flight on 2026-04-15")).toBe("2026-04-15");
  });

  it("extracts 'Month DD' format", () => {
    expect(extractDate("on April 15", TODAY)).toBe("2026-04-15");
    expect(extractDate("May 1st trip", TODAY)).toBe("2026-05-01");
  });

  it("extracts 'MM/DD' format", () => {
    expect(extractDate("depart 4/15", TODAY)).toBe("2026-04-15");
    expect(extractDate("on 04/15/2026")).toBe("2026-04-15");
  });

  it("handles 'tomorrow'", () => {
    const result = extractDate("need a flight tomorrow", TODAY);
    expect(result).toBe("2026-04-01");
  });

  it("handles 'next Friday'", () => {
    const result = extractDate("next Friday", TODAY);
    // March 31, 2026 is a Tuesday, so "next Friday" = April 10 (next week's Friday)
    expect(result).toBeDefined();
    // Parse as local date components to avoid UTC timezone shift
    const [y, m, day] = result!.split("-").map(Number);
    const d = new Date(y!, m! - 1, day);
    expect(d.getDay()).toBe(5); // Friday
  });

  it("returns null when no date found", () => {
    expect(extractDate("fly from NY to LA")).toBeNull();
  });
});

// ── Aircraft Class Extraction ────────────────────────────────────────────

describe("extractAircraftClass", () => {
  it("extracts explicit class mentions", () => {
    expect(extractAircraftClass("light jet")).toBe("light");
    expect(extractAircraftClass("midsize cabin")).toBe("midsize");
    expect(extractAircraftClass("super mid")).toBe("super_mid");
    expect(extractAircraftClass("heavy jet")).toBe("heavy");
  });

  it("infers from specific aircraft types", () => {
    expect(extractAircraftClass("Phenom 300")).toBe("light");
    expect(extractAircraftClass("G550")).toBe("heavy");
    expect(extractAircraftClass("Challenger 300")).toBe("super_mid");
  });

  it("infers from comfort hints", () => {
    expect(extractAircraftClass("need a spacious cabin")).toBe("heavy");
  });

  it("returns null when no class mentioned", () => {
    expect(extractAircraftClass("fly from NY to LA")).toBeNull();
  });
});

// ── Route Extraction ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────

describe("extractRoute", () => {
  it("extracts 'from X to Y'", () => {
    const [dep, arr] = extractRoute("from Teterboro to Palm Beach on April 15");
    expect(dep).toBe("KTEB");
    expect(arr).toBe("KPBI");
  });

  it("extracts 'X to Y' without 'from'", () => {
    const [dep, arr] = extractRoute("Chicago to Aspen on May 1");
    expect(dep).toBe("KORD");
    expect(arr).toBe("KASE");
  });

  it("extracts ICAO codes directly", () => {
    const [dep, arr] = extractRoute("KTEB to KPBI on April 10");
    expect(dep).toBe("KTEB");
    expect(arr).toBe("KPBI");
  });

  it("handles metro area names", () => {
    const [dep, arr] = extractRoute("NY to South Florida next week");
    expect(dep).toBe("KTEB");
    expect(arr).toBe("KPBI");
  });

  it("returns nulls when route not parseable", () => {
    const [dep, arr] = extractRoute("I want to fly somewhere");
    expect(dep).toBeNull();
    expect(arr).toBeNull();
  });
});

  it("returns null when no name found", () => {

// ── Special Requirements ─────────────────────────────────────────────────

describe("extractSpecialRequirements", () => {

    expect(extractSpecialRequirements("bringing 2 dogs")).toContain("pets");
  });

  it("extracts ski gear", () => {
    expect(extractSpecialRequirements("have ski gear")).toContain("ski_gear");
  });

  it("extracts multiple requirements", () => {

    expect(reqs).toContain("golf_clubs");

    expect(isRoundTrip("return flight to Miami")).toBe(true);

    expect(isRoundTrip("from NY to LA")).toBe(false);
  });
});

// ── Full Code-Layer Parser ───────────────────────────────────────────────

describe("parseRequestText", () => {
  it("parses clean text request with all fields", () => {
    const parsed = parseRequestText(FIXTURES.cleanText, TODAY);

    expect(parsed.departure).toBe("KTEB");
    expect(parsed.arrival).toBe("KPBI");
    expect(parsed.date).toBe("2026-04-15");
    expect(parsed.pax).toBe(4);
    expect(parsed.aircraftClass).toBe("light");
    expect(parsed.budget).not.toBeNull();
    expect(parsed.budget!.min).toBeGreaterThan(20000);
    expect(parsed.budget!.max).toBeLessThan(40000);
    expect(parsed.isRoundTrip).toBe(false);
  });

  it("parses email format with signature and contact", () => {
    const parsed = parseRequestText(FIXTURES.emailFormat, TODAY);

    expect(parsed.departure).toBe("KORD");  // Chicago
    expect(parsed.arrival).toBe("KASE");    // Aspen
    expect(parsed.date).toBe("2026-05-01");
    expect(parsed.pax).toBe(6);
    expect(parsed.specialRequirements).toContain("ski_gear");
    expect(parsed.clientName).toBe("Sarah Johnson");
    expect(parsed.clientContact).toBe("sarah@johnson.com");
    expect(parsed.budget).not.toBeNull();
    expect(parsed.budget!.min).toBe(25000);
    expect(parsed.budget!.max).toBe(35000);
  });

  it("resolves ambiguous airports", () => {
    const parsed = parseRequestText(FIXTURES.ambiguousAirport, TODAY);

    expect(parsed.departure).toBe("KTEB");  // "NY" → Teterboro
    expect(parsed.arrival).toBe("KPBI");    // "South Florida" → Palm Beach
  });

  it("parses request with missing budget", () => {
    const parsed = parseRequestText(FIXTURES.missingBudget, TODAY);

    expect(parsed.departure).toBe("KTEB");
    expect(parsed.arrival).toBe("KPBI");
    expect(parsed.pax).toBe(5);
    expect(parsed.budget).toBeNull();
  });

  it("parses complex request with special requirements", () => {
    const parsed = parseRequestText(FIXTURES.complexRequest, TODAY);

    expect(parsed.departure).toBe("KVNY");  // Van Nuys
    expect(parsed.arrival).toBe("MMSD");    // Cabo
    expect(parsed.pax).toBe(6);
    expect(parsed.isRoundTrip).toBe(true);
    expect(parsed.specialRequirements).toContain("pets");
    expect(parsed.specialRequirements).toContain("golf_clubs");
    expect(parsed.aircraftClass).toBe("heavy");
    expect(parsed.budget).not.toBeNull();
    expect(parsed.budget!.max).toBe(80000);
  });

  it("parses SMS shorthand", () => {
    const parsed = parseRequestText(FIXTURES.smsShorthand, TODAY);

    expect(parsed.departure).toBe("KORD");  // chicago
    expect(parsed.arrival).toBe("KMIA");    // miami
    expect(parsed.isRoundTrip).toBe(true);
  });
});

// ── Qualification Logic ──────────────────────────────────────────────────

describe("qualify", () => {
  it("qualifies fully complete request", () => {
    const parsed = parseRequestText(FIXTURES.cleanText, TODAY);
    const result = qualify(parsed);
    expect(result.qualified).toBe(true);
    expect(result.budgetMissing).toBe(false);
    expect(result.reason).toContain("Fully qualified");
  });

  it("qualifies request missing budget but flags it", () => {
    const parsed = parseRequestText(FIXTURES.missingBudget, TODAY);
    const result = qualify(parsed);
    expect(result.qualified).toBe(true);
    expect(result.budgetMissing).toBe(true);
    expect(result.reason).toContain("Awaiting budget");
  });

  it("rejects when no route found", () => {
    const parsed = parseRequestText(FIXTURES.vagueRequest, TODAY);
    const result = qualify(parsed);
    expect(result.qualified).toBe(false);
    expect(result.reason).toContain("No valid route");
  });

  it("rejects when no date", () => {
    const parsed: ParsedRequest = {
      departure: "KTEB", arrival: "KPBI", date: null, pax: 4,
      aircraftClass: null, budget: null, isRoundTrip: false,
      specialRequirements: [], clientName: null, clientContact: null,
    };
    const result = qualify(parsed);
    expect(result.qualified).toBe(false);
    expect(result.reason).toContain("date");
  });

  it("rejects when no pax", () => {
    const parsed: ParsedRequest = {
      departure: "KTEB", arrival: "KPBI", date: "2026-04-15", pax: null,
      aircraftClass: null, budget: null, isRoundTrip: false,
      specialRequirements: [], clientName: null, clientContact: null,
    };
    const result = qualify(parsed);
    expect(result.qualified).toBe(false);
    expect(result.reason).toContain("passenger");
  });

  it("qualifies with only departure (one end of route sufficient)", () => {
    const parsed: ParsedRequest = {
      departure: "KTEB", arrival: null, date: "2026-04-15", pax: 4,
      aircraftClass: null, budget: { min: 20000, max: 40000 }, isRoundTrip: false,
      specialRequirements: [], clientName: null, clientContact: null,
    };
    const result = qualify(parsed);
    expect(result.qualified).toBe(true);
  });
});

// ── Repeat Client Detection ──────────────────────────────────────────────

describe("detectRepeatClient", () => {
  function makeRecord(overrides: Partial<DealRecord> = {}): DealRecord {
    return {
      id: "deal_001",
      timestamp: "2026-03-15T10:00:00.000Z",
      mode: "live",
      route: { departure: "KTEB", arrival: "KPBI", distance: 950 },
      aviapagesEstimate: { low: 25000, high: 35000 },
      operatorQuote: 28000,
      osaPrice: 30400,
      estimateDelta: -0.07,
      operatorName: "Elite Aviation",
      operatorBase: "KTEB",
      operatorResponseTime: 3600,
      operatorConversion: true,
      aircraftType: "Phenom 300E",
      aircraftClass: "light",
      clientName: "John Smith",
      clientContact: "john@smith.com",
      clientType: "new",
      clientBudget: { min: 25000, max: 35000 },
      clientConversion: true,
      decisionTime: 1800,
      result: "closed",
      revenue: 2400,
      margin: 0.08,
      totalDealTime: 7200,
      dayOfWeek: 2,
      season: "shoulder",
      guardianFlags: [],
      ...overrides,
    };
  }

  function makeStoreWithRecords(records: DealRecord[]): { store: DealStore; cleanup: () => void } {
    const db = createTestDb();
    const store = new DealStore(db);
    for (const r of records) store.add(r);
    return { store, cleanup: () => db.close() };
  }

  it("detects repeat client by name", () => {
    const { store, cleanup } = makeStoreWithRecords([makeRecord()]);

    const parsed: ParsedRequest = {
      departure: "KTEB", arrival: "KPBI", date: "2026-05-10", pax: 4,
      aircraftClass: null, budget: null, isRoundTrip: false,
      specialRequirements: [], clientName: "John Smith", clientContact: null,
    };

    const result = detectRepeatClient(parsed, store);
    expect(result.isRepeat).toBe(true);
    expect(result.previousDealCount).toBe(1);
    expect(result.lastAircraftClass).toBe("light");
    expect(result.lastBudget).toEqual({ min: 25000, max: 35000 });

    cleanup();
  });

  it("detects repeat client by email", () => {
    const { store, cleanup } = makeStoreWithRecords([makeRecord()]);

    const parsed: ParsedRequest = {
      departure: "KTEB", arrival: "KPBI", date: "2026-05-10", pax: 4,
      aircraftClass: null, budget: null, isRoundTrip: false,
      specialRequirements: [], clientName: null, clientContact: "john@smith.com",
    };

    const result = detectRepeatClient(parsed, store);
    expect(result.isRepeat).toBe(true);

    cleanup();
  });

  it("returns not-repeat for unknown client", () => {
    const { store, cleanup } = makeStoreWithRecords([makeRecord()]);

    const parsed: ParsedRequest = {
      departure: "KTEB", arrival: "KPBI", date: "2026-05-10", pax: 4,
      aircraftClass: null, budget: null, isRoundTrip: false,
      specialRequirements: [], clientName: "Jane Doe", clientContact: null,
    };

    const result = detectRepeatClient(parsed, store);
    expect(result.isRepeat).toBe(false);
    expect(result.previousDealCount).toBe(0);

    cleanup();
  });

  it("returns not-repeat when no deal store", () => {
    const parsed: ParsedRequest = {
      departure: "KTEB", arrival: "KPBI", date: "2026-05-10", pax: 4,
      aircraftClass: null, budget: null, isRoundTrip: false,
      specialRequirements: [], clientName: "John Smith", clientContact: null,
    };

    const result = detectRepeatClient(parsed, null);
    expect(result.isRepeat).toBe(false);
  });

  it("returns most recent deal data for repeat client", () => {
    const records = [
      makeRecord({ id: "deal_001", timestamp: "2026-02-01T10:00:00.000Z", aircraftClass: "light", clientBudget: { min: 20000, max: 30000 } }),
      makeRecord({ id: "deal_002", timestamp: "2026-03-15T10:00:00.000Z", aircraftClass: "midsize", clientBudget: { min: 30000, max: 45000 } }),
    ];
    const { store, cleanup } = makeStoreWithRecords(records);

    const parsed: ParsedRequest = {
      departure: "KTEB", arrival: "KPBI", date: "2026-05-10", pax: 4,
      aircraftClass: null, budget: null, isRoundTrip: false,
      specialRequirements: [], clientName: "John Smith", clientContact: null,
    };

    const result = detectRepeatClient(parsed, store);
    expect(result.isRepeat).toBe(true);
    expect(result.previousDealCount).toBe(2);
    expect(result.lastAircraftClass).toBe("midsize"); // from the more recent deal
    expect(result.lastBudget).toEqual({ min: 30000, max: 45000 });

    cleanup();
  });
});

// ── Full Intake Capability ───────────────────────────────────────────────

describe("intake (full capability)", () => {
  beforeEach(() => _resetDealCounter());

  it("parses clean text request and qualifies", async () => {
    const ctx = createDealContext("sim");
    const result = await intake(FIXTURES.cleanText, ctx, { today: TODAY });

    expect(result.client.qualified).toBe(true);
    expect(result.client.type).toBe("new");
    expect(result.trip.departure).toBe("KTEB");
    expect(result.trip.arrival).toBe("KPBI");
    expect(result.trip.date).toBe("2026-04-15");
    expect(result.trip.pax).toBe(4);
    expect(result.trip.aircraftClass).toBe("light");
    expect(result.client.budget).not.toBeNull();
  });

  it("parses email format with client info", async () => {
    const ctx = createDealContext("sim");
    const result = await intake(FIXTURES.emailFormat, ctx, { today: TODAY });

    expect(result.client.qualified).toBe(true);
    expect(result.client.name).toBe("Sarah Johnson");
    expect(result.client.contact).toBe("sarah@johnson.com");
    expect(result.trip.departure).toBe("KORD");
    expect(result.trip.arrival).toBe("KASE");
    expect(result.trip.specialRequirements).toContain("ski_gear");
  });

  it("resolves ambiguous airports", async () => {
    const ctx = createDealContext("sim");
    const result = await intake(FIXTURES.ambiguousAirport, ctx, { today: TODAY });

    expect(result.trip.departure).toBe("KTEB");  // NY → Teterboro
    expect(result.trip.arrival).toBe("KPBI");    // South Florida → Palm Beach
  });

  it("qualifies but leaves budget null when not provided", async () => {
    const ctx = createDealContext("sim");
    const result = await intake(FIXTURES.missingBudget, ctx, { today: TODAY });

    expect(result.client.qualified).toBe(true);
    expect(result.client.budget).toBeNull();
    expect(result.client.qualificationReason).toContain("Awaiting budget");
  });

  it("detects repeat client and pre-populates from memory", async () => {
    const db = createTestDb();
    const store = new DealStore(db);

    const record: DealRecord = {
      id: "deal_001",
      timestamp: "2026-03-15T10:00:00.000Z",
      mode: "live",
      route: { departure: "KTEB", arrival: "KPBI", distance: 950 },
      aviapagesEstimate: { low: 25000, high: 35000 },
      operatorQuote: 28000,
      osaPrice: 30400,
      estimateDelta: -0.07,
      operatorName: "Elite Aviation",
      operatorBase: "KTEB",
      operatorResponseTime: 3600,
      operatorConversion: true,
      aircraftType: "Phenom 300E",
      aircraftClass: "light",
      clientName: "John Smith",
      clientContact: "john@smith.com",
      clientType: "new",
      clientBudget: { min: 25000, max: 35000 },
      clientConversion: true,
      decisionTime: 1800,
      result: "closed",
      revenue: 2400,
      margin: 0.08,
      totalDealTime: 7200,
      dayOfWeek: 2,
      season: "shoulder",
      guardianFlags: [],
    };
    store.add(record);

    const ctx = createDealContext("sim");
    const result = await intake(FIXTURES.repeatClientRequest, ctx, {
      today: TODAY,
      dealStore: store,
    });

    expect(result.client.type).toBe("repeat");
    expect(result.client.name).toBe("John Smith");
    // Budget comes from the request text ($30k)
    expect(result.client.budget).not.toBeNull();

    db.close();
  });

  it("fails qualification for vague request", async () => {
    const ctx = createDealContext("sim");
    const result = await intake(FIXTURES.vagueRequest, ctx, { today: TODAY });

    expect(result.client.qualified).toBe(false);
    expect(result.client.qualificationReason).toContain("No valid route");
  });

  it("uses LLM fallback when code-layer misses critical fields", async () => {
    const ctx = createDealContext("sim");

    // Mock LLM that fills in what code-layer missed
    const mockLlm = async (_raw: string) => ({
      departure_icao: "KTEB",
      arrival_icao: "KPBI",
      departure_city: "New York",
      arrival_city: "Palm Beach",
      date: "2026-04-15",
      pax: 3,
      aircraft_class: null as "light" | "midsize" | "super_mid" | "heavy" | "ultra_long_range" | null,
      budget_min: null,
      budget_max: null,
      is_round_trip: false,
      special_requirements: [] as string[],
      client_name: null,
      client_contact: null,
    });

    // Use a hard-to-parse request that the code layer will partially fail on
    const result = await intake(
      "I'd like to explore options for getting to Florida from the northeast mid-April, there'll be 3 of us",
      ctx,
      { today: TODAY, llmParser: mockLlm },
    );

    // LLM should have filled in departure/arrival/date/pax
    expect(result.trip.departure).toBe("KTEB");
    expect(result.trip.arrival).toBe("KPBI");
    expect(result.trip.pax).toBe(3);
  });

  it("handles complex request with all special requirements", async () => {
    const ctx = createDealContext("sim");
    const result = await intake(FIXTURES.complexRequest, ctx, { today: TODAY });

    expect(result.trip.departure).toBe("KVNY");
    expect(result.trip.arrival).toBe("MMSD");
    expect(result.trip.isRoundTrip).toBe(true);
    expect(result.trip.specialRequirements).toContain("pets");
    expect(result.trip.specialRequirements).toContain("golf_clubs");
    expect(result.trip.aircraftClass).toBe("heavy");
    expect(result.client.budget!.max).toBe(80000);
  });
});

// ── Fixture helpers ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────

function tmpStore(): { db: import("better-sqlite3").Database; dealStore: DealStore; msgStore: MessageStore; callStore: CallStore; autoStore: AutonomyStore; cleanup: () => void } {
  const db = createTestDb();
  return {
    db,
    dealStore: new DealStore(db),
    msgStore: new MessageStore(db),
    callStore: new CallStore(db),
    autoStore: new AutonomyStore(db),
    cleanup: () => db.close(),
  };
}

const mockFetch = async () => ({ ok: true, status: 200, json: async () => ({ id: "mock_001", message_id: "mock_001", sid: "mock_sid" }) });

function makeDealAtStage(stage: string, overrides?: Partial<Pick<DealContext, "client" | "trip" | "estimate" | "sourcing" | "quote" | "presentation">>): DealContext {
  const deal = createDealContext("live");
  updateDealContext(deal, {
    client: { name: "Sarah Chen", contact: "sarah@chen.com", type: "new", budget: { min: 25000, max: 40000 }, substitutionPref: null, qualified: true, qualificationReason: "test" },
    trip: { departure: "KTEB", arrival: "KPBI", date: "2026-04-15", pax: 4, aircraftClass: "light", aircraftType: null, isRoundTrip: false, specialRequirements: [] },
    estimate: { source: "aviapages", flightTime: 150, priceRange: { low: 25000, high: 35000 }, fuelBurn: 280, positioning: null, calculatedAt: new Date().toISOString() },
    sourcing: { selectedOperator: { operatorId: "op_001", operatorName: "Elite Aviation", phone: "op@elite.com", baseAirport: "KTEB", aircraftType: "Phenom 300E", aircraftTail: "N300EA", score: 0.85, scoreBreakdown: { proximity: 0.9, laneHistory: 0.5, responseTime: 0.5, pricingAccuracy: 0.5, relationship: 0.5 }, distanceToDeparture: 0 }, backupOperator: null, rankingReason: "test", laneHistory: [] },
    quote: { operatorPrice: 28000, osaPrice: 30435, margin: 0.08, validUntil: new Date(Date.now() + 7200000).toISOString(), aircraft: { type: "Phenom 300E", tail: "N300EA", year: 2022 }, quotedAt: new Date().toISOString() },
    presentation: { sentAt: new Date().toISOString(), viewedAt: null, clientDecision: "pending", decisionAt: null, timerStarted: new Date().toISOString() },
    ...overrides,
  });
  const path: Record<string, string[]> = {
    qualified: ["qualified"], estimated: ["qualified", "estimated"],
    sourced: ["qualified", "estimated", "sourced"],
    quoted: ["qualified", "estimated", "sourced", "quoted"],
    presented: ["qualified", "estimated", "sourced", "quoted", "presented"],
  };
  for (const s of path[stage] ?? []) advanceStage(deal, s as DealContext["stage"]);
  return deal;
}

function makeActiveDeal(deal: DealContext): ActiveDeal {
  return { deal, conversation: createConversation(deal.id) };
}

// ── Test a: Email inbound → full pipeline → closed ───────────────────────

describe("email inbound → new deal → full pipeline → closed", () => {
  it("processes complete deal lifecycle through email channel", async () => {
    const resend = new ResendClient({ apiKey: "test" }, mockFetch);
    const emailAdapter = new EmailAdapter(resend);
    const { dealStore, msgStore, cleanup: cleanupStores } = tmpStore();

    // 1. Inbound email arrives
    const webhook: ResendInboundWebhook = {
      type: "email.received",
      data: {
        id: "resend_in_001", from: "sarah@chen.com", from_name: "Sarah Chen",
        to: ["charter@osa.aero"], subject: "Charter request",
        text: "I need a flight from Teterboro to Palm Beach on April 15 for 4 passengers. Budget $30,000.",
        created_at: new Date().toISOString(),
      },
    };
    const inboundMsg = emailAdapter.receive(webhook);
    expect(inboundMsg.channel).toBe("email");
    expect(inboundMsg.from).toBe("sarah@chen.com");

    // 2. Router creates new deal (set client contact for thread matching)
    const routeResult = await routeMessage(inboundMsg, [], {
      createDeal: async () => {
        const d = createDealContext("live");
        updateDealContext(d, { client: { name: "Sarah Chen", contact: "sarah@chen.com", type: "new", budget: null, substitutionPref: null, qualified: true, qualificationReason: "test" } });
        return d;
      },
    });
    expect(routeResult.isNewDeal).toBe(true);
    msgStore.saveMessage(inboundMsg);

    // 3. Respond drafts operator RFQ
    const rfqDraft = await respond(routeResult.deal, inboundMsg, "quote_request");
    expect(rfqDraft.draft.draftedBy).toBe("dumont");
    msgStore.saveMessage(rfqDraft.draft);

    // 4. Confidence gate holds (no autonomy store → delayed or hold)
    const gate1 = evaluateConfidence(rfqDraft.draft, routeResult.deal, null);
    expect(["hold", "delayed"]).toContain(gate1.action);

    // 5. Operator replies with price
    const opReply: ResendInboundWebhook = {
      type: "email.received",
      data: {
        id: "resend_in_002", from: "op@elite.com", to: ["charter@osa.aero"],
        subject: "Re: Charter request", text: "We can do $28,000 all-in for the Phenom 300E N300EA.",
        headers: { "In-Reply-To": rfqDraft.draft.id },
        created_at: new Date().toISOString(),
      },
    };
    const opMsg = emailAdapter.receive(opReply);
    const ad = makeActiveDeal(routeResult.deal);
    addMessageToConversation(ad.conversation, inboundMsg);
    addMessageToConversation(ad.conversation, rfqDraft.draft);

    // Simulate deal advanced to sourced so operator routing works
    updateDealContext(routeResult.deal, { sourcing: { selectedOperator: { operatorId: "op_001", operatorName: "Elite Aviation", phone: "op@elite.com", baseAirport: "KTEB", aircraftType: "Phenom 300E", aircraftTail: "N300EA", score: 0.85, scoreBreakdown: { proximity: 0.9, laneHistory: 0.5, responseTime: 0.5, pricingAccuracy: 0.5, relationship: 0.5 }, distanceToDeparture: 0 }, backupOperator: null, rankingReason: "test", laneHistory: [] } });
    advanceStage(routeResult.deal, "qualified");
    advanceStage(routeResult.deal, "estimated");
    advanceStage(routeResult.deal, "sourced");

    const routeResult2 = await routeMessage(opMsg, [ad], { createDeal: async () => createDealContext("live") });
    expect(routeResult2.intent).toBe("quote_response");
    expect(routeResult2.action.type).toBe("advance_to_quoted");
    msgStore.saveMessage(opMsg);

    // 6. Client confirms
    advanceStage(routeResult.deal, "quoted");
    advanceStage(routeResult.deal, "presented");
    routeResult.deal.presentation.sentAt = new Date().toISOString();
    routeResult.deal.presentation.clientDecision = "pending";

    const confirmMsg: ResendInboundWebhook = {
      type: "email.received",
      data: {
        id: "resend_in_003", from: "sarah@chen.com", to: ["charter@osa.aero"],
        subject: "Re: Your Charter Quote", text: "Sounds great, let's do it!",
        created_at: new Date().toISOString(),
      },
    };
    const clientConfirm = emailAdapter.receive(confirmMsg);
    const routeResult3 = await routeMessage(clientConfirm, [ad], { createDeal: async () => createDealContext("live") });
    expect(routeResult3.intent).toBe("confirmation");
    expect(routeResult3.action.type).toBe("close_deal");

    // 7. Close deal → learning loop
    updateDealContext(routeResult.deal, { quote: { operatorPrice: 28000, osaPrice: 30435, margin: 0.08, validUntil: new Date(Date.now() + 7200000).toISOString(), aircraft: { type: "Phenom 300E", tail: "N300EA", year: 2022 }, quotedAt: new Date().toISOString() } });
