    closeDeal(routeResult.deal, { result: "closed", dealStore, operatorResponseTime: 1800 });

    // 8. Verify final state
    expect(routeResult.deal.stage).toBe("closed");
    expect(dealStore.count).toBe(1);
    expect(msgStore.count).toBe(3);

    cleanupStores();
  });
});

// ── Test b: Cross-channel threading ──────────────────────────────────────

describe("web form → email → SMS cross-channel threading", () => {
  it("maintains single deal across web form and SMS channels", async () => {
    const webAdapter = new WebFormAdapter();

    // 1. Web form arrives
    const formMsg = webAdapter.receive({
      name: "Sarah Chen", email: "sarah@chen.com", phone: "+1-312-555-0199",
      departure: "Teterboro", arrival: "Palm Beach", date: "2026-04-15", pax: 4,
    });
    expect(formMsg.channel).toBe("web_form");

    const routeResult = await routeMessage(formMsg, [], { createDeal: async () => {
      const deal = createDealContext("live");
      updateDealContext(deal, { client: { name: "Sarah Chen", contact: "+1-312-555-0199", type: "new", budget: null, substitutionPref: null, qualified: true, qualificationReason: "test" }, trip: { departure: "KTEB", arrival: "KPBI", date: "2026-04-15", pax: 4, aircraftClass: null, aircraftType: null, isRoundTrip: false, specialRequirements: [] } });
      return deal;
    } });
    expect(routeResult.isNewDeal).toBe(true);
    expect(routeResult.deal.client.budget).toBeNull();

    // 2. Client texts budget from same phone (manually constructed message)
    const smsMsg: Message = {
      id: generateMessageId(),
      dealId: null,
      channel: "bland_text",
      direction: "inbound",
      from: "+1-312-555-0199",
      to: "+1-773-555-0100",
      fromName: null,
      subject: null,
      body: "Our budget is around $30,000",
      bodyHtml: null,
      attachments: [],
      draftedBy: null,
      approvedBy: null,
      editDistance: null,
      originalDraft: null,
      receivedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      draftedAt: null,
      approvedAt: null,
      sentAt: null,
      responseToId: null,
      responseTimeMs: null,
      threadId: null,
      intent: null,
      parsedData: null,
      confidence: null,
      confidenceLevel: null,
      platformMessageId: null,
      platformMetadata: null,
    };
    const ad = makeActiveDeal(routeResult.deal);
    addMessageToConversation(ad.conversation, formMsg);

    const routeResult2 = await routeMessage(smsMsg, [ad], { createDeal: async () => createDealContext("live") });

    // 3. Verify: same deal, budget updated
    expect(routeResult2.isNewDeal).toBe(false);
    expect(routeResult2.deal.id).toBe(routeResult.deal.id);
    expect(routeResult2.updatedFields).toContain("budget");
    expect(routeResult2.deal.client.budget).not.toBeNull();
    expect(routeResult2.conversation.channelsUsed).toContain("web_form");
    expect(routeResult2.conversation.channelsUsed).toContain("bland_text");
  });
});

// ── Test d: Voice copilot → entity detection ─────────────────────────────

describe("voice copilot → entity detection → deal update", () => {
  it("detects entities from operator call transcript", async () => {
    const chunks: TranscriptChunk[] = [
      { chunkId: "c1", speaker: "counterparty", text: "We have a Phenom 300E available, tail N300EL", timestampMs: 5000, durationMs: 3000, confidence: 0.95, isFinal: true, detectedEntities: [] },
      { chunkId: "c2", speaker: "counterparty", text: "Best I can do is $28,500 all-in from KTEB", timestampMs: 12000, durationMs: 2500, confidence: 0.92, isFinal: true, detectedEntities: [] },
      { chunkId: "c3", speaker: "counterparty", text: "I can hold it for two hours", timestampMs: 18000, durationMs: 1500, confidence: 0.90, isFinal: true, detectedEntities: [] },
    ];

    // Per-chunk detection
    const chunk1Entities = processChunk(chunks[0]!);
    expect(chunk1Entities.some((e) => e.type === "aircraft_type")).toBe(true);
    expect(chunk1Entities.some((e) => e.type === "tail_number" && e.value === "N300EL")).toBe(true);

    const chunk2Entities = processChunk(chunks[1]!);
    expect(chunk2Entities.some((e) => e.type === "price")).toBe(true);
    expect(chunk2Entities.some((e) => e.type === "negotiation_signal" && e.value === "firm")).toBe(true);

    // Post-call batch extraction
    const allEntities = extractAllEntities(chunks);
    const types = allEntities.map((e) => e.type);
    expect(types).toContain("aircraft_type");
    expect(types).toContain("tail_number");
    expect(types).toContain("price");
    expect(types).toContain("airport");
    expect(types).toContain("hold_duration");

    // Summary
    const summary = await generateSummary(chunks, allEntities);
    expect(summary.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);

    // Call store
    const { callStore, cleanup } = tmpStore();
    const call: Call = {
      callId: "call_e2e", dealId: "deal_001", direction: "outbound_to_operator",
      participants: [{ name: "Phelipe", role: "broker", phone: "+1-773-555-0100" }, { name: "John", role: "operator", phone: "+1-555-0200" }],
      startedAt: new Date().toISOString(), endedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      durationSec: 25, transcript: chunks, detectedEntities: allEntities,
      summary, recordingUrl: null, recordingExpiresAt: null,
    };
    callStore.saveCall(call);
    expect(callStore.getByDealId("deal_001")).toHaveLength(1);

    // Voice adapter creates message
    const voiceAdapter = new VoiceCopilotAdapter();
    const msg = voiceAdapter.receive(call);
    expect(msg.channel).toBe("voice_copilot");
    expect(msg.body).toContain(summary);

    cleanup();
  });
});

// ── Test e: Stage regression through comms layer ─────────────────────────

describe("stage regression through comms layer", () => {
  it("regresses presented → estimated when client wants cheaper aircraft", async () => {
    const deal = makeDealAtStage("presented");
    const ad = makeActiveDeal(deal);

    // Client replies wanting cheaper option
    const resend = new ResendClient({ apiKey: "test" }, mockFetch);
    const emailAdapter = new EmailAdapter(resend);
    const clientMsg = emailAdapter.receive({
      type: "email.received",
      data: {
        id: "resend_neg_001", from: "sarah@chen.com", to: ["charter@osa.aero"],
        subject: "Re: Your Charter Quote",
        text: "Can you do a lower price? What about a lighter jet? I'd like to negotiate.",
        created_at: new Date().toISOString(),
      },
    } satisfies ResendInboundWebhook);

    addMessageToConversation(ad.conversation, clientMsg);
    const routeResult = await routeMessage(clientMsg, [ad], { createDeal: async () => createDealContext("live") });

    expect(routeResult.intent).toBe("negotiation");
    expect(routeResult.action.type).toBe("flag_for_human");

    // Human triggers regression
    regressStage(deal, "estimated", "Client wants a lighter jet");

    // Verify data clearing
    expect(deal.stage).toBe("estimated");
    expect(deal.sourcing.selectedOperator).toBeNull();
    expect(deal.quote.operatorPrice).toBeNull();
    expect(deal.presentation.sentAt).toBeNull();
    // Preserved
    expect(deal.client.name).toBe("Sarah Chen");
    expect(deal.trip.departure).toBe("KTEB");
    expect(deal.estimate.priceRange).not.toBeNull();
    // History
    const regressions = deal.stageHistory.filter((h) => h.regression != null);
    expect(regressions).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(regressions[0]!.regression!.from).toBe("presented");
    expect(regressions[0]!.regression!.to).toBe("estimated");
  });
});

// ── Test f: Autonomy graduation lifecycle ────────────────────────────────

describe("autonomy graduation lifecycle", () => {
  it("graduates after 10 zero-edit approvals, revokes on edit", async () => {
    const { autoStore, cleanup } = tmpStore();

    // Approve 10 follow-ups with 0 edits
    for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
      const graduated = autoStore.recordApproval("email", "follow_up", "client", 0);
      if (i < 9) expect(graduated).toBe(false);
      else expect(graduated).toBe(true);
    }
    expect(autoStore.isAutonomous("email", "follow_up", "client")).toBe(true);

    // 11th draft should auto-send
    const deal = makeDealAtStage("presented");
    const msg: Message = {
      id: generateMessageId(), dealId: deal.id, channel: "email", direction: "outbound",
      from: "charter@osa.aero", to: "sarah@chen.com", fromName: null, subject: "Follow up",
      body: "Any update?", bodyHtml: null, attachments: [],
      draftedBy: "dumont", approvedBy: null, editDistance: null, originalDraft: "Any update?",
      receivedAt: null, draftedAt: new Date().toISOString(), approvedAt: null, sentAt: null,
      responseToId: null, responseTimeMs: null, threadId: null,
      intent: "follow_up", parsedData: null, confidence: 0.8, confidenceLevel: "high",
      platformMessageId: null, platformMetadata: null,
    };
    const gate = evaluateConfidence(msg, deal, autoStore);
    expect(gate.action).toBe("auto_send");

    // Edit revokes autonomy
    autoStore.recordApproval("email", "follow_up", "client", 5);
    expect(autoStore.isAutonomous("email", "follow_up", "client")).toBe(false);
    expect(autoStore.getRule("email", "follow_up", "client").currentApprovals).toBe(0);

    // Next draft holds
    const gate2 = evaluateConfidence(msg, deal, autoStore);
    expect(gate2.action).not.toBe("auto_send");

    cleanup();
  });
});

// ── Test g: Draft evaluator catches bad draft ────────────────────────────

describe("draft evaluator catches bad draft in pipeline", () => {
  it("catches WiFi claim and revises", async () => {
    const deal = makeDealAtStage("quoted");

    const draftMsg: Message = {
      id: generateMessageId(), dealId: deal.id, channel: "email", direction: "outbound",
      from: "charter@osa.aero", to: "sarah@chen.com", fromName: null, subject: "Your quote",
      body: "Your Phenom 300E has WiFi and a fully stocked galley. All-in price: $30,435.",
      bodyHtml: null, attachments: [],
      draftedBy: "dumont", approvedBy: null, editDistance: null, originalDraft: null,
      receivedAt: null, draftedAt: new Date().toISOString(), approvedAt: null, sentAt: null,
      responseToId: null, responseTimeMs: null, threadId: null,
      intent: "quote_response", parsedData: null, confidence: 0.5, confidenceLevel: "low",
      platformMessageId: null, platformMetadata: null,
    };

    // Code-based evaluator catches WiFi
    const result = await evaluateDraft(draftMsg, deal);
    expect(result.issues.some((i) => i.includes("WiFi"))).toBe(true);

    // With reviser: revised draft removes WiFi
    const resultWithReviser = await evaluateDraft(draftMsg, deal, {
      reviser: async (original, issues) => {
        let revised = original;
        if (issues.some((i) => i.includes("WiFi"))) {
          revised = revised.replace(/has WiFi and a fully stocked galley/, "is configured for your trip");
        }
        return revised;
      },
    });
    expect(resultWithReviser.wasRevised).toBe(true);
    expect(resultWithReviser.draft).not.toContain("WiFi");
    expect(resultWithReviser.originalBeforeRevision).toContain("WiFi");
  });
});
// === tests/integration/error-paths.test.ts ===
/**
 * Integration tests — error paths and failure modes.
 * Per spec: communication-layer.md Sprint 7.
 */

import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach } from "vitest";
import { ResendClient } from "../../src/integrations/resend.js";
import { EmailAdapter } from "../../src/channels/email.js";
import { routeMessage } from "../../src/router/message-router.js";
import type { ActiveDeal } from "../../src/router/thread-matcher.js";
import { matchThread } from "../../src/router/thread-matcher.js";
import { evaluateConfidence } from "../../src/router/confidence-gate.js";
import { respond } from "../../src/capabilities/respond.js";
import { regressStage } from "../../src/orchestrator/stage-transitions.js";
import { PipelineError } from "../../src/utils/errors.js";
import { AutonomyStore } from "../../src/memory/autonomy-store.js";
import { MessageStore } from "../../src/memory/message-store.js";
import { createDealContext, updateDealContext, _resetDealCounter } from "../../src/orchestrator/deal-context-manager.js";
import { advanceStage } from "../../src/orchestrator/stage-transitions.js";
import { createConversation, addMessageToConversation } from "../../src/schemas/conversation.js";
import { generateMessageId } from "../../src/schemas/message.js";
import type { Message, MessageChannel } from "../../src/schemas/message.js";
import type { DealContext } from "../../src/schemas/deal-context.js";

import { createTestDb } from "../../src/memory/database.js";

beforeEach(() => _resetDealCounter());

const mockFetch = async () => ({ ok: true, status: 200, json: async () => ({ id: "m1" }) });

function tmpAutoStore(): { store: AutonomyStore; cleanup: () => void } {
  const db = createTestDb();
  return { store: new AutonomyStore(db), cleanup: () => db.close() };
}

function tmpMsgStore(): { store: MessageStore; cleanup: () => void } {
  const db = createTestDb();
  return { store: new MessageStore(db), cleanup: () => db.close() };
}

function makeMsg(overrides: Partial<Message> = {}): Message {
  return {
    id: generateMessageId(), dealId: null, channel: "email" as MessageChannel, direction: "inbound",
    from: "test@test.com", to: "charter@osa.aero", fromName: null, subject: null,
    body: "Test message", bodyHtml: null, attachments: [],
    draftedBy: null, approvedBy: null, editDistance: null, originalDraft: null,
    receivedAt: new Date().toISOString(), draftedAt: null, approvedAt: null, sentAt: null,
    responseToId: null, responseTimeMs: null, threadId: null,
    intent: null, parsedData: null, confidence: null, confidenceLevel: null,
    platformMessageId: null, platformMetadata: null, ...overrides,
  };
}

function makeDeal(stage?: string): DealContext {
  const deal = createDealContext("live");
  updateDealContext(deal, {
    client: { name: "Test", contact: "test@test.com", type: "new", budget: { min: 25000, max: 40000 }, substitutionPref: null, qualified: true, qualificationReason: "test" },
    trip: { departure: "KTEB", arrival: "KPBI", date: "2026-04-15", pax: 4, aircraftClass: "light", aircraftType: null, isRoundTrip: false, specialRequirements: [] },
    estimate: { source: "aviapages", flightTime: 150, priceRange: { low: 25000, high: 35000 }, fuelBurn: 280, positioning: null, calculatedAt: new Date().toISOString() },
    sourcing: { selectedOperator: { operatorId: "op_001", operatorName: "Elite", phone: "op@test.com", baseAirport: "KTEB", aircraftType: "Phenom 300E", aircraftTail: null, score: 0.85, scoreBreakdown: { proximity: 0.9, laneHistory: 0.5, responseTime: 0.5, pricingAccuracy: 0.5, relationship: 0.5 }, distanceToDeparture: 0 }, backupOperator: null, rankingReason: "t", laneHistory: [] },
    quote: { operatorPrice: 28000, osaPrice: 30435, margin: 0.08, validUntil: new Date(Date.now() + 7200000).toISOString(), aircraft: null, quotedAt: new Date().toISOString() },
    presentation: { sentAt: new Date().toISOString(), viewedAt: null, clientDecision: "pending", decisionAt: null, timerStarted: new Date().toISOString() },
  });
  const path: Record<string, string[]> = {
    qualified: ["qualified"], estimated: ["qualified", "estimated"],
    sourced: ["qualified", "estimated", "sourced"],
    quoted: ["qualified", "estimated", "sourced", "quoted"],
    presented: ["qualified", "estimated", "sourced", "quoted", "presented"],
    confirmed: ["qualified", "estimated", "sourced", "quoted", "presented", "confirmed"],
    closed: ["qualified", "estimated", "sourced", "quoted", "presented", "confirmed", "closed"],
  };
  for (const s of path[stage ?? "presented"] ?? []) advanceStage(deal, s as DealContext["stage"]);
  return deal;
}

// ── Error Path Tests ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────

describe("webhook signature rejection", () => {
  it("Resend rejects invalid signature", () => {
    const client = new ResendClient({ apiKey: "test", webhookSecret: "resend_secret" });
    expect(client.verifyWebhook('{"data":"x"}', "invalid")).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe("router handles edge cases", () => {
  it("handles malformed message body gracefully", async () => {
    const msg = makeMsg({ body: "" });
    const result = await routeMessage(msg, [], { createDeal: async () => createDealContext("live") });
    expect(result.isNewDeal).toBe(true);
    expect(result.deal).toBeDefined();
  });

  it("thread matcher returns most recent deal on ambiguous match", () => {
    const deal1 = makeDeal("estimated");
    deal1.updatedAt = "2026-04-01T10:00:00.000Z";
    const deal2 = makeDeal("sourced");
    deal2.updatedAt = "2026-04-02T10:00:00.000Z";

    const ad1: ActiveDeal = { deal: deal1, conversation: createConversation(deal1.id) };
    const ad2: ActiveDeal = { deal: deal2, conversation: createConversation(deal2.id) };

    const msg = makeMsg({ from: "test@test.com" });
    const match = matchThread(msg, [ad1, ad2]);
    expect(match).not.toBeNull();
    expect(match!.deal.id).toBe(deal2.id); // most recent wins
  });
});

describe("respond handles failure gracefully", () => {
  it("uses template fallback when no LLM provided", async () => {
    const deal = makeDeal("sourced");
    const inbound = makeMsg({ body: "Need a quote" });
    const result = await respond(deal, inbound, "follow_up");
    expect(result.draft.body.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(result.draft.draftedBy).toBe("dumont");
  });
});

describe("confidence gate blocks on NEVER list even if autonomous", () => {
  it("blocks client-facing price even with autonomy earned", () => {
    const { store: autoStore, cleanup } = tmpAutoStore();
    // Graduate autonomy
    for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) autoStore.recordApproval("email", "quote_response", "client", 0);
    expect(autoStore.isAutonomous("email", "quote_response", "client")).toBe(true);

    const deal = makeDeal("presented");
    const msg = makeMsg({
      direction: "outbound", to: "test@test.com", body: "Your price is $30,435.",
      intent: "quote_response", confidenceLevel: "high",
      draftedBy: "dumont", channel: "email",
    });
    const gate = evaluateConfidence(msg, deal, autoStore);
    expect(gate.action).toBe("blocked"); // price to client = NEVER
    cleanup();
  });
});

describe("stage regression from closed throws PipelineError", () => {
  it("throws when regressing from closed", () => {
    const deal = makeDeal("closed");
    expect(() => regressStage(deal, "estimated", "test")).toThrow(PipelineError);
  });

  it("throws when regressing from failed", () => {
    const deal = createDealContext("live");
    advanceStage(deal, "failed");
    expect(() => regressStage(deal, "estimated", "test")).toThrow(PipelineError);
  });
});

describe("MessageStore persists through save/load cycle", () => {
  it("messages survive store recreation on same db", () => {
    const db = createTestDb();

    // Save
    const store1 = new MessageStore(db);
    store1.saveMessage(makeMsg({ id: "persist_001", body: "Persisted message" }));
    expect(store1.count).toBe(1);

    // Recreate from same db
    const store2 = new MessageStore(db);
    expect(store2.count).toBe(1);
    expect(store2.getByMessageId("persist_001")).not.toBeNull();
    expect(store2.getByMessageId("persist_001")!.body).toBe("Persisted message");

    db.close();
  });
});
// === tests/stage-regression.test.ts ===
/**
 * Tests for stage regression — backward pipeline transitions.
 * Per spec: communication-layer.md section 2.7 (Dynamic Planning).
 *
 * The pipeline can go backwards when real-world deal flow changes:
 * - Client wants different aircraft → presented → estimated
 * - Client rejects operator → presented → sourced
 * - Quote expired → quoted → sourced
 * - Operator unavailable → sourced → estimated
 */

import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach } from "vitest";
import {
  regressStage,
  isAllowedRegression,
} from "../src/orchestrator/stage-transitions.js";
import { PipelineError } from "../src/utils/errors.js";
import { createDealContext, updateDealContext, _resetDealCounter } from "../src/orchestrator/deal-context-manager.js";
import { advanceStage } from "../src/orchestrator/stage-transitions.js";
import type { DealContext } from "../src/schemas/deal-context.js";

// ── Helpers ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

function advanceTo(ctx: DealContext, target: string) {
  const path: Record<string, string[]> = {
    qualified: ["qualified"],
    estimated: ["qualified", "estimated"],
    sourced: ["qualified", "estimated", "sourced"],
    quoted: ["qualified", "estimated", "sourced", "quoted"],
    presented: ["qualified", "estimated", "sourced", "quoted", "presented"],
    confirmed: ["qualified", "estimated", "sourced", "quoted", "presented", "confirmed"],
    closed: ["qualified", "estimated", "sourced", "quoted", "presented", "confirmed", "closed"],
  };
  for (const stage of path[target] ?? []) {
    advanceStage(ctx, stage as DealContext["stage"]);
  }
}

function makeFullDealCtx(): DealContext {
  const ctx = createDealContext("sim");
  updateDealContext(ctx, {
    trip: {
      departure: "KTEB", arrival: "KPBI", date: "2026-04-15",
      pax: 4, aircraftClass: "light", aircraftType: "Phenom 300E",
      isRoundTrip: false, specialRequirements: [],
    },
    client: {
      name: "Test Client", contact: "test@example.com", type: "new",
      budget: { min: 25000, max: 40000 }, substitutionPref: null,
      qualified: true, qualificationReason: "Fully qualified.",
    },
    sourcing: {
      selectedOperator: {
        operatorId: "op_001", operatorName: "Elite Aviation",
        phone: "+1-555-0100", baseAirport: "KTEB",
        aircraftType: "Phenom 300E", aircraftTail: "N300EA",
        score: 0.85,
        scoreBreakdown: { proximity: 0.9, laneHistory: 0.7, responseTime: 0.8, pricingAccuracy: 0.85, relationship: 0.8 },
        distanceToDeparture: 0,
      },
      backupOperator: null,
      rankingReason: "Elite Aviation selected",
      laneHistory: [],
    },
    estimate: {
      source: "aviapages", flightTime: 150,
      priceRange: { low: 25000, high: 35000 },
      fuelBurn: 280, positioning: null,
      calculatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    },
    quote: {
      operatorPrice: 28000, osaPrice: 30435, margin: 0.08,
      validUntil: new Date(Date.now() + 120 * 60_000).toISOString(),
      aircraft: { type: "Phenom 300E", tail: "N300EA", year: 2022 },
      quotedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    },
    presentation: {
      sentAt: new Date().toISOString(), viewedAt: null,
      clientDecision: "pending", decisionAt: null,
      timerStarted: new Date().toISOString(),
    },
  });
  return ctx;
}

// ── isAllowedRegression ──────────────────────────────────────────────────

describe("isAllowedRegression", () => {
  beforeEach(() => _resetDealCounter());

  it("allows presented → estimated", () => {
    expect(isAllowedRegression("presented", "estimated")).toBe(true);
  });

  it("allows presented → sourced", () => {
    expect(isAllowedRegression("presented", "sourced")).toBe(true);
  });

  it("allows quoted → sourced", () => {
    expect(isAllowedRegression("quoted", "sourced")).toBe(true);
  });

  it("allows sourced → estimated", () => {
    expect(isAllowedRegression("sourced", "estimated")).toBe(true);
  });

  it("blocks closed → anything", () => {
    expect(isAllowedRegression("closed", "estimated")).toBe(false);
    expect(isAllowedRegression("closed", "intake")).toBe(false);
  });

  it("blocks failed → anything", () => {
    expect(isAllowedRegression("failed", "estimated")).toBe(false);
    expect(isAllowedRegression("failed", "sourced")).toBe(false);
  });

  it("blocks regression to intake", () => {
    expect(isAllowedRegression("presented", "intake")).toBe(false);
    expect(isAllowedRegression("sourced", "intake")).toBe(false);
  });

  it("blocks regression to qualified", () => {
    expect(isAllowedRegression("estimated", "qualified")).toBe(false);
  });

  it("blocks forward transitions (not a regression)", () => {
    expect(isAllowedRegression("estimated", "sourced")).toBe(false);
    expect(isAllowedRegression("intake", "qualified")).toBe(false);
  });
});

// ── regressStage: data clearing ──────────────────────────────────────────

describe("regressStage data clearing", () => {
  beforeEach(() => _resetDealCounter());

  it("presented → estimated clears sourcing + quote + presentation", () => {
    const ctx = makeFullDealCtx();
    advanceTo(ctx, "presented");

    regressStage(ctx, "estimated", "Client wants a lighter jet");

    expect(ctx.stage).toBe("estimated");
    // Sourcing cleared
    expect(ctx.sourcing.selectedOperator).toBeNull();
    expect(ctx.sourcing.backupOperator).toBeNull();
    expect(ctx.sourcing.rankingReason).toBe("");
    // Quote cleared
    expect(ctx.quote.operatorPrice).toBeNull();
    expect(ctx.quote.osaPrice).toBeNull();
    expect(ctx.quote.aircraft).toBeNull();
    // Presentation cleared
    expect(ctx.presentation.sentAt).toBeNull();
    expect(ctx.presentation.clientDecision).toBeNull();
    expect(ctx.presentation.timerStarted).toBeNull();
  });

  it("presented → sourced clears quote + presentation but keeps estimate", () => {
    const ctx = makeFullDealCtx();
    advanceTo(ctx, "presented");

    regressStage(ctx, "sourced", "Client rejects operator");

    expect(ctx.stage).toBe("sourced");
    // Quote cleared
    expect(ctx.quote.operatorPrice).toBeNull();
    expect(ctx.quote.osaPrice).toBeNull();
    // Presentation cleared
    expect(ctx.presentation.sentAt).toBeNull();
    // Estimate preserved
    expect(ctx.estimate.priceRange).not.toBeNull();
    expect(ctx.estimate.priceRange!.low).toBe(25000);
  });

  it("quoted → sourced clears quote + presentation", () => {
    const ctx = makeFullDealCtx();
    advanceTo(ctx, "quoted");

    regressStage(ctx, "sourced", "Quote expired");

    expect(ctx.stage).toBe("sourced");
    expect(ctx.quote.operatorPrice).toBeNull();
    expect(ctx.quote.osaPrice).toBeNull();
    expect(ctx.presentation.sentAt).toBeNull();
    // Estimate preserved
    expect(ctx.estimate.priceRange).not.toBeNull();
  });

  it("sourced → estimated clears sourcing data", () => {
    const ctx = makeFullDealCtx();
    advanceTo(ctx, "sourced");

    regressStage(ctx, "estimated", "Operator not available, changes scope");

    expect(ctx.stage).toBe("estimated");
    expect(ctx.sourcing.selectedOperator).toBeNull();
    expect(ctx.sourcing.rankingReason).toBe("");
    // Estimate preserved
    expect(ctx.estimate.priceRange).not.toBeNull();
  });
});

// ── regressStage: error cases ────────────────────────────────────────────

describe("regressStage error cases", () => {
  beforeEach(() => _resetDealCounter());

  it("throws PipelineError for regression from closed", () => {
    const ctx = makeFullDealCtx();
    advanceTo(ctx, "closed");

    expect(() => regressStage(ctx, "estimated", "test")).toThrow(PipelineError);
  });

  it("throws PipelineError for regression from failed", () => {
    const ctx = createDealContext("sim");
    advanceStage(ctx, "failed");

    expect(() => regressStage(ctx, "estimated", "test")).toThrow(PipelineError);
  });

  it("throws PipelineError for regression to intake", () => {
    const ctx = makeFullDealCtx();
    advanceTo(ctx, "presented");

    expect(() => regressStage(ctx, "intake", "test")).toThrow(PipelineError);
  });

  it("error message includes from and to stages", () => {
    const ctx = makeFullDealCtx();
    advanceTo(ctx, "closed");

    try {
      regressStage(ctx, "estimated", "test");
      expect.unreachable("should have thrown");
    } catch (err) {
      expect(err).toBeInstanceOf(PipelineError);
      const pe = err as InstanceType<typeof PipelineError>;
      expect(pe.message).toContain("closed");
      expect(pe.message).toContain("estimated");
    }
  });
});

// ── regressStage: history logging ────────────────────────────────────────

describe("regressStage history logging", () => {
  beforeEach(() => _resetDealCounter());

  it("logs regression to stageHistory with reason", () => {
    const ctx = makeFullDealCtx();
    advanceTo(ctx, "presented");
    const historyBefore = ctx.stageHistory.length;

    regressStage(ctx, "estimated", "Client wants a lighter jet");

    // Should have added a regression entry + a new stage entry
    expect(ctx.stageHistory.length).toBe(historyBefore + 2);

    const regressionEntry = ctx.stageHistory[ctx.stageHistory.length - 2]!;
    expect(regressionEntry.stage).toBe("estimated");
    expect(regressionEntry.regression).toBeDefined();
    expect(regressionEntry.regression!.from).toBe("presented");
    expect(regressionEntry.regression!.to).toBe("estimated");
    expect(regressionEntry.regression!.reason).toBe("Client wants a lighter jet");

    const newStageEntry = ctx.stageHistory[ctx.stageHistory.length - 1]!;
    expect(newStageEntry.stage).toBe("estimated");
    expect(newStageEntry.duration).toBeNull();
  });

  it("updates updatedAt timestamp", () => {
    const ctx = makeFullDealCtx();
    advanceTo(ctx, "presented");

    regressStage(ctx, "estimated", "test");

    // updatedAt should be a valid ISO timestamp (may be same ms in fast tests)
    expect(ctx.updatedAt).toBeDefined();
    expect(new Date(ctx.updatedAt).getTime()).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    // Verify reason was stored in outcome notes
    expect(ctx.outcome.notes).toContain("Regression presented→estimated: test");
  });
});

// ── regressStage: preserves unrelated data ───────────────────────────────

describe("regressStage preserves client and trip data", () => {
  beforeEach(() => _resetDealCounter());

  it("preserves client data on regression", () => {
    const ctx = makeFullDealCtx();
    advanceTo(ctx, "presented");

    regressStage(ctx, "estimated", "test");

    expect(ctx.client.name).toBe("Test Client");
    expect(ctx.client.contact).toBe("test@example.com");
    expect(ctx.client.budget).toEqual({ min: 25000, max: 40000 });
    expect(ctx.client.qualified).toBe(true);
  });

  it("preserves trip data on regression", () => {
    const ctx = makeFullDealCtx();
    advanceTo(ctx, "presented");

    regressStage(ctx, "estimated", "test");

    expect(ctx.trip.departure).toBe("KTEB");
    expect(ctx.trip.arrival).toBe("KPBI");
    expect(ctx.trip.pax).toBe(4);
    expect(ctx.trip.date).toBe("2026-04-15");
  });

  it("preserves estimate data when regressing to sourced", () => {
    const ctx = makeFullDealCtx();
    advanceTo(ctx, "presented");

    regressStage(ctx, "sourced", "test");

    expect(ctx.estimate.source).toBe("aviapages");
    expect(ctx.estimate.priceRange!.low).toBe(25000);
    expect(ctx.estimate.flightTime).toBe(150);
  });

  it("preserves guardian data on regression", () => {
    const ctx = makeFullDealCtx();
    advanceTo(ctx, "presented");
    ctx.guardian.passes.push({
      stage: "intake", passed: true, checks: [], timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
    });

    regressStage(ctx, "estimated", "test");

    expect(ctx.guardian.passes.length).toBe(1);
  });
});

// ── Double regression ────────────────────────────────────────────────────

describe("double regression", () => {
  beforeEach(() => _resetDealCounter());

  it("supports presented → sourced → estimated", () => {
    const ctx = makeFullDealCtx();
    advanceTo(ctx, "presented");

    // First regression: presented → sourced
    regressStage(ctx, "sourced", "Client rejects operator");
    expect(ctx.stage).toBe("sourced");
    expect(ctx.quote.operatorPrice).toBeNull();
    // Estimate still intact
    expect(ctx.estimate.priceRange).not.toBeNull();

    // Second regression: sourced → estimated
    regressStage(ctx, "estimated", "Need different aircraft class");
    expect(ctx.stage).toBe("estimated");
    expect(ctx.sourcing.selectedOperator).toBeNull();
    // Estimate still intact (we regressed TO estimated, not past it)
    expect(ctx.estimate.priceRange).not.toBeNull();
  });

  it("logs both regressions to stageHistory", () => {
    const ctx = makeFullDealCtx();
    advanceTo(ctx, "presented");

    regressStage(ctx, "sourced", "Reason 1");
    regressStage(ctx, "estimated", "Reason 2");

    const regressions = ctx.stageHistory.filter((h) => h.regression != null);
    expect(regressions.length).toBe(2);
    expect(regressions[0]!.regression!.from).toBe("presented");
    expect(regressions[0]!.regression!.to).toBe("sourced");
    expect(regressions[1]!.regression!.from).toBe("sourced");
    expect(regressions[1]!.regression!.to).toBe("estimated");
  });
});
// === tests/comms-sprint1.test.ts ===
/**
 * Tests for Communication Layer Sprint 1:
 * Message schemas, Thread Matcher, Message Router, Intent Classifier.
 *
 * Per spec acceptance criteria (FR-1, FR-2, FR-7).
 */

import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach } from "vitest";
import { MessageSchema, generateMessageId } from "../src/schemas/message.js";
import type { Message, MessageIntent } from "../src/schemas/message.js";
import { ConversationSchema, createConversation, addMessageToConversation } from "../src/schemas/conversation.js";
import { CallSchema, TranscriptChunkSchema, DetectedEntitySchema } from "../src/schemas/call.js";
import { AutonomyRuleSchema, ChannelConfigSchema } from "../src/schemas/channel-config.js";
import { matchThread } from "../src/router/thread-matcher.js";
import type { ActiveDeal } from "../src/router/thread-matcher.js";
import { classifyIntent, classifySender } from "../src/router/intent-classifier.js";
import { routeMessage } from "../src/router/message-router.js";
import { createDealContext, updateDealContext, _resetDealCounter } from "../src/orchestrator/deal-context-manager.js";
import { advanceStage } from "../src/orchestrator/stage-transitions.js";
import type { DealContext } from "../src/schemas/deal-context.js";

// ── Helpers ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

beforeEach(() => _resetDealCounter());

function makeMsg(overrides: Partial<Message> = {}): Message {
  return {
    id: generateMessageId(),
    dealId: null,
    channel: "email",
    direction: "inbound",
    from: "client@example.com",
    to: "charter@osa.aero",
    fromName: null,
    subject: null,
    body: "I need a flight from Teterboro to Palm Beach",
    bodyHtml: null,
    attachments: [],
    draftedBy: null,
    approvedBy: null,
    editDistance: null,
    originalDraft: null,
    receivedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    draftedAt: null,
    approvedAt: null,
    sentAt: null,
    responseToId: null,
    responseTimeMs: null,
    threadId: null,
    intent: null,
    parsedData: null,
    confidence: null,
    confidenceLevel: null,
    platformMessageId: null,
    platformMetadata: null,
    ...overrides,
  };
}

function makeDeal(overrides?: {
  stage?: DealContext["stage"];
  clientContact?: string;
  operatorPhone?: string;
  operatorName?: string;
  updatedAt?: string;
}): DealContext {
  const ctx = createDealContext("live");
  updateDealContext(ctx, {
    client: {
      name: "Test Client",
      contact: overrides?.clientContact ?? "client@example.com",
      type: "new", budget: { min: 25000, max: 40000 },
      substitutionPref: null, qualified: true, qualificationReason: "Qualified.",
    },
    trip: {
      departure: "KTEB", arrival: "KPBI", date: "2026-04-15",
      pax: 4, aircraftClass: "light", aircraftType: null,
      isRoundTrip: false, specialRequirements: [],
    },
  });
  if (overrides?.operatorPhone || overrides?.operatorName) {
    updateDealContext(ctx, {
      sourcing: {
        selectedOperator: {
          operatorId: "op_001", operatorName: overrides.operatorName ?? "Elite Aviation",
          phone: overrides.operatorPhone ?? "+1-555-0100",
          baseAirport: "KTEB", aircraftType: "Phenom 300E", aircraftTail: null,
          score: 0.85, scoreBreakdown: { proximity: 0.9, laneHistory: 0.5, responseTime: 0.5, pricingAccuracy: 0.5, relationship: 0.5 },
          distanceToDeparture: 0,
        },
      },
    });
  }
  // Advance to target stage
  if (overrides?.stage) {
    const path: Record<string, string[]> = {
      qualified: ["qualified"],
      estimated: ["qualified", "estimated"],
      sourced: ["qualified", "estimated", "sourced"],
      quoted: ["qualified", "estimated", "sourced", "quoted"],
      presented: ["qualified", "estimated", "sourced", "quoted", "presented"],
    };
    for (const s of path[overrides.stage] ?? []) {
      advanceStage(ctx, s as DealContext["stage"]);
    }
  }
  if (overrides?.updatedAt) ctx.updatedAt = overrides.updatedAt;
  return ctx;
}

function makeActiveDeal(overrides?: Parameters<typeof makeDeal>[0]): ActiveDeal {
  const deal = makeDeal(overrides);
  const conversation = createConversation(deal.id);
  return { deal, conversation };
}

async function defaultCreateDeal(_body: string): Promise<DealContext> {
  return createDealContext("live");
}

// ── Schema Validation ────────────────────────────────────────────────────

describe("schema validation", () => {
  it("MessageSchema validates a well-formed message", () => {
    const msg = makeMsg();
    expect(MessageSchema.safeParse(msg).success).toBe(true);
  });

  it("MessageSchema rejects invalid channel", () => {
    const msg = { ...makeMsg(), channel: "pigeon" };
    expect(MessageSchema.safeParse(msg).success).toBe(false);
  });

  it("ConversationSchema validates an empty conversation", () => {
    const conv = createConversation("deal_001");
    expect(ConversationSchema.safeParse(conv).success).toBe(true);
  });

  it("CallSchema validates a minimal call", () => {
    const call = {
      callId: "call_001", dealId: "deal_001", direction: "outbound_to_operator",
      participants: [{ name: "Phelipe", role: "broker", phone: "+1-555-0000" }],
      startedAt: new Date().toISOString(), endedAt: null, durationSec: null,
      transcript: [], detectedEntities: [], summary: null,
      recordingUrl: null, recordingExpiresAt: null,
    };
    expect(CallSchema.safeParse(call).success).toBe(true);
  });

  it("DetectedEntitySchema validates a price entity", () => {
    const entity = {
      type: "price", value: "$28,000", raw: "twenty-eight thousand",
      speaker: "counterparty", confidence: 0.95, timestampMs: 12000,
    };
    expect(DetectedEntitySchema.safeParse(entity).success).toBe(true);
  });

  it("AutonomyRuleSchema validates defaults", () => {
    const rule = {
      channel: "email", intent: "follow_up", recipientType: "client",
      autoSendEnabled: false, autoSendAfter: 10, currentApprovals: 0, maxDelaySeconds: 30,
    };
    expect(AutonomyRuleSchema.safeParse(rule).success).toBe(true);
  });

  it("ChannelConfigSchema validates", () => {
    const cfg = {
      channel: "email", enabled: true, rateLimitPerHour: 50,
      currentHourCount: 0, currentHourStart: null,
    };
    expect(ChannelConfigSchema.safeParse(cfg).success).toBe(true);
  });

  it("generateMessageId produces unique IDs", () => {
    const ids = new Set(Array.from({ length: 100 }, () => generateMessageId()));
    expect(ids.size).toBe(100);
  });
});

// ── Conversation ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

describe("conversation", () => {
  it("tracks messages and updates metrics", () => {
    const conv = createConversation("deal_001");
    const msg1 = makeMsg({ channel: "email", responseTimeMs: 5000 });
    const msg2 = makeMsg({ channel: "bland_text", responseTimeMs: 3000 });

    addMessageToConversation(conv, msg1);
    addMessageToConversation(conv, msg2);

    expect(conv.touchpointCount).toBe(2);
    expect(conv.channelsUsed).toContain("email");
    expect(conv.channelsUsed).toContain("bland_text");
    expect(conv.avgResponseTimeMs).toBe(4000);
  });
});

// ── Thread Matcher ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────

describe("thread matcher", () => {
  it("matches by responseToId (email In-Reply-To)", () => {
    const ad = makeActiveDeal({ clientContact: "client@example.com" });
    const originalMsg = makeMsg({ id: "msg_original" });
    addMessageToConversation(ad.conversation, originalMsg);

    const reply = makeMsg({ responseToId: "msg_original" });
    const match = matchThread(reply, [ad]);
    expect(match).not.toBeNull();
    expect(match!.deal.id).toBe(ad.deal.id);
  });

  it("matches by phone number", () => {
    const ad = makeActiveDeal({ clientContact: "+1-555-1234" });
    const msg = makeMsg({ from: "+1-555-1234" });
    const match = matchThread(msg, [ad]);
    expect(match).not.toBeNull();
    expect(match!.deal.id).toBe(ad.deal.id);
  });

  it("matches by email address", () => {
    const ad = makeActiveDeal({ clientContact: "sarah@chen.com" });
    const msg = makeMsg({ from: "sarah@chen.com" });
    const match = matchThread(msg, [ad]);
    expect(match).not.toBeNull();
  });

  it("returns null for unknown sender (new deal)", () => {
    const ad = makeActiveDeal({ clientContact: "existing@client.com" });
    const msg = makeMsg({ from: "totally-new@person.com" });
    expect(matchThread(msg, [ad])).toBeNull();
  });

  it("most recent deal wins when multiple match same contact", () => {
    const older = makeActiveDeal({ clientContact: "client@example.com", updatedAt: "2026-04-01T10:00:00.000Z" });
    const newer = makeActiveDeal({ clientContact: "client@example.com", updatedAt: "2026-04-02T10:00:00.000Z" });

    const msg = makeMsg({ from: "client@example.com" });
    const match = matchThread(msg, [older, newer]);
    expect(match!.deal.id).toBe(newer.deal.id);
  });

  it("returns null for message replying to a closed deal", () => {
    const ad = makeActiveDeal({ clientContact: "client@example.com" });
    ad.deal.stage = "closed";
    const originalMsg = makeMsg({ id: "msg_in_closed_deal" });
    addMessageToConversation(ad.conversation, originalMsg);

    const reply = makeMsg({ responseToId: "msg_in_closed_deal" });
    expect(matchThread(reply, [ad])).toBeNull();
  });

  it("matches by threadId", () => {
    const ad = makeActiveDeal();
    const threadMsg = makeMsg({ threadId: "thread_abc" });
    addMessageToConversation(ad.conversation, threadMsg);

    const newMsg = makeMsg({ from: "someone-else@test.com", threadId: "thread_abc" });
    const match = matchThread(newMsg, [ad]);
    expect(match).not.toBeNull();
  });

  it("skips terminal deals for contact matching", () => {
    const closed = makeActiveDeal({ clientContact: "client@example.com" });
    closed.deal.stage = "closed";

    const active = makeActiveDeal({ clientContact: "client@example.com" });

    const msg = makeMsg({ from: "client@example.com" });
    const match = matchThread(msg, [closed, active]);
    expect(match!.deal.id).toBe(active.deal.id);
  });
});

// ── Intent Classifier ────────────────────────────────────────────────────

describe("intent classifier", () => {
  it("classifies operator quote response", () => {
    const deal = makeDeal({ operatorPhone: "+1-555-0100", stage: "sourced" });
    const msg = makeMsg({ from: "+1-555-0100", body: "We can do this trip for $28,000 all-in" });
    expect(classifySender(msg, deal)).toBe("operator");
    expect(classifyIntent(msg, deal, "operator")).toBe("quote_response");
  });

  it("classifies client confirmation", () => {
    const deal = makeDeal({ stage: "presented" });
    const msg = makeMsg({ body: "Sounds good, let's do it!" });
    expect(classifyIntent(msg, deal, "client")).toBe("confirmation");
  });

  it("classifies client decline", () => {
    const deal = makeDeal({ stage: "presented" });
    const msg = makeMsg({ body: "Too expensive, I'll pass" });
    expect(classifyIntent(msg, deal, "client")).toBe("decline");
  });

  it("classifies negotiation", () => {
    const deal = makeDeal({ stage: "presented" });
    const msg = makeMsg({ body: "Can you do a lower price? What about a discount?" });
    expect(classifyIntent(msg, deal, "client")).toBe("negotiation");
  });

  it("classifies info request with question mark", () => {
    const deal = makeDeal();
    const msg = makeMsg({ body: "What's the baggage limit?" });
    expect(classifyIntent(msg, deal, "client")).toBe("info_request");
  });

  it("classifies quote request from new client", () => {
    const deal = makeDeal();
    const msg = makeMsg({ body: "I need to charter a flight from Chicago to Miami" });
    expect(classifyIntent(msg, deal, "client")).toBe("quote_request");
  });

  it("identifies sender as client by email", () => {
    const deal = makeDeal({ clientContact: "sarah@chen.com" });
    const msg = makeMsg({ from: "sarah@chen.com" });
    expect(classifySender(msg, deal)).toBe("client");
  });

  it("identifies sender as operator by phone", () => {
    const deal = makeDeal({ operatorPhone: "+1-555-0100" });
    const msg = makeMsg({ from: "+1-555-0100" });
    expect(classifySender(msg, deal)).toBe("operator");
  });

  it("returns unknown for unrecognized sender", () => {
    const deal = makeDeal();
    const msg = makeMsg({ from: "stranger@unknown.com" });
    expect(classifySender(msg, deal)).toBe("unknown");
  });

  it("classifies follow-up when replying to existing conversation", () => {
    const deal = makeDeal({ stage: "estimated" });
    const msg = makeMsg({ body: "Also, we'll need catering", responseToId: "msg_prev" });
    expect(classifyIntent(msg, deal, "client")).toBe("follow_up");
  });
});

// ── Message Router ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────

describe("message router", () => {
  it("creates new deal for unknown sender", async () => {
    const msg = makeMsg({ from: "brand-new@client.com", body: "Need a jet from NY to Miami" });
    const result = await routeMessage(msg, [], { createDeal: defaultCreateDeal });

    expect(result.isNewDeal).toBe(true);
    expect(result.deal).toBeDefined();
    expect(msg.dealId).toBe(result.deal.id);
  });

  it("routes to existing deal for known contact", async () => {
    const ad = makeActiveDeal({ clientContact: "sarah@chen.com" });
    const msg = makeMsg({ from: "sarah@chen.com", body: "Any update on my quote?" });

    const result = await routeMessage(msg, [ad], { createDeal: defaultCreateDeal });

    expect(result.isNewDeal).toBe(false);
    expect(result.deal.id).toBe(ad.deal.id);
    expect(result.conversation.touchpointCount).toBe(1);
  });

  it("appends message to conversation", async () => {
    const ad = makeActiveDeal({ clientContact: "sarah@chen.com" });
    const msg1 = makeMsg({ from: "sarah@chen.com", body: "First message" });
    const msg2 = makeMsg({ from: "sarah@chen.com", body: "Second message" });

    await routeMessage(msg1, [ad], { createDeal: defaultCreateDeal });
    await routeMessage(msg2, [ad], { createDeal: defaultCreateDeal });

    expect(ad.conversation.touchpointCount).toBe(2);
  });

  it("re-runs extractors and captures pax update in follow-up", async () => {
    const ad = makeActiveDeal({ clientContact: "client@example.com" });
    ad.deal.trip.pax = 4;

    const msg = makeMsg({
      from: "client@example.com",
      body: "Actually there will be 6 passengers, not 4",
    });

    const result = await routeMessage(msg, [ad], { createDeal: defaultCreateDeal });

    expect(result.updatedFields).toContain("pax");
    expect(result.deal.trip.pax).toBe(6);
  });

  it("re-runs extractors and captures budget in follow-up", async () => {
    const ad = makeActiveDeal({ clientContact: "client@example.com" });
    ad.deal.client.budget = null;

    const msg = makeMsg({
      from: "client@example.com",
      body: "Our budget is around $30,000",
    });

    const result = await routeMessage(msg, [ad], { createDeal: defaultCreateDeal });

    expect(result.updatedFields).toContain("budget");
    expect(result.deal.client.budget).not.toBeNull();
  });

  it("returns advance_to_quoted for operator quote response", async () => {
    const ad = makeActiveDeal({
      clientContact: "client@example.com",
      operatorPhone: "+1-555-0100",
      operatorName: "Elite Aviation",
      stage: "sourced",
    });
    const msg = makeMsg({
      from: "+1-555-0100",
      body: "We can do this trip for $28,000 all-in",
    });

    const result = await routeMessage(msg, [ad], { createDeal: defaultCreateDeal });

    expect(result.intent).toBe("quote_response");
    expect(result.action.type).toBe("advance_to_quoted");
    if (result.action.type === "advance_to_quoted") {
      expect(result.action.operatorPrice).toBe(28000);
    }
  });

  it("returns close_deal for client confirmation on presented deal", async () => {
    const ad = makeActiveDeal({
      clientContact: "client@example.com",
      stage: "presented",
      operatorPhone: "+1-555-0100",
    });
    const msg = makeMsg({
      from: "client@example.com",
      body: "Sounds good, let's do it!",
    });

    const result = await routeMessage(msg, [ad], { createDeal: defaultCreateDeal });

    expect(result.intent).toBe("confirmation");
    expect(result.action.type).toBe("close_deal");
    if (result.action.type === "close_deal") {
      expect(result.action.result).toBe("closed");
    }
  });

  it("returns activate_backup for operator decline", async () => {
    const ad = makeActiveDeal({
      clientContact: "client@example.com",
      operatorPhone: "+1-555-0100",
      stage: "sourced",
    });
    const msg = makeMsg({
      from: "+1-555-0100",
      body: "Sorry, that aircraft is not available on that date",
    });

    const result = await routeMessage(msg, [ad], { createDeal: defaultCreateDeal });

    expect(result.intent).toBe("decline");
    expect(result.senderRole).toBe("operator");
    expect(result.action.type).toBe("activate_backup");
  });

  it("flags negotiation for human handling", async () => {
    const ad = makeActiveDeal({
      clientContact: "client@example.com",
      stage: "presented",
    });
    const msg = makeMsg({
      from: "client@example.com",
      body: "Can you do a lower price? What about a discount?",
    });

    const result = await routeMessage(msg, [ad], { createDeal: defaultCreateDeal });

    expect(result.intent).toBe("negotiation");
    expect(result.action.type).toBe("flag_for_human");
  });

  it("creates new deal for message matching closed deal by contact", async () => {
    const closed = makeActiveDeal({ clientContact: "client@example.com" });
    closed.deal.stage = "closed";

    const msg = makeMsg({ from: "client@example.com", body: "I need another flight" });
    const result = await routeMessage(msg, [closed], { createDeal: defaultCreateDeal });

    expect(result.isNewDeal).toBe(true);
  });

  it("extracts $Xk price format from operator", async () => {
    const ad = makeActiveDeal({
      clientContact: "client@example.com",
      operatorPhone: "+1-555-0100",
      stage: "sourced",
    });
    const msg = makeMsg({ from: "+1-555-0100", body: "Best I can do is $32k for this route" });

    const result = await routeMessage(msg, [ad], { createDeal: defaultCreateDeal });

    expect(result.action.type).toBe("advance_to_quoted");
    if (result.action.type === "advance_to_quoted") {
      expect(result.action.operatorPrice).toBe(32000);
    }
  });
});
// === tests/guardian.test.ts ===
/**
 * Tests for Guardian capability — stage-specific validation checks.
 * Covers all five validation stages per guardian.md spec:
 *   1. After INTAKE: airports, runway, range (haversine), pax, date
 *   2. After ESTIMATE: budget overlap, estimate sanity
 *   3. After SOURCING: operator certification, insurance, safety rating
 *   4. After QUOTE: quote vs estimate, margin policy, validity window
 *   5. Before PRESENTATION: final sanity
 *
 * Also verifies:
 *   - checkRange bug fix (old line 224): now uses haversine, not a no-op
 *   - Warning double-count bug fix (old line 361): pass/fail uses checks directly
 */

import { describe, it, expect } from "vitest";
import type { DealContext } from "../src/schemas/deal-context.js";
import type { AirportData, OperatorCertData } from "../src/capabilities/guardian.js";
import {
  guardian,
  checksAfterIntake,
  checksAfterEstimate,
  checksAfterSourcing,
  checksAfterQuote,
  checksBeforePresentation,
  AIRCRAFT_PERF,
  resolveAircraftPerf,
} from "../src/capabilities/guardian.js";
import { createDealContext, updateDealContext } from "../src/orchestrator/deal-context-manager.js";
import { _resetDealCounter } from "../src/orchestrator/deal-context-manager.js";

// ── Test Fixtures ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

/** Build a realistic deal context with trip data populated. */
function makeDealContext(overrides?: Partial<{
  departure: string;
  arrival: string;
  date: string;
  pax: number;
  aircraftType: string | null;
  aircraftClass: string | null;
}>): DealContext {
  const ctx = createDealContext("sim");
  updateDealContext(ctx, {
    trip: {
      departure: overrides?.departure ?? "KTEB",
      arrival: overrides?.arrival ?? "KPBI",
      date: overrides?.date ?? "2026-12-15",
      pax: overrides?.pax ?? 4,
      aircraftType: overrides?.aircraftType ?? "Phenom 300E",
      aircraftClass: overrides?.aircraftClass ?? "light",
      isRoundTrip: false,
      specialRequirements: [],
    },
    client: {
      name: "Test Client",
      contact: "test@example.com",
      type: "new",
      budget: { min: 20000, max: 40000 },
      substitutionPref: null,
      qualified: true,
      qualificationReason: "Budget provided, valid route",
    },
  });
  return ctx;
}

/** Airport database for tests. */
function makeAirports(): Map<string, AirportData> {
  return new Map([
    ["KTEB", { icao: "KTEB", latitude: 40.8501, longitude: -74.0608, longest_runway_ft: 7000, customs_capable: true }],
    ["KPBI", { icao: "KPBI", latitude: 26.6832, longitude: -80.0956, longest_runway_ft: 10008, customs_capable: true }],
    ["KASE", { icao: "KASE", latitude: 39.2232, longitude: -106.8688, longest_runway_ft: 7006, customs_capable: false }],
    // Short runway airport for testing
    ["K12N", { icao: "K12N", latitude: 40.5660, longitude: -74.9998, longest_runway_ft: 2500, customs_capable: false }],
    // Far away airport for range testing (Honolulu)
    ["PHNL", { icao: "PHNL", latitude: 21.3187, longitude: -157.9225, longest_runway_ft: 12300, customs_capable: true }],
  ]);
}

/** Operator certification data for tests. */
function makeCerts(): Map<string, OperatorCertData> {
  return new Map([
    ["op_good", { operatorId: "op_good", part135Active: true, insuranceCurrent: true, argusRating: "gold", wyvernRating: null }],
    ["op_expired", { operatorId: "op_expired", part135Active: false, insuranceCurrent: false, argusRating: null, wyvernRating: null }],
    ["op_silver", { operatorId: "op_silver", part135Active: true, insuranceCurrent: true, argusRating: "silver", wyvernRating: null }],
    ["op_unverified", { operatorId: "op_unverified", part135Active: true, insuranceCurrent: null, argusRating: null, wyvernRating: null }],
  ]);
}

/** Standard operator match object. */
function makeOperator(id: string = "op_good", overrides?: Partial<DealContext["sourcing"]["selectedOperator"]>) {
  return {
    operatorId: id,
    operatorName: "Test Aviation",
    phone: "+1-555-0100",
    baseAirport: "KTEB",
    aircraftType: "Phenom 300E",
    aircraftTail: "N300TE",
    score: 0.85,
    scoreBreakdown: { proximity: 0.9, laneHistory: 0.5, responseTime: 0.8, pricingAccuracy: 0.9, relationship: 0.7 },
    distanceToDeparture: 0,
    ...overrides,
  };
}

// ── Aircraft Performance Lookup ──────────────────────────────────────────

describe("resolveAircraftPerf", () => {
  it("returns exact match for known aircraft type", () => {
    const perf = resolveAircraftPerf("Phenom 300E", null);
    expect(perf).not.toBeNull();
    expect(perf!.range_nm).toBe(2010);
    expect(perf!.runway_required_ft).toBe(3209);
    expect(perf!.passenger_capacity).toBe(8);
  });

  it("falls back to class defaults when type unknown", () => {
    const perf = resolveAircraftPerf("Unknown Jet", "heavy");
    expect(perf).not.toBeNull();
    expect(perf!.category).toBe("heavy");
  });

  it("returns null when both type and class are unknown", () => {
    const perf = resolveAircraftPerf("Unknown", "unknown_class");
    expect(perf).toBeNull();
  });

  it("has data for all 10 aircraft types", () => {
    expect(Object.keys(AIRCRAFT_PERF)).toHaveLength(10);
    for (const perf of Object.values(AIRCRAFT_PERF)) {
      expect(perf.range_nm).toBeGreaterThan(0);
      expect(perf.runway_required_ft).toBeGreaterThan(0);
      expect(perf.passenger_capacity).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    }
  });
});

// ── After INTAKE ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

describe("checksAfterIntake", () => {
  it("passes for valid KTEB→KPBI Phenom 300E trip", () => {
    const ctx = makeDealContext();
    const checks = checksAfterIntake(ctx, makeAirports());

    const failed = checks.filter((c) => !c.passed);
    expect(failed).toHaveLength(0);

    const names = checks.map((c) => c.name);
    expect(names).toContain("departure_airport");
    expect(names).toContain("arrival_airport");
    expect(names).toContain("runway_departure");
    expect(names).toContain("runway_arrival");
    expect(names).toContain("range");
    expect(names).toContain("pax_capacity");
    expect(names).toContain("date_future");
  });

  it("fails on missing departure airport", () => {
    const ctx = makeDealContext({ departure: "" });
    const checks = checksAfterIntake(ctx, makeAirports());

    const depCheck = checks.find((c) => c.name === "departure_airport");
    expect(depCheck).toBeDefined();
    expect(depCheck!.passed).toBe(false);
    expect(depCheck!.severity).toBe("critical");
  });

  it("warns on unknown airport (not in database)", () => {
    const ctx = makeDealContext({ departure: "KZZZ" });
    const checks = checksAfterIntake(ctx, makeAirports());

    const depCheck = checks.find((c) => c.name === "departure_airport");
    expect(depCheck).toBeDefined();
    expect(depCheck!.severity).toBe("warning");
    expect(depCheck!.message).toContain("not in airport database");
  });

  // ── Runway Checks ──────────────────────────────────────────────────

  it("fails when runway too short for aircraft", () => {
    // K12N has 2500ft runway, Phenom 300E needs 3209 + 10% = 3530ft
    const ctx = makeDealContext({ departure: "K12N" });
    const checks = checksAfterIntake(ctx, makeAirports());

    const runwayCheck = checks.find((c) => c.name === "runway_departure");
    expect(runwayCheck).toBeDefined();
    expect(runwayCheck!.passed).toBe(false);
    expect(runwayCheck!.severity).toBe("critical");
    expect(runwayCheck!.message).toContain("2500");
    expect(runwayCheck!.message).toContain("too short");
  });

  it("passes when runway is adequate", () => {
    // KTEB has 7000ft, Phenom 300E needs 3530ft with margin
    const ctx = makeDealContext();
    const checks = checksAfterIntake(ctx, makeAirports());

    const runwayDep = checks.find((c) => c.name === "runway_departure");
    const runwayArr = checks.find((c) => c.name === "runway_arrival");
    expect(runwayDep!.passed).toBe(true);
    expect(runwayArr!.passed).toBe(true);
  });

  it("applies 10% safety margin to runway requirement", () => {
    const ctx = makeDealContext({ aircraftType: "Phenom 300E" });
    const checks = checksAfterIntake(ctx, makeAirports());

    // Phenom 300E: 3209ft * 1.10 = 3530ft margin
    const runwayCheck = checks.find((c) => c.name === "runway_departure");
    expect(runwayCheck!.message).toContain("3530"); // 3209 * 1.10 = 3529.9 → ceil = 3530
  });

  // ── Range Checks (haversine fix for old bug at line 224) ───────────

  it("passes for KTEB→KPBI (within range)", () => {
    // KTEB→KPBI is ~950nm, Phenom 300E has 2010nm range
    const ctx = makeDealContext();
    const checks = checksAfterIntake(ctx, makeAirports());

    const rangeCheck = checks.find((c) => c.name === "range");
    expect(rangeCheck).toBeDefined();
    expect(rangeCheck!.passed).toBe(true);
    expect(rangeCheck!.message).toContain("within");
  });

  it("fails for KTEB→PHNL with Phenom 300E (exceeds range)", () => {
    // KTEB→PHNL (Honolulu) is ~4300nm, Phenom 300E safe range is 2010/1.10 = ~1827nm
    const ctx = makeDealContext({ arrival: "PHNL" });
    const checks = checksAfterIntake(ctx, makeAirports());

    const rangeCheck = checks.find((c) => c.name === "range");
    expect(rangeCheck).toBeDefined();
    expect(rangeCheck!.passed).toBe(false);
    expect(rangeCheck!.severity).toBe("critical");
    expect(rangeCheck!.message).toContain("exceeds");
  });

  it("uses haversine to compute actual distance (not a no-op)", () => {
    // Verify that the range check actually computes distance
    const ctx = makeDealContext();
    const checks = checksAfterIntake(ctx, makeAirports());

    const rangeCheck = checks.find((c) => c.name === "range");
    expect(rangeCheck).toBeDefined();
    // The message should contain the computed distance
    expect(rangeCheck!.message).toMatch(/\d+nm/);
  });

  it("applies 10% IFR reserve margin to range", () => {
    // Phenom 300E range 2010nm → safe range = 2010/1.10 = 1827nm
    const ctx = makeDealContext();
    const checks = checksAfterIntake(ctx, makeAirports());

    const rangeCheck = checks.find((c) => c.name === "range");
    expect(rangeCheck!.message).toContain("1827");
  });

  // ── Pax Capacity ───────────────────────────────────────────────────

  it("fails when pax exceeds capacity", () => {
    // Phenom 300E seats 8
    const ctx = makeDealContext({ pax: 10 });
    const checks = checksAfterIntake(ctx, makeAirports());

    const paxCheck = checks.find((c) => c.name === "pax_capacity");
    expect(paxCheck!.passed).toBe(false);
    expect(paxCheck!.severity).toBe("critical");
    expect(paxCheck!.message).toContain("exceeds");
  });

  it("warns when pax equals capacity (no margin)", () => {
    // Phenom 300E seats 8
    const ctx = makeDealContext({ pax: 8 });
    const checks = checksAfterIntake(ctx, makeAirports());

    const paxCheck = checks.find((c) => c.name === "pax_capacity");
    expect(paxCheck!.passed).toBe(true);
    expect(paxCheck!.severity).toBe("warning");
    expect(paxCheck!.message).toContain("max capacity");
  });

  it("passes when pax well within capacity", () => {
    const ctx = makeDealContext({ pax: 4 });
    const checks = checksAfterIntake(ctx, makeAirports());

    const paxCheck = checks.find((c) => c.name === "pax_capacity");
    expect(paxCheck!.passed).toBe(true);
    expect(paxCheck!.severity).toBe("info");
    expect(paxCheck!.message).toContain("4 seats margin");
  });

  // ── Date ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  it("fails when date is in the past", () => {
    const ctx = makeDealContext({ date: "2020-01-01" });
    const checks = checksAfterIntake(ctx, makeAirports());

    const dateCheck = checks.find((c) => c.name === "date_future");
    expect(dateCheck!.passed).toBe(false);
    expect(dateCheck!.severity).toBe("critical");
    expect(dateCheck!.message).toContain("past");
  });

  it("passes when date is in the future", () => {
    const ctx = makeDealContext({ date: "2027-06-15" });
    const checks = checksAfterIntake(ctx, makeAirports());

    const dateCheck = checks.find((c) => c.name === "date_future");
    expect(dateCheck!.passed).toBe(true);
  });

  it("fails when no date specified", () => {
    const ctx = makeDealContext({ date: "" });
    const checks = checksAfterIntake(ctx, makeAirports());

    const dateCheck = checks.find((c) => c.name === "date_future");
    expect(dateCheck!.passed).toBe(false);
    expect(dateCheck!.message).toContain("No trip date");
  });

  // ── Class Fallback ─────────────────────────────────────────────────

  it("uses class defaults when aircraft type unknown", () => {
    const ctx = makeDealContext({ aircraftType: null, aircraftClass: "heavy" });
    const checks = checksAfterIntake(ctx, makeAirports());

    // Should still have range and runway checks using heavy defaults
    const rangeCheck = checks.find((c) => c.name === "range");
    expect(rangeCheck).toBeDefined();
    const runwayCheck = checks.find((c) => c.name === "runway_departure");
    expect(runwayCheck).toBeDefined();
  });
});

// ── After ESTIMATE ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────

describe("checksAfterEstimate", () => {
  it("passes when budget overlaps estimate range", () => {
    const ctx = makeDealContext();
    updateDealContext(ctx, {
      estimate: {
        source: "aviapages",
        flightTime: 150,
        priceRange: { low: 25000, high: 35000 },
        fuelBurn: 300,
        positioning: 0,
        calculatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      },
    });

    const checks = checksAfterEstimate(ctx);
    const budgetCheck = checks.find((c) => c.name === "budget_overlap");
    expect(budgetCheck).toBeDefined();
    expect(budgetCheck!.passed).toBe(true);
    expect(budgetCheck!.message).toContain("overlaps");
  });

  it("fails when budget max below estimate floor", () => {
    const ctx = makeDealContext();
    updateDealContext(ctx, {
      client: { budget: { min: 10000, max: 15000 } },
      estimate: {
        source: "aviapages",
        flightTime: 150,
        priceRange: { low: 25000, high: 35000 },
        fuelBurn: 300,
        positioning: 0,
        calculatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      },
    });

    const checks = checksAfterEstimate(ctx);
    const budgetCheck = checks.find((c) => c.name === "budget_overlap");
    expect(budgetCheck!.passed).toBe(false);
    expect(budgetCheck!.severity).toBe("critical");
    expect(budgetCheck!.message).toContain("below estimate floor");
  });

  it("warns when client min above estimate high (premium expectation)", () => {
    const ctx = makeDealContext();
    updateDealContext(ctx, {
      client: { budget: { min: 50000, max: 80000 } },
      estimate: {
        source: "aviapages",
        flightTime: 150,
        priceRange: { low: 25000, high: 35000 },
        fuelBurn: 300,
        positioning: 0,
        calculatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      },
    });

    const checks = checksAfterEstimate(ctx);
    const budgetCheck = checks.find((c) => c.name === "budget_overlap");
    expect(budgetCheck!.severity).toBe("warning");
    expect(budgetCheck!.message).toContain("premium");
  });

  it("warns on suspiciously low estimate (possible API error)", () => {
    const ctx = makeDealContext();
    updateDealContext(ctx, {
      estimate: {
        source: "aviapages",
        flightTime: 150,
        priceRange: { low: 500, high: 1000 },
        fuelBurn: 300,
        positioning: 0,
        calculatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      },
    });

    const checks = checksAfterEstimate(ctx);
    const sanityCheck = checks.find((c) => c.name === "estimate_sanity");
    expect(sanityCheck).toBeDefined();
    expect(sanityCheck!.severity).toBe("warning");
    expect(sanityCheck!.message).toContain("unusually low");
  });

  it("warns when no estimate available", () => {
    const ctx = makeDealContext();
    // estimate.priceRange is null by default

    const checks = checksAfterEstimate(ctx);
    const estCheck = checks.find((c) => c.name === "estimate_available");
    expect(estCheck!.severity).toBe("warning");
  });

  it("warns when no budget set", () => {
    const ctx = makeDealContext();
    updateDealContext(ctx, {
      client: { budget: null },
      estimate: {
        source: "aviapages",
        flightTime: 150,
        priceRange: { low: 25000, high: 35000 },
        fuelBurn: null,
        positioning: null,
        calculatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      },
    });

    const checks = checksAfterEstimate(ctx);
    const budgetCheck = checks.find((c) => c.name === "budget_overlap");
    expect(budgetCheck!.severity).toBe("warning");
    expect(budgetCheck!.message).toContain("not set");
  });
});

// ── After SOURCING ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────

describe("checksAfterSourcing", () => {
  it("passes for certified operator with gold rating", () => {
    const ctx = makeDealContext();
    updateDealContext(ctx, {
      sourcing: {
        selectedOperator: makeOperator("op_good"),
        backupOperator: null,
        rankingReason: "Closest operator",
        laneHistory: [],
      },
    });

    const checks = checksAfterSourcing(ctx, makeCerts());
    const certCheck = checks.find((c) => c.name === "part135_cert");
    const insCheck = checks.find((c) => c.name === "insurance");
    const safetyCheck = checks.find((c) => c.name === "safety_rating");

    expect(certCheck!.passed).toBe(true);
    expect(insCheck!.passed).toBe(true);
    expect(safetyCheck!.passed).toBe(true);
    expect(safetyCheck!.message).toContain("ARGUS gold");
  });

  it("fails for operator with expired Part 135", () => {
    const ctx = makeDealContext();
    updateDealContext(ctx, {
      sourcing: {
        selectedOperator: makeOperator("op_expired"),
        backupOperator: null,
        rankingReason: "Only option",
        laneHistory: [],
      },
    });

    const checks = checksAfterSourcing(ctx, makeCerts());
    const certCheck = checks.find((c) => c.name === "part135_cert");
    expect(certCheck!.passed).toBe(false);
    expect(certCheck!.severity).toBe("critical");
    expect(certCheck!.message).toContain("NOT active");
  });

  it("fails for operator with expired insurance", () => {
    const ctx = makeDealContext();
    updateDealContext(ctx, {
      sourcing: {
        selectedOperator: makeOperator("op_expired"),
        backupOperator: null,
        rankingReason: "Only option",
        laneHistory: [],
      },
    });

    const checks = checksAfterSourcing(ctx, makeCerts());
    const insCheck = checks.find((c) => c.name === "insurance");
    expect(insCheck!.passed).toBe(false);
    expect(insCheck!.severity).toBe("critical");
  });

  it("warns for silver safety rating", () => {
    const ctx = makeDealContext();
    updateDealContext(ctx, {
      sourcing: {
        selectedOperator: makeOperator("op_silver"),
        backupOperator: null,
        rankingReason: "Best available",
        laneHistory: [],
      },
    });

    const checks = checksAfterSourcing(ctx, makeCerts());
    const safetyCheck = checks.find((c) => c.name === "safety_rating");
    expect(safetyCheck!.severity).toBe("warning");
    expect(safetyCheck!.message).toContain("Silver");
  });

  it("warns when no cert data available (cold start)", () => {
    const ctx = makeDealContext();
    updateDealContext(ctx, {
      sourcing: {
        selectedOperator: makeOperator("op_unknown"),
        backupOperator: null,
        rankingReason: "New operator",
        laneHistory: [],
      },
    });

    // Pass cert map that doesn't have this operator
    const checks = checksAfterSourcing(ctx, makeCerts());
    const certCheck = checks.find((c) => c.name === "part135_cert");
    expect(certCheck!.severity).toBe("warning");
    expect(certCheck!.message).toContain("Verify manually");
  });

  it("warns when insurance status is null (unverified)", () => {
    const ctx = makeDealContext();
    updateDealContext(ctx, {
      sourcing: {
        selectedOperator: makeOperator("op_unverified"),
        backupOperator: null,
        rankingReason: "Only option",
        laneHistory: [],
      },
    });

    const checks = checksAfterSourcing(ctx, makeCerts());
    const insCheck = checks.find((c) => c.name === "insurance");
    expect(insCheck!.severity).toBe("warning");
    expect(insCheck!.message).toContain("unverified");
  });

  it("fails when no operator selected", () => {
    const ctx = makeDealContext();
    // sourcing.selectedOperator is null by default

    const checks = checksAfterSourcing(ctx);
    const opCheck = checks.find((c) => c.name === "operator_selected");
    expect(opCheck!.passed).toBe(false);
    expect(opCheck!.severity).toBe("critical");
  });

  it("warns when aircraft type mismatch", () => {
    const ctx = makeDealContext({ aircraftType: "Citation CJ3+" });
    updateDealContext(ctx, {
      sourcing: {
        selectedOperator: makeOperator("op_good", { aircraftType: "Phenom 300E" }),
        backupOperator: null,
        rankingReason: "Closest",
        laneHistory: [],
      },
    });

    const checks = checksAfterSourcing(ctx, makeCerts());
    const matchCheck = checks.find((c) => c.name === "aircraft_match");
    expect(matchCheck).toBeDefined();
    expect(matchCheck!.severity).toBe("warning");
    expect(matchCheck!.message).toContain("Citation CJ3+");
    expect(matchCheck!.message).toContain("Phenom 300E");
  });
});

// ── After QUOTE ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

describe("checksAfterQuote", () => {
  function ctxWithQuote(operatorPrice: number, margin: number, estimateLow: number, estimateHigh: number): DealContext {
    const ctx = makeDealContext();
    const osaPrice = operatorPrice / (1 - margin);
    updateDealContext(ctx, {
      estimate: {
        source: "aviapages",
        flightTime: 150,
        priceRange: { low: estimateLow, high: estimateHigh },
        fuelBurn: 300,
        positioning: 0,
        calculatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      },
      quote: {
        operatorPrice,
        osaPrice,
        margin,
        validUntil: new Date(Date.now() + 120 * 60_000).toISOString(), // 2 hours
        aircraft: { type: "Phenom 300E", tail: "N300TE", year: 2022 },
        quotedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      },
    });
    return ctx;
  }

  it("passes when quote within 25% of estimate median", () => {
    // Estimate: $25K-$35K, median $30K. Quote $28K → 6.7% off. Pass.
    const ctx = ctxWithQuote(28000, 0.08, 25000, 35000);
    const checks = checksAfterQuote(ctx);

    const vsEstimate = checks.find((c) => c.name === "quote_vs_estimate");
    expect(vsEstimate!.passed).toBe(true);
    expect(vsEstimate!.message).toContain("within");
  });

  it("warns when quote more than 25% off estimate median", () => {
    // Estimate: $25K-$35K, median $30K. Quote $42K → 40% off. Warn.
    const ctx = ctxWithQuote(42000, 0.08, 25000, 35000);
    const checks = checksAfterQuote(ctx);

    const vsEstimate = checks.find((c) => c.name === "quote_vs_estimate");
    expect(vsEstimate!.severity).toBe("warning");
    expect(vsEstimate!.message).toContain("40%");
  });

  it("passes when margin within policy (8-15%)", () => {
    const ctx = ctxWithQuote(28000, 0.10, 25000, 35000);
    const checks = checksAfterQuote(ctx);

    const marginCheck = checks.find((c) => c.name === "margin_policy");
    expect(marginCheck!.passed).toBe(true);
    expect(marginCheck!.message).toContain("within policy");
  });

  it("warns when margin below minimum (6%)", () => {
    const ctx = ctxWithQuote(28000, 0.04, 25000, 35000);
    const checks = checksAfterQuote(ctx);

    const marginCheck = checks.find((c) => c.name === "margin_policy");
    expect(marginCheck!.severity).toBe("warning");
    expect(marginCheck!.message).toContain("below minimum");
  });

  it("warns when margin above maximum (15%)", () => {
    const ctx = ctxWithQuote(28000, 0.20, 25000, 35000);
    const checks = checksAfterQuote(ctx);

    const marginCheck = checks.find((c) => c.name === "margin_policy");
    expect(marginCheck!.severity).toBe("warning");
    expect(marginCheck!.message).toContain("above maximum");
  });

  it("warns when quote validity under 30 minutes", () => {
    const ctx = makeDealContext();
    updateDealContext(ctx, {
      quote: {
        operatorPrice: 28000,
        osaPrice: 30400,
        margin: 0.08,
        validUntil: new Date(Date.now() + 15 * 60_000).toISOString(), // 15 min
        aircraft: { type: "Phenom 300E", tail: "N300TE", year: 2022 },
        quotedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      },
    });

    const checks = checksAfterQuote(ctx);
    const validityCheck = checks.find((c) => c.name === "quote_validity");
    expect(validityCheck!.severity).toBe("warning");
    expect(validityCheck!.message).toContain("Under");
  });

  it("warns when no operator quote entered yet", () => {
    const ctx = makeDealContext();
    const checks = checksAfterQuote(ctx);

    const quoteCheck = checks.find((c) => c.name === "quote_available");
    expect(quoteCheck!.severity).toBe("warning");
    expect(quoteCheck!.message).toContain("No operator quote");
  });
});

// ── Before PRESENTATION ──────────────────────────────────────────────────

describe("checksBeforePresentation", () => {
  it("passes when all required data present", () => {
    const ctx = makeDealContext();
    updateDealContext(ctx, {
      sourcing: {
        selectedOperator: makeOperator(),
        backupOperator: null,
        rankingReason: "Best match",
        laneHistory: [],
      },
      quote: {
        operatorPrice: 28000,
        osaPrice: 30400,
        margin: 0.08,
        validUntil: new Date(Date.now() + 120 * 60_000).toISOString(),
        aircraft: { type: "Phenom 300E", tail: "N300TE", year: 2022 },
        quotedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      },
    });

    const checks = checksBeforePresentation(ctx);
    const failed = checks.filter((c) => !c.passed);
    expect(failed).toHaveLength(0);
    expect(checks.find((c) => c.name === "pre_present_complete")).toBeDefined();
  });

  it("fails when no operator selected", () => {
    const ctx = makeDealContext();
    updateDealContext(ctx, {
      quote: {
        operatorPrice: 28000,
        osaPrice: 30400,
        margin: 0.08,
        validUntil: null,
        aircraft: null,
        quotedAt: null,
      },
    });

    const checks = checksBeforePresentation(ctx);
    const opCheck = checks.find((c) => c.name === "pre_present_operator");
    expect(opCheck!.passed).toBe(false);
    expect(opCheck!.severity).toBe("critical");
  });

  it("fails when no OSA price", () => {
    const ctx = makeDealContext();
    updateDealContext(ctx, {
      sourcing: {
        selectedOperator: makeOperator(),
        backupOperator: null,
        rankingReason: "Best match",
        laneHistory: [],
      },
    });
    // quote.osaPrice is null by default

    const checks = checksBeforePresentation(ctx);
    const priceCheck = checks.find((c) => c.name === "pre_present_price");
    expect(priceCheck!.passed).toBe(false);
  });
});

// ── Full Guardian (main entry point) ─────────────────────────────────────

describe("guardian (main entry)", () => {
  it("returns passed=true for valid intake", async () => {
    const ctx = makeDealContext();
    const result = await guardian("intake", ctx, { airports: makeAirports() });

    expect(result.pass.stage).toBe("intake");
    expect(result.pass.passed).toBe(true);
    expect(result.pass.checks.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(result.pass.timestamp).toBeDefined();
  });

  it("returns passed=false when any critical check fails", async () => {
    // Too many pax for Phenom 300E
    const ctx = makeDealContext({ pax: 12 });
    const result = await guardian("intake", ctx, { airports: makeAirports() });

    expect(result.pass.passed).toBe(false);
    const critical = result.pass.checks.filter((c) => !c.passed && c.severity === "critical");
    expect(critical.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  });

  it("returns passed=true when only warnings present (no double-count)", async () => {
    // Pax at max capacity → warning, but not a failure
    const ctx = makeDealContext({ pax: 8 }); // Phenom 300E seats exactly 8
    const result = await guardian("intake", ctx, { airports: makeAirports() });

    expect(result.pass.passed).toBe(true);
    // Verify warning exists but doesn't cause failure
    const warnings = result.pass.checks.filter((c) => c.severity === "warning");
