      expect(result.selectedOperator!.operatorName).toBe("Priester Aviation");
      expect(result.selectedOperator!.baseAirport).toBe("KPWK");
      expect(result.selectedOperator!.aircraftType).toBe("Citation XLS");
      // Should NOT include full OperatorMatch fields like score
      expect((result.selectedOperator as any).score).toBeUndefined();
    });

    it("returns null quote when operatorPrice is null", () => {
      const ctx = createDealContext("sim");
      const result = buildGuardianContext("intake", ctx);
      expect(result.quote).toBeNull();
    });

    it("returns populated quote when operatorPrice is set", () => {
      const ctx = makePopulatedCtx();
      const result = buildGuardianContext("quoted", ctx);
      expect(result.quote).not.toBeNull();
      expect(result.quote!.operatorPrice).toBe(24000);
      expect(result.quote!.osaPrice).toBe(26400);
      expect(result.quote!.margin).toBe(0.09);
    });

    it("returns clientBudget when set on ctx", () => {
      const ctx = makePopulatedCtx();
      const result = buildGuardianContext("estimated", ctx);
      expect(result.clientBudget).not.toBeNull();
      expect(result.clientBudget!.min).toBe(20000);
      expect(result.clientBudget!.max).toBe(40000);
    });

    it("returns null clientBudget when not set", () => {
      const ctx = createDealContext("sim");
      const result = buildGuardianContext("intake", ctx);
      expect(result.clientBudget).toBeNull();
    });
  });

  // ── buildEstimateContext ──────────────────────────────────────────────

  describe("buildEstimateContext", () => {
    it("returns trip fields and null routeIntel without stores", () => {
      const ctx = makePopulatedCtx();
      const result = buildEstimateContext(ctx);
      expect(result.trip.departure).toBe("KTEB");
      expect(result.trip.arrival).toBe("KMIA");
      expect(result.trip.aircraftClass).toBe("midsize");
      expect(result.routeIntel).toBeNull();
    });

    it("returns routeIntel when store has matching route and aircraftClass", () => {
      const ctx = makePopulatedCtx();
      const intel = makeRouteIntel();
      const stores = makeFakeStores({ routeIntel: intel });
      const result = buildEstimateContext(ctx, stores);
      expect(result.routeIntel).not.toBeNull();
      expect(result.routeIntel!.route).toBe("KTEB-KMIA");
      expect(result.routeIntel!.avgPrice30d).toBe(25000);
    });

    it("returns null routeIntel when aircraftClass is null (even if store has data)", () => {
      const ctx = makePopulatedCtx();
      ctx.trip.aircraftClass = null;
      const intel = makeRouteIntel();
      const stores = makeFakeStores({ routeIntel: intel });
      const result = buildEstimateContext(ctx, stores);
      expect(result.routeIntel).toBeNull();
    });

    it("returns null routeIntel when store has no matching route", () => {
      const ctx = makePopulatedCtx();
      ctx.trip.departure = "KJFK";
      const intel = makeRouteIntel(); // KTEB-KMIA
      const stores = makeFakeStores({ routeIntel: intel });
      const result = buildEstimateContext(ctx, stores);
      expect(result.routeIntel).toBeNull();
    });
  });

  // ── buildPriceWallContext ─────────────────────────────────────────────

  describe("buildPriceWallContext", () => {
    it("returns trip, physicsEstimate, and clientBudget when populated", () => {
      const ctx = makePopulatedCtx();
      const result = buildPriceWallContext(ctx);
      expect(result.trip.departure).toBe("KTEB");
      expect(result.trip.pax).toBe(4);
      expect(result.physicsEstimate).toEqual({ low: 22000, high: 28000 });
      expect(result.clientBudget).toEqual({ min: 20000, max: 40000 });
    });

    it("returns null physicsEstimate and clientBudget when empty ctx", () => {
      const ctx = createDealContext("sim");
      const result = buildPriceWallContext(ctx);
      expect(result.physicsEstimate).toBeNull();
      expect(result.clientBudget).toBeNull();
    });

    it("does not include aircraftClass in trip (only 4 fields)", () => {
      const ctx = makePopulatedCtx();
      const result = buildPriceWallContext(ctx);
      expect((result.trip as any).aircraftClass).toBeUndefined();
    });
  });

  // ── buildSourcingContext ──────────────────────────────────────────────

  describe("buildSourcingContext", () => {
    it("injects operatorProfiles from RouteIntel.topOperators via store", () => {
      const ctx = makePopulatedCtx();
      const intel = makeRouteIntel();
      const operators = [
        makeOperatorProfile("Priester Aviation"),
        makeOperatorProfile("NetJets"),
        makeOperatorProfile("Flexjet"),
      ];
      const stores = makeFakeStores({ routeIntel: intel, operators });
      const result = buildSourcingContext(ctx, stores);
      expect(result.operatorProfiles).toHaveLength(3);
      expect(result.operatorProfiles[0]!.name).toBe("Priester Aviation");
    });

    it("returns empty operatorProfiles when no routeIntel", () => {
      const ctx = makePopulatedCtx();
      ctx.trip.aircraftClass = null;
      const stores = makeFakeStores();
      const result = buildSourcingContext(ctx, stores);
      expect(result.operatorProfiles).toEqual([]);
    });

    it("returns empty operatorProfiles when routeIntel exists but no operatorProfiles store", () => {
      const ctx = makePopulatedCtx();
      const intel = makeRouteIntel();
      // Store with routeIntel but operatorProfiles store returns empty
      const stores = makeFakeStores({ routeIntel: intel });
      const result = buildSourcingContext(ctx, stores);
      expect(result.operatorProfiles).toEqual([]);
    });

    it("sets budgetCeiling from client.budget.max", () => {
      const ctx = makePopulatedCtx();
      const result = buildSourcingContext(ctx);
      expect(result.budgetCeiling).toBe(40000);
    });

    it("sets budgetCeiling to null when no budget", () => {
      const ctx = createDealContext("sim");
      const result = buildSourcingContext(ctx);
      expect(result.budgetCeiling).toBeNull();
    });
  });

  // ── buildQuoteContext ─────────────────────────────────────────────────

  describe("buildQuoteContext", () => {
    it("sets ghostPositioningValue from selectedOperator.distanceToDeparture", () => {
      const ctx = makePopulatedCtx();
      const result = buildQuoteContext(ctx);
      expect(result.ghostPositioningValue).toBe(42);
    });

    it("returns null ghostPositioningValue when no selectedOperator", () => {
      const ctx = createDealContext("sim");
      const result = buildQuoteContext(ctx);
      expect(result.ghostPositioningValue).toBeNull();
    });

    it("projects selectedOperator with score but without full scoreBreakdown", () => {
      const ctx = makePopulatedCtx();
      const result = buildQuoteContext(ctx);
      expect(result.selectedOperator).not.toBeNull();
      expect(result.selectedOperator!.operatorName).toBe("Priester Aviation");
      expect(result.selectedOperator!.score).toBe(0.92);
      expect((result.selectedOperator as any).scoreBreakdown).toBeUndefined();
    });

    it("returns null selectedOperator when not sourced", () => {
      const ctx = createDealContext("sim");
      const result = buildQuoteContext(ctx);
      expect(result.selectedOperator).toBeNull();
    });
  });

  // ── buildPresentContext ───────────────────────────────────────────────

  describe("buildPresentContext", () => {
    it("builds route string as departure-arrival", () => {
      const ctx = makePopulatedCtx();
      const result = buildPresentContext(ctx);
      expect(result.route).toBe("KTEB-KMIA");
    });

    it("includes clientName and osaPrice", () => {
      const ctx = makePopulatedCtx();
      const result = buildPresentContext(ctx);
      expect(result.clientName).toBe("John Doe");
      expect(result.osaPrice).toBe(26400);
    });

    it("returns null aircraft when quote.aircraft is null", () => {
      const ctx = createDealContext("sim");
      const result = buildPresentContext(ctx);
      expect(result.aircraft).toBeNull();
    });

    it("returns aircraft object when quote.aircraft is set", () => {
      const ctx = makePopulatedCtx();
      const result = buildPresentContext(ctx);
      expect(result.aircraft).not.toBeNull();
      expect(result.aircraft!.type).toBe("Citation XLS");
      expect(result.aircraft!.tail).toBe("N123PA");
      expect(result.aircraft!.year).toBe(2019);
    });

    it("returns null osaPrice when no quote", () => {
      const ctx = createDealContext("sim");
      const result = buildPresentContext(ctx);
      expect(result.osaPrice).toBeNull();
    });
  });

  // ── buildRespondContext ───────────────────────────────────────────────

  describe("buildRespondContext", () => {
    it("returns exactly 5 fields", () => {
      const ctx = makePopulatedCtx();
      const result = buildRespondContext(ctx, "quote_request");
      const keys = Object.keys(result);
      expect(keys).toHaveLength(5);
      expect(keys.sort()).toEqual(["clientName", "dealStage", "intent", "osaPrice", "route"].sort());
    });

    it("passes intent through", () => {
      const ctx = makePopulatedCtx();
      const result = buildRespondContext(ctx, "follow_up");
      expect(result.intent).toBe("follow_up");
    });

    it("uses ctx.stage as dealStage", () => {
      const ctx = makePopulatedCtx();
      const result = buildRespondContext(ctx, "greeting");
      expect(result.dealStage).toBe("quoted");
    });

    it("builds route string from trip", () => {
      const ctx = makePopulatedCtx();
      const result = buildRespondContext(ctx, "info");
      expect(result.route).toBe("KTEB-KMIA");
    });
  });

  // ── Mutation Safety ───────────────────────────────────────────────────

  describe("mutation safety", () => {
    it("buildGuardianContext trip is a new object (not a reference)", () => {
      const ctx = makePopulatedCtx();
      const result = buildGuardianContext("estimated", ctx);
      result.trip.pax = 99;
      expect(ctx.trip.pax).toBe(4);
    });

    it("buildPriceWallContext physicsEstimate is a new object", () => {
      const ctx = makePopulatedCtx();
      const result = buildPriceWallContext(ctx);
      result.physicsEstimate!.low = 999;
      expect(ctx.estimate.priceRange!.low).toBe(22000);
    });

    it("buildPriceWallContext clientBudget is a new object", () => {
      const ctx = makePopulatedCtx();
      const result = buildPriceWallContext(ctx);
      result.clientBudget!.min = 999;
      expect(ctx.client.budget!.min).toBe(20000);
    });

    it("buildPresentContext aircraft is a new object", () => {
      const ctx = makePopulatedCtx();
      const result = buildPresentContext(ctx);
      result.aircraft!.year = 1900;
      expect(ctx.quote.aircraft!.year).toBe(2019);
    });

    it("buildQuoteContext clientBudget is a new object", () => {
      const ctx = makePopulatedCtx();
      const result = buildQuoteContext(ctx);
      result.clientBudget!.max = 1;
      expect(ctx.client.budget!.max).toBe(40000);
    });
  });
});

/**
 * Tests for Voice Copilot Pipeline — Twilio + Deepgram + TranscriptProcessor.
 * Per spec: communication-layer.md Sprint 4, FR-5, section 2.3.
 */

import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach } from "vitest";
import { TwilioClient } from "../src/integrations/twilio.js";
import type { FetchFn as TwilioFetchFn } from "../src/integrations/twilio.js";
import { DeepgramClient, AVIATION_VOCABULARY } from "../src/integrations/deepgram.js";
import type { MockableWebSocket, TranscriptCallback } from "../src/integrations/deepgram.js";
import { processChunk, extractAllEntities, extractPrice, generateSummary } from "../src/capabilities/transcript-processor.js";
import type { TranscriptChunk, DetectedEntity, Call } from "../src/schemas/call.js";
import { VoiceCopilotAdapter, generateCallBrief } from "../src/channels/voice.js";
import { CallStore } from "../src/memory/call-store.js";
import { createDealContext, updateDealContext, _resetDealCounter } from "../src/orchestrator/deal-context-manager.js";
import { createTestDb } from "../src/memory/database.js";

beforeEach(() => _resetDealCounter());

// ── Mock helpers ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

function twilioFetch(responses: Record<string, unknown>[] = [{ sid: "CF_test_123", status: "init" }]): TwilioFetchFn {
  let i = 0;
  return async () => ({ ok: true, status: 200, json: async () => responses[Math.min(i++, responses.length - 1)]! });
}

function twilioFetchFail(): TwilioFetchFn {
  return async () => ({ ok: false, status: 500, json: async () => ({ error: "fail" }) });
}

function mockWsFactory(): { factory: (url: string) => MockableWebSocket; ws: MockableWebSocket; lastUrl: string } {
  let lastUrl = "";
  const ws: MockableWebSocket = {
    send: () => {},
    close: () => {},
    onmessage: null,
    onclose: null,
    onerror: null,
    readyState: 1,
  };
  return { factory: (url: string) => { lastUrl = url; return ws; }, ws, get lastUrl() { return lastUrl; } };
}

function makeChunk(text: string, speaker = "counterparty", timestampMs = 0): TranscriptChunk {
  return { chunkId: `chunk_${Date.now()}`, speaker: speaker as "broker" | "counterparty", text, timestampMs, durationMs: 1000, confidence: 0.95, isFinal: true, detectedEntities: [] };
}

function makeCall(): Call {
  return {
    callId: "call_001", dealId: "deal_001", direction: "outbound_to_operator",
    participants: [
      { name: "Phelipe", role: "broker", phone: "+1-773-555-0100" },
      { name: "John", role: "operator", phone: "+1-555-0200" },
    ],
    startedAt: new Date().toISOString(), endedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    durationSec: 330, transcript: [makeChunk("We can do $28,000 all-in for the Phenom 300E")],
    detectedEntities: [{ type: "price", value: "$28,000", raw: "$28,000", speaker: "counterparty", confidence: 0.95, timestampMs: 12000 }],
    summary: "Operator quoted $28,000 for Phenom 300E KTEB→KPBI.",
    recordingUrl: "https://recordings.twilio.com/call_001.wav",
    recordingExpiresAt: null,
  };
}

// ── Twilio Client ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

describe("TwilioClient", () => {
  it("createConference returns conference SID", async () => {
    const client = new TwilioClient({ accountSid: "AC_test", authToken: "token" }, twilioFetch([{ sid: "CF_abc", status: "init" }]));
    const conf = await client.createConference("deal_001");
    expect(conf.conferenceSid).toBe("CF_abc");
    expect(conf.friendlyName).toBe("deal_001");
  });

  it("addParticipant adds with correct label and muted flag", async () => {
    let capturedBody = "";
    const fetch: TwilioFetchFn = async (_url, init) => { capturedBody = init.body as string; return { ok: true, status: 200, json: async () => ({ call_sid: "CA_part1" }) }; };
    const client = new TwilioClient({ accountSid: "AC_test", authToken: "token" }, fetch);

    const part = await client.addParticipant("CF_abc", "+1-555-0100", { label: "broker", muted: false });
    expect(part.callSid).toBe("CA_part1");
    expect(part.label).toBe("broker");
    expect(capturedBody).toContain("Label=broker");
    expect(capturedBody).not.toContain("Muted");
  });

  it("addParticipant includes announceUrl for consent disclosure", async () => {
    let capturedBody = "";
    const fetch: TwilioFetchFn = async (_url, init) => { capturedBody = init.body as string; return { ok: true, status: 200, json: async () => ({ call_sid: "CA_op" }) }; };
    const client = new TwilioClient({ accountSid: "AC_test", authToken: "token" }, fetch);

    await client.addParticipant("CF_abc", "+1-555-0200", {
      label: "operator", muted: false, announceUrl: "/audio/disclosure.mp3",
    });
    expect(capturedBody).toContain("AnnounceUrl");
    expect(capturedBody).toContain("disclosure.mp3");
  });

  it("startStream sends correct WebSocket URL", async () => {
    let capturedBody = "";
    const fetch: TwilioFetchFn = async (_url, init) => { capturedBody = init.body as string; return { ok: true, status: 200, json: async () => ({ sid: "MZ_stream" }) }; };
    const client = new TwilioClient({ accountSid: "AC_test", authToken: "token" }, fetch);

    const stream = await client.startStream("CF_abc", "wss://transcribe.example.com");
    expect(stream.streamSid).toBe("MZ_stream");
    expect(capturedBody).toContain("transcribe.example.com");
  });

  it("endConference terminates cleanly", async () => {
    let capturedBody = "";
    const fetch: TwilioFetchFn = async (_url, init) => { capturedBody = init.body as string; return { ok: true, status: 200, json: async () => ({}) }; };
    const client = new TwilioClient({ accountSid: "AC_test", authToken: "token" }, fetch);

    await client.endConference("CF_abc");
    expect(capturedBody).toContain("Status=completed");
  });
});

// ── Deepgram Client ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────

describe("DeepgramClient", () => {
  it("createStreamingSession opens with correct config", () => {
    const { factory } = mockWsFactory();
    const client = new DeepgramClient({ apiKey: "dg_test" }, factory);

    const session = client.createStreamingSession();
    expect(session.model).toBe("nova-2");
    expect(session.language).toBe("en");
    expect(session.vocabularySize).toBeGreaterThan(20);
  });

  it("custom aviation vocabulary included", () => {
    expect(AVIATION_VOCABULARY).toContain("KTEB");
    expect(AVIATION_VOCABULARY).toContain("Phenom 300E");
    expect(AVIATION_VOCABULARY).toContain("Challenger 300");
    expect(AVIATION_VOCABULARY).toContain("all-in");
    expect(AVIATION_VOCABULARY).toContain("Part 135");
  });

  it("onTranscript fires callback with speaker + text + timing", () => {
    const { factory, ws } = mockWsFactory();
    const client = new DeepgramClient({ apiKey: "dg_test" }, factory);
    client.createStreamingSession();

    let received: Parameters<TranscriptCallback>[0] | null = null;
    client.onTranscript((chunk) => { received = chunk; });

    // Simulate Deepgram message
    ws.onmessage!({ data: JSON.stringify({
      channel: { alternatives: [{ transcript: "twenty-eight thousand all-in", confidence: 0.92, words: [{ speaker: 1 }] }] },
      start: 12.5, duration: 2.1, is_final: true,
    }) });

    expect(received).not.toBeNull();
    expect(received!.text).toBe("twenty-eight thousand all-in");
    expect(received!.speaker).toBe(1);
    expect(received!.confidence).toBe(0.92);
    expect(received!.isFinal).toBe(true);
  });

  it("close terminates session", () => {
    const { factory, ws } = mockWsFactory();
    let closed = false;
    ws.close = () => { closed = true; };
    const client = new DeepgramClient({ apiKey: "dg_test" }, factory);
    client.createStreamingSession();

    client.close();
    expect(closed).toBe(true);
  });
});

// ── TranscriptProcessor ──────────────────────────────────────────────────

describe("TranscriptProcessor", () => {
  it("detects price from '$28,000'", () => {
    const entities = processChunk(makeChunk("We can do this for $28,000 all-in"));
    const price = entities.find((e) => e.type === "price");
    expect(price).toBeDefined();
    expect(price!.value).toBe("$28,000");
  });

  it("detects price from 'twenty-eight five' (= $28,500)", () => {
    expect(extractPrice("twenty-eight five")).toBe(28500);
  });

  it("detects price from '$32k'", () => {
    expect(extractPrice("$32k")).toBe(32000);
  });

  it("detects tail number from 'november three hundred echo lima'", () => {
    const entities = processChunk(makeChunk("The tail is november three hundred echo lima"));
    const tail = entities.find((e) => e.type === "tail_number");
    expect(tail).toBeDefined();
    expect(tail!.value).toBe("N300EL");
  });

  it("detects direct tail number 'N300EA'", () => {
    const entities = processChunk(makeChunk("Aircraft N300EA is available"));
    const tail = entities.find((e) => e.type === "tail_number");
    expect(tail!.value).toBe("N300EA");
  });

  it("detects aircraft type 'Challenger 300'", () => {
    const entities = processChunk(makeChunk("We have a Challenger 300 available"));
    const ac = entities.find((e) => e.type === "aircraft_type");
    expect(ac).toBeDefined();
    expect(ac!.value).toContain("Challenger");
  });

  it("detects airport code 'KTEB'", () => {
    const entities = processChunk(makeChunk("Departing from KTEB to KPBI"));
    const airports = entities.filter((e) => e.type === "airport");
    expect(airports.length).toBe(2);
    expect(airports.map((a) => a.value)).toContain("KTEB");
    expect(airports.map((a) => a.value)).toContain("KPBI");
  });

  it("detects availability 'not available'", () => {
    const entities = processChunk(makeChunk("Sorry, that aircraft is not available"));
    const avail = entities.find((e) => e.type === "availability");
    expect(avail).toBeDefined();
    expect(avail!.value).toBe("false");
  });

  it("detects availability 'available'", () => {
    const entities = processChunk(makeChunk("Yes, the aircraft is available for that date"));
    const avail = entities.find((e) => e.type === "availability");
    expect(avail!.value).toBe("true");
  });

  it("detects hold duration 'hold it for two hours'", () => {
    const entities = processChunk(makeChunk("I can hold it for two hours"));
    const hold = entities.find((e) => e.type === "hold_duration");
    expect(hold).toBeDefined();
    expect(hold!.value).toBe("120min");
  });

  it("detects negotiation signal 'best I can do' as firm", () => {
    const entities = processChunk(makeChunk("That's the best I can do on this route"));
    const sig = entities.find((e) => e.type === "negotiation_signal");
    expect(sig).toBeDefined();
    expect(sig!.value).toBe("firm");
  });

  it("detects negotiation signal 'I might be able to' as flexible", () => {
    const entities = processChunk(makeChunk("I might be able to come down a bit"));
    const sig = entities.find((e) => e.type === "negotiation_signal");
    expect(sig!.value).toBe("flexible");
  });

  it("detects negotiation signal 'let me check' as checking", () => {
    const entities = processChunk(makeChunk("Let me check on the schedule"));
    const sig = entities.find((e) => e.type === "negotiation_signal");
    expect(sig!.value).toBe("checking");
  });

  it("returns empty array when no entities in chunk", () => {
    const entities = processChunk(makeChunk("Hello, good morning"));
    expect(entities).toHaveLength(0);
  });

  it("post-call batch extraction finds all entities", () => {
    const chunks = [
      makeChunk("The Phenom 300E N300EA is available from KTEB"),
      makeChunk("Best price is $28,000 all-in"),
      makeChunk("I can hold it for two hours"),
    ];
    const entities = extractAllEntities(chunks);

    const types = entities.map((e) => e.type);
    expect(types).toContain("aircraft_type");
    expect(types).toContain("tail_number");
    expect(types).toContain("airport");
    expect(types).toContain("price");
    expect(types).toContain("hold_duration");
  });

  it("post-call summary generation (mock)", async () => {
    const chunks = [makeChunk("Quoted $28,000 for Phenom 300E")];
    const entities: DetectedEntity[] = [
      { type: "price", value: "$28,000", raw: "$28,000", speaker: "counterparty", confidence: 0.95, timestampMs: 1000 },
    ];

    const summary = await generateSummary(chunks, entities, {
      summarize: async (transcript, ents) => `Call summary: ${ents.length} entities detected.`,
    });

    expect(summary).toContain("1 entities");
  });

  it("default summary without LLM includes prices and aircraft", async () => {
    const chunks = [makeChunk("Challenger 300 available at $32,000")];
    const entities: DetectedEntity[] = [
      { type: "price", value: "$32,000", raw: "$32,000", speaker: "counterparty", confidence: 0.9, timestampMs: 0 },
      { type: "aircraft_type", value: "Challenger 300", raw: "challenger 300", speaker: "counterparty", confidence: 0.9, timestampMs: 0 },
    ];

    const summary = await generateSummary(chunks, entities);
    expect(summary).toContain("$32,000");
    expect(summary).toContain("Challenger 300");
  });
});

// ── Voice Copilot Adapter ────────────────────────────────────────────────

describe("VoiceCopilotAdapter", () => {
  it("receive creates Message with channel=voice_copilot", () => {
    const adapter = new VoiceCopilotAdapter();

    expect(msg.channel).toBe("voice_copilot");
    expect(msg.direction).toBe("inbound");
    expect(msg.from).toBe("+1-555-0200"); // operator
    expect(msg.body).toContain("$28,000");
    expect(msg.threadId).toBe("call_001");
  });

  it("deliver is no-op", async () => {
    const adapter = new VoiceCopilotAdapter();
    const result = await adapter.deliver({} as any);
    expect(result.success).toBe(true);
  });

  it("supportsRealtime returns true", () => {
    expect(new VoiceCopilotAdapter().supportsRealtime()).toBe(true);
  });
});

// ── Call Brief ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

describe("generateCallBrief", () => {
  it("includes deal context (route, estimate, operator)", () => {
    const deal = createDealContext("live");
    updateDealContext(deal, {
      trip: { departure: "KTEB", arrival: "KPBI", date: "2026-04-15", pax: 4, aircraftClass: "light", aircraftType: null, isRoundTrip: false, specialRequirements: [] },
      client: { name: "Test", contact: "t@t.com", type: "new", budget: { min: 25000, max: 40000 }, substitutionPref: null, qualified: true, qualificationReason: "test" },
      estimate: { source: "aviapages", flightTime: 150, priceRange: { low: 25000, high: 35000 }, fuelBurn: 280, positioning: null, calculatedAt: new Date().toISOString() },
      sourcing: { selectedOperator: { operatorId: "op_001", operatorName: "Elite Aviation", phone: null, baseAirport: "KTEB", aircraftType: "Phenom 300E", aircraftTail: null, score: 0.85, scoreBreakdown: { proximity: 0.9, laneHistory: 0.5, responseTime: 0.5, pricingAccuracy: 0.5, relationship: 0.5 }, distanceToDeparture: 0 }, backupOperator: null, rankingReason: "test", laneHistory: [] },
    });

    const brief = generateCallBrief(deal);

    expect(brief.route).toBe("KTEB → KPBI");
    expect(brief.pax).toBe(4);
    expect(brief.operatorName).toBe("Elite Aviation");
    expect(brief.estimateRange).toContain("$25,000");
    expect(brief.talkingPoints.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(brief.talkingPoints.some((p) => p.includes("budget"))).toBe(true);
  });
});

// ── CallStore ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

describe("CallStore", () => {
  function makeStore(): { store: CallStore; cleanup: () => void } {
    const db = createTestDb();
    return { store: new CallStore(db), cleanup: () => db.close() };
  }

  it("save persists and getByDealId retrieves", () => {
    const { store, cleanup } = makeStore();
    store.saveCall(makeCall());

    expect(store.getByDealId("deal_001")).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(store.getByDealId("deal_001")[0]!.callId).toBe("call_001");
    cleanup();
  });

  it("getById returns correct call", () => {
    const { store, cleanup } = makeStore();
    store.saveCall(makeCall());

    expect(store.getById("call_001")).not.toBeNull();
    expect(store.getById("call_999")).toBeNull();
    cleanup();
  });

  it("count returns total calls", () => {
    const { store, cleanup } = makeStore();
    store.saveCall(makeCall());
    expect(store.count).toBe(1);
    cleanup();
  });
});

// ── Integration ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

describe("integration: entity detection updates deal context", () => {
  it("detected price entity can be used to update deal", () => {
    const chunks = [makeChunk("We can do $28,000 all-in for the Phenom 300E N300EA")];
    const entities = extractAllEntities(chunks);

    const prices = entities.filter((e) => e.type === "price");
    expect(prices.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);

    // Simulate: router would take this price and create an advance_to_quoted action
    const price = parseFloat(prices[0]!.value.replace(/[$,]/g, ""));
    expect(price).toBe(28000);
  });
});
// === tests/composite-stores.test.ts ===
/**
 * Tests for Composite Object Stores — RouteIntelStore, OperatorProfileStore,
 * ClientProfileStore, ValidationLedgerStore, and JsonFileStore base behavior.
 *
 * Coverage:
 * 1. RouteIntelStore: get/upsert/invalidate/stale TTL, count, getAll
 * 2. OperatorProfileStore: get/getMany/upsert/stale handling
 * 3. ClientProfileStore: case-insensitive lookup by name/email, upsert matching
 * 4. ValidationLedgerStore: append-only, getByRoute, getBooked, getByDateRange
 * 5. JsonFileStore base: load from file, corrupt JSON, missing file, save persistence
 */

import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach, afterEach } from "vitest";
import { createTestDb } from "../src/memory/database.js";
import type Database from "better-sqlite3";
import {
  RouteIntelStore,
  OperatorProfileStore,
  ClientProfileStore,
  ValidationLedgerStore,
} from "../src/memory/composite-stores.js";
import type {
  RouteIntel,
  OperatorProfile,
  ClientProfile,
  ValidationLedgerEntry,
} from "../src/schemas/composite-objects.js";

const NOW = new Date().toISOString();
const STALE = new Date(Date.now() - 25 * 60 * 60 * 1000).toISOString(); // 25h ago

function makeRouteIntel(overrides: Partial<RouteIntel> = {}): RouteIntel {
  return {
    route: "KTEB-KMIA",
    aircraftClass: "light",
    avgPrice30d: 32000,
    dealCount: 5,
    demandTrend: "stable",
    topOperators: ["Elite Aviation", "NetJets"],
    seasonalFactor: 1.0,
    avgMarginDelta: -0.03,
    lastUpdated: NOW,
    ...overrides,
  };
}

function makeOperatorProfile(overrides: Partial<OperatorProfile> = {}): OperatorProfile {
  return {
    name: "Elite Aviation",
    homeBase: "KTEB",
    certs: ["Part 135", "IS-BAO"],
    fleet: ["Phenom 300E", "Citation CJ3+"],
    avgResponseTime: 45,
    reliabilityScore: 0.92,
    pricingDelta: 0.05,
    paymentSpeed: 3,
    quoteToBookRate: 0.7,
    cancelRate: 0.02,
    dealCount: 12,
    lastUpdated: NOW,
    ...overrides,
  };
}

function makeClientProfile(overrides: Partial<ClientProfile> = {}): ClientProfile {
  return {
    name: "John Smith",
    email: "john@smith.com",
    dealHistory: [
      { route: "KTEB-KMIA", price: 30000, date: "2026-03-15", outcome: "booked" },
    ],
    avgPaymentTime: 4.5,
    totalSpend: 30000,
    tripFrequency: 3,
    preferredAircraftClass: "light",
    preferredRoutes: ["KTEB-KMIA"],
    lastUpdated: NOW,
    ...overrides,
  };
}

function makeLedgerEntry(overrides: Partial<ValidationLedgerEntry> = {}): ValidationLedgerEntry {
  return {
    route: "KTEB-KMIA",
    date: "2026-04-01",
    mode: "live",
    leadTimeHours: 48,
    rliAtQuote: "high",
    dumontPhysicsEstimate: 28000,
    ghostPositioningValue: 1200,
    operatorQuotedPrice: 30000,
    osaClientPrice: 32700,
    realizedMarginPct: 0.083,
    rfqsSent: 1,
    quotesReceived: 1,
    operatorResponseMinutes: 35,
    clientDecisionMinutes: 90,
    outcome: "booked",
    fleetResilienceUsed: false,
    tripCompleted: true,
    notes: "Clean deal",
    ...overrides,
  };
}

// ── SQLite Store Base Behavior ───────────────────────────────────────────

describe("SQLite store base behavior", () => {
  let db: Database.Database;

  beforeEach(() => {
    db = createTestDb();
  });
  afterEach(() => db.close());

  it("starts empty with fresh database", () => {
    const store = new RouteIntelStore(db);
    expect(store.count).toBe(0);
    expect(store.getAll()).toEqual([]);
  });

  it("persists data within the same database instance", () => {
    const store = new RouteIntelStore(db);
    store.upsert(makeRouteIntel());
    expect(store.count).toBe(1);

    // Another store instance on the same db sees the data
    const store2 = new RouteIntelStore(db);
    expect(store2.count).toBe(1);
  });

  it("upsert() persists and is immediately readable", () => {
    const store = new RouteIntelStore(db);
    store.upsert(makeRouteIntel());

    const all = store.getAll();
    expect(all).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(all[0]!.route).toBe("KTEB-KMIA");
  });
});

// ── RouteIntelStore ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────

describe("RouteIntelStore", () => {
  let db: Database.Database;
  let store: RouteIntelStore;

  beforeEach(() => {
    db = createTestDb();
    store = new RouteIntelStore(db);
  });
  afterEach(() => db.close());

  it("get() returns null for missing data", () => {
    expect(store.get("KTEB-KMIA", "light")).toBeNull();
  });

  it("get() returns null for stale data (>24h old)", () => {
    store.upsert(makeRouteIntel({ lastUpdated: STALE }));
    expect(store.get("KTEB-KMIA", "light")).toBeNull();
  });

  it("get() returns data when fresh", () => {
    const intel = makeRouteIntel();
    store.upsert(intel);

    const result = store.get("KTEB-KMIA", "light");
    expect(result).not.toBeNull();
    expect(result!.route).toBe("KTEB-KMIA");
    expect(result!.avgPrice30d).toBe(32000);
  });

  it("getIncludingStale() returns stale data", () => {
    store.upsert(makeRouteIntel({ lastUpdated: STALE }));

    const result = store.getIncludingStale("KTEB-KMIA", "light");
    expect(result).not.toBeNull();
    expect(result!.route).toBe("KTEB-KMIA");
  });

  it("getIncludingStale() returns null for missing data", () => {
    expect(store.getIncludingStale("NOPE-NADA", "heavy")).toBeNull();
  });

  it("upsert() inserts new records", () => {
    expect(store.count).toBe(0);
    store.upsert(makeRouteIntel());
    expect(store.count).toBe(1);
  });

  it("upsert() updates existing records (same route + aircraftClass)", () => {
    store.upsert(makeRouteIntel({ avgPrice30d: 30000 }));
    store.upsert(makeRouteIntel({ avgPrice30d: 35000 }));

    expect(store.count).toBe(1);
    expect(store.get("KTEB-KMIA", "light")!.avgPrice30d).toBe(35000);
  });

  it("upsert() inserts separately for different aircraftClass", () => {
    store.upsert(makeRouteIntel({ aircraftClass: "light" }));
    store.upsert(makeRouteIntel({ aircraftClass: "heavy" }));

    expect(store.count).toBe(2);
  });

  it("invalidate() marks record as stale", () => {
    store.upsert(makeRouteIntel());
    expect(store.get("KTEB-KMIA", "light")).not.toBeNull();

    store.invalidate("KTEB-KMIA", "light");
    expect(store.get("KTEB-KMIA", "light")).toBeNull();

    // But still accessible via getIncludingStale
    expect(store.getIncludingStale("KTEB-KMIA", "light")).not.toBeNull();
  });

  it("invalidate() is a no-op for missing records", () => {
    store.invalidate("NOPE-NADA", "light"); // should not throw
    expect(store.count).toBe(0);
  });

  it("count property reflects current record count", () => {
    expect(store.count).toBe(0);
    store.upsert(makeRouteIntel());
    expect(store.count).toBe(1);
    store.upsert(makeRouteIntel({ route: "KORD-KASE" }));
    expect(store.count).toBe(2);
  });

  it("getAll() returns copies (mutations do not affect store)", () => {
    store.upsert(makeRouteIntel());
    const all = store.getAll();
    all.pop();
    expect(store.count).toBe(1);
  });
});

// ── OperatorProfileStore ─────────────────────────────────────────────────

describe("OperatorProfileStore", () => {
  let db: Database.Database;
  let store: OperatorProfileStore;

  beforeEach(() => {
    db = createTestDb();
    store = new OperatorProfileStore(db);
  });
  afterEach(() => db.close());

  it("get() returns profile by name", () => {
    store.upsert(makeOperatorProfile());
    const result = store.get("Elite Aviation");
    expect(result).not.toBeNull();
    expect(result!.homeBase).toBe("KTEB");
  });

  it("get() returns null for missing operator", () => {
    expect(store.get("Ghost Operator")).toBeNull();
  });

  it("get() returns null for stale profile", () => {
    store.upsert(makeOperatorProfile({ lastUpdated: STALE }));
    expect(store.get("Elite Aviation")).toBeNull();
  });

  it("getIncludingStale() returns stale profile", () => {
    store.upsert(makeOperatorProfile({ lastUpdated: STALE }));
    const result = store.getIncludingStale("Elite Aviation");
    expect(result).not.toBeNull();
    expect(result!.name).toBe("Elite Aviation");
  });

  it("getMany() filters stale entries", () => {
    store.upsert(makeOperatorProfile({ name: "Fresh Op" }));
    store.upsert(makeOperatorProfile({ name: "Stale Op", lastUpdated: STALE }));

    const results = store.getMany(["Fresh Op", "Stale Op"]);
    expect(results).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(results[0]!.name).toBe("Fresh Op");
  });

  it("getMany() returns empty for unknown names", () => {
    const results = store.getMany(["Unknown1", "Unknown2"]);
    expect(results).toEqual([]);
  });

  it("getMany() returns multiple fresh profiles", () => {
    store.upsert(makeOperatorProfile({ name: "Op A" }));
    store.upsert(makeOperatorProfile({ name: "Op B" }));

    const results = store.getMany(["Op A", "Op B"]);
    expect(results).toHaveLength(2);
  });

  it("upsert() updates existing by name", () => {
    store.upsert(makeOperatorProfile({ reliabilityScore: 0.8 }));
    store.upsert(makeOperatorProfile({ reliabilityScore: 0.95 }));

    expect(store.count).toBe(1);
    expect(store.get("Elite Aviation")!.reliabilityScore).toBe(0.95);
  });
});

// ── ClientProfileStore ───────────────────────────────────────────────────

describe("ClientProfileStore", () => {
  let db: Database.Database;
  let store: ClientProfileStore;

  beforeEach(() => {
    db = createTestDb();
    store = new ClientProfileStore(db);
  });
  afterEach(() => db.close());

  it("get() case-insensitive name matching", () => {
    store.upsert(makeClientProfile());

    expect(store.get("john smith")).not.toBeNull();
    expect(store.get("JOHN SMITH")).not.toBeNull();
    expect(store.get("John Smith")).not.toBeNull();
  });

  it("get() case-insensitive email matching", () => {
    store.upsert(makeClientProfile());

    expect(store.get("JOHN@SMITH.COM")).not.toBeNull();
    expect(store.get("john@smith.com")).not.toBeNull();
  });

  it("get() returns null for empty string", () => {
    store.upsert(makeClientProfile());
    expect(store.get("")).toBeNull();
  });

  it("get() returns null for unknown client", () => {
    expect(store.get("nobody@nowhere.com")).toBeNull();
  });

  it("get() trims whitespace from search string", () => {
    store.upsert(makeClientProfile());
    expect(store.get("  john smith  ")).not.toBeNull();
  });

  it("upsert() matches on email first, then name", () => {
    store.upsert(makeClientProfile({ name: "John Smith", email: "john@smith.com", totalSpend: 30000 }));

    // Update with same email, different name — should match by email
    store.upsert(makeClientProfile({ name: "Jonathan Smith", email: "john@smith.com", totalSpend: 60000 }));

    expect(store.count).toBe(1);
    expect(store.get("john@smith.com")!.name).toBe("Jonathan Smith");
    expect(store.get("john@smith.com")!.totalSpend).toBe(60000);
  });

  it("upsert() falls back to name match when email differs", () => {
    store.upsert(makeClientProfile({ name: "John Smith", email: "john@old.com" }));

    // Same name, different email, no email match — falls back to name
    store.upsert(makeClientProfile({ name: "John Smith", email: "john@new.com" }));

    expect(store.count).toBe(1);
    expect(store.get("John Smith")!.email).toBe("john@new.com");
  });

  it("upsert() inserts new client when no match", () => {
    store.upsert(makeClientProfile({ name: "Alice", email: "alice@ex.com" }));
    store.upsert(makeClientProfile({ name: "Bob", email: "bob@ex.com" }));

    expect(store.count).toBe(2);
  });
});

// ── ValidationLedgerStore ────────────────────────────────────────────────

describe("ValidationLedgerStore", () => {
  let db: Database.Database;
  let store: ValidationLedgerStore;

  beforeEach(() => {
    db = createTestDb();
    store = new ValidationLedgerStore(db);
  });
  afterEach(() => db.close());

  it("append() adds entries", () => {
    store.append(makeLedgerEntry());
    expect(store.count).toBe(1);

    store.append(makeLedgerEntry({ route: "KORD-KASE" }));
    expect(store.count).toBe(2);
  });

  it("append() is truly append-only (no dedup)", () => {
    const entry = makeLedgerEntry();
    store.append(entry);
    store.append(entry);
    expect(store.count).toBe(2);
  });

  it("getByRoute() filters correctly", () => {
    store.append(makeLedgerEntry({ route: "KTEB-KMIA" }));
    store.append(makeLedgerEntry({ route: "KORD-KASE" }));
    store.append(makeLedgerEntry({ route: "KTEB-KMIA" }));

    const results = store.getByRoute("KTEB-KMIA");
    expect(results).toHaveLength(2);
  });

  it("getByRoute() returns empty for unknown route", () => {
    store.append(makeLedgerEntry());
    expect(store.getByRoute("NOPE-NADA")).toEqual([]);
  });

  it("getBooked() returns only booked outcomes", () => {
    store.append(makeLedgerEntry({ outcome: "booked" }));
    store.append(makeLedgerEntry({ outcome: "lost_price" }));
    store.append(makeLedgerEntry({ outcome: "booked" }));
    store.append(makeLedgerEntry({ outcome: "lost_ghosted" }));

    const booked = store.getBooked();
    expect(booked).toHaveLength(2);
    expect(booked.every((e) => e.outcome === "booked")).toBe(true);
  });

  it("getBooked() returns empty when no booked deals", () => {
    store.append(makeLedgerEntry({ outcome: "lost_price" }));
    store.append(makeLedgerEntry({ outcome: "lost_timing" }));

    expect(store.getBooked()).toEqual([]);
  });

  it("getByDateRange() inclusive filtering", () => {
    store.append(makeLedgerEntry({ date: "2026-03-15" }));
    store.append(makeLedgerEntry({ date: "2026-04-01" }));
    store.append(makeLedgerEntry({ date: "2026-04-15" }));
    store.append(makeLedgerEntry({ date: "2026-05-01" }));

    const results = store.getByDateRange("2026-04-01", "2026-04-15");
    expect(results).toHaveLength(2);
  });

  it("getByDateRange() returns empty for out-of-range dates", () => {
    store.append(makeLedgerEntry({ date: "2026-01-01" }));
    store.append(makeLedgerEntry({ date: "2026-06-01" }));

    expect(store.getByDateRange("2026-03-01", "2026-03-31")).toEqual([]);
  });

  it("getByDateRange() includes boundary dates exactly", () => {
    store.append(makeLedgerEntry({ date: "2026-04-01" }));
    store.append(makeLedgerEntry({ date: "2026-04-30" }));

    const results = store.getByDateRange("2026-04-01", "2026-04-30");
    expect(results).toHaveLength(2);
  });

  it("persists entries across store instances on same db", () => {
    const store1 = new ValidationLedgerStore(db);
    store1.append(makeLedgerEntry());
    store1.append(makeLedgerEntry({ route: "KORD-KASE" }));

    // New store instance on same database
    const store2 = new ValidationLedgerStore(db);
    expect(store2.count).toBe(2);
    expect(store2.getByRoute("KORD-KASE")).toHaveLength(1);
  });
});
// === tests/edge-cases.test.ts ===
/**
 * Edge case tests — covers boundary conditions and unusual inputs
 * across all capabilities. Added during QA audit.
 */

import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach } from "vitest";
import {
  extractPax, extractBudget, extractDate, extractRoute,
  extractClientName, parseRequestText, qualify, intake,
} from "../src/capabilities/intake.js";
import type { ParsedRequest } from "../src/capabilities/intake.js";
import { checksAfterIntake, checksAfterQuote } from "../src/capabilities/guardian.js";
import type { AirportData } from "../src/capabilities/guardian.js";
import { estimate, quote, calcEstimateDelta } from "../src/capabilities/quote.js";
import { sourcing } from "../src/capabilities/sourcing.js";
import type { OperatorRecord, AirportCoords } from "../src/capabilities/sourcing.js";
import { checkTimer } from "../src/capabilities/scenario.js";
import { AviapagesClient } from "../src/integrations/aviapages.js";
import type { FetchFn } from "../src/integrations/aviapages.js";
import { createDealContext, updateDealContext, _resetDealCounter } from "../src/orchestrator/deal-context-manager.js";

beforeEach(() => _resetDealCounter());

// ── Intake Edge Cases ────────────────────────────────────────────────────

describe("intake edge cases", () => {
  it("handles empty string input", () => {
    const parsed = parseRequestText("");
    expect(parsed.departure).toBeNull();
    expect(parsed.arrival).toBeNull();
    expect(parsed.pax).toBeNull();
    expect(parsed.date).toBeNull();
  });

  it("handles very long input (1000+ chars)", () => {
    const longText = "I need a flight from Teterboro to Palm Beach on April 15 for 4 passengers. Budget $30,000. " +
      "Additional context: ".repeat(50) + "End of message.";
    expect(longText.length).toBeGreaterThan(1000);
    const parsed = parseRequestText(longText, new Date(2026, 2, 31));
    expect(parsed.departure).toBe("KTEB");
    expect(parsed.arrival).toBe("KPBI");
    expect(parsed.pax).toBe(4);
  });

  it("handles Unicode in client name", () => {
    const name = extractClientName("Hi, this is José García");
    // May or may not extract depending on accent handling
    // The important thing is it doesn't crash
    expect(typeof name === "string" || name === null).toBe(true);
  });

  it("handles nested quotes in budget", () => {
    const budget = extractBudget('They said "$25k" is the target');
    expect(budget).not.toBeNull();
    expect(budget!.min).toBeGreaterThan(20000);
    expect(budget!.max).toBeLessThan(30000);
  });

  it("handles budget with no space after dollar sign", () => {
    const budget = extractBudget("budget$30,000");
    // May or may not parse — should not crash
    expect(typeof budget === "object" || budget === null).toBe(true);
  });

  it("extractPax returns null for zero passengers", () => {
    expect(extractPax("0 passengers")).toBe(0);
  });

  it("extractPax handles 'for 1' at end of flight context", () => {
    expect(extractPax("charter for 1")).toBe(1);
  });

  it("extractDate handles date at very start of string", () => {
    expect(extractDate("2026-05-15 fly from NY")).toBe("2026-05-15");
  });

  it("extractRoute handles all-caps city names", () => {
    const [dep, arr] = extractRoute("CHICAGO to MIAMI on April 10");
    // Should resolve even with caps
    expect(dep).toBe("KORD");
    expect(arr).toBe("KMIA");
  });

  it("qualify rejects when pax is zero", () => {
    const parsed: ParsedRequest = {
      departure: "KTEB", arrival: "KPBI", date: "2026-04-15", pax: 0,
      aircraftClass: null, budget: null, isRoundTrip: false,
      specialRequirements: [], clientName: null, clientContact: null,
    };
    expect(qualify(parsed).qualified).toBe(false);
  });

  it("intake handles whitespace-only request", async () => {
    const ctx = createDealContext("sim");
    const result = await intake("   \n\t  ", ctx, { today: new Date(2026, 2, 31) });
    expect(result.client.qualified).toBe(false);
  });
});

// ── Guardian Edge Cases ──────────────────────────────────────────────────

describe("guardian edge cases", () => {
  const airports: Map<string, AirportData> = new Map([
    ["KTEB", { icao: "KTEB", latitude: 40.8501, longitude: -74.0608, longest_runway_ft: 7000, customs_capable: true }],
    ["KPBI", { icao: "KPBI", latitude: 26.6832, longitude: -80.0956, longest_runway_ft: 10008, customs_capable: true }],
  ]);

  it("handles null aircraftType and null aircraftClass with valid airports", () => {
    const ctx = createDealContext("sim");
    updateDealContext(ctx, {
      trip: {
        departure: "KTEB", arrival: "KPBI", date: "2026-06-15",
        pax: 4, aircraftClass: null, aircraftType: null,
        isRoundTrip: false, specialRequirements: [],
      },
    });
    const checks = checksAfterIntake(ctx, airports);
    // Should have airport checks but skip runway/range/pax (no aircraft data)
    const depCheck = checks.find((c) => c.name === "departure_airport");
    expect(depCheck).toBeDefined();
    expect(depCheck!.passed).toBe(true);
    // No runway or range checks when no aircraft info
    const runwayCheck = checks.find((c) => c.name === "runway_departure");
    expect(runwayCheck).toBeUndefined();
  });

  it("handles midnight boundary date (start of day)", () => {
    const ctx = createDealContext("sim");
    // Tomorrow at midnight
    const tomorrow = new Date();
    tomorrow.setDate(tomorrow.getDate() + 1);
    const isoDate = `${tomorrow.getFullYear()}-${String(tomorrow.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0")}-${String(tomorrow.getDate()).padStart(2, "0")}`;
    updateDealContext(ctx, {
      trip: {
        departure: "KTEB", arrival: "KPBI", date: isoDate,
        pax: 4, aircraftClass: "light", aircraftType: "Phenom 300E",
        isRoundTrip: false, specialRequirements: [],
      },
    });
    const checks = checksAfterIntake(ctx, airports);
    const dateCheck = checks.find((c) => c.name === "date_future");
    expect(dateCheck).toBeDefined();
    expect(dateCheck!.passed).toBe(true);
  });

  it("handles today's date (parsed as UTC midnight may appear as yesterday in local tz)", () => {
    const ctx = createDealContext("sim");
    const today = new Date();
    const isoDate = `${today.getFullYear()}-${String(today.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0")}-${String(today.getDate()).padStart(2, "0")}`;
    updateDealContext(ctx, {
      trip: {
        departure: "KTEB", arrival: "KPBI", date: isoDate,
        pax: 4, aircraftClass: "light", aircraftType: "Phenom 300E",
        isRoundTrip: false, specialRequirements: [],
      },
    });
    const checks = checksAfterIntake(ctx, airports);
    const dateCheck = checks.find((c) => c.name === "date_future");
    expect(dateCheck).toBeDefined();
    // Result depends on timezone — just verify it doesn't crash and returns a boolean
    expect(typeof dateCheck!.passed).toBe("boolean");
  });
});

// ── Quote Edge Cases ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────

describe("quote edge cases", () => {
  it("handles zero operator price", async () => {
    const ctx = createDealContext("sim");
    const result = await quote(ctx, { operatorPrice: 0 });
    expect(result.quote.operatorPrice).toBe(0);
    expect(result.quote.osaPrice).toBe(0); // 0 / (1 - 0.08) = 0
  });

  it("clamps very small margin to 1% floor", async () => {
    const ctx = createDealContext("sim");
    const result = await quote(ctx, { operatorPrice: 28000, margin: 0.001 });
    // margin clamped to 0.01 minimum → $28,000 / 0.99 = $28,283
    expect(result.quote.osaPrice).toBe(Math.round(28000 / 0.99));
    expect(result.quote.margin).toBe(0.01);
  });

  it("calcEstimateDelta handles identical low and high", () => {
    const delta = calcEstimateDelta({ low: 30000, high: 30000 }, 30000);
    expect(delta).toBe(0);
  });

  it("calcEstimateDelta handles very large delta", () => {
    const delta = calcEstimateDelta({ low: 10000, high: 20000 }, 100000);
    // median 15000, delta = (100000 - 15000) / 15000 = 5.67
    expect(delta).toBeCloseTo(5.667, 2);
  });

  it("estimate returns manual source when departure is empty", async () => {
    const ctx = createDealContext("sim");
    updateDealContext(ctx, { trip: { departure: "", arrival: "" } });
    const mockFetch: FetchFn = async () => ({ ok: true, status: 200, json: async () => ({}) });
    const client = new AviapagesClient({ apiKey: "test" }, mockFetch);
    const result = await estimate(ctx, { aviapages: client });
    expect(result.estimate.source).toBe("manual");
  });
});

// ── Sourcing Edge Cases ──────────────────────────────────────────────────

describe("sourcing edge cases", () => {
  it("returns no match when all operators filtered by aircraft class", async () => {
    const ctx = createDealContext("sim");
    updateDealContext(ctx, {
      trip: {
        departure: "KTEB", arrival: "KPBI", date: "2026-04-15",
        pax: 4, aircraftClass: "ultra_long_range", aircraftType: null,
        isRoundTrip: false, specialRequirements: [],
      },
    });

    const operators: OperatorRecord[] = [
      {
        operatorId: "op_1", operatorName: "Light Only Air",
        phone: null, baseAirport: "KTEB", baseLatitude: 40.85, baseLongitude: -74.06,
        aircraftType: "Phenom 300E", aircraftTail: null,
        aircraftCategory: "light", passengerCapacity: 8, rangeNm: 2010,
      },
      {
        operatorId: "op_2", operatorName: "Midsize Only Air",
        phone: null, baseAirport: "KTEB", baseLatitude: 40.85, baseLongitude: -74.06,
        aircraftType: "Citation Latitude", aircraftTail: null,
        aircraftCategory: "midsize", passengerCapacity: 9, rangeNm: 2700,
      },
    ];

    const result = await sourcing(ctx, {
      operators,
      departureAirport: { icao: "KTEB", latitude: 40.85, longitude: -74.06 },
    });
    expect(result.sourcing.selectedOperator).toBeNull();
    expect(result.sourcing.rankingReason).toContain("No operators matched");
  });

  it("handles empty operator list", async () => {
    const ctx = createDealContext("sim");
    updateDealContext(ctx, {
      trip: {
        departure: "KTEB", arrival: "KPBI", date: "2026-04-15",
        pax: 4, aircraftClass: "light", aircraftType: null,
        isRoundTrip: false, specialRequirements: [],
      },
    });
    const result = await sourcing(ctx, { operators: [], departureAirport: null });
    expect(result.sourcing.selectedOperator).toBeNull();
  });
});

// ── Scenario Timer Edge Cases ────────────────────────────────────────────

describe("scenario timer edge cases", () => {
  it("returns expired at exact 100% boundary", () => {
    const start = new Date("2026-04-15T10:00:00Z");
    const end = new Date("2026-04-15T12:00:00Z");
    const atExact100 = new Date(end); // exactly at expiry
    const status = checkTimer(start.toISOString(), end.toISOString(), atExact100);
    expect(status.level).toBe("expired");
    expect(status.remainingMinutes).toBe(0);
  });

  it("returns red at 99.9% (just before expiry)", () => {
    const start = new Date("2026-04-15T10:00:00Z");
    const end = new Date("2026-04-15T12:00:00Z");
    const justBefore = new Date(end.getTime() - 100); // 100ms before expiry
    const status = checkTimer(start.toISOString(), end.toISOString(), justBefore);
    expect(status.level).toBe("red");
    expect(status.remainingMinutes).toBe(0); // rounds to 0
  });

  it("handles timer where start is after end (invalid window)", () => {
    const start = new Date("2026-04-15T14:00:00Z"); // after end
    const end = new Date("2026-04-15T12:00:00Z");
    const now = new Date("2026-04-15T13:00:00Z");
    const status = checkTimer(start.toISOString(), end.toISOString(), now);
    expect(status.level).toBe("expired");
  });
});

// ── Error Type Verification ──────────────────────────────────────────────

import {
  DumontError, IntakeError, GuardianError, PipelineError,
  ScenarioError as ScenarioErrorType,
} from "../src/utils/errors.js";

describe("error types", () => {
  it("DumontError carries dealId, stage, and timestamp", () => {
    const err = new DumontError("test", { dealId: "LIVE_001", stage: "intake" });
    expect(err.dealId).toBe("LIVE_001");
    expect(err.stage).toBe("intake");
    expect(err.timestamp).toBeDefined();
    expect(err.name).toBe("DumontError");
    expect(err instanceof Error).toBe(true);
  });

  it("IntakeError defaults stage to intake", () => {
    const err = new IntakeError("parse failed");
    expect(err.stage).toBe("intake");
    expect(err.name).toBe("IntakeError");
  });

  it("GuardianError carries blockReason", () => {
    const err = new GuardianError("blocked", { stage: "estimated", blockReason: "Budget mismatch" });
    expect(err.blockReason).toBe("Budget mismatch");
    expect(err.stage).toBe("estimated");
  });

  it("PipelineError carries outcome", () => {
    const err = new PipelineError("failed", { outcome: "failed_guardian" });
    expect(err.outcome).toBe("failed_guardian");
  });

  it("ScenarioError extends DumontError with stage=presented", () => {
    const err = new ScenarioErrorType("missing data");
    expect(err.stage).toBe("presented");
    expect(err instanceof Error).toBe(true);
  });
});
// === tests/sprint5-respond.test.ts ===
/**
 * Tests for Sprint 5: Respond + Draft Evaluator + Edit Memory + SDR + Confidence Gate.
 * Per spec: communication-layer.md Sprint 5, FR-4, FR-6, FR-9, FR-10.
 */

import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach } from "vitest";
import { getEditHistory, buildEditMemoryPromptSection } from "../src/memory/edit-memory.js";
import { AutonomyStore } from "../src/memory/autonomy-store.js";
import { evaluateConfidence, getRecipientType } from "../src/router/confidence-gate.js";
import { classifyLead } from "../src/capabilities/sdr.js";
import { evaluateDraft, summarizeDealForEval } from "../src/capabilities/draft-evaluator.js";
import { respond, buildRespondSystemPrompt } from "../src/capabilities/respond.js";
import { MessageStore } from "../src/memory/message-store.js";
import type { Message } from "../src/schemas/message.js";
import { generateMessageId } from "../src/schemas/message.js";
import { createDealContext, updateDealContext, _resetDealCounter } from "../src/orchestrator/deal-context-manager.js";
import { advanceStage } from "../src/orchestrator/stage-transitions.js";
import { createTestDb } from "../src/memory/database.js";

beforeEach(() => _resetDealCounter());

// ── Helpers ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

function makeStore(): { msgStore: MessageStore; autoStore: AutonomyStore; cleanup: () => void } {
  const db = createTestDb();
  return { msgStore: new MessageStore(db), autoStore: new AutonomyStore(db), cleanup: () => db.close() };
}

function makeMsg(overrides: Partial<Message> = {}): Message {
  return {
    id: generateMessageId(), dealId: "deal_001", channel: "email", direction: "outbound",
    from: "charter@osa.aero", to: "client@test.com", fromName: null, subject: null,
    body: "Your quote is ready.", bodyHtml: null, attachments: [],
    draftedBy: "dumont", approvedBy: null, editDistance: null, originalDraft: "Your quote is ready.",
    receivedAt: null, draftedAt: new Date().toISOString(), approvedAt: null,
    sentAt: new Date().toISOString(), responseToId: null, responseTimeMs: null, threadId: null,
    intent: "follow_up", parsedData: null, confidence: 0.8, confidenceLevel: "high",
    platformMessageId: null, platformMetadata: null, ...overrides,
  };
}

function makeDeal(opts?: { stage?: string; operatorPhone?: string }) {
  const deal = createDealContext("live");
  updateDealContext(deal, {
    client: { name: "Sarah", contact: "sarah@chen.com", type: "new", budget: { min: 25000, max: 40000 }, substitutionPref: null, qualified: true, qualificationReason: "test" },
    trip: { departure: "KTEB", arrival: "KPBI", date: "2026-04-15", pax: 4, aircraftClass: "light", aircraftType: null, isRoundTrip: false, specialRequirements: [] },
    estimate: { source: "aviapages", flightTime: 150, priceRange: { low: 25000, high: 35000 }, fuelBurn: 280, positioning: null, calculatedAt: new Date().toISOString() },
  });
  if (opts?.operatorPhone) {
    updateDealContext(deal, {
      sourcing: { selectedOperator: { operatorId: "op_001", operatorName: "Elite Aviation", phone: opts.operatorPhone, baseAirport: "KTEB", aircraftType: "Phenom 300E", aircraftTail: null, score: 0.85, scoreBreakdown: { proximity: 0.9, laneHistory: 0.5, responseTime: 0.5, pricingAccuracy: 0.5, relationship: 0.5 }, distanceToDeparture: 0 }, backupOperator: null, rankingReason: "test", laneHistory: [] },
      quote: { operatorPrice: 28000, osaPrice: 30435, margin: 0.08, validUntil: new Date(Date.now() + 7200000).toISOString(), aircraft: null, quotedAt: new Date().toISOString() },
    });
  }
  if (opts?.stage) {
    const path: Record<string, string[]> = { qualified: ["qualified"], estimated: ["qualified", "estimated"], sourced: ["qualified", "estimated", "sourced"], quoted: ["qualified", "estimated", "sourced", "quoted"], presented: ["qualified", "estimated", "sourced", "quoted", "presented"] };
    for (const s of path[opts.stage] ?? []) advanceStage(deal, s as any);
  }
  return deal;
}

// ── Edit Memory ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

describe("edit memory", () => {
  it("returns empty array on cold start", () => {
    const { msgStore, cleanup } = makeStore();
    const history = getEditHistory(msgStore, "follow_up", "email");
    expect(history).toHaveLength(0);
    cleanup();
  });

  it("returns edited drafts sorted by recency", () => {
    const { msgStore, cleanup } = makeStore();
    msgStore.saveMessage(makeMsg({ intent: "follow_up", editDistance: 3, originalDraft: "Draft v1", body: "Edited v1", sentAt: "2026-04-01T10:00:00.000Z" }));
    msgStore.saveMessage(makeMsg({ intent: "follow_up", editDistance: 2, originalDraft: "Draft v2", body: "Edited v2", sentAt: "2026-04-02T10:00:00.000Z" }));

    const history = getEditHistory(msgStore, "follow_up", "email");
    expect(history).toHaveLength(2);
    expect(history[0]!.dumontWrote).toBe("Draft v2"); // more recent first
    expect(history[0]!.brokerChangedTo).toBe("Edited v2");
    cleanup();
  });

  it("filters by intent + channel", () => {
    const { msgStore, cleanup } = makeStore();
    msgStore.saveMessage(makeMsg({ intent: "follow_up", channel: "email", editDistance: 1, originalDraft: "A", body: "B" }));
    msgStore.saveMessage(makeMsg({ intent: "quote_request", channel: "email", editDistance: 1, originalDraft: "C", body: "D" }));
    msgStore.saveMessage(makeMsg({ intent: "follow_up", channel: "bland_text", editDistance: 1, originalDraft: "E", body: "F" }));

    expect(getEditHistory(msgStore, "follow_up", "email")).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(getEditHistory(msgStore, "quote_request", "email")).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(getEditHistory(msgStore, "follow_up", "bland_text")).toHaveLength(1);
    cleanup();
  });

  it("caps at limit (default 10)", () => {
    const { msgStore, cleanup } = makeStore();
    for (let i = 0; i < 15; i++) {
      msgStore.saveMessage(makeMsg({ intent: "follow_up", editDistance: 1, originalDraft: `D${i}`, body: `E${i}`, sentAt: new Date(Date.now() + i * 1000).toISOString() }));
    }
    expect(getEditHistory(msgStore, "follow_up", "email")).toHaveLength(10);
    cleanup();
  });

  it("prompt section includes examples when available", () => {
    const { msgStore, cleanup } = makeStore();
    msgStore.saveMessage(makeMsg({ intent: "follow_up", editDistance: 2, originalDraft: "Dumont wrote this", body: "Broker sent this" }));

    const section = buildEditMemoryPromptSection(msgStore, "follow_up", "email");
    expect(section).toContain("STYLE LEARNING");
    expect(section).toContain("Dumont wrote this");
    expect(section).toContain("Broker sent this");
    cleanup();
  });

  it("prompt section is empty when no history", () => {
    const { msgStore, cleanup } = makeStore();
    expect(buildEditMemoryPromptSection(msgStore, "follow_up", "email")).toBe("");
    cleanup();
  });
});

// ── Autonomy Store ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────

describe("autonomy store", () => {
  it("creates default rule with autoSendEnabled=false", () => {
    const { autoStore, cleanup } = makeStore();
    const rule = autoStore.getRule("email", "follow_up", "client");
    expect(rule.autoSendEnabled).toBe(false);
    expect(rule.currentApprovals).toBe(0);
    expect(rule.autoSendAfter).toBe(10);
    cleanup();
  });

  it("graduates after 10 approvals with 0 edits", () => {
    const { autoStore, cleanup } = makeStore();
    for (let i = 0; i < 9; i++) {
      expect(autoStore.recordApproval("email", "follow_up", "client", 0)).toBe(false);
    }
    // 10th approval triggers graduation
    expect(autoStore.recordApproval("email", "follow_up", "client", 0)).toBe(true);
    expect(autoStore.isAutonomous("email", "follow_up", "client")).toBe(true);
    cleanup();
  });

  it("resets on any edit (editDistance > 0)", () => {
    const { autoStore, cleanup } = makeStore();
    for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) autoStore.recordApproval("email", "follow_up", "client", 0);
    // Edit resets
    autoStore.recordApproval("email", "follow_up", "client", 3);
    expect(autoStore.getRule("email", "follow_up", "client").currentApprovals).toBe(0);
    expect(autoStore.isAutonomous("email", "follow_up", "client")).toBe(false);
    cleanup();
  });

  it("manual revoke works", () => {
    const { autoStore, cleanup } = makeStore();
    for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) autoStore.recordApproval("email", "follow_up", "client", 0);
    expect(autoStore.isAutonomous("email", "follow_up", "client")).toBe(true);
    autoStore.revokeAutonomy("email", "follow_up", "client");
    expect(autoStore.isAutonomous("email", "follow_up", "client")).toBe(false);
    cleanup();
  });
});

// ── Confidence Gate ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────

describe("confidence gate", () => {
  it("blocks client-facing messages with dollar amounts", () => {
    const deal = makeDeal();
    const msg = makeMsg({ body: "Your all-in price is $30,435", to: "sarah@chen.com" });
    const decision = evaluateConfidence(msg, deal, null);
    expect(decision.action).toBe("blocked");
  });

  it("blocks negotiation intent", () => {
    const deal = makeDeal();
    const msg = makeMsg({ intent: "negotiation", body: "Counter offer" });
    const decision = evaluateConfidence(msg, deal, null);
    expect(decision.action).toBe("blocked");
  });

  it("blocks first contact with operator", () => {
    const deal = makeDeal({ operatorPhone: "+1-555-0100", stage: "sourced" });
    const msg = makeMsg({ to: "+1-555-0100", body: "Requesting quote", intent: "quote_request" });
    const decision = evaluateConfidence(msg, deal, null);
    expect(decision.action).toBe("blocked");
  });

  it("auto-sends when autonomy earned", () => {
    const { autoStore, cleanup } = makeStore();
    for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) autoStore.recordApproval("email", "follow_up", "client", 0);

    const deal = makeDeal({ operatorPhone: "+1-555-0100", stage: "presented" });
    const msg = makeMsg({ intent: "follow_up", body: "Any update?", to: "sarah@chen.com", confidenceLevel: "high" });
    const decision = evaluateConfidence(msg, deal, autoStore);
    expect(decision.action).toBe("auto_send");
    cleanup();
  });

  it("holds with delay for medium confidence", () => {
    const deal = makeDeal({ operatorPhone: "+1-555-0100", stage: "presented" });
    const msg = makeMsg({ intent: "info_request", body: "Here is the info", to: "sarah@chen.com", confidenceLevel: "medium" });
    const decision = evaluateConfidence(msg, deal, null);
    expect(decision.action).toBe("delayed");
  });

  it("holds for approval by default", () => {
    const deal = makeDeal();
    const msg = makeMsg({ body: "General message", intent: "general", confidenceLevel: "low" });
    const decision = evaluateConfidence(msg, deal, null);
    expect(decision.action).toBe("hold");
  });
});

// ── SDR Qualification ────────────────────────────────────────────────────

describe("SDR classification", () => {
  it("qualifies with budget when all fields present", () => {
    const result = classifyLead("I need a flight from Teterboro to Palm Beach on April 15 for 4 passengers. Budget $30,000.", null, new Date(2026, 2, 31));
    expect(result.classification).toBe("qualified_with_budget");
    expect(result.confidence).toBeGreaterThan(0.9);
    expect(result.extractedFields).toContain("departure");
    expect(result.extractedFields).toContain("budget");
  });

  it("qualifies without budget when route+date+pax present", () => {
    const result = classifyLead("Charter from Teterboro to Palm Beach on April 15, 4 passengers.", null, new Date(2026, 2, 31));
    expect(result.classification).toBe("qualified_no_budget");
    expect(result.shouldAutoRespond).toBe(true);
  });

  it("not qualified when missing critical fields", () => {
    const result = classifyLead("I want to fly somewhere nice");
    expect(result.classification).toBe("not_qualified");
    expect(result.reason).toContain("Missing");
  });

  it("budget mismatch when budget below estimate", () => {
    const deal = makeDeal();
    const result = classifyLead("Teterboro to Palm Beach April 15, 4 pax. Budget $10,000.", deal, new Date(2026, 2, 31));
    expect(result.classification).toBe("budget_mismatch");
  });
});

// ── Draft Evaluator ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────

describe("draft evaluator", () => {
  it("passes clean draft unchanged (no LLM)", async () => {
    const deal = makeDeal({ operatorPhone: "+1-555-0100", stage: "quoted" });
    const msg = makeMsg({ body: "Following up on your KTEB to KPBI request." });

    const result = await evaluateDraft(msg, deal);
    expect(result.wasRevised).toBe(false);
    expect(result.issues).toHaveLength(0);
  });

  it("catches unverifiable WiFi claim", async () => {
    const deal = makeDeal({ operatorPhone: "+1-555-0100", stage: "quoted" });
    const msg = makeMsg({ body: "The aircraft has WiFi and a fully stocked bar." });

    const result = await evaluateDraft(msg, deal);
    expect(result.issues.some((i) => i.includes("WiFi"))).toBe(true);
  });

  it("triggers revision when evaluator finds issues", async () => {
    const deal = makeDeal({ operatorPhone: "+1-555-0100", stage: "quoted" });
    const msg = makeMsg({ body: "WiFi available on this flight." });

    const result = await evaluateDraft(msg, deal, {
      evaluator: async () => ({ issues: ["WiFi claim unverifiable"], pass: false }),
      reviser: async (original, issues) => original.replace("WiFi available", "Contact us about amenities"),
    });

    expect(result.wasRevised).toBe(true);
    expect(result.draft).toContain("Contact us");
    expect(result.originalBeforeRevision).toContain("WiFi");
  });

  it("catches price mismatch", async () => {
    const deal = makeDeal({ operatorPhone: "+1-555-0100", stage: "quoted" });
    const msg = makeMsg({ body: "Your all-in price is $25,000 for this trip." });

    const result = await evaluateDraft(msg, deal);
    expect(result.issues.some((i) => i.includes("price") || i.includes("Price"))).toBe(true);
  });

  it("summarizes deal context correctly", () => {
    const deal = makeDeal({ operatorPhone: "+1-555-0100", stage: "quoted" });
    const summary = summarizeDealForEval(deal);
    expect(summary).toContain("KTEB");
    expect(summary).toContain("KPBI");
    expect(summary).toContain("Elite Aviation");
  });
});

// ── Respond Capability ───────────────────────────────────────────────────

describe("respond capability", () => {
  it("generates draft with correct intent and confidence", async () => {
    const deal = makeDeal();
    const inbound = makeMsg({ direction: "inbound", body: "Any update?", from: "sarah@chen.com" });

    const result = await respond(deal, inbound, "follow_up");

    expect(result.draft.draftedBy).toBe("dumont");
    expect(result.draft.intent).toBe("follow_up");
    expect(result.draft.direction).toBe("outbound");
    expect(result.confidenceLevel).toBe("high");
    expect(result.draft.body).toContain("KTEB");
  });

  it("system prompt includes edit memory when available", () => {
    const { msgStore, cleanup } = makeStore();
    msgStore.saveMessage(makeMsg({ intent: "follow_up", editDistance: 2, originalDraft: "Dumont wrote", body: "Broker fixed" }));

    const prompt = buildRespondSystemPrompt("follow_up", "email", msgStore);
    expect(prompt).toContain("STYLE LEARNING");
    expect(prompt).toContain("Dumont wrote");
    cleanup();
  });

  it("system prompt omits edit section on cold start", () => {
    const { msgStore, cleanup } = makeStore();
    const prompt = buildRespondSystemPrompt("follow_up", "email", msgStore);
    expect(prompt).not.toContain("STYLE LEARNING");
    cleanup();
  });

  it("quote_response gets confidence=never", async () => {
    const deal = makeDeal({ operatorPhone: "+1-555-0100", stage: "quoted" });
    const inbound = makeMsg({ direction: "inbound", body: "Send me the quote" });
    const result = await respond(deal, inbound, "quote_response");
    expect(result.confidenceLevel).toBe("never");
  });

  it("uses LLM draft function when provided", async () => {
    const deal = makeDeal();
    const inbound = makeMsg({ direction: "inbound", body: "Need a quote" });
    const result = await respond(deal, inbound, "follow_up", {
      draftFn: async () => "Custom LLM-generated draft content.",
    });
    expect(result.draft.body).toBe("Custom LLM-generated draft content.");
  });
});
// === tests/email-webform-adapter.test.ts ===
/**
 * Tests for Email Adapter (Resend), Web Form Adapter, and MessageStore.
 * Per spec: communication-layer.md Sprint 3.
 */

import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach } from "vitest";
import { ResendClient } from "../src/integrations/resend.js";
import type { FetchFn, ResendInboundWebhook } from "../src/integrations/resend.js";
import { EmailAdapter } from "../src/channels/email.js";
import { WebFormAdapter } from "../src/channels/web-form.js";
import type { WebFormPayload } from "../src/channels/web-form.js";
import { MessageStore } from "../src/memory/message-store.js";
import { renderTemplate, _clearTemplateCache } from "../src/templates/render.js";
import { routeMessage } from "../src/router/message-router.js";
import type { ActiveDeal } from "../src/router/thread-matcher.js";
import { createConversation, addMessageToConversation } from "../src/schemas/conversation.js";
import { generateMessageId } from "../src/schemas/message.js";
import type { Message } from "../src/schemas/message.js";
import { createDealContext, updateDealContext, _resetDealCounter } from "../src/orchestrator/deal-context-manager.js";
import { advanceStage } from "../src/orchestrator/stage-transitions.js";
import { createHmac } from "node:crypto";
import { createTestDb } from "../src/memory/database.js";

beforeEach(() => { _resetDealCounter(); _clearTemplateCache(); });

// ── Helpers ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

function mockFetch(body: Record<string, unknown> = { id: "resend_msg_001" }): FetchFn {
  return async () => ({ ok: true, status: 200, json: async () => body });
}

function mockFetchFail(status = 500): FetchFn {
  return async () => ({ ok: false, status, json: async () => ({}) });
}

function inboundEmail(overrides?: Partial<ResendInboundWebhook["data"]>): ResendInboundWebhook {
  return {
    type: "email.received",
    data: {
      id: "resend_in_001",
      from: "operator@elite-aviation.com",
      from_name: "John at Elite Aviation",
      to: ["charter@osa.aero"],
      subject: "Re: Quote Request KTEB→KPBI",
      text: "We can do this for $28,000 all-in. Phenom 300E N300EA, 2022.",
      html: "<p>We can do this for $28,000 all-in.</p>",
      headers: {
        "In-Reply-To": "<msg_original@osa.aero>",
        "References": "<msg_original@osa.aero>",
      },
      attachments: [],
      created_at: "2026-04-03T14:30:00.000Z",
      ...overrides,
    },
  };
}

function makeActiveDeal(opts?: { clientContact?: string; operatorEmail?: string; stage?: string }): ActiveDeal {
  const deal = createDealContext("live");
  updateDealContext(deal, {
    client: { name: "Test", contact: opts?.clientContact ?? "client@test.com", type: "new", budget: { min: 25000, max: 40000 }, substitutionPref: null, qualified: true, qualificationReason: "test" },
    trip: { departure: "KTEB", arrival: "KPBI", date: "2026-04-15", pax: 4, aircraftClass: "light", aircraftType: null, isRoundTrip: false, specialRequirements: [] },
  });
  if (opts?.operatorEmail) {
    updateDealContext(deal, {
      sourcing: {
        selectedOperator: {
          operatorId: "op_001", operatorName: "Elite Aviation", phone: opts.operatorEmail,
          baseAirport: "KTEB", aircraftType: "Phenom 300E", aircraftTail: null, score: 0.85,
          scoreBreakdown: { proximity: 0.9, laneHistory: 0.5, responseTime: 0.5, pricingAccuracy: 0.5, relationship: 0.5 },
          distanceToDeparture: 0,
        },
      },
    });
  }
  if (opts?.stage) {
    const path: Record<string, string[]> = {
      qualified: ["qualified"], estimated: ["qualified", "estimated"],
      sourced: ["qualified", "estimated", "sourced"],
      quoted: ["qualified", "estimated", "sourced", "quoted"],
      presented: ["qualified", "estimated", "sourced", "quoted", "presented"],
    };
    for (const s of path[opts.stage] ?? []) advanceStage(deal, s as any);
  }
  return { deal, conversation: createConversation(deal.id) };
}

// ── Resend Client ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

describe("ResendClient", () => {
  it("sends email with correct payload", async () => {
    let captured: unknown;
    const fetch: FetchFn = async (_url, init) => { captured = JSON.parse(init.body as string); return { ok: true, status: 200, json: async () => ({ id: "msg_001" }) }; };
    const client = new ResendClient({ apiKey: "re_test" }, fetch);

    const result = await client.sendEmail("op@test.com", "Quote Request", "<p>Hello</p>", { headers: { "In-Reply-To": "<prev>" } });

    expect(result.success).toBe(true);
    expect(result.messageId).toBe("msg_001");
    expect((captured as any).to).toEqual(["op@test.com"]);
    expect((captured as any).subject).toBe("Quote Request");
    expect((captured as any).headers["In-Reply-To"]).toBe("<prev>");
  });

  it("handles API error", async () => {
    const client = new ResendClient({ apiKey: "re_test" }, mockFetchFail());
    const result = await client.sendEmail("op@test.com", "Test", "<p>x</p>");
    expect(result.success).toBe(false);
    expect(result.error).toContain("500");
  });

  it("verifies valid webhook signature", () => {
    const secret = "whsec_resend_test";
    const client = new ResendClient({ apiKey: "test", webhookSecret: secret });
    const payload = JSON.stringify(inboundEmail());
    const sig = createHmac("sha256", secret).update(payload).digest("hex");
    expect(client.verifyWebhook(payload, sig)).toBe(true);
  });

  it("rejects invalid webhook signature", () => {
    const client = new ResendClient({ apiKey: "test", webhookSecret: "secret" });
    expect(client.verifyWebhook("payload", "wrong")).toBe(false);
  });
});

// ── Email Adapter receive ────────────────────────────────────────────────

describe("EmailAdapter receive", () => {
  it("parses inbound email into Message with channel=email", () => {
    const client = new ResendClient({ apiKey: "test" }, mockFetch());
    const adapter = new EmailAdapter(client);

    const msg = adapter.receive(inboundEmail());

    expect(msg.channel).toBe("email");
    expect(msg.direction).toBe("inbound");
    expect(msg.from).toBe("operator@elite-aviation.com");
    expect(msg.fromName).toBe("John at Elite Aviation");
    expect(msg.subject).toBe("Re: Quote Request KTEB→KPBI");
    expect(msg.body).toContain("$28,000");
    expect(msg.bodyHtml).toContain("<p>");
  });

  it("extracts In-Reply-To as responseToId", () => {
    const client = new ResendClient({ apiKey: "test" }, mockFetch());
    const adapter = new EmailAdapter(client);

    const msg = adapter.receive(inboundEmail());
    expect(msg.responseToId).toBe("<msg_original@osa.aero>");
  });

  it("extracts References as threadId", () => {
    const client = new ResendClient({ apiKey: "test" }, mockFetch());
    const adapter = new EmailAdapter(client);

    const msg = adapter.receive(inboundEmail());
    expect(msg.threadId).toBe("<msg_original@osa.aero>");
  });

  it("handles email with attachments", () => {
    const client = new ResendClient({ apiKey: "test" }, mockFetch());
    const adapter = new EmailAdapter(client);

    const msg = adapter.receive(inboundEmail({
